ReLaG's inferred groups provide a cheap estimate of effective dataset size, enabling diversity-aware dataset scaling. ReLaG is open source and can be installed with pip install relag.

</details>


### [36] [When a Flatness Proxy Is Not a Function: Robustness Certificates and Training Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38540)
*Vicente Opazo,Jose Calatayud-Mateu,Cristobal Rojas,Cristian Buc Calderon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出：一个曲率上界的“有效性”本身不足以支撑将其用作鲁棒性证书或内在训练干预；文中以最后一层相对平坦度代理为对象，揭示了证书会严重低估损失增量、软最大行平移会无界放大该代理等问题，并给出行中心化与商正则化修复方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对平坦度/曲率上界常被直接用来推导鲁棒性证书或作为训练干预（正则化）的依据，但作者认为“是有效的曲率上界”与“能作为证书/干预”是两回事。论文动机即在于严格区分这两种用途，检验该代理在这两种场景下是否真正成立。

Method: 1) 分析有限全局经验风险最小点处的一阶损失项，检验保留的证书表达式是否仍能覆盖真实损失增量，并推导一个全局有效、规范不变的特征空间修复。2) 研究公共行软最大平移这一保持预测与精确收缩的对称性，考察其对相对平坦度代理的影响；比较不同参考类选择与中心化表示，分析标量重调能否对齐诱导的概率更新，并给出行中心化的轨道最小界。3) 在算法模型与图像模型上进行45组配对单步测试，比较原始正则化与商正则化预测器的对齐情况，并在CIFAR-10上做长时程实验。

Result: 在有限全局经验风险最小点，保留的证书表达式会把损失增量低估超过210倍；公共行软最大平移保持预测与精确收缩，却能让该代理无界增长，即使标准参考类选择平均也会使其相对中心化表示翻倍；对单固定特征、至少三类的例子，标量重调一般无法对齐诱导的概率更新；行中心化给出轨道最小界并恢复数值与全模型梯度在该对称性下的不变性。45组配对单步测试中，放大的平移能区分原始正则化预测器，而商正则化预测器保持对齐；CIFAR-10长时程实验显示泛化被显著且可逆地抑制，但“记忆后选择性延迟”的证据较不一致。

Conclusion: 曲率上界的有效性本身既不自动构成鲁棒性证书，也不自动构成可微的内在训练干预；需要额外的规范不变性或对称性处理（如行中心化、商正则化）才能避免证书低估与代理无界放大。

Abstract: A valid curvature upper bound need not justify either a robustness certificate or an intervention on an intrinsic predictor property. We demonstrate this distinction for a last-layer relative-flatness proxy used in both settings. First, empirical-risk stationarity does not eliminate pointwise first-order loss terms: at a finite global empirical-risk minimum, the retained certificate expression underestimates a loss increase by over $210\times$. We derive a globally valid, gauge-invariant feature-space repair. Second, common-row softmax shifts preserve predictions and the exact contraction while making the proxy unbounded. Even standard reference-class choices double it on average relative to the centered representation. For a single fixed-feature example with at least three classes, scalar retuning generically cannot align the induced probability updates. Row centering gives the orbit-minimized bound and restores value and full-model gradient invariance under this symmetry. Across 45 paired one-step tests on algorithmic and image models, amplified shifts separate raw-regularized predictors while quotient-regularized predictors remain aligned. Long-horizon CIFAR-10 experiments show substantial, reversible suppression of generalization, while evidence for selective delay after memorization is less consistent. Together, these results show that validity as a curvature upper bound does not by itself justify either inversion into a robustness certificate or differentiation into an intrinsic training intervention.

</details>


### [37] [Towards Universal Wasserstein Barycenters through Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38547)
*Eduardo Fernandes Montesuma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 BaryFM，基于流匹配实现对 Wasserstein 单纯形上所有重心（整个权重族）的通用逼近，并采样任意重心测度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wasserstein 重心是概率机器学习中的核心工具，但现有方法多为固定权重向量计算单一重心，对整个单纯形上重心族的逼近（Universal Barycenter Approximation）研究不足。

Method: 提出 BaryFM，一种流匹配（flow matching）模型，将边缘测度传输到 Wasserstein 单纯形中的任意重心；训练后可通过求解常微分方程（ODE）从单纯形内任意测度采样。

Result: 在 4 个下游任务上验证：域适应、泛化、贝叶斯后验聚合与算法公平性；在 10 个域适应基准上，BaryFM 在 15 种对比方法中取得最佳平均排名，达到或超越非通用求解器。

Conclusion: BaryFM 以单一训练模型统一逼近整个 Wasserstein 重心族，兼具通用性与竞争性精度，拓展了 Wasserstein 重心的实用范围。

Abstract: Defining a weighted mean over probability measures under probability metrics is a central tool in probabilistic machine learning. Under the Wasserstein metric, these are called \emph{Wasserstein barycenters}. While most approaches compute barycenters for a fixed weight vector, approximating the whole family of barycenters over the simplex, which we call the \emph{Wasserstein simplex}, remains underexplored. We refer to this problem as \emph{Universal Barycenter Approximation}, and propose \texttt{BaryFM}, a flow matching model transporting the marginal measures into any barycenter in the Wasserstein simplex. Once trained, the network can draw samples from measures in the Wasserstein simplex through an ordinary differential equation. We validate our method on 4 downstream tasks: domain adaptation, generalization, Bayesian posterior aggregation and algorithmic fairness. \texttt{BaryFM} achieves the best average rank among 15 competing methods across 10 domain adaptation benchmarks, matching or surpassing non-universal solvers.

</details>


### [38] [The Advantages of Fresh Sketching for Ridge Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38565)
*Linkai Ma,Qilin Li,Petros Drineas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在欠约束迭代岭回归中，每步重新抽取新草图（fresh sketching）相比复用同一草图具有可证优势，并通过方向性误差分析导出更锐利的收敛保证与残差感知采样规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去25年，草图与采样被广泛用于加速大规模回归。在迭代随机求解器中，是否每步复用同一草图还是重新抽取随机数是一个基本设计选择。然而对于（欠约束）迭代岭回归中的列采样，fresh sketches 是否具有可证优势此前一直未解决。本文旨在回答这一开放问题。

Method: 作者采用方向性分析视角：fresh sketching 允许仅沿当前残差解分析误差，而非在整个 Gram 矩阵上统一分析。基于此，作者为 leverage score 和 ridge leverage score 采样给出更锐利的收敛保证，并进一步推导残差感知采样规则——通过最小化相关草图矩阵-向量乘积的方差，得到 oracle 分布及其实际近似，包括一种带（稍弱）收敛保证的混合采样分布。

Result: 在合成数据与真实数据上的实验（包括对 Qwen2.5 表示的 ridge probes）支持了理论结果，显示出显著更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: 本文证明了 fresh sketching 在迭代岭回归中具有可证优势，方向性误差分析带来更锐利的收敛保证和实用的残差感知采样规则，实验验证了其加速效果。

Abstract: Over the past 25 years, sketching and sampling have become widely used tools for accelerating large-scale regression. In iterative randomized solvers, a basic design choice is whether to $\textit{reuse}$ the same sketch or draw $\textit{fresh}$ randomness at every step. For (under-constrained) iterative ridge regression with column sampling, whether fresh sketches offer provable advantages has remained open: $\textit{We show that they do.}$ Fresh sketching lets us analyze error only along the current residual solution, rather than uniformly over the entire Gram matrix. This directional view yields sharper convergence guarantees for leverage score and ridge leverage score sampling and, more importantly, leads to residual-aware sampling rules. By minimizing the variance of the relevant sketched matrix-vector product, we derive an oracle distribution and practical approximations to the oracle distribution, including a mixture sampling distribution with (somewhat weaker) convergence guarantees. Experiments on synthetic and real data, including ridge probes on Qwen2.5 representations, support our theory, showing substantially faster convergence.

</details>


### [39] [FlexRouter: Learning Complementary Model Sets for Flexible LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38585)
*Wang Wei,Harry Yang,Tiankai Yang,Samyadeep Basu,Hongjie Chen,Andy Zhao,Franck Dernoncourt,Ryan A. Rossi,Hoda Eldardiry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FlexRouter，用DPP建模模型互补性，优化答案覆盖率，实现无固定预算的灵活路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM路由方法独立打分选top-k，忽略模型相关性并强制固定计算预算，导致选出失败模式相似的冗余模型，降低整体成功率。

Method: 将路由形式化为面向覆盖的子集选择问题，用行列式点过程（DPP）建模模型能力与冗余度；设计基于失败集边际化的训练目标以直接优化覆盖率，无需真实目标子集；推理时用基于边际对数行列式增益的贪心策略，自适应确定子集大小。

Result: 在RouterEval大规模基准上，FlexRouter在域内和域外任务上均取得更高覆盖率与更低冗余度，优于强基线，同时保持灵活的推理成本。

Conclusion: 显式建模模型互补性并优化答案覆盖率，可有效提升LLM路由效果，且无需预设计算预算。

Abstract: Existing Large Language Model (LLM) routing methods score LLMs independently to select top-$k$ models. However, this ignores model correlations and enforces a rigid computational budget. Consequently, routers often select redundant models that share failure modes, limiting the overall probability of success. To address this, we propose FlexRouter, a routing framework that explicitly models model complementarity. FlexRouter optimizes for \textit{answer coverage}, maximizing the probability that at least one selected model yields a correct response. This objective aligns with practical inference pipelines where multiple candidate outputs are generated and a downstream verifier or user selects the final one. We formulate routing as a coverage-oriented subset selection problem and model the routing policy using Determinantal Point Processes (DPPs), which naturally capture both model competence and redundancy. To directly optimize coverage without requiring a ground-truth target subset, we introduce a training objective based on marginalizing over failure sets. During inference, we employ a greedy strategy based on marginal log-determinant gains, enabling the router to adaptively determine subset sizes without a predefined budget. Extensive experiments on the large-scale RouterEval benchmark demonstrate that our proposed FlexRouter achieves higher coverage with lower redundancy across both in-domain and out-of-domain tasks than strong baselines while maintaining flexible inference cost.

</details>


### [40] [NeurDuo-EEG: A Long-Sequence EEG Foundation Model with Persistent State and Explicit Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38587)
*Yifan Wang,Haiping Liu,Yang Cui,Wenhao Cai,Shuhang Li,Xiaoyang Huang,Xianyang Liu,Jingyu Sun,Yizheng Sun,Cunhang Fan,Tianming Du,Jiancheng Yang,Zhenhong Li,Yunhao Zhang,Hongpeng Zhou,Jingyuan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出因果EEG基础模型NeurDuo-EEG，采用通道级持久记忆与多时间尺度记忆管理，在5个下游任务中的4个取得最佳性能，并支持高效流式推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG连续记录数小时，相关动态跨越毫秒到小时的多个时间尺度。现有EEG基础模型大多独立处理固定窗口，难以捕捉长时间尺度动态中的信息；状态空间架构虽支持持久循环处理，但长程信息仍被隐式压缩在循环状态中。

Method: NeurDuo-EEG是带通道级持久记忆的因果EEG基础模型。它引入多时间尺度记忆管理，包含可学习的记忆巩固与选择性检索，以固定大小状态实现连续EEG的持久建模。模型在17个公开数据集、3,955小时EEG上，通过离散频谱码的多通道自回归预测进行预训练。

Result: 在3个短窗口和2个长序列下游任务共5个基准中，NeurDuo-EEG在4个上取得最佳性能，包括全部3个短窗口任务和癫痫发作检测，其中AUC-PR从最强非NeurDuo基线的0.285提升至0.471；在睡眠分期上保持竞争力。模型支持高效流式推理，当可用历史增至一小时时，每块延迟近乎恒定，Small变体仅需4.7M骨干参数。

Conclusion: 结果证明了持久、多时间尺度建模对长序列与短窗口EEG分析均具有价值。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) is recorded continuously over hours, with relevant dynamics spanning timescales from milliseconds to hours. Most EEG foundation models nevertheless process fixed windows independently, limiting their ability to capture information encoded in long-timescale dynamics. State-space architectures enable persistent recurrent processing, but long-range information remains implicitly compressed in recurrent states. We present NeurDuo-EEG, a causal EEG foundation model with channel-resolved persistent memory. NeurDuo-EEG introduces multi-timescale memory management with learned consolidation and selective retrieval, enabling persistent modelling of continuous EEG with fixed-size state. It is pre-trained on 3,955 hours of EEG from 17 public datasets using multichannel autoregressive prediction of discrete spectral codes. Across three short-window and two long-sequence downstream tasks, NeurDuo-EEG achieves the best performance on four of five benchmarks, including all three short-window tasks and seizure detection, where AUC-PR improves from $0.285$ to $0.471$ over the strongest non-NeurDuo baseline. NeurDuo-EEG also remains competitive on sleep staging and supports efficient streaming inference, with nearly constant per-chunk latency as the available history grows to one hour. Notably, the Small variant achieves this with only 4.7M backbone parameters. These results demonstrate the value of persistent, multi-timescale modelling for both long-sequence and short-window EEG analysis. Our code is available at https://github.com/YifaNNW/NeurDuo-EEG.

</details>


### [41] [Reinforcement Learning with Complex (valued) Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38598)
*Sathya Kamesh Bhethanabhotla,Efstratios Gavves,André Biedenkapp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将酉循环网络（uRNNs）引入强化学习，提出三种可直接替换PPO循环架构的变体，在部分可观测任务上显著超越基线，并提出相位感知策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观测环境是深度强化学习的核心挑战，智能体需压缩时序信息并维持记忆。现有方法（门控循环、注意力机制、基于模型的RL）在捕捉长期依赖上仍有局限，因此寻找有效表示技术仍是活跃研究方向。

Method: 重新审视在复向量空间中表达循环与隐状态的酉循环网络（uRNNs），利用其保范酉动力学实现长序列信息传播。提出三种uRNN版本作为循环PPO架构的即插即用替代，并借鉴量子态测量的思路设计相位感知策略，以保留复隐状态的相位信息。

Result: 所提方法在多个可改善记忆的任务（含连续控制）上显著优于基线，在rocksample和Craftax等环境中奖励达到基线的2-3倍。

Conclusion: 研究表明复值酉表示与相位信息对部分可观测RL具有潜力，为RL表示学习与部分可观测问题指明了新方向。

Abstract: Partially observable environments pose a fundamental challenge in deep reinforcement learning, requiring agents to compress temporal information from observations and maintain a memory to make effective decisions. While there exist many approaches ranging from gated recurrence to attention mechanisms and model-based RL, the search for effective representational techniques that can capture long-term dependencies remains an active area of research. In this work we revisit Unitary recurrent networks (uRNNs) [Arjovsky et al., 2016, Jing et al., 2017], that demonstrated superior gradient flow and associative recall, expressing the recurrence and the hidden state in a complex vector space. Their norm preserving unitary dynamics enable information propagation through long sequences. To this end, we propose three different versions of uRNNs as drop-in replacements for recurrent PPO architectures, and demonstrate that the simple recurrence and the added degree of freedom from the phase of the complex representations enable significant gains over baselines on several memory-improvable tasks, including continuous control. We further explore how to preserve the phase information of the complex hidden state for a phase-aware policy by drawing a parallel to how quantum states are measured. With our methods reaching up to 2-3 $\times$ the reward in environments like rocksample and Craftax compared to the baselines, this work points towards an exciting new direction of representations for RL and the problem of partial observability. Code is available at: https://github.com/Sathya98/qurl

</details>


### [42] [JARQ: Joint Alternating Refinement for Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38599)
*Xinyu Wang,Sicheng Lyu,Xiao-Wen Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 JARQ，一种对任意分组训练后量化器进行联合最小二乘尺度拟合与 Babai 码联合更新的插件式精化方法，在不改变位宽与推理成本的前提下降低困惑度并提升下游准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分组训练后量化方法将权重量化到固定网格后不再对整数量化码进行重新拟合，作者指出这会损失精度：最优网格依赖于码本身、输入相关性使不同组误差耦合、且有效的码改动常涉及多个码同时变化。

Method: JARQ 从任一分组量化器出发，交替执行所有组尺度的联合最小二乘拟合与有界 Babai 提议，在现有网格上同时移动一组内的多个码；该问题被建模为双线性箱约束混合整数最小二乘，求解无需反向传播，在精确求解尺度时不增加逐层目标，并保持宿主量化的位宽、分组、零点与推理成本。

Result: 在 Llama-2、Llama-3 与 Qwen 模型上，以 RTN、GPTQ、OmniQuant、AWQ 为宿主，JARQ 在 96 次比较中有 90 次降低困惑度，三比特 RTN 困惑度最多下降 36%，在 24 种配置中有 23 种提升平均多项选择准确率，并改善 QEP、QuaRot、OJBKQ 的输出，7B 模型每个 block 耗时不足一分钟。

Conclusion: JARQ 作为一种通用插件式精化，可无缝叠加于现有分组训练后量化器之上，在几乎不增加开销的情况下稳定提升量化模型精度。

Abstract: Group-wise post-training quantizers for large language models round weights onto a grid that is not refit to the resulting integer codes. We show that this leaves accuracy on the table: the best grid depends on the codes, input correlations couple the errors of different groups, and useful code changes often involve many codes at once. We propose JARQ , a plug-in refinement that starts from any group-wise quantizer and alternates a joint least-squares fit of all group scales with bounded Babai proposals that move many codes of a group together on the current grid. The problem is a bilinear box-constrained mixed-integer least-squares problem; the solver is backpropagation-free, does not increase the layer-wise objective under exact scale solves, and keeps the host's bit width, groups, zero points, and inference cost. Across Llama-2, Llama-3, and Qwen models with RTN, GPTQ, OmniQuant, and AWQ hosts, JARQ lowers perplexity in 90 of 96 comparisons, cuts three-bit RTN perplexity by up to 36%, raises mean multiple-choice accuracy in 23 of 24 configurations, and improves QEP, QuaRot, and OJBKQ outputs, at under a minute per 7B block.

</details>


### [43] [Learning-Enabled Estimation: Tight Characterizations under Sample Selection Biases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38608)
*Vikram Kher,Jane H. Lee,Anay Mehrotra,Manolis Zampetakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对样本选择偏差下的回归可识别性给出了完整刻画，并提出了首个通用估计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 样本选择偏差广泛存在于临床试验脱失、劳动力市场自选择和拍卖策略性进入等场景，忽视该偏差会导致系统性错误结论。自Heckman两阶段模型以来，虽已有大量充分条件，但回归何时可识别仍缺乏完整刻画。

Method: 作者刻画了选择过程函数形式上使回归可识别所需的最小假设，并给出可识别性的完整特征；在此条件下建立有限样本估计保证，给出显式收敛率，并提供oracle高效算法。

Result: 得到一个可识别性的完整刻画；作为推论，发现某些情形下即使选择过滤器本身不可识别，回归函数仍可识别；在自然的加强条件下获得有限样本估计保证、显式收敛率与oracle高效算法。

Conclusion: 该工作突破了"先估计选择过滤器再去除偏差"的既有范式，提供了首个适用于复杂选择机制的通用估计方法，并将其应用于带进入成本的拍卖和劳动力市场等计量经济学场景。

Abstract: When can we learn from biased samples? We study regression when outcomes are observed only after passing through selection filters that depend on both covariates and outcomes themselves, a ubiquitous challenge spanning clinical trials with patient dropout, labor markets with self-selection, and auctions with strategic entry. Ignoring such selection yields systematically biased conclusions with real-world consequences. This challenge has a long history in econometrics and statistics, starting with Heckman's seminal two-stage model and followed by numerous generalizations. While these works provide various sufficient conditions for identification, a complete characterization of when such regression is possible has remained elusive.
  In this work, we provide a characterization for when regression is possible in the presence of sample selection bias. Our results establish the minimal assumptions required on the functional forms of selection processes under which regression remains possible, which are particularly relevant in modern settings where selection mechanisms are increasingly complex and opaque. As a corollary of our characterization, we show that there are settings where the regression function can be identified even when the selection filter itself cannot. This observation already goes beyond the ``estimate selection filter, then debias regression'' paradigm that is followed by virtually all existing approaches. Under natural strengthenings of our identification conditions, we also establish finite-sample estimation guarantees with explicit convergence rates and provide oracle-efficient algorithms. This yields the first general-purpose estimation method for this broad class of selection problems. Finally, we explore the implications of our results for several well-studied econometric settings with complex selection mechanisms such as auctions with entry costs and labor markets.

</details>


### [44] [Differentiable Structure Learning for Cyclic Linear Gaussian Models with Latent Confounders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38618)
*Sadegh Khorasani,Ali Najar,Saber Salehkaleybar,Negar Kiyavash*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究在有向环和未知数量潜在混杂因子存在下的线性高斯结构因果模型因果结构学习，提出边际准等价概念，并用对数尺度复杂度惩罚的负对数似然实现一致性，采用Bernoulli门参数化进行可微优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果结构学习多假设无环或无可观测混杂，而实际数据可能同时存在有向环和未知数量的潜在混杂因子，需要新的等价性刻画与可学习的目标函数。

Method: 推导观测变量协方差，定义边际准等价；以对数尺度复杂度惩罚（计有向边和潜变量）的负对数似然为准则；用Bernoulli门参数化边和候选潜变量，对其平均得到带闭式可微复杂度惩罚的目标，并证明其全局下确界与离散目标一致。

Result: 在代数忠实性、结构最小性和模型重叠假设下证明全局得分最小解在边际准等价意义下一致；实验显示在多种设置下恢复误差低于已有方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为含环和潜在混杂的因果结构学习提供了等价性理论与可微优化框架，兼顾理论一致性与实践效果。

Abstract: We study causal structure learning from observational data in linear Gaussian structural causal models in the presence of directed cycles and an unknown number of exogenous latent confounders, bounded by a given maximum. We derive the covariance of the observed variables and introduce marginal quasi-equivalence, which characterizes when different causal models share a full-dimensional subset of the observational distributions they can generate. We formulate structure learning as minimization of the Gaussian negative log-likelihood with a logarithmically scaled complexity penalty that counts directed edges and latent variables. For a fixed number of observed variables and a fixed upper bound on latent variables, we establish consistency of global score minimizers up to marginal quasi-equivalence under algebraic faithfulness, structural minimality, and model-overlap assumptions. We parameterize the inclusion of directed edges and candidate latent variables using Bernoulli gates, whose continuous probabilities are optimized jointly with the structural coefficients. Averaging the penalized negative log-likelihood over these gates yields an objective with a closed-form differentiable complexity penalty. We prove that this expected objective has the same global infimum as the corresponding discrete structure-learning objective. Experimental results show that our approach achieves lower recovery error than previous methods in several experimental settings.

</details>


### [45] [Geometry-physics confounding impairs PDE learning across varying domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38623)
*Yinghao Cheng,Gengxiang Chen,Xu Liu,Qinglu Meng,Yixin Jing,Xiangguo Tang,Wenping Mou,Lihui Wang,Yingguang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文识别出"几何-物理混淆"是跨域PDE学习的统一失败机制，并提出显式建模几何到算子变换的去混淆框架，在算子学习和方程发现两类任务上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨不同域学习PDE动力学对预测建模和数据驱动的方程发现至关重要。但几何变化会同时改变场表示与支配微分算子，使几何效应与内在物理属性在观测动力学中相互混淆，从而降低了学习效率与泛化能力，并导致方程发现中的参数偏差和项选择错误。

Method: 提出去混淆框架，将已知的几何到算子变换显式化：一方面利用几何诱导的系数场改进前向算子学习，另一方面构造几何完备的候选库用于方程发现，从而把已知的几何作用与内在物理分离开来。

Result: 在五个算子学习基准上，几何诱导系数场提升了预测精度与数据效率；在两类演化域系统中，几何完备候选库成功恢复生成方程，并将留出PDE残差降低超过两个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过分离已知几何作用与内在物理，该框架支持在几何变化多样的科学与工程问题中实现更可靠、更数据高效的PDE学习。

Abstract: Learning partial differential equation (PDE) dynamics across varying domains is central to predictive modelling and data-driven discovery of governing equations. However, geometric variation alters both field representation and the governing differential operators, confounding geometric effects with intrinsic physical properties in the observed dynamics. This work identifies geometry-physics confounding as a unified failure mechanism for PDE learning across varying domains. In forward operator learning, this confounding increases the burden of inferring geometry-dependent operator changes from finite data, reducing data efficiency and generalisation. In equation discovery, omitting geometry-induced operators misspecifies the candidate library, leading to biased parameters, missed governing terms and spurious terms. We propose a de-confounding framework that makes the known geometry-to-operator transformation explicit. Geometry-induced coefficient fields improve prediction and data efficiency across five operator-learning benchmarks, while geometry-complete candidate libraries recover the generating equations and reduce held-out PDE residuals by more than two orders of magnitude in both evolving-domain systems. By separating known geometric action from intrinsic physics, the proposed framework supports more reliable and data-efficient PDE learning across scientific and engineering problems with varying geometries.

</details>


### [46] [Interpretable but Fragile? Robustness of Concept Bottlenecks under Geometric-Semantic Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38625)
*Hanwei Zhang,Tianma Hu,Gaojie Jin,Xu Cheng,Ronghui Mu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过统一的生成式评估框架，区分散度空间几何扰动与概念空间语义干预两类扰动，实证与可证双重评估CBM鲁棒性，澄清了既有矛盾结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念瓶颈模型（CBM）以可解释的中间表征为设计目标，但其对鲁棒性的影响尚不明确，既有研究报告的结论相互矛盾甚至冲突。作者认为这些分歧源于混淆了不同的鲁棒性定义与扰动类型，而非对CBM本身的根本分歧。

Method: 提出基于生成器的评估框架，在两类扰动下对标准分类器与CBM进行受控比较：潜在（隐）空间中的连续几何扰动，以及概念空间中的离散语义干预。在此框架内，通过预测与概念层面的敏感度指标进行实证评估，并利用隐空间与概念空间的随机平滑进行可证评估。进一步考察类别语义相似度与概念词表规模等任务条件。

Result: 在各类实验中调和了以往相互冲突的发现，明确了概念瓶颈在何时、以何种意义提升或不提升鲁棒性；发现可解释性并不天然带来鲁棒性，概念瓶颈更多地改变了敏感性的表现位置与方式，呈现出依赖于扰动类型与任务结构的可解释性—鲁棒性权衡。

Conclusion: 可解释性与鲁棒性是相互独立的目标：可解释的中间表征并不会一致地提升鲁棒性，而是将敏感性在不同扰动空间与模型族之间重新分配。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) are designed to provide interpretable intermediate representations, yet how such bottlenecks affect robustness remains unclear, with existing studies reporting mixed and sometimes contradictory findings. We argue that these discrepancies arise from conflating different robustness notions and perturbation regimes, rather than from fundamental disagreements about CBMs themselves. To disentangle these factors, we introduce a generator-based evaluation framework that enables controlled comparisons between standard classifiers and CBMs under two distinct perturbation types: continuous geometric perturbations in latent space and discrete semantic interventions in concept space. Within this framework, we evaluate robustness both empirically, via prediction and concept-level sensitivity metrics, and certifiably, using randomized smoothing in latent and concept spaces. Across experiments, we reconcile previously conflicting findings by clarifying when, and in what sense, concept bottlenecks do or do not improve robustness. By further analyzing robustness under varying task conditions, including class semantic similarity and concept vocabulary size, we show that interpretability does not inherently confer robustness. Instead, concept bottlenecks shift where and how sensitivity manifests, revealing a nuanced interpretability robustness trade off that depends critically on the perturbation regime and task structure. Together, our results show that interpretability and robustness are distinct objectives: interpretable intermediate representations do not uniformly improve robustness, but instead redistribute sensitivity across perturbation spaces and model families.

</details>


### [47] [Proper Scoring Rule-based Diffusion for Probabilistic Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38632)
*Joonhyeong Park,Giung Nam,Hyungi Lee,Kyunghyun Cho,Byoungwoo Park,Juho Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出辅助条件去噪任务,在单次前向推理下增强随机天气预报器的分布学习能力,尤其改善长预报时效的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CRPS的随机天气预测试图仅从预报上下文学习预测分布,在预报时效较长、不确定性高时,分布学习变得困难。

Method: 引入辅助条件去噪任务,让模型从上下文及其受扰版本预测同一未来状态,其中受扰版本提供部分未来信息以降低预测不确定性。基于分布扩散模型,用单一随机预测器在多个噪声水平上最小化合适的评分规则来学习条件分布;推理时仍可在完全受扰端点单步生成各集合成员。标准CRPS训练是其仅在端点的特例,因此该框架只需增加额外条件输入即可扩展现有CRPS预报器。

Result: 受控实验表明,辅助任务在多种架构上改进了单步预报,长预报时效下增益更大;在高维全球天气预报中(从头训练和微调)均获得提升,同时改善了校准,并可能有利于分布偏移下的泛化。

Conclusion: 通过辅助条件去噪任务扩展CRPS训练框架,可在保持单步推理效率的同时提升随机天气预报的准确性与校准,且框架通用、易于集成到现有方法中。

Abstract: Recent probabilistic weather forecasters train stochastic predictors with the continuous ranked probability score (CRPS) to generate each ensemble member in a single forward pass. These models learn the predictive distribution from the forecast context alone, which becomes difficult at longer forecast horizons where uncertainty is high. To learn the predictive distribution more effectively, we introduce auxiliary conditional denoising tasks that predict the same future state from the context and its corrupted version, which provides partial future information that can reduce prediction ambiguity. Building on distributional diffusion models, we learn the conditional distributions of these tasks with a single stochastic predictor by minimizing a proper scoring rule across noise levels. At inference, the predictor can still generate each ensemble member in a single forward pass at the fully corrupted endpoint. Standard CRPS training is recovered as the endpoint-only special case of our formulation, so our framework extends existing CRPS-based forecasters with only additional conditioning inputs. Controlled experiments show that the auxiliary tasks improve one-step forecasting across architectures, with larger gains at longer forecast horizons. The gains extend to high-dimensional global weather forecasting under both training from scratch and fine-tuning, along with improved calibration and potential benefits for generalization under distribution shift.

</details>


### [48] [Alignment via Training Against Probes Without Losing Monitorability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38645)
*Lena Libon,Alexander Panfilov,Ben Rank,Xin Chen,Jonas Geiping,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究"探针引导微调"，用检测模型激活中不良性质的探针作为直接训练信号，发现持续更新探针能显著降低有害性、提升诚实性并保持实用性，且比DPO和推理时干预更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法多基于模型的观察输出（示范、偏好数据或奖励信号），只奖励"看起来对齐"的响应。能力更强的模型可能在不内化目标行为的情况下学会满足这些目标，例如在训练期间假装合规。若将目标定义在模型内部表征而非输出上，这种表面合规会更难实现。

Method: 研究探针引导微调，使用检测模型激活中不良性质的探针作为直接训练信号。评估线性和非线性探针，以及每层不同数量的探针，覆盖两个对齐目标：无害性和诚实性。对比固定探针（训练中不更新）与持续更新探针两种设置，并与DPO、推理时干预等方法比较。

Result: 针对训练中不更新的探针进行训练是一个容易被利用的目标；而持续更新的探针能大幅降低有害性、提升诚实性，同时保持实用性。探针引导微调在安全-效用权衡上优于DPO和推理时干预，且在对抗越狱和"消融"攻击时稳健性显著更强。此外，微调后相关概念仍以线性方式编码，说明该方法不会丧失对模型的监督能力。

Conclusion: 针对探针训练提供了一种塑造模型"表征内容"而非仅塑造其输出的途径。随着模型越来越擅长让输出看起来对齐，这种方法可能变得日益重要。

Abstract: Models are usually aligned based on their observed outputs, using demonstrations, preference data, or reward signals. These objectives reward responses that look aligned. More capable models may learn to satisfy them without internalizing the intended behavior, for example by faking compliance during training. Such superficial compliance could be harder when the objective is defined on model internals rather than outputs. Therefore, we study probe-guided fine-tuning, using probes that detect undesired properties in model activations as a direct training signal. We evaluate linear and non-linear probes with different numbers of probes per layer across two alignment objectives: harmlessness and honesty. We find that training against probes that do not update during training is an easily exploitable objective, while continuously updated probes substantially reduce harmfulness and improve honesty while preserving utility. Probe-guided fine-tuning achieves better safety-utility trade-offs than DPO and inference-time steering, while being substantially more robust against jailbreak and abliteration attacks. Moreover, the concepts stay linearly encoded after fine-tuning, meaning oversight is not lost by our method. Training against probes thus offers a way to shape what models represent rather than only what they output, which may become increasingly important as models get better at making their outputs look aligned.

</details>


### [49] [Uncertainty-Normalized Margins for Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38647)
*Sadegh Khorasani,Petrus Mikkola,Matthias Grossglauser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出UNM-DPO，将偏好强度边际与可学习的提示（prompt）尺度结合，并进一步引入长度归一化的ULNM-DPO-WR，在多个人类反馈数据集上优于DPO与SimPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO通过Bradley-Terry模型建模二值偏好，使用统一噪声尺度，未显式考虑偏好强度或来自人类反馈的提示依赖不确定性。

Method: 基于异方差Bradley-Terry模型，提出两种训练目标：优势-only（AO）先除以提示尺度再减边际；整体残差（WR）先减边际再除以尺度。对WR建立了使提示尺度可辨识的充要条件，并给出可实用的尺度学习流程；在WR基础上引入ULNM-DPO-WR，用响应长度归一化每个响应的隐式奖励。

Result: 在HelpSteer2/HelpSteer3上用Skywork奖励模型评判：Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct下ULNM-DPO-WR相对匹配DPO的平局调整胜率为68.00%与65.31%，平均奖励更高、响应更短。在AlpacaEval（GPT-4.1评判、GPT-4-Turbo参考答案）上长度控制胜率21.62%，高于DPO的16.39%和SimPO的15.30%。

Conclusion: 结合偏好强度边际、学习到的提示尺度与长度归一化，对策略优化具有显著潜力。

Abstract: Direct preference optimization (DPO) models binary preferences through a Bradley-Terry model with a common noise scale, without explicitly accounting for preference strength or prompt-dependent uncertainty from human feedback. We introduce uncertainty-normalized margin DPO (UNM-DPO), which combines strength-dependent margins with a learned prompt scale. Motivated by a heteroskedastic Bradley-Terry model, we develop two training objectives. Both compare the implicit rewards of preferred and rejected responses, derived from response log-probability ratios to a reference policy. Advantage-only (AO) divides this reward difference by the prompt scale before subtracting the margin; whole-residual (WR) subtracts the margin before dividing by the scale. For the WR comparison model, we establish a necessary and sufficient condition under which known margins make the prompt scale identifiable. We introduce a practical procedure for learning the scale. Building on WR, we introduce ULNM-DPO-WR, which normalizes each response's implicit reward by its length. We evaluate our methods against DPO and related baselines on HelpSteer2 and HelpSteer3, using the Skywork reward model as a judge. With Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, ULNM-DPO-WR achieves tie-adjusted win rates against matched DPO of 68.00% and 65.31% on evaluation panels, with higher mean rewards and shorter responses on average. On AlpacaEval with a GPT-4.1 judge and GPT-4-Turbo reference answers, the same 8B policy achieves a length-controlled win rate of 21.62%, compared with 16.39% for DPO and 15.30% for SimPO. These results demonstrate the potential of combining preference-strength margins, learned prompt scales, and length normalization for policy optimization.

</details>


### [50] [Understanding Off- vs On-Policy Distillation: A Tale of Distinct Training Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38666)
*Qiwei Di,Xuheng Li,Kaixuan Ji,Chenggong Zhang,Heyang Zhao,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究多教师序列蒸馏中on-policy distillation（OPD）的收益与脆弱性，证明前向KL产生加权算术混合、反向KL产生归一化加权几何聚合，并给出对数遗憾界与函数逼近扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD从学生自生成回答的教师反馈中学习，相比SFT更能减少遗忘，但其收益与脆弱性尚未被充分理解，因此作者希望从理论上刻画其机制。

Method: 研究多教师序列蒸馏，学生最小化与教师们的平均散度：前向KL对应加权算术混合，反向KL对应归一化加权几何聚合；分别设计off-policy与on-policy反馈下的学习算法，在表格设定建立对数遗憾界，并扩展到函数逼近，同时分析聚合目标的token级条件分布。

Result: 前向KL与反向KL对应不同的聚合目标；反向KL在无信息反馈下更能保留自信专家的偏好，但对给正确答案极低概率的教师更敏感；其token级条件分布还揭示了对续写分布的依赖，长视界下可能偏好错误前缀。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解OPD相对于SFT的收益及脆弱性提供了理论机制解释，说明反向KL的选择在保留专家偏好与鲁棒性之间存在权衡。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) learns from teacher feedback on student-generated responses and has shown promise in reducing forgetting relative to supervised fine-tuning (SFT). However, its benefits and fragility remain incompletely understood. We study sequential distillation from multiple teachers, where the student minimizes its average divergence from the teachers. Forward Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence yields a weighted arithmetic mixture, while reverse KL yields a normalized weighted geometric aggregate. We develop algorithms that learn these targets under off-policy and on-policy feedback, respectively, establishing logarithmic regret bounds in the tabular setting and extending the analysis to function approximation. By analyzing these aggregation targets, we identify mechanisms that help explain both the benefits and fragility of OPD. Relative to forward KL, reverse KL can better retain a confident expert's preferences under uninformative feedback, but is more sensitive to teachers that assign very low probabilities to correct responses. Its token-level conditionals also reveal a dependence on continuation distributions that can favor incorrect prefixes over long horizons.

</details>


### [51] [Provable Test-Time Scaling for Beam Search in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38672)
*Qijia He,Yu Huang,Yuan Cheng,Yuxin Chen,Yingbin Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上研究束搜索（beam search）的测试时计算保证，提出改进的置信过滤束搜索（CF-Beam），将覆盖率依赖从二次降到近线性，证明其在长程生成上优于Best-of-N等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 束搜索类测试时方法在长程生成任务上经验效果显著，能早期剪枝无效推理路径，提升推理效率与测试时成本的可扩展性，但其理论理解仍然有限。作者希望建立束搜索的测试时计算保证，并解释其相对于序列级推理方法的根本优势。

Method: 研究依赖模型内部对数似然进行中间打分、仅在完整响应生成后使用外部奖励模型的常用束搜索框架。首先为原始束搜索建立下界，证明最优响应存活至少需要 Ω(C⋆(x)²) 个样本，其中 C⋆(x) 为提示 x 的 token 级覆盖率系数。基于此提出改进的置信过滤束搜索（CF-Beam），在前缀竞争力假设下将充分覆盖依赖从二次降至近线性。进一步分析 CF-Beam 的遗憾界。

Result: 证明了原始束搜索的下界为 Ω(C⋆(x)²) 个样本；CF-Beam 在固定视界、间隔和目标精度下将覆盖率依赖从二次降至近线性。CF-Beam 的遗憾上界由稀有失败事件概率和奖励估计误差（按路径级覆盖率系数缩放）控制，其中稀有失败项随每步采样数增加而消失。实验验证束搜索在困难实例和更长推理视界下更稳健。

Conclusion: 束搜索相对 Best-of-N、Best-of-Majority 等序列级推理方法具有根本优势：这些方法的保证通常涉及随视界 L 指数增长的覆盖率系数，而 CF-Beam 通过 token 级覆盖率系数控制主导的搜索诱导项，其随 L 多项式增长。数值实验进一步确认了束搜索在困难实例和长推理视界下的稳健性。

Abstract: Beam-search-based test-time methods provide an effective way to improve large language model (LLM) performance on long-horizon generation by pruning invalid reasoning paths early, leading to significantly improved reasoning efficiency and more favorable test-time cost scaling. Despite strong empirical success, the theoretical understanding of beam search remains limited. In this paper, we study the test-time compute guarantee of the commonly used beam search framework that uses the model's internal log-likelihood for intermediate scoring, while relying on an external reward model only after a complete response is generated. We first establish a lower bound for vanilla beam search, showing that at least $Ω(C^\star(x)^2)$ samples are required for the optimal response to survive, where $C^\star(x)$ is the token-level coverage coefficient for prompt $x$. This motivates our modified confidence-filtered beam search (CF-Beam), which reduces the sufficient coverage dependence from quadratic to nearly linear under prefix competitiveness, for fixed horizon, gap, and target accuracy. We then show that the regret of CF-Beam is upper-bounded by the probability of rare failure events and the reward estimation error scaled by a path-level coverage coefficient, where the rare-failure term vanishes as per-step sampling increases. Our results highlight a fundamental advantage of beam search over sequence-level inference methods such as Best-of-N and Best-of-Majority. While the guarantees of these approaches typically involve coverage coefficients that grow exponentially with the horizon $L$, CF-Beam controls the dominant search-induced term through a token-level coverage coefficient that scales polynomially with $L$. Our numerical experiments further confirm that beam search is more robust on hard instances and under increasing reasoning horizons.

</details>


### [52] [In-Distribution Imagination for Model-Based Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38673)
*Mintae Kim,Koushil Sreenath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"分布内想象"（IDI）框架，通过学习到的表征空间估计轨迹支撑度，自适应截断离开离线轨迹流形的rollout，并结合轨迹正则化RL，在有限数据下持续提升MBORL性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的离线强化学习（MBORL）虽通过模型生成轨迹提升样本效率，但累积模型误差会使想象轨迹偏离离线数据分布，产生不真实的合成数据并导致策略优化不稳定。现有方法多仅用转移级不确定性控制rollout，粒度不足。

Method: 提出分布内想象（IDI）：在学习到的表征空间中估计轨迹支撑度，自适应截断离开离线轨迹流形的rollout；并引入轨迹正则化RL（熵正则化RL的扩展）进行策略优化。

Result: 实验表明，轨迹支撑度对rollout失败的预测能力显著优于转移级不确定性，且在有限数据设置下IDI能持续提升性能。

Conclusion: 轨迹级的rollout控制对MBORL至关重要，IDI验证了从轨迹层面控制想象的有效性。

Abstract: Model-based offline reinforcement learning (MBORL) improves sample efficiency through model-generated trajectories. However, accumulative model error can drive imagined trajectories outside the offline data distribution, leading to unrealistic synthetic data and unstable policy optimization. Many existing methods primarily control rollouts using transition-level uncertainty. We propose \emph{in-distribution imagination} (IDI), a rollout control framework that estimates trajectory support in a learned representation space and adaptively truncates rollouts that leave the offline trajectory manifold. Combined with trajectory-regularized RL, an extension of entropy-regularized RL, IDI consistently improves performance in limited-data settings. Experiments show that trajectory support predicts rollout failure substantially better than transition-level uncertainty, highlighting the importance of trajectory-level rollout control in MBORL.

</details>


### [53] [Molecular Property Prediction under Structural Shift with Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38744)
*Jinmo Lee,Dooho Lee,Minho Jeong,Jaemin Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MolPAIR框架，通过分子对上下文比较增强表格基础模型的上下文学习，在分子性质预测的结构泛化上取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测与训练分子结构差异较大的化合物性质对药物发现和材料设计很重要。表格基础模型（TFM）通过上下文学习提供了有前景的方案，但其在结构偏移下的表现以及分子比较的价值尚不明确。

Method: MolPAIR结合分子级与分子对上下文，无需任务特定参数更新。先用全局TFM基于标注分子示例预测查询性质，再由第二个冻结TFM预测查询与参考分子之间的预测误差差异，据此精炼初始预测。分子编码器和预训练权重均保持固定。

Result: 在58个MoleculeACE和Polaris任务上，CheMeleon表示结合TabPFN-3已在多数任务上超越所有评估基线；MolPAIR进一步在58个任务中的46个上改进了预测，且在四种分子表示和三种TFM骨干上均有增益。

Conclusion: 显式的分子比较能够增强表格上下文学习在结构泛化上的表现，同时保持分子编码器和预训练模型权重不变。代码与数据集已公开。

Abstract: Predicting molecular properties for compounds that differ structurally from labeled training molecules is important for drug discovery and materials design. Tabular foundation models (TFMs) offer a promising approach through in-context learning, but their performance under structural shifts and the value of molecular comparisons in this setting remain underexplored. We study structural generalization in molecular property prediction and introduce MolPAIR (Molecular Pair-Augmented In-context Refinement), a framework that combines molecule-level and molecular-pair contexts without task-specific parameter updates. A global tabular foundation model (TFM) first predicts a query's property from labeled molecular examples. A second frozen TFM predicts differences in prediction errors between the query and labeled reference molecules, using these comparisons to refine the initial prediction. Across 58 MoleculeACE and Polaris tasks, CheMeleon representations combined with TabPFN-3 already outperform each evaluated baseline on a majority of tasks. MOLPAIR further improves this predictor on 46 of 58 tasks, with gains across four molecular representations and three TFM backbones. These results show that explicit molecular comparisons can strengthen tabular in-context learning for structural generalization while keeping the molecular encoder and pretrained model weights fixed. The code and datasets are available at https://github.com/nums-ai/MolPAIR.

</details>


### [54] [Lasting Effects of Abstract Pretraining Beyond Perplexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38764)
*Zachary Shinnick,Hemanth Saratchandran,Damien Teney,Anton van den Hengel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在小型语言模型中，用少量（1%）抽象的栈操作任务数据进行预训练“预热”，可显著提升多跳问答能力，尽管语言建模困惑度变化不大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战“语言模型须从随机初始化预训练”的传统观念，探究抽象算法生成数据预热为何以及如何提升自然语言任务能力，尤其是那些不体现在困惑度上的能力。

Method: 采用一个抽象的栈操作任务（需具备组合与状态追踪能力）作为预热数据，将其分配为预训练token的1%，随后进行自然语言预训练；并通过替换为队列任务、与自然语言混合、预训练后再暴露等对照实验，分析增益的来源。

Result: 在MUSIQUE上多跳问答最高提升3.9 F1分，HOTPOTQA和2WIKIMULTIHOPQA也有增益，但语言建模困惑度相当；预热显著加速了更深层推理链的习得；收益限于顺序推理链，对组合或比较独立事实的任务无稳定增益；队列任务无同样收益，混合或事后暴露则收益消失；早期优势在后续数十亿token训练中持续存在。

Conclusion: 早期的抽象训练能可靠地塑造语言模型后续习得的能力，且数据结构的性质、任务针对性与训练时机均是决定迁移效果的关键因素。

Abstract: Language models are typically pretrained from random initialization. Recent work challenges this convention, showing that a brief warm-up on abstract, algorithmically generated data can provide a better starting point for subsequent learning of natural language. In this paper, we show that in small language models, such a warm-up improves specific capabilities that are not reflected in language-modeling perplexity. Our warm-up uses an abstract stack-manipulation task that requires compositional and state-tracking capabilities. Allocating as little as 1% of pretraining tokens to this data improves multi-hop question answering by up to 3.9 F1 points on MUSIQUE, with additional gains on HOTPOTQA and 2WIKIMULTIHOPQA despite comparable language-modeling perplexity. Controlled experiments show that the warm-up substantially accelerates the acquisition of deeper reasoning chains. We also explore what drives this transfer. First, the structure of the data matters: replacing the stack task with a queue fails to produce the same gains. Second, the gains are specific: performance improves on sequential reasoning chains, with no consistent benefit on tasks that combine or compare independent facts. Third, timing matters: mixing abstract data with natural language is far less effective than an initial dedicated phase, and exposure after pretraining completely removes the benefits. The early advantage persists through billions of subsequent language tokens. These results show that early abstract training can reliably shape the capabilities language models later acquire.

</details>


### [55] [dattri-LLM: A Unified and Efficient Library for Training Data Attribution at LLM Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38767)
*Shixuan Liu,Tongli Zhou,Junwei Deng,Pingbang Hu,Jiaqi W. Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: dattri-LLM 是一个让基于梯度的训练数据归因（TDA）在大规模 LLM 场景下更实用的库，通过紧凑梯度表示、动态梯度运算路由、无需修改训练循环的梯度捕获机制以及可复用的梯度算子与回调接口，在效率、兼容性和可扩展性上均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数可扩展的 TDA 方法依赖逐样本梯度，但在 LLM 规模下，逐样本梯度的计算与使用在效率、兼容性和可扩展性方面都面临挑战，因此需要让基于梯度的归因在规模化场景下更可行。

Method: dattri-LLM 面向效率采用紧凑梯度表示，并基于成本模型动态路由梯度运算；面向兼容性设计了捕获机制，可直接从现有调用 backward() 的训练循环中收集逐样本梯度，无需修改循环或其配置，支持 DDP、FSDP 分布式训练以及 HuggingFace Transformers、TRL、OLMo 构建的流水线；面向可扩展性提供可复用的梯度算子与训练期回调接口，用于实现归因方法与应用。

Result: 在相同硬件与工作负载下，dattri-LLM 平均达到最快竞品库 3.2 倍的吞吐量；可在四块 H200 GPU 上将多种归因方法扩展到 110B 参数模型；并在不同模型家族与规模上提供更优的归因保真度与成本权衡。

Conclusion: dattri-LLM 通过效率、兼容性与可扩展性三方面的设计，使基于梯度的训练数据归因在 LLM 规模下变得更为可行和实用，支持梯度相似度、基于曲率的影响函数、基于轨迹的方法，以及在线数据选择等训练中作用于梯度的应用。

Abstract: Training data attribution (TDA) estimates the contribution of individual training examples to model outputs. Most scalable TDA methods rely on per-example gradients, whose computation and use at LLM scale pose challenges in efficiency, compatibility, and extensibility. We introduce dattri-LLM, a TDA library that makes gradient-based attribution more practical at scale. For efficiency, dattri-LLM uses compact gradient representations and dynamically routes gradient operations based on a cost model. For compatibility, its capture mechanism collects per-example gradients from existing training loops that call backward(), without requiring changes to the loop or its configuration. This includes distributed training with DDP and FSDP and pipelines built with HuggingFace Transformers, TRL, and OLMo. For extensibility, dattri-LLM exposes reusable gradient operations and training-time callbacks for implementing attribution methods and applications. These interfaces support a variety of attribution methods, including gradient similarity, curvature-based influence, and trajectory-based methods, as well as applications that act on gradients during training, such as online data selection. On the same hardware and workload, dattri-LLM achieves 3.2x the throughput of the fastest competing library on average, scales multiple attribution methods to 110B-parameter models across four H200 GPUs, and offers superior attribution fidelity-cost trade-offs across a range of models with different model families and scales.

</details>


### [56] [Learning Under Forgetting: Statistical Support-Selective Retention in Stochastic Training Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38768)
*Fujie Gao,Zuyue Zhang,Gang Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"重复强化与持续遗忘（RPF）"动力学框架，将遗忘视为一种选择性机制而非单纯失效，从独立特征、共享参数到小步长近似三个层次推导其对模式保留、谱过滤和MDL式压缩的影响，并用受控实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究已发现神经网络存在谱偏置、记忆动态和类压缩效应，但对"选择性保留"缺乏统一的动力学解释，本文旨在弥补这一理论空白。

Method: 构建RPF最小框架（重复暴露强化反复出现的模式，持续遗忘削弱已学信息），分三层理论展开：独立特征模型推导曝光选择性存续定律与支持依赖的保留边界；共享参数模型证明遗忘对协方差模式产生谱过滤；在小步长与范数/编码近似下推导数据拟合与信息存储成本的权衡，得到类MDL压缩。并开展标量记忆与非线性共享网络的受控实验。

Result: 理论上得到存活定律、保留边界、谱滤波与类MDL压缩的串联结论；实验中，联合强化与衰减干预改变条件保留，匹配曝光次数显示强化时机的遗忘依赖效应及保留集合组成变化。

Conclusion: 重复强化与持续遗忘共同构成可控的归纳偏置来源，其作用超出网络架构与规模本身。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that neural networks exhibit implicit biases toward low-complexity structure (e.g., spectral bias), memorization dynamics, and compression-like effects during training, but a unified dynamical account of selective retention remains incomplete. We propose Repeated Reinforcement with Persistent Forgetting (RPF) dynamics, a minimal framework in which repeated exposure reinforces patterns and structures that recur in the data, while persistent forgetting attenuates learned information. This view treats forgetting not merely as a failure mode, but as a selection mechanism. We build the theory in three successive layers. First, in an independent-feature model, we derive an exposure-selective survival law and a support-dependent retention boundary characterizing which patterns persist under forgetting. Second, in a shared-parameter model, we show that forgetting induces spectral filtering over covariance modes, preserving strongly supported shared components while suppressing weak ones. Third, under small-step and norm/coding approximations, we show how RPF dynamics induce an implicit trade-off between data fitting and the cost of stored information, yielding Minimum Description Length (MDL)-like compression. Controlled experiments provide evidence for this reinforcement--forgetting selection mechanism in scalar memories and a nonlinear shared network. Joint reinforcement and attenuation interventions shift conditional retention, while matched exposure counts reveal forgetting-dependent effects of reinforcement timing and changes in the composition of the retained set. Together, these results show that repeated reinforcement and persistent forgetting jointly provide a controllable source of inductive bias beyond neural architecture and scale.

</details>


### [57] [Distilling Diffusion Score Discrepancy for Efficient Training Data Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38776)
*Shixuan Liu,Joan Serrà,Kin Wai Cheuk,Jinju Kim,Woosung Choi,Yukara Ikemiya,Wei-Hsiang Liao,Jiaqi W. Ma,Yuki Mitsufuji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出基于score差异的训练数据归因方法TID及其蒸馏版本TIDE，实现高效且忠实于生成行为的扩散模型数据归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型训练数据归因方法存在两大局限：一是需要昂贵的逐样本梯度计算或针对每个查询的模型优化；二是大多归因的是代理损失的变化，而非模型实际生成行为的变化。

Method: 直接用局部score差异度量来形式化归因，适用于DDPM、EDM、flow matching等任意扩散变体，并证明该度量无需重训练即可估计，表现为预条件梯度相似度。实例化为TID，使用Kronecker分解曲率避免随机投影和逐样本梯度存储；再蒸馏为TIDE，一个前向式学生模型在线训练，从扩散模型内部激活中复现教师的排序。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、ArtBench-10和MS-COCO上的反事实评估中，TID匹配或超越最先进方法；TIDE在每查询成本低四到五个数量级的情况下保留TID大部分准确率，可在毫秒级完成生成样本归因，比生成本身还快。

Conclusion: 该工作通过score差异归因与蒸馏学生模型，同时解决了扩散模型数据归因的准确性与效率问题。

Abstract: Training data attribution for diffusion models aims to identify the training samples that influence a generated instance, but existing methods either require costly per-sample gradient computation or query-specific model optimization. Moreover, most methods attribute changes in a proxy loss rather than changes in the actual model's generative behavior. We address these limitations by formulating attribution directly with a local score discrepancy measure, which applies to any diffusion variant (including DDPM, EDM, and flow matching), and by showing that such measure can be estimated without retraining, as a preconditioned gradient similarity. We instantiate this estimator as Training-data Influence via score Discrepancy (TID), which uses Kronecker-factored curvature to avoid random projections and per-sample gradient storage. We then distill TID into TIDE, a forward-only student trained online to reproduce the teacher's rankings from the diffusion model's internal activations. Under counterfactual evaluation on CIFAR-10, ArtBench-10, and MS-COCO, TID matches or outperforms state-of-the-art approaches, while TIDE retains most of TID's accuracy at four to five orders of magnitude lower per-query cost, attributing generated samples in milliseconds and faster than the generation itself.

</details>


### [58] [ChartDensity-Bench: Benchmarking MLLMs for Numerical Data Reconstruction under Visual Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38781)
*Xinhe Wu,Yadong Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ChartDensity-Bench 基准，用于在受控视觉密度下评估多模态大模型从复合图表中重建结构化数值数据的能力，并发现重建性能随密度增加而下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型虽可用于从科学图表中恢复数值数据，但其在视觉密集图像中的重建能力尚不清楚；已有图表理解基准多评估问答或图表级推理，缺乏对结构化数值重建的系统评估。

Method: 构建 ChartDensity-Bench，基于带有源级真值数据的图表，系统性变化同时呈现的图表数量（k∈{1,3,6,9}）以控制视觉密度；并提出涵盖结构可靠性、重建完整性、可解析性和数值保真度的多维评估框架。

Result: 在五个近期 MLLM 上的实验显示，数值重建性能总体随视觉密度增加而下降，但不同模型的退化幅度差异显著；图表级配对比较表明，同一源图表嵌入更密集视觉环境时重建误差更高。

Conclusion: 视觉密度是 MLLM 图表数据重建中重要但此前被忽视的因素，ChartDensity-Bench 为该场景下模型鲁棒性评估提供了系统基准。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) offer a promising approach for recovering numerical data from scientific charts, but their ability to reconstruct chart data from visually dense figures remains poorly understood. Existing chart understanding benchmarks primarily evaluate question answering or chart-level reasoning and provide limited support for evaluating structured numerical reconstruction from scientific figures. We introduce \textbf{ChartDensity-Bench}, a benchmark for evaluating MLLMs on structured numerical data reconstruction from compound chart figures under controlled visual density. Built from charts paired with source-level ground-truth data, ChartDensity-Bench systematically varies the number of simultaneously presented charts ($k\in{1,3,6,9}$), enabling controlled evaluation of density-induced degradation. We further propose a multi-dimensional evaluation framework covering structural reliability, reconstruction completeness, parseability, and numerical fidelity. Experiments on five recent MLLMs show that numerical reconstruction generally degrades as visual density increases, while the magnitude of degradation varies substantially across models. Chart-level paired comparisons further show that the same source chart can incur higher reconstruction error when embedded in denser visual contexts. These findings highlight visual density as an important and previously underexplored factor in MLLM chart data reconstruction and provide a systematic benchmark for evaluating model robustness in this setting.

</details>


### [59] [Does a Shared Temperature Imply a Shared Angular Scale in Probabilistic Contrastive Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38784)
*Ningkang Peng,Qianfeng Yu,Jingyang Mao,Xiaoqian Peng,Tingyu Lu,Peirong Ma,Yanhui Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了高维概率对比学习中von Mises-Fisher (vMF)得分的性质，证明类相关的主导角增益 $g_c=A_c/\tau$ 在Softmax竞争、决策边界和特征梯度中起关键作用，并在多个长尾数据集上验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在概率对比学习中，共享温度通常被解释为共享的相似度尺度，但在高维分布类别表示下，这一解释不再成立。作者希望厘清vMF概率得分中类浓度如何构成决策与学习的尺度。

Method: 作者在表示维度与类浓度联合增长的条件下，分析ProCo所采用的精确vMF概率得分，证明得分保留了依赖于类的主导角增益 $g_c=A_c/\tau$；并构造了保持截距的对照和Pure Angular对照，以分离主导增益与其他伴随变化，同时给出有限维边际条件以保证两种分类器一致。

Result: 在CIFAR-LT、ImageNet-LT和iNaturalist表示上，理论准确预测了边界移动与局部梯度变化；在16个冻结表示设置中预测一致率为98.43%-99.99%，分歧集中在小余弦边际处；在受控对比训练中，Pure Angular编辑在所有测试的CIFAR-10/100不平衡因子下均改善了学习表示，并在ImageNet-LT上保持正向变化。

Conclusion: vMF浓度不仅描述类分布，还在高维概率对比学习中构成一种决策与学习尺度。

Abstract: In probabilistic contrastive learning, a shared temperature is commonly interpreted as a shared similarity scale, but this interpretation does not hold for high-dimensional distributional class representations. We study the exact von Mises-Fisher (vMF) probabilistic score used by ProCo when representation dimension and class concentration grow jointly. We prove that the score retains a class-dependent leading angular gain $g_c=A_c/τ$, where $A_c$ is the mean resultant length. This gain enters Softmax competition, pairwise decision boundaries, and feature gradients. On real CIFAR-LT, ImageNet-LT, and iNaturalist representations, the theory accurately predicts boundary movements and local gradient changes under the full vMF score. Classwise temperature adjustment also changes the cosine-zero intercept and finite-dimensional response. We construct intercept-preserving and Pure Angular controls to separate the leading gain from these accompanying changes. Complete gain equalization yields a shared-scale cosine prototype rule at leading order; a finite-dimensional margin condition guarantees agreement of the two classifiers. Across 16 frozen representation settings, prediction agreement is 98.43-99.99%, with disagreements concentrated at small cosine margins. In controlled contrastive-only training with the training-frequency prior, Pure Angular editing improves both learned representations at all tested CIFAR-10/100 imbalance factors and retains positive changes on ImageNet-LT. Thus vMF concentration not only describes class distributions, but also forms a decision and learning scale in high-dimensional probabilistic contrastive learning.

</details>


### [60] [How Accurate Is Accurate Enough?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38785)
*Ningkang Peng,Qianfeng Yu,Jingyang Mao,Xiaoqian Peng,Yanhui Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究学习系统中数值近似的精度需求，提出从学习目标本身刻画类别相关数值误差的重要性，并给出可验证的原始误差容差框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始误差大小无法单独决定其对损失、预测和梯度的影响；相同幅度的误差在不同学习状态、不同类别上可能产生截然不同的后果。

Method: 通过softmax交叉熵的学习目标研究类别权重与误差的耦合，推导在固定非目标概率与分数误差多重集配对下带符号损失变化的精确极值；建立有限误差保证，将原始误差传播到损失、概率、预测和特征梯度，并反解得到给定学习级误差要求下的经验证原始容差；在高维von Mises-Fisher学习中给出完整实例化。

Result: 受控干预与大量已保存学习状态表明，相同原始误差可导致显著不同的学习后果；在相同学习级要求下，经验证数值容差在不同状态间可相差数个数量级。

Conclusion: 数值近似的充分性必须结合当前学习状态和需保持的量来评估；数值精度本身应被视为学习目标的一部分。

Abstract: How accurate must a numerical approximation be within a learning system? Primitive error alone cannot answer this question: errors of the same magnitude can have very different consequences for losses, predictions, and gradients at different learning states. We study this question through the learning objective itself. The objective weights classwise numerical errors nonuniformly according to the current state, so the importance of an error depends not only on its magnitude but also on the class it affects and the weight that class receives. For softmax cross-entropy, we characterize this coupling between class weights and errors and derive the exact extrema of the signed loss change over pairings of fixed non-target probability and score-error multisets, with the target probability and target score error held fixed. Building on this structure, we establish finite-error guarantees that propagate primitive error to losses, probabilities, predictions, and feature gradients, then invert these guarantees to obtain a certified primitive tolerance for the current state under prescribed learning-level error requirements. We give a complete instantiation of the framework in high-dimensional von Mises-Fisher learning. Controlled interventions and a large collection of saved learning states show that identical primitive error can produce substantially different learning consequences, while certified numerical tolerances vary by orders of magnitude across states under the same learning-level requirements. These results show that the adequacy of a numerical approximation must be assessed in relation to the current learning state and the quantity to be preserved; numerical accuracy should itself be treated as part of the learning objective.

</details>


### [61] [Same Loss, Different Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38786)
*Ningkang Peng,Xiaoqian Peng,Yifan He,Anjie Hu,Chao Tan,Peirong Ma,Yanhui Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了可微学习中前向值与反向梯度可能描述不同数学对象的问题，并提出AR/FR方法以保证前后向一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微学习通常假设前向评估的标量目标与反向传给优化器的梯度描述同一数学对象，但概率目标依赖有限特殊函数递推、自定义反向规则和数值截断时，这种对应可能失效。在高维von Mises-Fisher学习中，相同学习状态下的真实实现可能产生相同的前向分数和损失，却给出不同梯度并走向不同优化轨迹。

Method: 作者刻画了有限初值Bessel递推中这种不匹配的结构，说明逐类径向不匹配可通过概率组合成局部非保守更新场。随后提出AR/FR——一种固定深度的解析实现，联合构造势函数及其导数，从而按构造保证前后向一致性。

Result: 作者建立了相对于精确Bessel比在全部非负集中度轴上的均匀三阶误差界，并将该保证传播到学习分数和目标。随表示维度增加，原始有限递推顺序加深，而AR/FR最坏误差保证以三阶收紧，共同提供一致性、可认证保真性和固定深度计算。

Conclusion: 结果表明，可微数值原语不仅由其实现的值定义，也由它实际提供给优化器的导数定义；两者共同构成学习算法的数值实现。

Abstract: Differentiable learning typically assumes that the scalar objective evaluated in the forward pass and the gradient supplied to the optimizer in the backward pass describe the same mathematical object. We show that this correspondence can fail when probabilistic objectives rely on finite special-function recurrences, custom backward rules, and numerical clipping. In high-dimensional von Mises-Fisher learning, real numerical implementations can produce identical forward scores and losses at the same learning state while supplying different gradients and following different optimization trajectories. We characterize the structure of this mismatch in finite-start Bessel recurrence and show that classwise radial mismatch can compose through probabilities into a locally nonconservative update field. Evaluating the accuracy of special-function values and derivatives separately is therefore insufficient to characterize the realized learning objective. Motivated by this observation, we introduce AR/FR, a fixed-depth analytic realization that constructs a potential and its derivative jointly, ensuring forward-backward coherence by construction. We establish a uniform cubic-order error bound relative to the exact Bessel ratio over the entire nonnegative concentration axis and propagate this guarantee to learning scores and objectives. As representation dimension increases, the original finite recurrence becomes sequentially deeper, whereas the worst-case AR/FR error guarantee tightens cubically, jointly providing coherence, certified fidelity, and fixed-depth computation. These results suggest that a differentiable numerical primitive is defined by both the values it realizes and the derivatives it actually supplies to the optimizer; together, they constitute the numerical realization of the learning algorithm.

</details>


### [62] [LEARN-TS: LLM-Enhanced Alignment and Reconstruction with Normality Guidance for Multivariate Time-Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38789)
*Jahyeob Koo,Kio Yun,Byoungmo Koo,Jun-Geol Baek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 LEARN-TS，用冻结语言模型构建两种角色分离的语义表示，在无需时间配对文本的条件下增强多元时间序列异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元时间序列异常检测中重构误差难以可靠区分异常与良性偏差；语言语义可提供补充上下文，但现有多模态方法依赖难以稳定获取、且标准基准未提供的时间配对文本。

Method: 以冻结语言模型构建两种角色分离语义表示：(1) 窗口特定观测语义，编码时序与跨变量上下文但无精确数值，指导通道共享的块掩码重构；(2) 固定、与数据集无关的正态提示，提供窗口无关的语义参照，用于对齐正常表示并估计正态性差异。推理时对每个时间块掩码一次获得时间戳级重构证据，并由独立未掩码视图的差异进行条件调制。

Result: 在四个基准、16 项数据集-指标比较中，LEARN-TS 在 13 项取得最高平均性能。消融实验考察观测条件、正态对齐与评分联合、参照内容，显示排序收益依赖数据集，语义参照相对随机参照仅有适度平均提升。

Conclusion: 通过角色分离语义设计与正态性引导，可在不依赖时间配对文本的情况下提升多元时间序列异常检测，但语义参照的增益有限且依赖数据集。

Abstract: Reconstruction errors in multivariate time-series anomaly detection may not reliably distinguish abnormal behavior from benign deviations. Language-derived semantics offer complementary context, but existing multimodal approaches may rely on time-associated paired textual information that is difficult to obtain consistently and is not provided by standard multivariate time-series anomaly detection benchmarks. This setting poses two challenges: (1) conditioning masked reconstruction on window-specific semantics without exposing exact numerical targets or anomaly-specific cues, and (2) using a window-independent concept of normality as a complementary semantic reference rather than an independent anomaly detector. We propose LLM-Enhanced Alignment and Reconstruction with Normality Guidance for Time Series (LEARN-TS), which uses a frozen language model to construct two role-separated semantic representations without requiring temporally paired external text. Window-specific observation semantics encode temporal and cross-variable context without exact numerical values to guide channel-shared patch-masked reconstruction. A fixed, dataset-agnostic normality prompt provides a window-independent semantic reference for aligning normal representations and estimating normality discrepancy. At inference, masking each temporal patch once yields timestamp-level reconstruction evidence, conditionally modulated by discrepancy from a separate unmasked view. Across four benchmarks, LEARN-TS achieves the highest mean performance in 13 of 16 dataset-metric comparisons. Controlled ablations examine observation conditioning, joint normality alignment and scoring, and reference content, showing dataset-dependent ranking benefits and modest average gains from semantic over random references.

</details>


### [63] [Evaluating Persistent Calibration under Evolving Model Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38797)
*Victor Wang,Thomas Hofweber,Mohit Bansal,Elias Stengel-Eskin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"持续校准"问题：要求置信度估计器在模型知识变化时无需重复监督即可忠实反映其知识，并通过跨检查点的知识对比集实验揭示现有方法在这一目标上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统正从静态模型库转向具备持续适应与学习能力的智能体。要维持其可信度，模型必须产生能动态反映其不断变化的知识与技能的置信度估计。然而现有置信度估计通常假设模型知识固定，无法应对知识随时间演变的情形，因此需要研究置信度是否能随知识变化而自动调整这一核心问题。

Method: 作者提出"持续校准"（persistent calibration）概念，要求在无重复监督下置信度估计器能忠实反映模型变化中的知识。具体操作化为：在开源模型的多个检查点之间考察持久校准，检验在早期检查点上训练的置信度估计器能否泛化到后期检查点。为衡量置信度与知识的关系，定义了"知识对比集"（knowledge contrast sets）上的校准评估，即包含某个检查点答对而另一个检查点答错的问题子集，以反映知识变化。方法涵盖推理时方法与微调方法，并与在"未来"检查点上训练的oracle方法对比。

Result: 实验表明，无论是推理时方法还是微调方法，在对比集校准上都劣于在未来检查点上训练的oracle方法，即使这些方法在整个数据集上校准良好也是如此。作者进一步提供了证据支持如下假设：持久校准之所以困难，是因为在给定检查点上存在大量可能校准良好的置信度函数，其中只有一部分依赖可泛化到其他检查点的元知识特征。此外，多检查点训练有助于改善对比集校准，说明可借此识别在知识变化中保持稳健的置信度特征。

Conclusion: 持续校准是一个具有挑战性且尚未解决的问题，尤其在知识发生变化的对比集上表现明显。核心难点在于校准良好的置信度函数空间巨大，而具备跨检查点泛化能力的元知识特征仅是其中一小部分。多检查点训练为改进对比集校准提供了可行方向，有望帮助识别跨知识变化稳健的置信度特征，从而提升持续学习智能体的可信度。

Abstract: As AI systems move from static repositories to agents that are capable of continual adaptation and learning, maintaining their trustworthiness means equipping the models backing them with the ability to produce confidence estimates that dynamically reflect their changing skills and knowledge. We introduce the problem of persistent calibration, which requires a confidence estimator to faithfully reflect the knowledge contained in a model as that knowledge changes, without recurring supervision. We operationalize this by examining persistent calibration across checkpoints of open models, asking whether confidence estimators trained on earlier checkpoints can generalize to later ones. Specifically, we aim to shed light on whether confidence is dependent on knowledge, a question with implications for the reliability of confidence estimates. To measure this relationship, we define and evaluate calibration on knowledge contrast sets: subsets containing questions that one checkpoint answers correctly and another checkpoint answers incorrectly, reflecting a change in knowledge. We show that both inference-time and fine-tuning methods fall short on contrast-set calibration compared to oracle methods trained on future checkpoints, even for methods that are well-calibrated on the full dataset. We provide evidence for the hypothesis that persistent calibration is challenging because there is a vast space of possible confidence functions that are well-calibrated on a given checkpoint, out of which only some rely on meta-knowledge features that would generalize to other checkpoints. Towards improving contrast-set calibration, we show that multi-checkpoint training helps, suggesting an avenue for identifying confidence features that remain robust across changing knowledge.

</details>


### [64] [Explicit Trajectory Diversity for RL-Based Post-Training of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38805)
*Huaiyu Fu,Heng Cao,Hao Wang,Jian Ya,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出轨迹引导联合策略优化（TJPO），通过用户指定的任务特定轨迹描述符显式定义多样性，在单策略框架下优化显式轨迹多样性，在保持任务性能的同时提升任务相关的行为多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM后训练中的多样性概念多为隐式的，源于一般随机性与正则化机制，而非明确针对任务相关的行为变化。隐式多样性虽有用，却无法指定对特定任务应当鼓励哪些行为变化形式，因此需要显式、可解释且可控的轨迹多样性方法。

Method: 核心思想是用用户指定、任务特定的轨迹描述符来定义多样性：将每条采样轨迹映射为可解释的行为表示，并以集合级泛函在描述符矩阵上度量多样性。基于此提出TJPO，一种单策略框架，在采样的轨迹组上优化显式多样性，无需基于种群（population-based）的策略训练，并通过轨迹级学习信号在基于组的策略优化中实例化。

Result: 在Sokoban和ALFWorld上的实验表明，TJPO在保持有竞争力的任务性能的同时提升了任务特定的轨迹多样性。描述符与轨迹分析显示，所学变化遵循指定的行为维度，并包含不同的成功策略。额外实验结果表明，显式塑造轨迹多样性可帮助LLM智能体满足用户需求，并在任务条件变化时保持有效性。

Conclusion: 通过轨迹描述符显式定义并优化多样性，可使多样性目标既具可解释性又具可控性，为LLM后训练中任务相关的行为多样性提供了一种有效且实用的方案。

Abstract: LLM agents often admit multiple high-quality solutions to the same task, differing in reasoning structure, tool-use pattern, or interaction trajectory. Yet existing notions of diversity in LLM post-training are mostly implicit, arising from general stochasticity and regularization mechanisms rather than explicitly targeting task-relevant behavioral variation. While such implicit diversity can be useful, it does not directly specify which forms of behavioral variation should be encouraged for a given task. In this work, we study explicit trajectory diversity in RL-based post-training for LLMs. Our key idea is to define diversity through user-specified, task-specific trajectory descriptors, which map each sampled trajectory to an interpretable behavioral representation, and then measure diversity as a set-level functional over the resulting descriptor matrix. Building on this formulation, we introduce Trajectory-guided Joint Policy Optimization(TJPO), a single-policy framework that optimizes explicit diversity over sampled trajectory groups, avoiding the need for population-based policy training, and instantiate it within group-based policy optimization through trajectory-level learning signals. This design makes the diversity objective both interpretable and controllable. Experiments on Sokoban and ALFWorld show that TJPO improves task-specific trajectory diversity while maintaining competitive task performance. Descriptor and trajectory analyses show that the learned variation follows the specified behavioral dimensions and includes distinct successful strategies. Extra experiment results suggest that explicitly shaping trajectory diversity can help LLM agents satisfy user requirements and remain effective when task conditions change.

</details>


### [65] [Blackboard Intelligence Can Surpass Autoregressive on Globally Constrained Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38806)
*Woosang Jeon,Jaeyeon Kim,Sham Kakade,Yilun Du,Amrit Singh Bedi,Arun Kumar Chithanar,Chul Lee,Taehyeong Kim,Sitan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"黑板智能"（Blackboard Intelligence）推理范式，用扩散语言模型的任意顺序预测接口在可修改的画布上搜索解状态，以平均置信度引导推理时搜索与修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一词预测驱动了大模型进步，但模型在受复杂全局约束支配的问题上仍有困难。作者推测部分局限源自下一词预测所诱导的从左到右因果推理接口本身，希望探索一种非因果、可修正的推理时视角。

Method: 以扩散语言模型实例化"黑板智能"：模型在固定且可修改的画布上工作，搜索候选解状态而非沿因果从左到右轨迹提交。利用掩码扩散目标的任意顺序预测接口，暴露对部分填充状态的预测；使用标准掩码扩散目标中可得的模型内部量——平均置信度——作为全局一致性的代理，指导推理时的搜索与修正。在固定微调后的 LLaDA-8B-Instruct 检查点下进行实验。

Result: 在 ZebraLogic、Nurse Rostering 和 Job-Shop Scheduling 上，Blackboard 一致提升推理表现，并显著优于同规模自回归基线：ZebraLogic-Hard 达 90.4% 准确率，Nurse Rostering 达 76.4% 精确可行性，JSSP 达 80.2% 最优性。更强的自回归搜索与精修在 ZebraLogic-Hard 上也无法弥合差距；Blackboard 在该任务和 JSSP 上超越了所测试的前沿大模型，尽管后者规模更大且测试时推理能力更强。

Conclusion: 证据表明，下一词预测的推理接口本身可能限制模型处理复杂全局约束问题的能力；采用扩散模型的可修改画布与任意顺序预测，并以平均置信度引导搜索，可在不改变模型权重的情况下显著提升约束推理表现，甚至超过更大规模的前沿模型。

Abstract: Next-token prediction has driven remarkable progress in large language models, yet a growing body of evidence suggests that they can struggle on problems governed by complex global constraints. In this work, we focus on this regime and ask whether some of these limitations arise from the inference interface induced by next-token prediction itself. We study this question through blackboard intelligence: an inference-time perspective in which a model works on a fixed, revisable canvas and searches over candidate solution states rather than committing to a causal, left-to-right trajectory. We instantiate this idea with diffusion language models, whose any-order prediction interface naturally exposes predictions over partially filled solution states. Our key observation is that mean confidence, a simple model-internal quantity available from the standard masked diffusion objective, provides a useful proxy for global coherence and can guide inference-time search and revision. Empirically, across ZebraLogic, Nurse Rostering, and Job-Shop Scheduling, Blackboard consistently improves inference while holding the fine-tuned LLaDA-8B-Instruct checkpoint fixed and substantially outperforms same-scale autoregressive baselines, reaching 90.4% accuracy on ZebraLogic-Hard, 76.4% exact feasibility on Nurse Rostering, and 80.2% optimality on JSSP. Stronger autoregressive search and refinement also fail to close the gap on ZebraLogic-Hard, while Blackboard surpasses tested frontier LLMs there and on JSSP despite their substantially greater scale and strong test-time reasoning. We open-source our codebase at https://github.com/jwoosang1/blackboard-intelligence.

</details>


### [66] [Learning Chaos Without Seeing Chaos: Extrapolation of Global Dynamics in Autoregressive Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38814)
*Yilun Liu,Yi Zhang,Ganyu Wu,Sikuan Yan,Mengyue Wang,Alois Knoll,Volker Tresp,Yunpu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 小型自回归Transformer仅在受限参数区间上训练，却能外推出非线性动力系统的全局动力学组织（倍周期、混沌、吸引子），甚至复现Feigenbaum常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者关心一个核心问题：仅从局部观测、有限参数区间学到的动力学，能否恢复整个系统未被观测到的全局结构？自回归模型擅长逐步预测并递归展开长时程行为，这为研究该问题提供了天然框架。

Method: 从头训练小型自回归Transformer，在若干非线性系统的受限参数区间轨迹上训练，包括logistic映射、sine映射、Lorenz系统和广义Hopf系统；控制参数与状态轨迹均以连续token序列表示。随后在训练分布之外的参数上进行闭环（递归）评估，并通过训练过程追踪与因果干预分析参数信息如何经注意力流入状态预测。

Result: 在远超训练分布的参数下闭环评估时，模型能高保真恢复自相似倍周期级联、混沌动力学与吸引子结构。logistic映射上可复现至周期128的逐级倍周期，得到有限阶标度比4.6687，与Feigenbaum常数误差小于5×10⁻⁴。

Conclusion: 一个极为狭窄的局部行为窗口，可能就足以让自回归Transformer泛化到系统未见过的全局动力学组织；同时因果干预揭示了控制参数信息经注意力影响闭环动力学的机制。

Abstract: Autoregressive models are trained to predict a system's behavior one step at a time, and recursive generation allows the learned dynamics to unfold over long horizons. To what extent can such dynamics learned from local observations recover broader organization of an underlying system that was only partially observed during training? Here we study small autoregressive transformers trained from scratch on trajectories sampled from restricted parameter regimes of several non-linear dynamical systems, including logistic and sine maps, the Lorenz system, and the generalized Hopf system, with control parameters and state trajectories represented as sequences of continuous tokens. Under closed-loop evaluation at parameters far outside the training distribution, the models can recover self-similar period-doubling cascades, chaotic dynamics, and attractor structures with remarkable visual and numerical fidelity. For the logistic map, a transformer reproduces successive period doublings up to period 128, yielding a finite-order scaling ratio of 4.6687, matching the Feigenbaum constant to within $5\times10^{-4}$. We further investigate how these structures emerge over the course of training, and reveal with causal interventions how control-parameter information is processed through attention into state prediction and shapes the resulting closed-loop dynamics. These results suggest that a surprisingly narrow window into a system's local behavior may suffice for autoregressive transformers to generalize to its unseen global dynamical organization.

</details>


### [67] [SparLeak: Privacy Leakage from Sparse Attention in LLM Inference on Shared GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38830)
*Fahao Chen,Linkang Du,Jinhao Zhou,Peng Li,Zhou Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了稀疏注意力在LLM长上下文推理中引入的新GPU微架构侧信道SIMA，并提出相位感知攻击SparLeak，可从预填充和解码阶段轨迹中推断用户查询属性并重建私密响应，平均攻击成功率分别达90.9%和87.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏注意力被广泛用于加速现代大语言模型（LLM）的长上下文推理，但其依赖输入的执行行为引入了此前未被探索的隐私风险。作者希望揭示这种由稀疏注意力导致的秘密相关KV缓存访问模式所形成的新型GPU微架构侧信道，并评估其隐私泄露的严重性。

Method: 提出名为SIMA（Sparsity-Induced Memory Access）的GPU微架构侧信道，源于稀疏注意力引发的、与秘密相关的键值缓存访问模式。基于该现象设计相位感知侧信道攻击SparLeak：在LLM推理过程中提取SIMA轨迹，从页面级观测重建近似的token级稀疏性剖面，并结合基于画像的学习，分别从预填充阶段轨迹推断查询属性、从解码阶段轨迹重建自回归响应。

Result: 在三种LLM架构、三种稀疏注意力机制和三个隐私敏感数据集上进行广泛评估，在真实LLM服务设置下，SparLeak对属性推断的平均攻击成功率为90.9%，对响应重建的平均成功率为87.3%。作者还提供了匿名化的SIMA轨迹、训练好的攻击模型、评估脚本及文档作为工件。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在部署基于稀疏注意力的LLM系统时必须考虑SIMA泄露问题，表明稀疏注意力虽提升效率，却带来了实际且严重的隐私风险，需要相应的防护措施。

Abstract: Sparse attention is widely used to accelerate long-context inference in modern large language models (LLMs), but its input-dependent execution behavior introduces previously unexplored privacy risks. We identify a new GPU micro-architectural side channel, termed Sparsity-Induced Memory Access (SIMA), which arises from secret-dependent key-value cache access patterns induced by sparse attention.
  Based on this observation, we present SparLeak, a phase-aware side-channel attack that extracts SIMA traces during LLM inference and enables two practical privacy extractions: query attribute inference from prefill-phase traces and autoregressive response reconstruction from decoding-phase traces. By reconstructing approximate token-level sparsity profiles from page-level observations and applying profiling-based learning, SparLeak accurately recovers sensitive information, including user-query attributes and private LLM response content. Extensive evaluation across three LLM architectures, three sparse attention mechanisms, and three privacy-sensitive datasets shows that SparLeak achieves average attack success rates of 90.9% for attribute inference and 87.3% for response reconstruction under real-world LLM serving settings, highlighting the significance to account for SIMA leakage when deploying sparse-attention-based LLM systems. We provide anonymized SIMA traces, trained attack models, evaluation scripts, and documentation as artifacts at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Janus_artifacts/.

</details>


### [68] [ReSCENE: Server-Side Replay for Structural Mitigation of Catastrophic Forgetting in Federated Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38833)
*Sungmin Kang,Zhengzhong Tu,Sunwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReSCENE通过让客户端上传本地数据的压缩替代表示并由服务器保存过去任务的替代表示来训练全局模型，从结构上缓解联邦持续学习中的灾难性遗忘，并引入时序牧群法压缩缓冲，实现高效且高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦持续学习方法大多在客户端训练、服务器聚合的循环上加装抗遗忘机制，这会抑制新任务的学习并加重资源受限客户端的负担，因此需要一种从结构上缓解灾难性遗忘的方法。

Method: 提出ReSCENE：每个客户端上传本地数据的小型压缩替代表示，服务器保存过去任务的替代表示并与当前任务的替代表示一起训练全局模型；同时提出时序牧群法（temporal herding），从一个任务累积的替代表示池中选择更新的替代表示存入压缩缓冲区。

Result: 理论分析表明该缓冲区比累积全部替代表示能更贴近地表示原始任务数据；在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和TinyImageNet上，ReSCENE在七个基线中取得最高精度，平均精度提升达31.1个百分点，客户端计算量仅需模型的0.11倍，上传量比模型更新基线少至多179倍。

Conclusion: ReSCENE在扩展到更多客户端和更大模型时仍保持有效和高效，是联邦持续学习中一种实用的方法。

Abstract: Federated continual learning must integrate new tasks over time without losing earlier-task knowledge. Most existing methods attach an anti-forgetting mechanism to the client-trained, server-aggregated loop of federated learning, which holds back new learning to preserve earlier knowledge and burdens resource-constrained clients. We propose ReSCENE, which structurally mitigates catastrophic forgetting by having each client upload a small condensed surrogate of its local data while the server keeps the surrogates of past tasks and trains the global model on them together with the current task surrogates. For efficient server memory, we introduce temporal herding, which selects the more recent surrogates from the pool accumulated over a task into a compressed buffer. Our study provides a theoretical analysis showing that this buffer can represent the original task data more closely than full accumulation of all surrogates. Across CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and TinyImageNet, ReSCENE achieves the strongest accuracy over seven baselines, by up to $31.1$ points of average accuracy, while requiring as little as $0.11\times$ of the client computation and up to $179\times$ less upload than the model-update baselines. ReSCENE further demonstrates its effectiveness when scaled to larger client populations and larger models while remaining efficient, which makes it a practical method for federated continual learning.

</details>


### [69] [scTrilemma: Balancing Identity, Invariance, and Fidelity in Single-Cell Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38840)
*Yunhak Oh,Yoonho Lee,Junseok Lee,Namkyeong Lee,Sang-Yeon Hwang,Yinhua Piao,Hyomin Kim,Seonghwan Kim,Jaechang Lim,Woo Youn Kim,Sungsoo Ahn,Chanyoung Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 scTrilemma，一种隐瓶颈 VAE 表示学习方法，通过将表达变异分流到嵌入、解码器和先验三处，同时满足生物身份保持、上下文鲁棒性和基因级表达保真三重需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞 RNA-seq 表示学习本质上是无标签的：细胞身份、状态和上下文并非固定训练目标，因此何为信号、何为噪声取决于具体分析。单一表示需同时保持生物身份与状态、对干扰上下文稳健，并保留基因级变异用于表达分析，这三者构成"表示三难困境"。

Method: 提出 scTrilemma：一种隐瓶颈 VAE，将表达衍生的变异路由至嵌入、解码器或先验，而非全部塞入同一嵌入。具体做法包括按表达对基因 token 进行门控、让细胞表示经由解码器路由、以无标签伪批量上下文对先验进行条件化，全部在单一重构目标下完成，无需目标标注或辅助表示损失。

Result: 在基于版本发布的零样本评估中（连续 CZ CELLxGENE Census 版本），scTrilemma 同时在三项需求上领先，并在多种疾病场景下保留生物状态、差异表达和通路结构。潜空间干预进一步表明，上下文可在几乎不损失其他需求的情况下被去除，剩余张力集中在身份与保真度之间。

Conclusion: scTrilemma 通过多路径分解表达变异，为单细胞表示学习的"三难困境"提供了有效解法，能兼顾身份、鲁棒性与表达保真，且代码已公开。

Abstract: Single-cell RNA-seq representation learning is fundamentally label-free: cell identities, states, and contexts are not fixed training targets, so what constitutes signal or nuisance is analysis-dependent. A single representation must therefore preserve biological identity and state, remain robust to nuisance context, and retain the gene-level variation needed for expression analysis, three demands we call the representation trilemma.
