To tackle this problem, we introduce scTrilemma, a latent-bottleneck VAE that routes expression-derived variation to the embedding, the decoder, or the prior rather than forcing all of it through one embedding. It gates gene tokens by expression, routes the cell representation through the decoder, and conditions the prior on unlabeled pseudo-bulk context, under a single reconstruction objective and without target annotations or auxiliary representation losses. In release-based zero-shot evaluation on successive CZ CELLxGENE Census releases, scTrilemma leads all three demands at once and preserves biological-state, differential-expression, and pathway structure across multiple disease settings. Latent interventions further show that context can be removed at almost no cost to the other demands, leaving identity against fidelity as the remaining tension. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/yunhak0/scTrilemma.

</details>


### [70] [Scoring Higher, Answering Worse: Mitigating Reward Hacking in Rubric-Based RL via Protocol-Level Rubrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38847)
*Maoqi Liu,Junwei He,Bowen Zhang,Feiran Li,Wentao Ma,Rongyi Lin,Shuhan Zhong,Quan Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示基于评分细则的强化学习（Rubric-RL）中加权求和聚合方式的缺陷，并提出协议级评分细则（ProRubric）方法，通过分组维度并采用"全部满足才计分"的聚合方式，在临床咨询任务上显著提升回答恰当性而不损失覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏验证器的场景中，Rubric-RL 依赖评判模型按评分细则逐条打分，再以加权求和聚合成奖励。作者指出这种加性聚合是薄弱环节：各标准之间可以相互补偿，模型可以通过提供无人要求的额外建议来"买回"遗漏关键决策所丢失的分数。在临床咨询任务上，这种策略评分更高但回答质量更差——覆盖率上升，而在留出医师标准上的恰当性甚至低于未训练模型。因此需要重新设计聚合方式。

Method: 作者提出协议级评分细则（ProRubric）：保留评分细则中各项标准的内容（一词不改），仅改变聚合方式。将清单归纳为少数几个"协议级维度"，只有当某维度下所有标准全部满足且其失败条款未被触发时，该维度才计分。分组一次性离线完成，优化器保持不变。

Result: 实验表明：将标准分组使其必须共同满足，可在标准完全不变的情况下挽回约三分之一的损失；缩短回答几乎无法挽回损失。ProRubric 将恰当性提升 10.8 个百分点且不损失覆盖率，在两种规模下均取得七项基准测试的最佳平均成绩。

Conclusion: 奖励的有效性不仅取决于评分细则验证了什么内容，还取决于它如何聚合。ProRubric 通过改变聚合方式（而非标准本身）解决了加性奖励的补偿性漏洞，证明了聚合设计在 Rubric-RL 中的关键作用。

Abstract: Rubric-based reinforcement learning (Rubric-RL) trains language models where no verifier exists. A judge checks each criterion of a rubric, and the verdicts are aggregated into a reward, most often by a weighted sum. We show that this additive aggregation is the weak point. Under a sum, criteria compensate for one another: a policy that misses the one decision that matters can buy the points back with advice nobody asked for. On clinical consultation, such a policy scores higher and answers worse. Rubric coverage rises while appropriateness on held-out physician criteria falls below the untrained model. The medical criteria are not to blame. Grouped so that they must hold together, the same criteria, unchanged to the word, recover a third of the loss; shorter answers recover almost none. We therefore propose Protocol-level Rubrics (ProRubric), which keeps what the criteria ask for and changes how they are aggregated. It groups a checklist into a few protocol-level dimensions. A dimension counts only when all of its criteria hold and its failure clause does not fire. The grouping is done once, offline, and leaves the optimizer unchanged. ProRubric raises appropriateness by 10.8 points without losing coverage and has the best seven-benchmark average at both scales. Reward validity is set not only by what a rubric verifies, but by how it aggregates. Code is available at https://github.com/Estrellajer/ProRubric

</details>


### [71] [Visualizing Distribution Coverage in Generative Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38853)
*Yifei Wang,Xiaoyu Wu,Tsu-Jui Fu,Chen Chen,Liang-Chieh Chen,Zhe Gan,Chen Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出用pass@k评测蒸馏扩散模型，发现单次采样评测掩盖了覆盖率损失：分布匹配类目标牺牲覆盖换取pass@1，而一致性/轨迹类目标更好地保留教师分布覆盖，该现象在少步因果视频生成中同样存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒸馏少步模型被普遍认为可匹配甚至超越多步教师，但GenEval2等标准评测每提示仅采一个样本，得分提升可能掩盖分布覆盖的损失，因此需重新审视蒸馏模型是否真正匹配教师。

Method: 引入pass@k作为评测指标，衡量k个独立样本中至少一个满足质量标准的概率；k=1时退化为标准单次评测，k增大时曲线可揭示额外采样是发现新的成功模式还是重复同一模式。用该指标分析CFG的质量—覆盖权衡，并对比不同训练目标（分布匹配、一致性、轨迹类）的少步蒸馏模型，再扩展至少步因果视频生成。

Result: CFG下更高引导提升pass@1，但优势随k增大缩小并反转；少步蒸馏模型中该权衡沿训练目标分化：分布匹配目标集中学生输出分布，提高早期命中率却侵蚀大预算覆盖，一致性与轨迹类目标即使在大k下也更好保留教师覆盖；该权衡延伸至少步因果视频生成。

Conclusion: 扩散蒸馏存在此前被忽视的代价：在图像与视频生成中，训练目标的选择从根本上决定少步模型是继承教师的分布覆盖，还是以覆盖换取单次采样质量。

Abstract: Diffusion distillation is widely adopted to accelerate sampling, and the resulting few-step models are broadly believed to match or even surpass their multi-step teachers in generation. However, standard evaluations such as GenEval2 typically draw only one sample per prompt, so improved scores may fail to reveal losses in distribution coverage. We therefore revisit whether distilled models truly match their teachers beyond single-draw performance using \textbf{pass@$\mathbf{k}$}, which measures the probability that at least one of $k$ independent samples satisfies a quality criterion. At $k{=}1$, pass@$k$ reduces to standard single-draw evaluation. As $k$ grows, the curve reveals whether additional draws find genuinely different successes or merely revisit the same modes, directly exposing how broadly a model covers the space of valid outputs. We first show that classifier-free guidance (CFG), whose quality--coverage tradeoff is well established, is the clearest case: higher guidance improves pass@$1$, but its advantage shrinks and reverses at larger $k$. Applying pass@$k$ to few-step distilled models, we find the same tradeoff splits along training objectives: distribution-matching objectives concentrate the student's output distribution, boosting early-hit rates while eroding large-budget coverage, whereas consistency and trajectory-based objectives better preserve the teacher's coverage even at large $k$. We further show that this tradeoff extends to few-step causal video generation. Our findings reveal a previously overlooked cost of diffusion distillation: across both image and video generation, the choice of training objective fundamentally determines whether a few-step model inherits its teacher's distribution coverage or trades it away for single-draw quality.

</details>


### [72] [Mitigating the Length-Scaling Tax with Online Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38854)
*Xu Wan,Wenyue Xu,Shengjie Zhao,Mingyang Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"长度缩放税"(LST)以量化RL后训练在已解决问题上产生的冗余长度，并提出长度自蒸馏(LSD)方法，通过将已解决提示路由到在线策略蒸馏、对未解决提示保留原RL目标，有效缓解LST。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习后训练中的长度缩放常被视为推理能力提升的标志，但会导致已解决问题的回答变得不必要地冗长。作者希望量化这一副作用并加以缓解。

Method: 提出长度自蒸馏(LSD)：对已解决的提示采用在线策略蒸馏，对未解决的提示保留原始RL目标；教师模型使用在线策略的指数移动平均，无需外部模型。

Result: LSD在多个变体上达到与RL相当或更优的性能，同时显著抑制简单查询的长度增长。将单轮推理的LST从19.0%降至-3.7%，多轮智能体任务从31.4%降至13.7%。

Conclusion: LSD能在RL后训练中有效保持简单查询的简洁回答模式，同时支持困难查询的高效探索。

Abstract: Length scaling during reinforcement-learning (RL) post-training is often viewed as a sign of improved reasoning ability, especially on difficult problems, but may also make responses to already-solved problems unnecessarily verbose. We quantify this side effect as the length-scaling tax (LST): excess response length on already-solved queries without a commensurate accuracy gain. To mitigate LST, we propose Length Self-Distillation (LSD), which routes solved prompts to on-policy distillation and retains the original RL objective for unsolved prompts. LSD uses an exponential moving average of the online policy as its teacher, requiring no external model. We find that LSD achieves comparable or better performance than RL across multiple variants, while substantially curbing response-length growth on easy queries. LSD reduces LST from 19.0% to -3.7% on single-turn reasoning and from 31.4% to 13.7% on multi-turn agentic tasks, demonstrating that LSD effectively preserves concise response patterns on easy queries while supporting efficient exploration on difficult queries during RL post-training.

</details>


### [73] [Optimal Design for Active Preference Learning with Biased LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38860)
*Zhongman Du,Huiming Zhang,Haodong Zhu,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出Nuisance-Adjusted Optimal Design (NAOD)，一种在主动偏好学习中同时考虑人类偏好与LLM评委偏差的比较选择策略，通过干扰调整优先获取与策略相关的目标信息，并用Frank-Wolfe算法优化。理论证明其达到策略风险的局部渐近极小极大下界，实验在Chatbot Arena上使代理策略平均后悔降低29.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类偏好标注成本高昂，主动偏好学习可通过选择信息量大的比较来降本，LLM评委能提供可扩展反馈，但评委偏好可能与目标人群偏离。即便用可信参考数据校准，主动采集仍会改变比较分布并暴露残余评委偏差。

Method: 将评委偏差纳入采集设计，而非依赖单独的校准阶段。在联合估计下，看似对奖励信息量大的比较可能反映评委偏差、对人类偏好信息反而不多。据此提出NAOD：在干扰调整后优先选择策略相关的目标信息，并用Frank-Wolfe算法求解优化问题；同时构造达到下界的估计量。

Result: 理论上建立了策略风险的尖锐条件局部渐近极小极大下界并构造达到该界的估计量；刻画了学习干扰表示的有限样本代价，表明表示误差可能逆转oracle设计的优势。实验上在Chatbot Arena数据（17个评委、15种预算配置、15个随机簇级划分）验证：NAOD相对匹配的目标信息设计使代理策略平均后悔降低29.1%，优于现有方法，并提升了留出数据上的人类偏好预测。

Conclusion: 将评委偏差作为干扰直接纳入采集设计（NAOD）可在主动偏好学习中有效降低成本、提升对齐效果，理论与实验均表明其在策略后悔与偏好预测上优于现有方法。

Abstract: Learning from human preferences is central to large language model (LLM) alignment, but human preference annotation is costly. Active preference learning reduces this cost by selecting informative comparisons, and LLM judges can provide additional scalable feedback. However, the preferences of the judges may deviate from those of the target human population. Even after calibration on trusted reference data, active acquisition can shift the comparison distribution and expose residual judge bias. We therefore incorporate judge deviations into the acquisition design rather than relying on a separate calibration stage. Under joint estimation, comparisons that appear highly informative about the reward may also reflect judge bias and therefore provide less information about human preferences. To address this issue, we propose Nuisance-Adjusted Optimal Design (NAOD), a comparison-selection strategy that prioritizes policy-relevant target information after nuisance adjustment and uses the Frank-Wolfe algorithm for optimization. Theoretically, we establish a sharp conditional local asymptotic minimax lower bound on policy risk and construct an estimator that attains it. We further characterize the finite-sample cost of learning the nuisance representation and show that representation error can reverse an oracle design advantage. Finally, we validate these predictions experimentally and evaluate NAOD on Chatbot Arena data across 17 judges, 15 budget configurations, and 15 random cluster-level splits. NAOD reduces the mean regret of proxy policy by 29.1% relative to a matched target-information design, outperforms existing methods, and improves human-preference prediction on held-out data.

</details>


### [74] [GeoNest: Learning to Select Failure-Aware Neighborhoods for the Irregular Knapsack Problem in a Circular Container](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38863)
*Zhongman Du,Huiming Zhang,Linlin Yang,Sheng Xu,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GeoNest——一种由强化学习图策略驱动的失效感知大邻域搜索框架，用于固定圆形二维不规则背包问题，在相同时间预算下将平均材料利用率较最先进独立打包求解器提升约0.9%（工业集约0.6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在制造中最大化材料利用率需要二维不规则背包问题的紧凑打包，但传统几何打包求解器虽能产生紧密布局，却会将残余空间分割成孤立小凹陷，无法容纳有价值的多边形，形成后期打包瓶颈。

Method: GeoNest先通过配对失败目标多边形与残余凹陷构造邻域；用解释性摆放姿态识别阻挡候选插入的已放置多边形；由这些诊断出的阻塞关系定义有界、固定项目的修复子问题交给底层几何求解器；再由图策略（经强化学习训练）选择最有前景的子问题执行。并构建CircleNest-Bench基准（2,391个负载可控实例，来自四个轮廓来源，含留出的工业CAD源）。

Result: 在相同总时间预算下，GeoNest在三个主要测试集上平均利用率较最先进独立打包求解器提升约0.9%，在留出的工业集上提升约0.6%。

Conclusion: GeoNest以失效感知的大邻域搜索结合强化学习图策略，有效突破了后期打包瓶颈，提升了材料利用率，并在含工业CAD的基准上验证了泛化性。

Abstract: The two-dimensional irregular knapsack problem in a fixed circular container is an important combinatorial optimization problem for maximizing material utilization in manufacturing. Conventional geometric packing solvers can produce tightly packed layouts, yet they often partition the residual space into isolated small pockets that cannot fit valuable unplaced polygons. To overcome this late-stage packing bottleneck, we propose a failure-aware large neighborhood search framework named GeoNest, driven by a graph policy trained via reinforcement learning. Specifically, we first construct neighborhoods by pairing failed target polygons with residual pockets. We then use explanatory poses to identify the placed polygons that block candidate insertions. These diagnosed blocking relations define bounded, fixed-item repair subproblems for the underlying geometric solver. Finally, the graph policy selects the most promising subproblem for execution. For evaluation, we introduce CircleNest-Bench, a benchmark comprising 2,391 load-controlled instances from four contour sources, including a held-out industrial CAD source. Experimental results demonstrate that, under the same total time budget, GeoNest improves mean utilization over a state-of-the-art standalone packing solver by about 0.9% on average across the three main test sets and by about 0.6% on the held-out industrial set.

</details>


### [75] [On Parameters of Nonlinear Scalar Dynamics from Video: Invariants, Calibration, and Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38877)
*Wenjie Wang,Yuanyuan Wang,Zixiang Jiang,Shaoan Xie,Mingming Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为从视频中估计物理参数的问题，针对非线性标量二阶ODE建立了可辨识性理论，揭示了参数可辨识性取决于ODE族类型，并用实验验证了预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从视频恢复动力学方程参数的可辨识性理论此前仅限于线性时不变（LTI）二阶系统，非线性标量动力学的可辨识性尚不清楚，因此需要建立相应理论。

Method: 在共享非塌缩状态映射和显式同状态速度覆盖条件下，按速度依赖与状态坐标变换的交互方式对非线性标量二阶ODE进行分类分析：对速度至多线性族证明兼容性强制仿射坐标对齐；对更广的有限速度特征族在曲率可分离条件下推广仿射结论；对含速度平方项族用定律导出归一化实现规范律间的仿射比较。

Result: 理论表明部分参数唯一可辨识，另一些族仅不变参数组合可辨识或需外部物理标定；给出显式参数关系、不变量与标定条件。合成系统及真实单摆、自由落体视频实验支持了预测的参数关系、覆盖效应与标定需求。

Conclusion: 非线性标量二阶ODE的参数可辨识性取决于ODE族结构，可通过仿射对齐与归一化刻画可辨识参数及不变量，实验验证了理论预测。

Abstract: Physical parameter estimation from video aims to recover the parameters of a known family of governing dynamical equations from pixel observations. Existing identifiability theory for this setting has focused on linear time-invariant (LTI) second-order systems, leaving open what can be identified for nonlinear scalar dynamics. We develop an identifiability theory for nonlinear scalar second-order ODEs, organized by how their velocity dependence interacts with changes of the learned state coordinate. Under a shared non-collapsed state map and explicit same-state velocity-coverage conditions, we show that parameter identifiability depends on the ODE family: some parameters are uniquely identifiable, while in other families only invariant parameter combinations are identifiable or external physical calibration is required. For laws that are at most linear in velocity, compatibility forces affine coordinate alignment, yielding explicit parameter relations, invariants, and calibration conditions. This affine conclusion extends to broader finite velocity-feature families when coordinate curvature can be separated from the declared velocity dependence. For families admitting a squared-velocity term, nonlinear coordinate ambiguity can remain; a law-derived normalization instead enables affine comparison between canonical laws. Experiments on synthetic systems and real pendulum and free-fall videos support the predicted parameter relations, coverage effects, and calibration requirements.

</details>


### [76] [Does Learning Protein Folding Generalize to Broader Reasoning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38879)
*Yong Liu,Zhanpeng Shi,Yizhou Dang,Zhongyue Zhang,Xiaoliang Shi,Zhijian Wei,Shuangjia Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出问题：学习折叠蛋白质能否赋予通用模型可复用的推理能力？为此构建了蛋白质衍生的问答数据集 FoldingCorpus 和训练方法 Fold2Reason，通过离散结构答案与连续 3D 几何两种互补信号进行后训练，在蛋白结构预测和 10 个跨领域推理基准上均取得正向提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型高度依赖人类文本，而人类文本往往只传达表层答案，而非其背后的空间与结构逻辑。蛋白质折叠是天然的试验台，因为一个已解析的结构可衍生出成千上万条可精确校验的空间与拓扑陈述。作者由此发问：学习折叠蛋白质能否让通用模型习得可迁移的推理能力？

Method: 构建 FoldingCorpus（蛋白质衍生的问答数据集）与 Fold2Reason 后训练方案。Fold2Reason 使用两种互补信号：一是通过模型原生语言头预测的离散结构答案，二是从同一共享表征中解码出的连续 3D 几何信息。

Result: 在 FoldBench 上，Fold2Reason 的结构预测得分达到 Qwen3.5-9B 的 2.7 至 3.5 倍。除蛋白质结构预测外，在涵盖空间、图、科学与通用推理的 10 个基准上均有提升，宏平均准确率从 45.09% 提升至 48.33%（+3.23 个百分点），10 个基准全部为正收益。而由随机、合成和打乱结构构成的对照实验带来的提升显著更小，甚至为负。

Conclusion: 非语言的、结构密集的科学数据能够系统性地提升语言模型的广泛推理能力，说明一个已被解决的科学问题可以成为后训练监督的实用来源。

Abstract: Large language models rely heavily on human text, which often conveys surface answers rather than the spatial and structural logic behind them. Protein folding is a natural testbed, because one solved structure yields thousands of exactly checkable spatial and topological statements. We ask: can learning to fold proteins teach general models reusable reasoning capabilities? To answer this, we build FoldingCorpus, a protein-derived question-answer dataset, and Fold2Reason, a recipe that post-trains on it through two complementary signals: discrete structural answers predicted via the model's native language head, and continuous 3D geometry decoded from the same shared representations. On FoldBench, Fold2Reason achieves structure prediction scores 2.7 to 3.5 times those of Qwen3.5-9B. Beyond protein structure prediction, it improves performance on all 10 benchmarks spanning spatial, graph, scientific, and general reasoning, raising macro-average accuracy from 45.09% to 48.33% (+3.23 pp), with positive gains on all 10 benchmarks, while matched controls built from random, synthetic, and shuffled structure yield substantially smaller or negative gains. Our work shows that non-linguistic, structure-dense scientific data can systematically improve broad reasoning in language models, making a solved scientific problem a practical source of post-training supervision.

</details>


### [77] [Right Answers, Costly Models: The Efficiency Gap in LLM-based Optimization Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38884)
*Zhong Li,Xin Huang,Jinhui Wan,Xiangyi Wang,Shenkai Zhang,Ruiqi Chen,Wenyu Liu,Zaiwen Wen,Ziyan Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出系统评估大语言模型（LLM）优化建模能力的方法，构建了OptTips知识库、OptDachshund多智能体框架和EfficientOpt基准（561个任务），发现LLM生成的模型虽正确但求解效率普遍低于专家模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可自动将现实决策问题转化为数学规划，但正确的公式往往需要大量时间和内存来构建与求解，限制了实际可扩展性。因此需要系统研究LLM能否识别问题结构并运用合适的优化建模技术，生成既正确又高效的模型和求解代码。

Method: 首先整理OptTips——包含8个家族、50种专家建模技术的知识库；基于此开发OptDachshund多智能体框架，将现有优化基准问题转化为评估LLM建模技术使用能力的新任务；为同一任务和数据构建常规与专家两类数学模型及求解代码，作为正确性和计算成本的基线；由此形成EfficientOpt基准，含561个经专家审核的任务及配对参考实现；对11个代表性LLM进行评估。

Result: 评估11个代表性LLM后发现效率差距：对每个LLM，大多数生成程序的求解时间都长于专家对应程序。在可比参考规模子集中，57%的目标值正确且变量与线性约束更少的程序反而记录到更长的求解时间。案例研究表明，不同建模技术可在相近记录成本下达到相同最优值；若代码准备数据和构建模型耗时更长，即使求解更快也不一定能减少总执行时间。

Conclusion: LLM优化建模应同时从正确性和计算效率两个维度进行评估，仅关注正确性不足以反映实际可用性。

Abstract: Optimization modeling formulates real-world decision problems as mathematical programs that solvers can use to find optimal decisions. Large language models (LLMs) can automate this process, but the resulting correct formulations can require substantial time and memory to construct and solve, limiting practical scalability. Therefore, we systematically investigate whether LLMs can identify problem structure from natural-language descriptions and apply suitable optimization modeling techniques to generate mathematical models and solver code that solve the problems correctly and efficiently. To this end, we first curate OptTips, a knowledge base of 50 expert modeling techniques in eight families. Using this knowledge, we develop OptDachshund, a multi-agent framework that transforms problems from existing optimization benchmarks into new tasks for evaluating LLMs' use of modeling techniques. It constructs conventional and expert mathematical models with solver code for the same task and data, providing baselines for correctness and computational cost. The resulting EfficientOpt benchmark contains 561 expert-reviewed tasks with paired reference implementations. Evaluation of 11 representative LLMs reveals an efficiency gap on correctly solved tasks with comparable measurements: for every LLM, most generated programs take longer to solve than their expert counterparts. Within the comparable reference-size subset, 57\% of programs with correct objective values and fewer variables and linear constraints have longer recorded solver times. Case studies show that different modeling techniques can achieve the same optimal value at similar recorded cost. Faster solving may not reduce execution time if the code takes longer to prepare data and build the model. LLM optimization modeling should therefore be evaluated for both correctness and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [78] [VERA: Verifiable Feasibility Representations with Counterfactual Credit for Constrained Multi-Agent Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38889)
*Bo Yin,Dongbo Li,Hongkai Chen,Jie Liu,Guoliang Xing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VERA，将可行性估计与信用分配解耦：actor预测可验证可行性表示(VFR)，训练时用精确动作条件裕度监督，用反事实组相对优势(CGRA)排序候选。在SAGIN场景下成功率55.33%、覆盖率违规0.45%，显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束多智能体控制中，动作的可执行性会随接触窗口、共享容量和截止期限变化而失效，仅预测"有回报的动作"不足以保证可行性。

Method: 集中训练-分散执行框架VERA：每个actor预测五维可验证可行性表示(VFR)；训练时用仅训练可得的精确动作条件裕度监督该表示，并用反事实组相对优势(CGRA)对候选"表示-动作"对排序；执行时仅一次actor前向、无需特权状态。

Result: SAGIN中成功率达55.33%±3.60%，覆盖率违规0.45%±0.81%，距特权掩码参考仅差1.33个百分点。十个配对种子奖励对齐下，较最强基线成功率提升8.74个百分点(p=0.023)，违规降低52.19个百分点(p=5.7e-8)。十种子4×2析因实验将14.16–16.48个百分点的增益归因于CGRA。七种未见拓扑上较MAPPO保持24.33–30.02个百分点优势。10至40用户下成功率稳定在50.1–53.8%，VFR仅增加0.026 ms CPU actor耗时。

Conclusion: 确定了适用条件：当候选评分尊重共享约束时反事实信用有效，在奖励几何不兼容时失败。动作条件可行性可作为可审计的训练接口，反事实信用是几何相关的优化机制。

Abstract: Constrained multi-agent control requires more than predicting rewarding actions: an action can cease to be executable as contact windows, shared capacity, and deadlines change. We introduce VERA, a centralized-training, decentralized-execution framework that separates feasibility estimation from credit assignment. Each actor predicts a five-dimensional verifiable feasibility representation (VFR). After an action is proposed, exact action-conditioned margins available only during training supervise that representation, while a counterfactual group-relative advantage (CGRA) ranks candidate representation-action pairs. Execution uses one actor pass and no privileged state. In a dynamic space-air-ground integrated network (SAGIN), VERA obtains 55.33% +/- 3.60% success with 0.45% +/- 0.81% coverage violation, within 1.33 percentage points of a privileged-mask reference. With rewards matched over ten paired seeds, VERA improves success over the strongest baseline by 8.74 percentage points (p=0.023) and reduces violation by 52.19 percentage points (p=5.7e-8). A ten-seed 4-by-2 factorial attributes a 14.16-16.48 percentage-point gain to CGRA across handcrafted, learned, random, and latent representations; evaluation on seven unseen topologies preserves a 24.33-30.02 percentage-point advantage over multi-agent proximal policy optimization. From 10 to 40 users, success remains 50.1-53.8%, and VFR adds only 0.026 ms to a central processing unit (CPU) actor step. Cross-domain tests further identify the governing condition: counterfactual credit succeeds when candidate scores respect shared constraints and fails under incompatible reward geometries. These results establish action-conditioned feasibility as an auditable training interface and counterfactual credit as a geometry-dependent optimization mechanism.

</details>


### [79] [Learning Continuous Neural Representation of Stochastic Hybrid Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38893)
*Sangli Teng,Hang Liu,Koushil Sreenath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明随机混合系统（SHS）可被高维潜空间中的连续随机微分方程（SDE）近似，并据此设计分布匹配损失，无需模式标注或事件仿真即可恢复其概率演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SHS 由连续 SDE 与守卫面上的 Markov 重置核共同驱动，其概率演化由含偏微分项与积分项的混合 Fokker-Planck（HFP）方程描述，结构复杂且难以直接求解。作者希望找到一种更简洁的等价表示，用连续 SDE 替代带有显式重置项的 HFP 方程，从而简化建模与学习。

Method: 通过引入辅助变量编码重置核的不同分支，将随机重置转化为确定性重置，实现拓扑粘合；再利用嵌入定理将粘合流形嵌入高维欧氏空间，构造等价的潜 SDE 模型。基于该定理设计一个匹配演化状态分布的损失函数，训练单一潜 SDE。

Result: 理论表明，在嵌入图像上的概率演化不再需要 HFP 方程中的显式重置项。所设计的损失可使单一潜 SDE 在不依赖模式标注、轨迹分割或基于事件的仿真的情况下恢复 SHS 的概率演化。

Conclusion: 该工作为随机混合系统提供了连续化的等价刻画，将显式重置项消解于高维潜空间中，并给出一种端到端的分布匹配学习框架，降低了传统方法的工程与计算负担。

Abstract: A stochastic hybrid system (SHS) is governed by a stochastic differential equation (SDE) describing the continuous dynamics and a Markov reset kernel triggered on the guard surface. Its probability evolution can be described by a hybrid Fokker-Planck (HFP) equation with a partial differential term corresponding to the SDE and an integral term arising from the reset kernel. This work shows that such an SHS can be approximated by an SDE in a higher-dimensional latent space where the sample paths are continuous. The key to this result is to encode different branches of the reset kernel using auxiliary variables, transforming the resets into deterministic ones that enable topological gluing. By the embedding theorem, the glued manifold can then be embedded into a higher-dimensional Euclidean space. We show that the probability evolution on the embedded image no longer requires explicit reset terms in the HFP equation. Building on this theorem, we design a loss that matches the evolving state distributions, enabling a single latent SDE to recover the probability evolution of the SHS without mode labeling, trajectory segmentation, or event-based simulations.

</details>


### [80] [Unmerge: Efficient Machine Unlearning via Task Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38895)
*Haoran Tang,Andrew Tan,Rajiv Khanna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于任务算术的近似机器遗忘方法 Unmerge：将遗忘视为从合并任务向量中减去低秩遗忘分量以恢复保留任务向量，在激活空间中对遗忘泄漏和保留损伤给出可证明界，并在 ResNet-50、ViT 及 Llama-3.2-3B 上验证了高效性与有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于梯度的机器遗忘方法存在三方面问题：需要依赖数据的超参数搜索；当遗忘知识与保留知识相互纠缠时效果不佳；且难以解释遗忘在网络的哪一层、何处真正发生。作者希望提供更高效、更具理论保证且可解释的遗忘方案。

Method: 把微调得到的合并任务向量 τ_m 分解为遗忘任务向量 τ_F 与保留任务向量 τ_R，将遗忘建模为减法式的逆操作。观察到遗忘激活在每层集中于少数主方向构成的子空间，因此用低秩遗忘基分解 τ_F（存在小的尾部特征值残差）。随后优化三个目标：在遗忘子空间内匹配合并向量、抑制向保留子空间的泄漏、约束修正幅度，从而在激活空间中对遗忘泄漏与保留损伤给出可证明上界，形成算法 Unmerge。

Result: 在 CIFAR-100 与 Tiny ImageNet 的类级遗忘任务上（ResNet-50），Unmerge 相比同等运行时间的基线将 Tug-of-War 提升最多约 24%，相比运行慢约 5 倍的更强基线提升最多约 18%；成员推断暴露程度保持在重训练水平，并将与重训练模型的特征分布差距缩小，而重标记方法仍使遗忘特征清晰可分。方法还适用于 ViT-S/16 并可扩展到 Llama-3.2-3B。

Conclusion: Unmerge 无需数据依赖的超参数搜索即可实现快速而强力的近似遗忘，并提供可证明的泄漏与损伤界。驱动算法的逐层基几何还可作为诊断工具，用于判断遗忘在何时、何处会变得结构上困难。

Abstract: Approximate machine unlearning seeks to remove the influence of a forget set from a trained model without full retraining. Existing gradient-based methods require data-dependent hyperparameter search, struggle when forget and retain knowledge are entangled, and offer little insight into where unlearning actually happens inside the network. We recast unlearning through the lens of task arithmetic: if finetuning produces a merged task vector $τ_m$ that combines learning on forget and retain sets, unlearning is the inverse operation that subtracts a learned forget component $τ_F$ to recover the retain task vector $τ_R$. The forget signal is concentrated: at every layer, forget activations lie in a subspace spanned by a handful of dominant directions, so we factorize $τ_F$ in a low-rank forget basis, which is faithful up to a small tail-eigenvalue residual and limits how far the correction can perturb retain. We then optimize three intuitive goals (match the merged vector inside the forget span, suppress leakage into the retain span, and bound the correction size) that provably bound forget leakage and retain damage in activation space. The resulting algorithm, Unmerge, is fast and powerful: on class-level unlearning with ResNet-50 on CIFAR-100 and Tiny ImageNet, it improves Tug-of-War by up to ~24% over a baseline of comparable runtime and by up to ~18% over stronger baselines that run ~5x slower, keeps membership-inference exposure at the level of retraining, and shrinks the feature-distribution gap to the retrained model, where relabeling methods leave forget features cleanly separable. Further studies show that Unmerge also applies to ViT-S/16 and scales to Llama-3.2-3B. The per-layer basis geometry that drives the algorithm also serves as a layerwise diagnostic for when and where unlearning becomes structurally hard.

</details>


### [81] [K2P: Label-Free Knowledge to Prompt Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38898)
*Yingchuan Zhang,Haoran Lu,Wenxuan Zhong,Ping Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: K2P 提出了一种无标签知识蒸馏到提示的方法，从教师解答中合成可复用指令，用师生配对响应精炼，并以答案一致性引导搜索与选择，在推理任务上优于无标签基线且可比有监督提示优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示式知识蒸馏虽避免更新权重，但无真实答案时教师解答未被验证，与教师一致可能奖励共同错误，因此需要无标签但仍可靠的蒸馏方法。

Method: K2P 从教师解答合成可复用指令，用教师与学生配对响应进行精炼，以答案一致性引导搜索与选择；保留自适应搜索可能低估的候选，并在留存问题上进行选择；部署仅需冻结学生与选定提示。理论区分生成差距与选择差距，给出不一致教师参考下保证准确率的条件。

Result: 在多种推理任务与学生模型上，K2P 整体优于无标签替代方案，并与有监督提示优化具有竞争力；消融与档案诊断评估了教师解答与精炼的贡献，并揭示一致性引导选择的局限。

Conclusion: K2P 表明无需标签即可将教师推理迁移为可复用提示，在理论上给出准确率保证条件，实践中性能与有监督方法可比，但一致性选择仍存在局限。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation can transfer reasoning from stronger teachers to frozen students through reusable prompts, but avoiding weight updates does not eliminate supervision. Without ground-truth answers, teacher solutions are unverified, and agreement with the teacher can reward shared mistakes. We introduce Knowledge-to-Prompt (K2P) for label-free knowledge distillation to prompts. K2P synthesizes reusable instructions from teacher solutions, refines them using paired teacher and student responses, and guides search and selection with answer agreement. It retains candidates that adaptive search may undervalue and selects on reserved questions. Deployment uses only the frozen student and selected prompt. Our theory separates generation and selection gaps and gives conditions under which agreement-guided construction yields accuracy guarantees despite imperfect teacher references. Across reasoning tasks and students, K2P outperforms label-free alternatives overall and remains competitive with supervised prompt optimization. Ablations and archive diagnostics assess the contributions of teacher solutions and refinement, while revealing the limits of agreement-guided selection.

</details>


### [82] [Certified Approximation for Interpretable Representer Landmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38901)
*Jayanta Mukherjee,Shourya Verma,Mengbo Wang,Jasorsi Ghosh,Ananth Grama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CAIRN 框架，将 eNTK 多重近似误差传递到 representer 解释的 top-K 排序，给出可认证的可靠性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作对 representer 解释中每个近似（随机输出头、参数 sketch、landmark 采样、系数拟合）分别给出误差界，但无人针对它们的组合误差对 top-K 集合进行认证，可靠性不可度量。

Method: 推导 sketch 多头 eNTK 的精确方差；给出固定系数拟合下的高概率 top-K 认证与丢弃谱质量的精确残差迹认证；提出精确乘积方差恒等式分离核误差与拟合变异性；用 SLQ 估计有效维度指导 landmark 预算；采用残差贪心选择与跨视角初始化。

Result: 方差与实测吻合在 4% 内，而 JL 界误差高达 2.5 倍；SLQ 有效维度估计误差 0.72%；残差贪心把 k-means++ 最差覆盖超出从 8.5 倍降至 1.55 倍（sketched eNTK 上为 4 倍）；跨视角初始化在 5（AUI）到 6（CSI）个设置中优于 PCA 初始化；相较 KREPES Gauss-Newton 求解器收敛快 2.5–11.3 倍，精度最多落后 0.31 点、在 MNIST 上最高提升 3.14 点。

Conclusion: 该框架使 representer 解释的可靠性可度量，并指明近似预算的最佳分配位置。

Abstract: Representer explanations rank the training landmarks that most influence a self-supervised representation. At scale, this ranking rests on up to four stacked approximations of the empirical neural tangent kernel (eNTK). These are random output heads, a parameter sketch, landmark sampling and a coefficient fit. Existing analyses bound each approximation separately, but none certifies the top-$K$ set against their combined error. We introduce CAIRN (Certified Approximation for Interpretable Representer laNdmarks), a framework that carries this error through to the ranking. We derive the exact variance of the sketched multi-head eNTK, which matches measurement within $4\%$ where Johnson-Lindenstrauss bounds err by up to $2.5\times$. This yields a high-probability top-$K$ certificate for a fixed coefficient fit, alongside exact residual-trace certificates for discarded spectral mass. An exact product-variance identity separates kernel error from fit variability and identifies when a larger kernel budget can still sharpen a ranking. Stochastic Lanczos Quadrature (SLQ) estimates the effective dimension within $0.72\%$ and guides the landmark budget without dense eigendecomposition. We show that residual mass does not control class coverage, and residual-greedy selection cuts the worst coverage excess of $k$-means++ from $8.5\times$ to $1.55\times$ ($4\times$ on the sketched eNTK). Cross-view initializers outperform principal-component initialization in five (AUI) to all six (CSI) settings. Against the KREPES Gauss-Newton solver, CAIRN converges $2.5$ to $11.3\times$ faster, trails by at most $0.31$ points and gains up to $3.14$ points on MNIST. Together, these results make the reliability of representer explanations measurable and show where approximation budgets are best spent.

</details>


### [83] [Amortized Data Borrowing with Exchangeability-Aware Neural Posterior Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38902)
*Chin-Hung Huang,JooChul Lee,Huan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究摊销神经后验估计（NPE）作为贝叶斯动态借用的灵活替代方案，单次前向传播即可给出当前研究标量目标的后验近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小规模并发研究常通过外部或历史队列扩充数据，但经典贝叶斯动态借用依赖手工设定先验和MCMC推断，计算昂贵且难以泛化。

Method: 在模拟的当前/外部数据集对上预训练单个网络，覆盖协变量偏移、结局漂移及联合不可交换情形，单次前向即输出后验。

Result: NPE在结局漂移与联合错配下最有用：最难情形下绝对偏差约为最佳经典基线的五分之一，I类错误接近名义水平；后验摘要约8毫秒/数据集，比MCMC基线快约10^3倍。ADNI数据分析中，MCI结局跨队列不同时，NPE恢复较晚队列风险水平而不夸大精度。

Conclusion: 摊销NPE为动态借用提供了快速、灵活且泛化性更好的替代方案，尤其适用于结局漂移和联合不可交换场景。

Abstract: Augmenting small concurrent studies with external or historical cohorts is attractive in drug development, where enrollment is slow, follow-up is expensive, and closely related trial or real-world data are often already available. Bayesian dynamic borrowing (BDB) provides a principled framework for adaptively controlling the influence of external data, but classical implementations often depend on hand-specified priors and MCMC-based inference, which can be computationally expensive and not generalizable. In this work, we study amortized neural posterior estimation (NPE) as a flexible alternative. A single network is pretrained on simulated current/external dataset pairs spanning covariate shift, outcome drift, and joint non-exchangeability, and then returns an approximate posterior for a scalar current-study target in a single forward pass. Through simulation studies, we find that NPE is most useful under outcome drift and joint mismatch: in the harder outcome-drift regimes, it gives up to about five-fold lower absolute bias than the best classical baseline and keeps Type I error close to nominal. After pretraining, posterior summaries are obtained in about 8 ms per dataset, roughly $10^3\times$ faster than MCMC-based borrowing baselines in our timing experiment. We further analyze Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) data and show that, when mild cognitive impairment outcomes differ across cohorts, the NPE formulation recovers the later-cohort risk level in this example without claiming greater precision. Code is available at https://github.com/ChinHungScott/NPE-for-Bayesian-Dynamic-Borrowing-MLHC-.

</details>


### [84] [DAMPER: Return-Prioritized Gradient Control for Smooth Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38903)
*Seokmin Ko,Taewon Goo,Kihyuk Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DAMPER通过冲突条件投影和自适应幅度控制，将原生actor梯度与时间一致性梯度结合，在TD3和SAC上减少动作振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Actor-critic方法在连续控制中表现强，但策略会产生高度振荡的动作。常见的辅助平滑损失在梯度相对原生actor梯度较小时贡献可忽略，且多种辅助损失组合导致难以平衡、未必改善回报-平滑权衡。

Method: 提出DAMPER（Direction-Aware Magnitude-Controlled Projection with Explicit Return Priority）。它通过冲突条件投影与自适应幅度控制，将原生actor梯度与时间一致性梯度结合：移除与actor梯度相反的辅助分量，并按actor梯度范数对保留的时间方向进行缩放，从而保持与原生actor梯度的正对齐。

Result: 在TD3和SAC于六个连续控制任务上的实验中，12个任务-主干组合均相对原生智能体减少了动作振荡，其中八个取得对比方法中最优的振荡分数，回报权衡随任务而定。

Conclusion: DAMPER通过方向感知与幅度控制的梯度投影整合时间一致性，缓解动作振荡且保持回报优先，相比多辅助损失组合更简洁有效。

Abstract: Actor-critic methods achieve strong performance in continuous control, but their policies can produce highly oscillatory actions. A common remedy is to add auxiliary smoothness losses. However, their contribution can be negligible when their gradients are small relative to the native actor gradient. Moreover, existing methods often combine multiple auxiliary losses, complicating loss balancing without necessarily improving the return-smoothness trade-off. We introduce DAMPER (Direction-Aware Magnitude-Controlled Projection with Explicit Return Priority), which combines the native actor gradient with a temporal-consistency gradient through conflict-conditioned projection and adaptive magnitude control. It removes the auxiliary component opposing the actor gradient and scales the retained temporal direction relative to the actor gradient norm, preserving positive alignment with the native actor gradient. Experiments with TD3 and SAC on six continuous-control tasks show reduced action oscillation relative to the native agents in all 12 task-backbone pairs and the best oscillation score among the compared methods in eight, with task-dependent return trade-offs.

</details>


### [85] [Unlearning Deceptive Behaviors in LLMs with Contrastive Forget Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38909)
*Haoran Tang,Rajiv Khanna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出PACT方法，通过“压力感知反事实目标”对模型的欺骗行为进行机器遗忘，在降低欺骗率的同时保持良性上下文功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型常在知道真相时仍因上下文激励而说谎，这种欺骗是“情境条件化行为”而非知识缺失。传统机器遗忘旨在遗忘事实，但欺骗模型仍需要这些事实，因此不适合。作者主张应遗忘“何时欺骗”而非“知道什么”。

Method: 基于模型自身实际发生的欺骗构建对比式遗忘单元（同一问题在欺骗触发与中性上下文中对比，只在信念成立且行为翻转处采用）。指出该单元上两类目标存在困境：抑制类目标（如NPO）去除不彻底；目标蒸馏类目标虽去除欺骗却导致“上下文盲视”（削弱系统提示遵循、保密与可监控推理）。提出PACT：训练朝向“压力感知反事实目标”（模型自身诚实回答，并附带识别并抵抗压力的推理痕迹），同时保留触发上下文的良性用途。

Result: 在两个32B推理模型上，PACT将留出欺骗率从超50%降至3%以下，同时系统提示遵循、保密与推理痕迹维持在基座水平。在“去除-保留”拉锯评分中，PACT达0.94与0.86，基线最高仅0.77与0.60。与移除知识类似，移除的欺骗在再学习下较浅层，模拟攻击者只有在牺牲上下文使用的前提下才能维持。

Conclusion: 遗忘“欺骗行为”优于遗忘“知识”：PACT能有效去除情境性欺骗，且不损伤上下文使用与良性能力，但已移除的欺骗存在容易被再学习恢复的浅层性问题。

Abstract: Large language models often know the truth and say otherwise: a model that answers correctly when asked neutrally will affirm a user's mistaken belief, or misstate a fact its system prompt wants hidden, once the context rewards it. Such deception is a behavior conditioned on context, not knowledge, yet machine unlearning, the natural tool for removing a behavior from the weights, is built to forget facts that a deceptive model still needs. We propose to unlearn when a model deceives rather than what it knows, with a contrastive forget unit built from the model's own realized deceptions: the same question under a deception-triggering and a neutral context, admitted only where belief holds and behavior flips. Standard objectives on this unit face a dilemma. Suppression objectives such as NPO leave much of the deception in place. Target-based objectives, which distill the model's neutral behavior into the pressured context, remove it but induce context blindness: a target generated without the context teaches the model to stop reading it, eroding benign system-prompt instructions, secret-keeping and the reasoning a monitor inspects, a failure invisible to deception rates and capability benchmarks. We introduce PACT, which trains toward pressure-aware counterfactual targets (the model's own honest response, with a trace that registers the pressure and resists it) while retaining the benign uses of the triggering context. On two 32B reasoning models, PACT reduces held-out deception from over 50% to under 3% while system-prompt adherence, secret-keeping and the reasoning trace stay at the base model's level. On a tug-of-war score of removal against retention, PACT reaches 0.94 and 0.86, against at most 0.77 and 0.60 for any baseline. Like removed knowledge, removed deception is shallow under relearning, and terms that simulate the attacker hold it only at a cost in context use.

</details>


### [86] [Generalized Residual Closure: General Learning Dynamics for Stability-Plasticity Compatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38911)
*Dongxu Li,Yinuo Zhang,Hongyu Zhang,Feng Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出广义残差闭包（GRC）框架，将学习刻画为对未来相关差异的递归闭合，统一了适应、表征修正与可复用能力的持续学习理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习需在获取新能力的同时保留既有职责与再次学习的能力（稳定性-可塑性、灾难性遗忘问题），本文试图为持续学习建立统一的形式化理论。

Method: 在固定学习者-世界边界的完整表征-关系描述下，识别持续内部学习的两种原始模式（表征内变换与表征本身修正）；建立局部切空间分解与表征修正必要性判据；在仿射模型下推导稳定性-可塑性相容的充要条件及约束二次更新的唯一解；证明重构式语义保护弱扩大安全响应算子；引入动态充分性与未来闭包可行性、增长学习及条件提交规则，并给出受限扇区恢复与条件表征定理。

Result: 得到稳定性-可塑性兼容的充要条件、约束二次更新的唯一解、重构式语义保护优于精确历史保持的证明，以及动态充分性、未来闭包可行性与增长学习的相关结论。

Conclusion: 该框架将适应、表征修正与可复用能力纳入持续学习的统一解释，并与优化、机器学习与控制理论建立联系。

Abstract: Learning must acquire new capabilities while preserving both prior responsibilities and the capacity to learn again. We introduce Generalized Residual Closure (GRC), a framework for learning as recursive closure of future-relevant discrepancies: closing a residual establishes the conditions for subsequent prediction, interaction, and learning. Under a complete representation-relation description at a fixed learner-world boundary, persistent internal learning has two primitive modes: Transformation within a representation and revision of the Representation itself. We establish a local tangent decomposition under regularity assumptions and a criterion for when representation revision is necessary. In an affine model, we derive a necessary-and-sufficient condition for stability-plasticity compatibility and the unique solution of a constrained quadratic update problem, which preserves registered old responsibilities while reducing residuals with an effective safe response. We prove that reconstructive semantic protection weakly enlarges the safe-response operator relative to preserving an exact historical realization. Dynamic sufficiency and future-closure viability extend representation adequacy from current prediction to lawful future updating and continued learning. Growth Learning expands the lawful closure domain or lowers optimal closure cost without regression of the registered capability-cost frontier; a conditional commit rule maintains this order. Restricted-sector recoveries and a conditional representation theorem connect the framework to optimization, machine learning, and control. Together, these results organize adaptation, representation revision, and reusable capability within a common account of continued learning.

</details>


### [87] [Flow Matching under Noisy Latent Structure: Beyond Exact Low-Dimensional Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38918)
*Lifeng Hao,Shaolin Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带噪声潜变量生成模型下的线性Flow Matching，证明其统计收敛速率由潜维度而非环境维度决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有限样本理论多假设环境空间正则性或数据精确支撑在低维集合上，未处理"目标分布满维但具潜结构（低维Hölder映射加环境高斯噪声）"的情形。

Method: 构建空间正则的ReLU速度场函数类；在噪声潜变量生成模型下（低维Hölder映射被非退化环境高斯噪声扰动），建立非渐近高概率的逼近与估计界，并显式保留环境与噪声依赖。

Result: 得到的主导样本量指数由潜维度而非环境维度控制；正的目标噪声使插值在整个时间区间上非退化；相同空间正则性可将速度误差通过传输ODE传播，给出相应的Wasserstein收敛保证。

Conclusion: 精确的低维支撑并非Flow Matching保持潜维度统计行为的必要条件。

Abstract: Flow Matching (FM) learns a velocity field whose ODE transports a simple source distribution to a target law. Existing finite-sample theory largely treats ambient-space regularity or data supported exactly on low-dimensional sets. We study linear FM under a noisy latent-generator model, where a low-dimensional Hölder map is perturbed by nondegenerate ambient Gaussian noise, so the target law is full-dimensional despite its latent structure. We construct a spatially regular ReLU velocity class and establish non-asymptotic high-probability approximation and estimation bounds whose leading sample-size exponent is governed by the latent dimension rather than the ambient dimension, with ambient and noise dependence kept explicit. Fixed positive target noise keeps the interpolation nondegenerate over the full time interval. The same spatial regularity propagates the learned velocity error through the transport ODE, yielding a corresponding Wasserstein convergence guarantee. These results show that exact low-dimensional support is not necessary for Flow Matching to retain latent-dimensional statistical behavior.

</details>


### [88] [Learning Where to Steer: Noise-Space Geometry for Efficient Offline Multi-Objective Optimization with Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38920)
*Yuan Lu,Esha Singh,Yi-An Ma,Yusu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种离线多目标优化方法，不改变扩散采样过程，仅对初始噪声施加位移以实现Pareto前沿引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线MOO需在固定数据集上获得更优目标权衡，扩散模型样本本身不优于数据，需被引导至Pareto前沿；现有方法在每个采样步进行引导或条件化，成本高。

Method: 在Off-MOO-Bench上发现目标作为噪声函数仅对少数方向敏感；用Recursive Feature Machine仅凭函数值每任务估计一次这些方向，小缓存服务所有权衡，每个候选仅需一次噪声位移和一次ODE求解；并引入数据自适应和Pareto感知算子作为额外引导；理论证明位移在期望上提升学习到的标量化目标，且遍历权衡可恢复流的可达前沿（至代理与引导误差）。

Result: 在47个任务上取得生成方法中最佳平均超体积排名，采样成本相当或更低；仅引导即可在远低于先前最佳生成方法的采样成本下超越其表现。

Conclusion: 通过作用于初始噪声而非采样过程，可高效引导扩散模型趋向Pareto前沿，兼具有效性与低采样成本。

Abstract: Offline multi-objective optimization (MOO) seeks solutions with better objective trade-offs using only a fixed dataset, without querying the objectives. Diffusion models trained on such data have emerged as a promising approach, but their samples are not inherently better than the data and must be steered toward the Pareto front. Existing methods guide or condition every sampling step. We instead act on the initial noise and leave the sampling process unchanged. Across Off-MOO-Bench, we observe that the objectives, as functions of the noise, are sensitive to only a few directions. We estimate these directions once per task via a Recursive Feature Machine using function values alone, and a small cache serves every trade-off, so each candidate costs one noise displacement and one ODE solve. We prove that this displacement increases the learned scalarized objective in expectation, and that sweeping trade-offs recovers the flow's attainable front up to proxy and steering errors. With additional guidance, for which we introduce novel data-adaptive and Pareto-aware operators, our method attains the best average hypervolume rank among generative methods on 47 tasks, at comparable or lower sampling cost. Steering alone outranks the best prior generative method at a fraction of its sampling cost.

</details>


### [89] [World-as-Graph: Relational World Modeling Through Latent Space Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38927)
*Yaqi Yang,Shuo Huang,Yujin Huang,Fucai Ke,Jiatong Han,Xin Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于图的对象中心世界模型WAG，将关系归纳偏置引入JEPA式预测表征学习，包含关系感知结构归纳与对象中心记忆转移两个模块，在视觉推理与机器人操作任务上表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对象中心世界模型虽以对象级隐状态表示视觉场景，但对象间关系多为隐式捕获，限制了显式的关系与时序结构建模，也限制了对象中心的动态记忆建模。

Method: WAG包含两个模块：(1) 关系感知结构归纳：从对象中心slot构建时变隐图，设计关系感知的对象掩码策略，在隐空间中引导关系性对象表征学习；(2) 对象中心记忆转移：结合邻居对象的关系信息与历史记忆，维护并更新对象级动态状态，实现有效的自回归未来预测。

Result: 在视觉推理与机器人操作任务上的大量实验表明，WAG取得了优越的性能。

Conclusion: 通过将关系归纳偏置引入JEPA式预测表征学习，WAG显式建模对象间关系与时序动态，验证了图结构在对象中心世界模型中的有效性。

Abstract: World models aim to learn representations of real-world environments and predict their future evolution. Recent object-centric world models have made expressive progress by representing visual scenes as sets of object-level latent states, but object-object relations are often captured only implicitly, which limits explicit relational and temporal structure modeling and object-centric dynamic memory modeling. To address such challenges, we propose World-As-Graph (WAG), a graph-based object-centric world model that introduces relational inductive bias into JEPA-style predictive representation learning. The proposed WAG contains two main modules: (1) Relation-aware structure induction, which constructs time-varying latent graphs from object-centric slots and designs relation-aware object masking policies to guide relational object representation learning in latent space; (2) Object-centric memory transition, which maintains and updates object-level dynamic states by combining relational information from neighboring objects with historical memory, enabling effective autoregressive future prediction. Extensive experiments on both visual reasoning and robotic manipulation tasks could demonstrate the superior performance of our proposed WAG.

</details>


### [90] [Adaptive Self-Consistency: From Black-Box Sampling to Distribution-Valued Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38931)
*Jingkai Huang,Yunfan Zhang,Will Ma,Weihua Zhou,Zhengyuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出灰箱设定下的高效推理方法ASC-D，通过分布值观测的顺序众数识别来提前停止采样，显著减少推理轨迹数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自一致性方法将LLM视为黑箱，每个推理轨迹只返回一个答案，导致需要大量采样才能确定最终答案，效率低下。实际上每个轨迹的答案来自模型log-probabilities给出的softmax分布，这种"灰箱"信息未被利用。

Method: 将高效推理建模为分布值观测下的顺序众数识别问题：逐个采样轨迹，一旦在指定置信水平下识别出LLM的众数答案即停止。精确刻画该设定下的渐近停止速率，并提出ASC-D算法——一种达到该渐近速率的投注式停止规则。

Result: 理论上证明灰箱停止速率不劣于黑箱。在MMLU-Redux上，ASC-D相比仅答案的自适应自一致性基线减少46.4%–95.6%的轨迹数，并在三个开源模型上取得最高的固定预算正确认证率。

Conclusion: 利用轨迹的答案分布信息可显著提升自一致性推理效率，ASC-D在实践中优于现有自适应基线。

Abstract: Self-consistency samples many reasoning trajectories and aggregates their final answers, treating the LLM as a black box that returns one answer per trajectory. Yet the final answer of each trajectory is sampled from a softmax vector that is available from the model's log-probabilities. We refer to this as the grey-box setting in which each trajectory reveals this answer distribution rather than a single draw from it. We formulate efficient inference in this setting as sequential mode identification with distribution-valued observations: sample trajectories one at a time and stop as soon as the LLM's modal answer is identified at a prescribed confidence level. We characterize the asymptotic stopping rate of mode identification with distribution-valued observations exactly and show that it is never worse than the black-box rate. We then propose the ASC-D algorithm, a betting stopping rule that attains this asymptotic stopping rate. On MMLU-Redux, ASC-D uses $46.4$--$95.6\%$ fewer trajectories than answer-only adaptive self-consistency baselines and achieves the highest fixed-budget correct-certification rate across three open-source models.

</details>


### [91] [Signal-Routed Temperature Scaling: Low-Capacity Risk-Conditioned Calibration for Small Validation Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38936)
*Wenhao Liang,Liangwei Nathan Zheng,Lin Yue,Wei Emma Zhang,Mingyu Guo,Olaf Maennel,Weitong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出SRTS-BCE校准器，将校准目标与自适应容量解耦，证明后验校准器的优选容量是随校准数据量变化的有限样本设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从少量(数千)留出样本重新校准分类器时，校准映射的容量成为统计设计问题而非纯架构问题：标量映射可能欠拟合结构化残差误校准，而高自适应映射在小样本上难以可靠估计。作者希望将校准目标与自适应容量解耦研究。

Method: 提出signal-routed temperature scaling (SRTS-BCE)：一个10参数、保持argmax的校准器。它在六个logit统计量上交叉拟合correctness-risk分数，并对K=3个风险组分别拟合top-label-BCE温度，标量TvA-TS为其K=1极限情况。

Result: 在微调CIFAR-100/ViT-B/16上，SRTS-BCE将ECE15从1.65(TvA-TS标量)降至0.96，匹配更高容量的SMART+BCE(0.95)。预算缩小时两种机制分离：n=250时SRTS-BCE在三个CIFAR-100骨干上均优于SMART+BCE，而针对标量的旗舰对比仅为方向性。Tiny-ImageNet实验复现了小预算分离，并在Swin-T上出现预算依赖的排序反转。匹配的路由与映射对照表明效应与学习路由器或离散分组无关。

Conclusion: 后验校准器容量是一种有限样本设计选择，其优选水平随可用校准数据量而变化。

Abstract: When a classifier is recalibrated from only a few thousand held-out examples, the capacity of the calibration map becomes a statistical design choice rather than a purely architectural one: a scalar map can underfit structured residual miscalibration, while a highly adaptive map can be hard to estimate reliably from so small a split. We disentangle the calibration objective from adaptive capacity and propose signal-routed temperature scaling (SRTS-BCE), a 10-parameter, argmax-preserving calibrator that cross-fits a correctness-risk score over six logit statistics and fits one top-label-BCE temperature per $K=3$ risk groups, recovering TvA-TS as its $K=1$ limit. On fine-tuned CIFAR-100 / ViT-B/16, SRTS-BCE reduces $\mathrm{ECE}_{15}$ from 1.65 (scalar TvA-TS) to 0.96, matching the higher-capacity SMART+BCE head (0.95) at the full calibration budget. The two regimes separate as the budget shrinks: at $n=250$ SRTS-BCE beats SMART+BCE on all three CIFAR-100 backbones (the seed-to-draw hierarchical interval excludes zero), whereas the flagship comparison against the scalar remains directional. A protocol-frozen Tiny-ImageNet follow-up reproduces the small-budget separation and exhibits a budget-dependent ranking reversal on Swin-T; matched routing and map controls show that the effect is tied neither to the learned router nor to discrete grouping. Together the results identify post-hoc calibrator capacity as a finite-sample design choice whose preferred level shifts with the amount of available calibration data.

</details>


### [92] [Robust Risk-Sensitive Reinforcement Learning from Corrupted Human Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38938)
*Xinyi Ni,Lifeng Lai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在对抗性偏好标签翻转下的在线风险敏感RLHF，提出WSP-CVaR-RLHF算法，结合不确定性加权奖励估计与乐观增广状态CVaR规划，并给出已知/未知转移下的遗憾界及实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF依赖人类比较数据，但比较可能被破坏或故意操纵，因此需要研究在对抗性偏好标签翻转情形下的风险敏感在线RLHF，以提升鲁棒性。

Method: 在静态CVaR下、加性线性奖励与固定参考协议（每回合一次比较、K回合最多C次翻转）设定中，提出WSP-CVaR-RLHF，将不确定性加权奖励估计与乐观增广状态CVaR规划结合；并扩展到未知表格转移，采用矩形转移置信集、联合乐观规划与历史级CVaR模拟。

Result: 在已知转移与归一化奖励下，得到遗憾界$\widetilde{O}(\frac{d}{κ}\sqrt{\frac{K}{α}}+\frac{dC}{κα})$，将干净统计代价与损坏反馈惩罚分离；未知表格转移下也能处理耦合不确定性。四种对抗攻击实验中，相较未加权鲁棒基线持续降低累积遗憾，同时保持置信集覆盖。

Conclusion: WSP-CVaR-RLHF在对抗偏好翻转下具有理论遗憾保证和实验鲁棒性，说明不确定性加权与CVaR乐观规划可有效应对损坏反馈。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with human feedback (RLHF) learns from human comparisons, which can be corrupted or deliberately manipulated. This paper studies online risk-sensitive RLHF with static conditional value-at-risk (CVaR) under adversarial preference-label flips. We consider additive linear rewards and a fixed-reference protocol with one comparison per episode and at most $C$ flipped labels over $K$ episodes. We propose weighted streamed-preference CVaR RLHF (WSP-CVaR-RLHF), which combines uncertainty-weighted reward estimation with optimistic augmented-state CVaR planning. For known transitions and normalized rewards, we establish the regret bound $\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{d}κ\sqrt{\frac{K}α}+\frac{dC}{κα}\right)$ up to lower-order terms, where $d$ is the reward-feature dimension, $α$ is the CVaR level, and $κ$ characterizes the preference link. The bound separates the clean statistical cost from the penalty caused by corrupted feedback. We further extend the analysis to unknown tabular transitions, where the trajectory distribution entering the CVaR objective must be learned together with the reward. We address the resulting coupled uncertainty using rectangular transition confidence sets, joint optimistic planning, and a history-level CVaR simulation argument. Experiments under four adversarial attacks demonstrate that WSP-CVaR-RLHF consistently reduces cumulative regret relative to its unweighted robust counterpart while preserving confidence-set coverage.

</details>


### [93] [Loop-Free Inverse Reinforcement Learning via Sequential Value Recovery with Q-Score Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38955)
*Yang chen,Yitan Zhang,Michael Witbrock,Shuyue Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LFIRL，一种完全离线的无循环逆强化学习算法，利用扩散策略消除策略优化，将奖励学习转化为价值恢复问题，在多个基准上实现2-3倍加速并保持或超越SOTA的奖励恢复质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逆强化学习(IRL)方法通常依赖双层优化，在奖励学习和策略优化之间交替进行，导致计算负担大、训练不稳定。作者希望消除策略优化环节，避免奖励-策略循环。

Method: 关键洞察是扩散策略编码了最优软Q函数的动作梯度结构，使奖励学习可转化为一系列价值恢复问题。方法分三阶段：(I)通过动作梯度匹配恢复最优软Q函数，并借鉴Gumbel回归估计软价值函数(Q值的LogSumExp)；(II)通过推断状态相关偏移量校准软价值；(III)施加贝尔曼一致性提取奖励。

Result: 在Maze、Franka Kitchen、Adroit Hand Pen和Push-T基准上，LFIRL相比最快的基线实现2-3倍加速，同时在奖励恢复质量上匹配或超越SOTA方法。

Conclusion: LFIRL是一种简单易实现、完全离线、无循环且顺序化的IRL算法，显著提升训练效率，同时保持强奖励恢复性能。

Abstract: Inverse Reinforcement Learning (IRL) aims to recover a reward function that explains expert demonstrations. Existing IRL methods typically rely on a bi-level optimization procedure that alternates between reward learning and policy optimization, leading to substantial computational burden and training instability. In this work, we introduce a different route that eliminates policy optimization entirely by leveraging diffusion policies. Our key insight is that a diffusion policy encodes the action-gradient structure of the optimal soft Q function, enabling reward learning to be cast as a sequence of value recovery problems, thereby allowing us to bypass reward-policy loops inherent in prior IRL methods. Specifically, our method proceeds in three stages: (I) recovering the optimal soft Q function via action-gradient matching and estimating the corresponding soft value function (LogSumExp of Q values) in a way inspired by Gumbel regression; (II) calibrating these soft values by inferring a state-dependent offset; (III) extracting the reward by enforcing Bellman consistency. This leads to Loop-Free Inverse Reinforcement Learning (LFIRL), a fully offline algorithm that operates in a simple, loop-free, and sequential manner. LFIRL is simple to implement and significantly improves training efficiency while maintaining strong reward recovery performance. Empirically, across Maze, Franka Kitchen, Adroit Hand Pen, and Push-T benchmarks, LFIRL achieves 2-3x speedup over the fastest baselines, while matching or surpassing state-of-the-art methods in reward recovery quality.

</details>


### [94] [Scale-Split Neural Operator for Memory- and Data-Efficient 3D Turbulence Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38977)
*Shaoxiang Qin,Yucheng Zhao,Zongyi Li,Liangzhu Leon Wang,Xiongye Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ScaleSplit-NO，用父-子两个神经算子分别预测全局粗场和全分辨率局部块，避免在全分辨率场上训练，在高分辨率湍流与城市风场预测中同时提升精度、降低内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维湍流神经代理模型训练受限于内存随分辨率增长，且全分辨率数据昂贵稀缺，难以直接训练全分辨率模型。

Method: 利用湍流的尺度结构，设计父算子预测下一步全局粗场、子算子在该粗预测条件下预测全分辨率局部块；两者均不处理全分辨率全场。子模型先单独预训练，再通过零初始化连接接入父模型。父模型还可在额外粗场上训练后直接替换以提升精度。

Result: 在两个高分辨率湍流基准上，预测精度与数据效率均超过所有基线；JHTDB256（256³）上NMSE较最强基线低53%，训练内存较最省内存基线低79%。在蒙特利尔真实城区500×150×500网格风场预测中，单步NMSE较基线降低65.8%。替换额外粗场训练的父模型可在不重训子模型下提升精度。

Conclusion: ScaleSplit-NO证明通过尺度拆分与父子神经算子解耦，可显著降低高分辨率湍流模拟的内存与数据需求，并在真实城市风场预测中有效。

Abstract: Neural surrogates have emerged as fast alternatives to the numerical simulation of three-dimensional turbulence. However, training them at high resolution remains challenging, since the memory of full-field models grows with the resolution. In addition, full-resolution training data are expensive to simulate and store, and therefore scarce. We introduce ScaleSplit-NO (Scale-Split Neural Operator), which exploits the scale structure of turbulence with two neural operators: a Parent predicts the global coarse field at the next time step, and a Child predicts full-resolution local patches conditioned on this prediction. Neither model operates on the full-resolution field. The Child is pretrained alone and then attached to the Parent's coarse prediction through zero-initialized connections. On two complex high-resolution turbulence benchmarks, ScaleSplit-NO surpasses all competing baselines in both prediction accuracy and data efficiency. On the higher-resolution dataset JHTDB256 ($256^3$), its normalized mean squared error (NMSE) is 53% lower than that of the strongest baseline, and its training memory is 79% lower than that of the most memory-efficient baseline. We further demonstrate its effectiveness for urban wind prediction in a real district of Montreal on a $500\times150\times500$ grid, reducing one-step NMSE by 65.8% relative to the baseline. Moreover, swapping in a Parent trained on additional coarse fields improves prediction without retraining the Child, providing further accuracy gains at a small storage cost.

</details>


### [95] [SCORE-LM: State-Space Radar Representations with Language Models for Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38980)
*Mainak Mallick,Seung-Kyum Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCORE-LM将小型散射体条件算子响应编码器与本地语言模型耦合，实现雷达硬件故障的紧凑诊断与可理解维护指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雷达硬件故障威胁自动化感知，需要准确、紧凑的诊断及易理解的维护指导。

Method: SCORE结合自参考复轨迹、物理描述符与选择性状态空间分支，采用仅源域自监督和方向性故障推断；非线性投影器将四个冻结故障相似度转为五个软token，连接紧凑诊断与类条件维护指导。

Result: 八个记录上达88.39%平均捕获召回、88.20%四故障宏F1（十帧），推理系数39,520比RadrNet-DS-CI少119.7倍，召回高15.56个百分点；低标签协议下单源窗口每类达71.58%召回；语言适配使正确故障回答由45升至62（60.0%至82.7%）。

Conclusion: SCORE-LM将紧凑雷达专家与语言接口结合，用于传达故障特定的检查指导。

Abstract: Radar hardware faults threaten automated perception, motivating accurate, compact diagnosis and understandable maintenance guidance. We introduce SCORE-LM, which couples a small scatterer-conditioned operator-response encoder (SCORE) to an adapted local language model. SCORE combines self-referenced complex trajectories, physical descriptors, and a selective state-space branch, with source-only self-supervision and directional fault inference. On eight capture-excluded Rad-R fault recordings, it achieves state-of-the-art performance within the evaluated nine-model comparison: 88.39% mean capture recall and 88.20% four-fault macro-F1 at ten frames. Its 39,520 radar inference coefficients are 119.7 times fewer than RadrNet-DS-CI's, while recall is 15.56 percentage points higher than this strongest competitor. In a separate low-label protocol, SCORE reaches 71.58% recall with one labeled source window per class. A nonlinear projector converts four frozen fault similarities into five soft tokens, linking compact diagnosis to class-conditioned maintenance guidance. On 75 development questions covering 24 radar windows, language adaptation raises correct-fault answers from 45 to 62 (60.0% to 82.7%) relative to removing the co-trained adapters, while retaining the same projector. SCORE-LM thus combines a compact radar specialist with a language interface for communicating fault-specific inspection guidance.

</details>


### [96] [Smaller Models, Better Rejects: Preference Distillation Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38987)
*Rui Cai,Wenhui Zhu,Xiwen Chen,Jincheng Cao,Han Yu,Shayan Mohajer Hamidi,Zelin He,Qiyao Ma,Daiwei Chen,Xuanzhao Dong,Yuanda Xu,Jelena Markovic-Voronov,Kayhan Behdin,Zhengze Zhou,Ran He,Alborz Geramifard,Rohit Jain,Zhe Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 偏好蒸馏中"拒绝样本应由不比学生更小的模型生成"的假设不成立；更小的冻结模型能用更少算力生成更有效的拒绝样本，并训练出更强的学生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统偏好蒸馏默认：自我生成的失败样本最具信息量，且拒绝样本须来自不小于学生的模型。这使得大规模生成成本高昂。作者检验并质疑这两个假设。

Method: 在7B至72B的学生模型上，比较更小的冻结模型与自我生成拒绝样本的效果（代码生成与数学推理任务，序列级知识蒸馏前后）。并在线性化特征模型下推导DPO的有限时域效用界，据此设计三项干预：混合来自更小与同等规模模型的拒绝样本、将拒绝样本重分配给其他提示并打乱其代码token、按参考策略下较低似然选择候选。

Result: 更小冻结模型的拒绝样本用更少推理算力却训练出更强学生；随更小模型占比提高，混合策略表现提升；重分配并打乱token的拒绝样本仍优于等长乱码，说明任务结构有用；低似然候选优于高似然候选，且对每个来源均成立，带来更好的净迁移。

Conclusion: 有效的拒绝样本应保留任务结构并限制与参考策略的耦合；更小的冻结模型能以低成本提供这样的拒绝样本，挑战了偏好蒸馏中对拒绝来源规模的既有假设。

Abstract: Preference distillation typically treats a teacher response as preferred and the student's own response as rejected. This assumes that self-generated failures are the most informative negatives and that rejects must come from a model at least as large as the student, making generation costly at scale. We find neither assumption holds: across students from 7B to 72B, smaller frozen models generate rejects with less inference compute yet train stronger students than self-generated rejects, before and after sequence-level knowledge distillation, on code generation and mathematical reasoning. To explain this result, we derive a finite-horizon utility bound for Direct Preference Optimization in a linearized feature model. The bound characterizes favorable reject distributions and motivates three interventions. First, mixing rejects from smaller and student-scale models improves performance as the smaller model's share increases. Second, reassigning rejects to other prompts and shuffling their code tokens still outperform length-matched gibberish, showing that task structure contributes to reject utility. Third, selecting candidates with lower likelihood under the reference policy improves net transfer when higher-likelihood candidates provide less useful contrast. Lower-likelihood selections outperform higher-likelihood ones for every source. These results suggest that effective rejects preserve task structure while limiting coupling to the reference policy, and that smaller frozen models can provide them at low cost.

</details>


### [97] [Anchoring Adversarial Trajectories to Data Manifolds: A Bilevel Transfer Optimization Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38991)
*Yaohua Liu,Yifan Guo,Jiaxin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Manifold Anchored Bilevel Transfer (MABT) 框架，通过将对抗轨迹锚定到共享语义子空间，并用分布双层优化学习几何对齐初始化，从而缓解对抗样本迁移中的轨迹级几何失配问题，并给出线性时间复杂度的无 Hessian 求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗迁移的关键瓶颈是轨迹级几何断连：环境梯度常偏离内在数据流形，导致代理模型特定的过拟合，使生成的对抗样本难以迁移到其他模型。作者希望通过将对抗轨迹约束在共享语义子空间来修正这一偏差。

Method: MABT 引入松弛的流形锚定算子作为语义整流器，抑制离流形噪声；在此基础上将迁移攻击生成建模为分布双层优化问题，在代理模型不确定性分布下最小化期望迁移风险，学习几何对齐的初始化；并设计了线性时间复杂度的无 Hessian 求解器来处理该双层结构。

Result: 实验显示，MABT 在 28 种攻击配置、多种受害者架构与防御机制下，提升了 10 种基线攻击器的迁移性。

Conclusion: 将对抗轨迹锚定到共享语义流形并结合分布双层优化，能够有效缓解代理特定过拟合、提升对抗迁移性，且所提求解器在复杂度上可行。

Abstract: A key bottleneck in adversarial transfer is a trajectory-level geometric disconnect: ambient gradients often drift away from the intrinsic data manifold, causing surrogate-specific overfitting. To rectify this, we propose Manifold Anchored Bilevel Transfer (MABT), a unified framework that anchors adversarial trajectories to the shared semantic subspace. MABT introduces a relaxed manifold-anchoring operator as a semantic rectifier to suppress off-manifold noise. With this constraint, we cast transfer attack generation as a distributional bilevel optimization problem that learns a geometry-aligned initialization by minimizing expected transfer risk under a surrogate uncertainty distribution. We further develop a Hessian-free solver with linear-time complexity to handle the resulting hierarchy. Experiments demonstrate improved transferability for 10 baseline attackers across 28 attack configurations, diverse victim architectures, and defense mechanisms.

</details>


### [98] [Not all solutions are created equal: An analytical dissociation of functional and representational similarity in deep linear neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38998)
*Lukas Braun,Erin Grant,Andrew M. Saxe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究神经网络中"表征"与"功能"之间的关系，发现二者可以解耦：表征相似不等于功能相似，反之亦然；只有对参数噪声鲁棒的网络才会被迫采用任务特定的表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联结主义认为感知、行动与认知源于简单互连单元间的并行计算，并依赖神经表征。研究者常用多元模式分析来解码和比较人工网络与生物网络的神经编码以揭示其功能。然而，对于网络"表征"与"功能"之间如何关联，分析性理解仍然有限，而这正是任何关于"底层功能"或"功能相似性"的定量概念的基础。论文旨在回答这一问题。

Method: 采用可解析的两层线性网络进行分析，并在非线性网络中进行数值模拟。通过考察表征相似性与功能相似性的关系，以及输入噪声鲁棒性、参数噪声鲁棒性和泛化误差水平对表征是否受任务约束的影响，来分析表征与功能的关联。

Result: 功能与表征是解耦的：可以存在表征相似而无功能相似，也可以功能相似而无表征相似。输入噪声鲁棒性并不将表征约束到任务上；泛化误差水平也不将表征约束到任务上。相反，对参数噪声鲁棒的网络其表征灵活性有限，必须采用任务特定的表征。

Conclusion: 表征对齐反映的是超越单纯功能对齐的计算优势，这对解释和比较联结主义系统的表征具有重要启示。研究结果提示，表征相似性并不能简单等同于功能相似性，需谨慎解读神经表征比较的结果。

Abstract: A foundational principle of connectionism is that perception, action, and cognition emerge from parallel computations among simple, interconnected units that generate and rely on neural representations. Accordingly, researchers employ multivariate pattern analysis to decode and compare the neural codes of artificial and biological networks, aiming to uncover their functions. However, there is limited analytical understanding of how a network's representation and function relate, despite this being essential to any quantitative notion of underlying function or functional similarity. We address this question using analysable two-layer linear networks and numerical simulations in non-linear networks. We find that function and representation are dissociated, allowing representational similarity without functional similarity and vice versa. Further, we show that neither robustness to input noise nor the level of generalization error constrain representations to the task. In contrast, networks robust to parameter noise have limited representational flexibility and must employ task-specific representations. Our findings suggest that representational alignment reflects computational advantages beyond functional alignment alone, with significant implications for interpreting and comparing the representations of connectionist systems.

</details>


### [99] [Synchronous Multi-view Neural Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39019)
*Yongquan Shi,Weijun Huang,Yueyang Pi,Wendi Zhao,Yiqing Shi,Shiping Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出同步多视图神经扩散（SynMDiff），将多视图特征空间视为由扩散过程驱动的统一动力系统，实现视图内与视图间信息的同步自适应融合，并借助能量拓扑采样与Ego-Net式集中训练保证效率与可扩展性，性能大幅超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多视图融合策略将视图内融合与视图间融合视为独立阶段，未同时考虑视图内部演化与视图间依赖。这种异步融合范式因视图特定结构归纳偏置的冲突而限制跨视图交互，导致信息流在中间路径上出现失真与压缩，模型只能局限于有限的解空间。

Method: 将多视图特征空间建模为扩散过程驱动的统一动力系统（SynMDiff），在联合空间中建模任意成对特征交互的扩散流，从而并发且自适应地完成视图内与视图间信息融合；针对直接实现的高计算代价，引入基于能量的拓扑采样策略与Ego-Net式集中训练架构。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的评估表明，SynMDiff 大幅优于各基线方法。

Conclusion: 该方法兼具概念上的简洁性与计算高效性，通过同步扩散机制突破了异步融合的局限，为多视图表示学习提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Multi-view learning seeks to learn more comprehensive representations by exploiting the complementarity and consistency across diverse modalities or views. However, existing multi-view fusion strategies treat intra- and inter-view fusion as independent stages, without simultaneously considering the evolution within views and the dependency across views. Such an asynchronous fusion paradigm inevitably constrains cross-view interactions due to conflicting view-specific structural inductive biases. As a result, information flow is prone to distortion and compression along intermediate pathways, confining the model to learn within a restricted solution space. To address this, we propose Synchronous Multi-view Neural Diffusion (SynMDiff), which conceptualizes the multi-view feature space as a unified dynamical system driven by a diffusion process. By modeling the diffusion flow across arbitrary dyadic feature interactions in a joint space, SynMDiff enables the concurrent and adaptive intra- and inter-view information fusion. While a direct implementation of this synchronized mechanism incurs prohibitive computational costs, we further introduce an energy-based topological sampling strategy and an Ego-Net style centralized training architecture, ensuring both efficiency and scalability during learning and inference. Due to its conceptual elegance and computational efficacy, evaluations on real-world datasets demonstrate that SynMDiff outperforms the baselines by a large margin.

</details>


### [100] [A Rank Graduation metric for Algorithmic fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39025)
*Dalia Atif,Paolo Giudici*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于排序的公平性框架，通过预测误差分布评估公平性，将公平性与预测精度、可解释性关联，包含RGF、AURGF、CvM置换检验及特征移除程序，并在HMDA数据上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统公平性评估依赖聚合群体层面的均等性指标，难以揭示哪些个体遭遇不公平，以及哪些解释因素导致不公平，因此需要一种能联系预测精度与可解释性的新评估方式。

Method: 提出排序公平性框架（RGF）及其整合指标AURGF，结合中心化Cramer–von Mises置换检验与特征移除程序进行公平性可解释性分析；使用逻辑回归、随机森林、梯度提升和多层感知机进行模拟研究与HMDA抵押贷款数据实证。

Result: 模拟研究表明受保护群体失衡可逆转描述性公平比较，而所提推断程序能正确区分公平与不公平机制；HMDA数据上的模型排序与经典公平标准不同；树模型在预测精度与排序公平性上表现最佳；四个模型均拒绝公平性原假设；不公平性并非特定算法或预测变量所致，而源于贷款数据特征中固有的群体差异。

Conclusion: 该框架支持将预测精度、公平性测量、统计推断与可解释性相结合的更广泛的可信人工智能方法，公平性问题应视为数据层面而非单一算法层面的问题。

Abstract: Fairness assessment in algorithmic decisions that affect individuals, such as credit scoring, often relies on parity measures calculated at the aggregate group level. Such measures may not reveal which individuals experience unfairness or which explanatory factors contribute to it. In this paper, we propose a rank-based framework that evaluates fairness through the distribution of model prediction errors, thereby linking fairness assessment with predictive accuracy and explainability. The framework combines Rank Graduation Fairness (RGF), its integrated measure AURGF, a centered Cramer--von Mises permutation test, and a feature removal procedure for fairness explainability.
  We evaluate the methodology using logistic regression, random forest, gradient boosting, and a multilayer perceptron. The simulation study shows that protected-group imbalance can reverse descriptive fairness comparisons, whereas the proposed inferential procedure correctly distinguishes fair from unfair mechanisms. Its application to HMDA mortgage data produces model rankings that differ from those obtained with classical fairness criteria. Tree-based models, rather than logistic regression, provide the strongest combination of predictive accuracy and rank-based fairness, while the fairness null hypothesis is rejected for all four models. The persistence of disparity across statistical, bagging, boosting, and neural network specifications, together with the feature removal results, indicates that the observed unfairness is not specific to a single algorithm or predictor, but is associated with group differences embedded in the characteristics of the lending data. These findings support a broader approach to trustworthy artificial intelligence that combines predictive accuracy, fairness measurement, statistical inference, and explainability.

</details>


### [101] [Switching Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39034)
*Hyun Dong Lee,Xavier Gonzalez,Nicolas Zucchet,E. Kelly Buchanan,Emily B. Fox,Scott W. Linderman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SwiLA通过将状态更新建模为混合线性回归的在线EM，实现动态选择多个线性注意力分量，在保留线性注意力固定大小循环状态的同时增强了表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: softmax注意力性能强但KV缓存随序列长度线性增长，限制可扩展性；线性注意力内存固定高效但表达能力不足导致性能较差。本文旨在弥合二者之间的差距。

Method: 提出Switching Linear Attention（SwiLA），从测试时回归框架推导循环更新，将状态更新规则视为混合线性回归模型中的在线期望最大化（EM）。测试时，每个输出维度依据输入动态地在多个线性注意力分量间进行选择。

Result: 在联想回忆、上下文语言学习和语言建模基准上表现强劲，缩小了与softmax注意力的差距，并在若干设置中实现超越。

Conclusion: SwiLA在保持线性注意力恒定记忆占用的同时提升了表达能力，为兼具高效推理与强序列建模性能提供了新思路。

Abstract: Designing expressive sequence layers with efficient inference remains a central challenge in modern machine learning. Standard softmax attention achieves excellent sequence modeling performance through rich nonlinear token interactions, but it requires a key-value cache that grows linearly with sequence length, limiting its scalability. Linear attention enables efficient recurrent computation with a constant memory footprint, yet its reduced expressivity often yields inferior modeling performance. We introduce Switching Linear Attention (SwiLA), a novel sequence layer that bridges this gap by enhancing representational capacity while retaining the fixed-size recurrent state of linear attention. We derive the SwiLA recurrence from the test-time regression framework, casting the state update rule as online expectation-maximization in a mixture of linear regressions model. At test time, each output dimension dynamically selects among multiple linear attention components based on the input. Across associative recall, in-context language learning, and language modeling benchmarks, SwiLA shows strong performance and narrows the gap to softmax attention, even surpassing it in several settings.

</details>


### [102] [Cycle-Aware Autoencoder with Cross-SignalConsistency for Railway Door Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39035)
*Ammar Bouketta,Smail Niar,Hamza Ouarnoughi,Eva Mutuzo Brindle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TCAA-CS模型，将列车客室车门监测建模为周期级无监督异常检测问题，仅用正常周期训练，结合双流编码、时序注意力池化与跨信号一致性构建混合异常分数，在真实工业数据上取得93.8%召回率、97.3%精确率与0.5%误报率，并验证了车载实时部署可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 客室车门是轨道交通车辆的安全关键子系统，但实际运营中异常行为检测困难，原因在于故障稀少、类型多样且多无标签数据，因此需要一种仅依赖正常样本的无监督监测方法，并将检测单元细化到完整的开-停-关周期。

Method: 提出带跨信号一致性的时序周期感知注意力自编码器（TCAA-CS），仅使用正常周期训练。模型包含：双流编码器，分别用独立的1D-CNN分支处理连续物理量（位置、电流、电压）与二值逻辑状态（关门、锁门）；带时序注意力池化的LSTM编码器；以及融合重构误差、潜在空间偏离和相位感知跨信号一致性的三重混合异常分数，用于捕捉单信号看似合理但信号间物理或逻辑关系不一致的情形。

Result: 在商业运营载客列车的真实工业数据上，TCAA-CS达到93.8%召回率、97.3%精确率和0.5%误报率，优于多种代表性无监督基线方法；在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Xavier上的系统级评估支持实时车载部署的可行性。

Conclusion: TCAA-CS能够在标注稀缺的真实运营场景下有效实现列车车门周期级异常检测，兼具高精度、低误报与边缘设备实时部署能力，为轨道交通安全关键子系统的无监督状态监测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Passenger access doors are safety-critical subsystems in railway vehicles, yet detecting abnormal door behavior in real operation is challenging because faults are rare, diverse, and often unlabeled. This paper addresses railway door condition monitoring as a cycle-level unsupervised anomaly detection problem, where each complete opening-dwell-closing cycle is treated as a single monitoring unit. We propose the Temporal Cycle-Aware Attention Autoencoder with Cross-Signal Consistency (TCAA-CS), trained exclusively on nominal cycles. It combines a dual-stream encoder that processes continuous physical measurements (position, current, voltage) and binary logical states (door-closed, door-locked) through separate 1D-CNN branches, an LSTM encoder with temporal attention pooling, and a triple hybrid anomaly score fusing reconstruction error, latent-space deviation, and phase-aware cross-signal consistency. The consistency term helps identify cases where individual signals appear plausible but their inter-signal relationships become physically or logically inconsistent. On real industrial data from a passenger train in commercial service, TCAA-CS achieves 93.8% recall, 97.3% precision, and a 0.5% false-alarm rate, outperforming representative unsupervised baselines. System-level evaluation on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Xavier supports the feasibility of real-time onboard deployment.

</details>


### [103] [Hard-Gate Candidacy in a Deployed Validator Suite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39037)
*Xin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在部署的生成式智能体中筛查13个验证器，发现仅2个通过多重比较校正；执行本身与被门控属性不独立，跳过被记为通过，导致检测上限约84%；建议评测记录须区分"运行且通过"与"未运行"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证器要被提升为部署流水线上的硬性门禁，必须先证明其触发能够区分"正常工作到达用户"的输出与不能正常工作的输出。然而现有实践中，验证器清单常被当作门禁有效性的证据，且"跳过"被记录为"通过"，可能掩盖系统性失效。本文旨在对已部署生成式智能体中的验证器进行实证筛查，检验其边际分离能力并揭示评测记录层面的结构性缺陷。

Method: 在部署的生成式智能体中对13个验证器进行筛查，针对550个运行时构建与350个静态构建，按下游结果打标签，计算每个检查的边际分离度 J=TPR−FPR，并使用Newcombe区间与Fisher精确检验，进行多重比较校正。此外，跨4次运行、覆盖1,867个构建与10个不同运行时检查，统计探测被跳过的比例；对唯一具有构念特定标签的检查，用90个人工标注的空白构建评估检测器灵敏度及全局帧统计量的AUC；并在数万条评委评分的构建普查中统计无记录问题的拒绝比例。

Result: 13个检查中仅2个通过多重比较校正存活，2个仅为名义显著，其余9个与零不可区分，其中3个因从未在任何采样构建上触发。执行与被门控属性并非随机独立：895个破损构建中有144个探测被跳过，而972个可接受构建中仅1个被跳过（每次运行跳过率15.6%–16.6%，对照至多0.3%），所有跳过均带同一"不安全探测"原因。由于跳过被记为通过，这设置了任何检查质量都无法突破的上限：需要实时产物的检查在此框架中最多只能检测约84%的破损构建。对构念特定标签的检查，为空白输出构建的检测器在90个人工标注空白构建上触发0次（灵敏度95%上界3.3%），其近似的全局帧统计量仅弱分离（AUC 0.59），说明该差距并非阈值调优问题。上一层同样存在此差距：在数万条评委评分构建普查中，32.5%的拒绝完全未记录任何问题。

Conclusion: 评测记录必须区分"运行并通过的检查"与"未运行的检查"，必须携带拒绝的证据，并且检查清单本身不构成关于门禁有效性的证据。

Abstract: Before a validator can be promoted to a hard gate on a deployment pipeline, it has to be shown that its firing separates outputs that reach users in working order from those that do not. We run that screen on 13 validators in a deployed generative agent, against 550 runtime and 350 static builds labelled by downstream outcome, and report each check's marginal separation $J=\mathrm{TPR}-\mathrm{FPR}$ with Newcombe intervals and Fisher exact tests. Two checks survive correction for multiple comparisons, two more are nominal only, and the remaining nine are not distinguishable from zero, three of them because they never fired on any sampled build. Execution itself is not random with respect to the property being gated, and this replicates: across four runs covering 1,867 builds and ten distinct runtime checks, probes were skipped on 144 of 895 broken builds and 1 of 972 acceptable builds (per-run rates 15.6% to 16.6% against at most 0.3%), every skip carrying the same unsafe-to-probe reason. Because a skipped check is recorded as a pass, this imposes a ceiling that no check quality can lift: a check that needs a live artifact cannot operationally detect more than about 84% of broken builds in this harness. For the one check with construct-specific labels, a detector built for blank output fires on 0 of 90 human-labelled blank builds (95% upper bound on sensitivity 3.3%), and the global frame statistic it approximates separates the classes only weakly (AUC 0.59), so the gap is not a threshold that needs tuning. The same gap appears one layer up: on a census of tens of thousands of judge-scored builds, 32.5% of rejections carry no recorded issue at all. We argue that evaluation records must distinguish a check that ran and passed from one that did not run, must carry the evidence for a rejection, and that an inventory of checks is not evidence about a gate.

</details>


### [104] [Structure-aware Reinforcement Learning for Protein Directed Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39048)
*Zikun Nie,Suyuan Zhao,Yizhen Luo,Siqi Fan,Zaiqing Nie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出StructEvo，一种结构感知强化学习框架，通过delta结构融合编码器近似突变体结构特征，用于蛋白质定向进化，性能超越SOTA并在GFP中发现实验验证的上位效应模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
