Result: 与H.265集成时BD-rate降低34.86%，与DCVC-RT集成时降低18.01%；硬件测试台空口实验显示，H.265下PSNR最高提升1.448 dB，DCVC-RT下LPIPS最多降低0.033。

Conclusion: SAJSCO以轻量插件形式有效提升视频传输效率，学习到的策略可直接应用于不同视频编码器而无需针对性重训练或微调，兼顾传统与深度学习编码器。

Abstract: Video semantic communication has attracted increasing attention as a promising approach to improving video transmission efficiency. However, most existing approaches rely on computationally intensive deep learning-based video encoders and decoders, which hinders their deployment in resource-constrained scenarios. To address this issue, we propose a lightweight semantic-aware joint source-channel optimization (SAJSCO) scheme that can be integrated into existing digital video communication systems as a plug-in module. Specifically, we develop a video communication system model in which the transmitter jointly optimizes source and channel coding parameters based on the inter-frame semantic importance of the input video and estimated channel state information. On this basis, we formulate an optimization problem that maximizes semantic importance weighted video reconstruction quality under a maximum bitrate constraint. To solve it, we first quantify inter-frame semantic importance using a cosine similarity-based metric with a shifted window mechanism. We then develop a multi-actor proximal policy optimization (MPPO) algorithm to solve the formulated problem by jointly adapting the source compression rate and channel coding rate. The learned policy can be directly applied to different video encoders without encoder-specific retraining or fine-tuning. SAJSCO achieves Bjøntegaard Delta rate reductions of 34.86\% and 18.01\% when integrated with H.265, a conventional video encoder, and DCVC-RT, a deep learning-based video encoder. Over-the-air experiments on a hardware testbed further demonstrate a PSNR gain of up to 1.448 dB with H.265 and an LPIPS reduction of up to 0.033 with DCVC-RT compared with the respective best-performing fixed-parameter baselines.

</details>


### [141] [ReSAIL: Mitigating Collapse in Iterative Agent Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39306)
*Shengjie Jin,Hengbo Xu,Zelong Sun,YuJie Guo,Zhiwu Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReSAIL 是一种即插即用的迭代自蒸馏增强方法，通过优先选择信息量大的交互步骤并保留特权信息（PI）条件下的行为，缓解 LLM 智能体迭代部署中的性能崩溃问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迭代自蒸馏使 LLM 智能体能够从连续部署中学习，有望实现递归自我改进（RSI）。然而现有方法在多轮循环中出现部署性能崩溃，且特权信息（PI）条件下的任务性能也下降。

Method: 提出 ReSAIL（Retentive and Selective Augmentation for Iterative Self-Distillation），一种即插即用的迭代 PI 自蒸馏增强方法。它选择 PI 最能改变教师预测的交互步骤，并在轨迹间平衡蒸馏损失；同时将学生的 PI 条件输出分布正则化到冻结教师的分布上（包括选中与未选中的步骤），以在下轮循环中保留 PI 条件行为。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 和 TextCraft 上，ReSAIL 在三个循环中跨模型规模持续取得显著增益，添加到自蒸馏基线后，最终循环成功率平均绝对提升 22.5%。在 AITZ 上，基于敏感性引导的离线数据选择也提升了多模态 GUI 智能体的动作预测准确率。

Conclusion: 该研究首次表明，更稳健的学习机制能够有效缓解迭代智能体自蒸馏在部署轨迹中的性能崩溃。

Abstract: Iterative self-distillation enables LLM agents to learn from successive deployments, offering a path toward recursive self-improvement (RSI). Yet our experiments with existing methods reveal a collapse in deployment performance across cycles, while task performance with privileged information (PI) also declines. We address this collapse by prioritizing informative interaction steps for distillation and preserving PI-conditioned behavior as the student becomes the next teacher. We introduce Retentive and Selective Augmentation for Iterative Self-Distillation (ReSAIL), a plug-in augmentation for iterative PI-based self-distillation. ReSAIL selects interaction steps where PI most strongly changes the teacher's predictions and balances the resulting distillation losses across trajectories. It also regularizes the student's PI-conditioned output distributions toward those of the frozen teacher at selected and unselected steps to preserve PI-conditioned behavior for supervision in the next cycle. On ALFWorld and TextCraft, ReSAIL sustains substantial gains across model scales over three cycles, with an average absolute gain of 22.5% in final-cycle success rates when added to self-distillation baselines. Sensitivity-guided selection of offline data also improves action prediction accuracy for multimodal GUI agents on AITZ. These findings provide the first evidence that a more robust learning mechanism can effectively mitigate performance collapse in iterative agent self-distillation over deployment trajectories.

</details>


### [142] [GRPO Training Dynamics for Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39321)
*Rajat Ghosh,Vaishnavi Bhargava,Henry Wong,Aryan Singhal,Debojyoti Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了在单节点8xA100预算下，对1.5B–7B小语言模型进行GRPO微调的训练动态，分析组大小、张量级更新及LoRA配置的影响，并给出实用建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO作为一种节省内存的强化微调技术已被用于推理任务，但其在小语言模型上的训练动态尚不清楚，限制了在开放和资源受限环境中的可靠采用与复现。

Method: 在单节点8xA100计算预算下，对1.5B至7B参数的小语言模型进行GRPO微调的系统性研究，涵盖多个模型家族与推理领域（数学、代码、科学MCQ）；分析组大小对策略收敛、训练稳定性和下游基准性能的影响；刻画张量级更新动态，并探究LoRA目标模块与层选择的影响；基于机制评估改进LoRA与奖励塑形配置。

Result: 初步GRPO微调模型在约80%的数学基准评测上优于基座模型，但在MCQ和代码推理任务上能力有限；经LoRA与奖励塑形配置优化后，在这后两类领域上性能得到提升。

Conclusion: 该研究为小语言模型的GRPO训练提供了实用指导，有助于其在资源受限环境中的可靠采用。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has emerged as a memory-efficient reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) technique for reasoning-intensive tasks. How- ever, GRPO training dynamics on small language models (SLMs) remain poorly understood, limiting its reliable adoption and reproducibility in open and resource- constrained environments. In this work, we present a systematic study of GRPO fine-tuning for SLMs ranging from 1.5B to 7B parameters under a practical single- node 8xA100 compute budget. Our study spans multiple model families and reasoning domains, including mathematics, coding, and multiple-choice question answering (MCQ) in science. Across these settings, we analyze how group size affects policy convergence, training stability, and downstream benchmark per- formance. We further characterize tensor-level update dynamics during GRPO training and investigate whether the choice of LoRA target modules and layers can improve the performance of GRPO-tuned models. While our initial GRPO-tuned models outperform their base counterparts on approximately 80% of mathematical benchmark evaluations, they demonstrate limited capability on MCQ and code reasoning tasks. Guided by our mechanistic evaluations, we refined our LoRA and reward-shaping configurations to improve performance in latter domains. These findings provide practical guidance for GRPO training for SLMs.

</details>


### [143] [PatchKV: Weight-Space Compensation of KV Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39329)
*Chanryeol Lee,Chanhyuk Lee,Yeonwoo Choi,Donggyun Kim,Seunghoon Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 PatchKV，一种免训练框架，通过将部分上下文信息编码进模型权重补丁，来补偿 KV 缓存压缩带来的性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理中 KV 缓存随长度线性增长导致内存瓶颈；现有压缩方法（如 token 淘汰或近似）在激进压缩预算下性能急剧下降。

Method: PatchKV 将现成的压缩 KV 缓存与一个上下文专属的权重补丁配对；该补丁在加载上下文时计算一次，通过岭回归闭式求解，使上下文派生的参考查询 token 在分块激活上对齐全缓存与压缩缓存；合并进模型后不影响前向图与单上下文多查询场景下的每查询推理成本。

Result: 在三种模型架构上，于长上下文问答（SCBench 最高 170K token、SQuAD、NIAH）与数学（GSM8K）基准中，PatchKV 一致地提升了各种缓存压缩方法的表现。

Conclusion: PatchKV 为在激进压缩预算下补偿 KV 缓存压缩方法提供了新方向。

Abstract: Long-context inference with Large Language Models (LLMs) is bottlenecked by the linearly growing memory of the key-value (KV) cache. Existing compression methods reduce the cache through token eviction or approximation, but degrade sharply at aggressive compression budgets. We propose PatchKV, a training-free framework that compensates KV cache compression methods by carrying part of the context in the model's weights. PatchKV pairs an off-the-shelf compressed KV cache with a context-specific weight patch, which is computed once at context-loading time and served for downstream queries for the context. The weight patch is derived in closed form via ridge regression, by aligning the block-wise activations of context-derived reference query tokens under the full cache and the compressed cache. Once merged into the model, the patch leaves the forward graph and per-query inference cost unchanged in the single-context, multi-query setting. Across long-context QA (SCBench with up to 170K tokens, SQuAD, NIAH) and math (GSM8K) benchmarks on three model architectures, PatchKV consistently improves cache compression methods, suggesting an alternative direction to compensate them at aggressive budgets.

</details>


### [144] [How Many Samples Are Enough for Learning Across Domains?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39336)
*Hong Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过建立泛化界，提出每个训练域样本需求量的判据，揭示了训练域数量与每域所需样本数之间的反线性缩放规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明增加训练域数量或扩大域间分布偏移能提升泛化能力，但前提是每个域含足够多样本；然而"样本是否充分"的条件尚未被探索，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于所提出的学习界，建立每域样本需求量的判据，并证明域内学习与域外泛化之间的紧密关系，讨论若干关键论点。

Result: 判据揭示了训练域数量与每域所需样本数之间呈反线性缩放关系，并解释了数据充分性假设背后的根本原因，为评估现有数据集充分性和构建数据集提供理论指导。

Conclusion: 所需样本数与训练域数量高度相关，这不同于经典学习理论；本文为数据集评估与构建提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Understanding the fundamental mechanisms of learning is essential for designing systems with strong generalization. Recent studies have shown that increasing the number of training domains, or enlarging the distribution shift among them, improves generalization when each domain contains sufficiently many data samples. However, the conditions under which the data samples can be considered sufficient remain unexplored. In this work, we fill this gap by establishing criteria for per-domain sample requirements based on the presented learning bounds. These criteria not only reveal an inverse linear scaling law between the number of training domains and the number of samples required per domain, but also explain the fundamental rationale behind the assumption of data sufficiency, thereby providing theoretical guidance for assessing the adequacy of existing datasets and constructing datasets. This differs from classical learning theory, as the number of samples required is highly dependent on the number of training domains. Additionally, we prove the close relationship between in-domain learning and out-of-domain generalization through the presented generalization bounds, and lastly discuss some key arguments.

</details>


### [145] [WinoTS: Wavelet-based Self-Distillation for Time Series Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39337)
*Noam Major,Kathy Razmadze,Yoli Shavit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出WinoTS，一种基于小波的时序自蒸馏预训练范式，通过时频增强构建多尺度结构视图，在预测、零样本迁移与异常检测上超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序自监督预训练以「下一token预测」和「重建」为主，在连续值域中易把模型容量浪费在高频逐点噪声上，忽略不变结构。基于不变性的自蒸馏在视觉中很成功，但在时序领域探索不足。而直接套用视觉增强（如裁剪、抖动）会打乱周期时序或变化不足。

Method: WinoTS：基于不变性的预训练范式，核心是用时频（小波）增强生成多尺度结构视图，在不破坏信号动态的前提下构造正样本对，进行自蒸馏学习。该框架与具体骨干网络无关。

Result: 在长期预测、跨域零样本迁移、无监督异常检测上均优于SOTA基线；冻结表示上的线性探测常超过从头训练的完全监督模型；消融显示增益跨多种时序骨干成立。

Conclusion: 时频变换为视觉式空间增强提供了有原则的替代方案，WinoTS是灵活、架构无关的时序预训练框架。

Abstract: Self-supervised pre-training of time series models is currently dominated by next-token prediction and reconstruction objectives. In continuous-valued domains, these paradigms often waste model capacity on high-frequency, point-wise noise at the expense of learning invariant structure. While invariance-based self-distillation has proven highly effective in computer vision, its application to temporal data remains largely underexplored. Effectively adapting such methods to time series requires carefully designed augmentations: spatial operations like cropping can shift the timing of repeating cycles or distort the signal, while basic jittering may provide limited variation. We introduce Wavelet-based self-distillation for time series (WinoTS), an invariance-based pre-training paradigm designed specifically for temporal signals. At its core, WinoTS leverages time-frequency augmentations to construct multi-scale structural views without distorting underlying signal dynamics. Across extensive evaluations, WinoTS outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in long-term forecasting, cross-domain zero-shot transfer, and unsupervised anomaly detection. Notably, linear probing on frozen WinoTS representations frequently surpasses fully supervised models trained from scratch. Systematic ablations demonstrate that WinoTS is a flexible, architecture-agnostic framework yielding gains across time series backbones, and establish that time-frequency transformations provide a principled alternative to vision-style spatial augmentations.

</details>


### [146] [Learning Beyond Full Imitation: Task-Preserving Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39338)
*Qianfeng Yuan,Wenbing Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了知识蒸馏中"完全模仿"与"条件学习"的精确分离，并提出保持任务的知识蒸馏方法TPKD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生模型对正确类的判别可能已强于教师，进一步模仿反而会迫使学生放弃已获得的判别能力，因此完全模仿并不一定带来更好的学生模型。

Method: 作者证明了完全模仿与条件学习之间的精确分离条件：当学生在每个错误类上的概率都不高于教师时，教师到学生的完整KL最小化被阻断；据此提出TPKD，保持标签梯度不变，并最小化修正条件梯度，使输出更新保留标签步骤相对各错误类的收益。

Result: 修正后的条件方向在同一步长下保留了超过一半的原始一阶条件下降，且界是紧的；在固定正条件目标和足够小的常数输出步长下，标签误差和条件误差同时消失。实验上TPKD在CIFAR-100达到88.05%、CLINC150达到93.81%，在三组随机种子下分别比标准蒸馏提升0.47和0.35个百分点。

Conclusion: 完全教师KL最小化与条件概率学习可以分离，TPKD通过保持标签梯度并最小修正条件梯度，在不牺牲判别能力的前提下完成知识传递，实验验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation transfers knowledge by encouraging a student to match a teacher's predicted class probabilities. These probabilities express not only confidence in the correct class, but also relations among incorrect alternatives. Yet closer imitation does not necessarily yield a better student. A student may already distinguish the correct class more sharply than its teacher, so further imitation can require giving back discrimination it has acquired. Our main result is an exact separation between full imitation and conditional learning. When the correct class's score advantage over each alternative must be preserved, full teacher-to-student KL minimization is blocked exactly when the student assigns no more probability than the teacher to every incorrect class. Crucially, the teacher's relative probabilities among incorrect classes remain fully learnable. We characterize the exact price of this transfer: a minimum increase in correct-class log-odds that compensates for the largest conditional-probability mismatch. Label fitting and conditional matching can therefore be completed even as full teacher KL diverges. This separation motivates task-preserving knowledge distillation (TPKD), which keeps the label gradient intact and minimally corrects the conditional gradient so that its output update preserves the label step's gains against every incorrect alternative. The corrected conditional direction retains more than half of the original first-order conditional descent at the same step size, with a tight bound. For a fixed positive conditional target and sufficiently small constant output steps, label and conditional errors vanish together. Experiments trace this learning from exact head updates to ordinary network training. TPKD reaches 88.05% accuracy on CIFAR-100 and 93.81% on CLINC150, improving over standard distillation by 0.47 and 0.35 percentage points across three seeds.

</details>


### [147] [ElectrolyteFM: Unifying Electrolyte Property Prediction through Cross-Property Knowledge Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39340)
*Jiaxin Yu,Shuo Wang,Peng Wang,Yongcai Wang,Deying Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ElectrolyteFM多属性统一预测模型，通过属性专属表征与共享专家池+路由器+残差适配器，在避免跨属性干扰的同时利用可迁移知识，12项电解液属性平均NMAE降低14.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电解液配方设计需平衡多种理化性质，但现有模型通常只关注有限属性子集。单独学习每个属性会忽略可迁移的化学信息，而不加区分地共享信息又会引入跨属性干扰。定向迁移分析表明，两属性联合学习相对单独训练可能提升也可能降低预测性能，且任务间存在非对称迁移效应。

Method: ElectrolyteFM独立学习属性专属表征，并通过单独训练的专家池捕获跨属性知识；路由器为每个配方与目标属性选择相关的共享信息，属性专属残差适配器将该信息转化为对相应表征的修正以用于预测。

Result: 在Electrolyte12上，相比最强电解液专用基线，12项电解液属性的归一化平均绝对误差（NMAE）平均降低14.8%；在训练未见的独立钠电解液数据集上，电导率MAE相比最佳基线降低6.7%。

Conclusion: 通过有效识别并利用属性专属特征与跨属性共享知识，ElectrolyteFM能在多属性预测中兼顾可迁移信息利用与跨属性干扰抑制，并展现对未见电解液体系的泛化能力。

Abstract: Electrolyte formulation design requires balancing multiple physicochemical properties, yet existing models often focus on a limited subset. Learning each property in isolation can overlook transferable chemical information, whereas indiscriminate sharing can introduce cross-property interference. Our directed transfer analysis shows that jointly learning two property prediction tasks can improve or degrade prediction relative to separate training, with asymmetric transfer effects between the tasks. We propose ElectrolyteFM, a unified multi-property prediction model which can more accurately predict multiple properties of each electrolyte by effectively identifying and utilizing property-specific features and knowledge shared across properties. More specifically, ElectrolyteFM learns property-specific representations independently and captures cross-property knowledge through a separately trained expert pool. A router selects relevant shared information for each formulation and target property, and property-specific residual adapters convert this information into corrections to the corresponding representation for prediction. Experiments on Electrolyte12 show that ElectrolyteFM reduces normalized mean absolute error averaged across 12 electrolyte properties by 14.8% relative to the strongest electrolyte-specific baseline. On an independent sodium-electrolyte dataset unseen during training, it reduces conductivity mean absolute error by 6.7% relative to the best-performing baseline.

</details>


### [148] [Robustifying Asynchronous SGD via Soft Throttling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39357)
*Kaoru Otsuka,Maxime Meyer,Yuki Takezawa,Makoto Yamada,Anastasia Koloskova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Throttle，一种异步 SGD 的拜占庭鲁棒泛化算法，通过对较快客户端的更新按因子 q 指数降权来抵御攻击，并统一了异步 SGD 与同步鲁棒 SGD 两种极端情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步 SGD 中每个客户端的梯度更新到达即应用，虽然带来加速，但也使系统更易受攻击：快速客户端会主导总更新。因此需要一种既能保留异步加速、又能抵御拜占庭攻击的方法。

Method: 提出 Throttle：在异步 SGD 框架下，对来自较快客户端的更新施加指数降权，权重因子为 q。当 q=1 时退化为标准异步 SGD，当 q→∞ 时退化为同步拜占庭鲁棒 SGD。作者从理论上分析其收敛速率，并从理论与实验两方面验证鲁棒性。

Result: 理论分析给出了 Throttle 的收敛速率，并从理论和实验上验证了其对攻击的鲁棒性。实验还意外发现，该指数降权机制即使在非拜占庭（无攻击）场景下也能提升性能，优于标准异步 SGD。

Conclusion: Throttle 通过按速度对客户端更新指数降权，成功统一并泛化了异步 SGD 与同步拜占庭鲁棒 SGD，在保持异步加速的同时提升鲁棒性，且在没有攻击时也能改善性能。

Abstract: Asynchronous SGD is a popular algorithm for distributed learning where each client's gradient update is applied on arrival. This leads to a speed-up, but also an increased vulnerability to attacks, as fast clients can dominate the total update. We introduce Throttle, a Byzantine-robust generalization of asynchronous SGD where the key idea is to exponentially down-weight updates from faster clients by a factor $q$. Both asynchronous SGD ($q=1$) and synchronous Byzantine-robust SGD ($q\to\infty$) correspond to specific settings of Throttle. We provide a theoretical analysis of the convergence rate and validate the robustness to attacks both theoretically and empirically. Remarkably, our experiments show that this down-weighting mechanism can also improve performance over standard asynchronous SGD even in the non-Byzantine setting.

</details>


### [149] [LampAttention: Look-Ahead Mixed-Precision FlashAttention for Dedicated Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39361)
*Stanislav Budzinskiy,Marian Gloser,Tolunay Yilmaz,Ying Hong Tham,Yuanyi Lin,Wenyi Fang,Fan Wu,Philipp Petersen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出混合精度FlashAttention，在8位下计算注意力并自适应地用16位重算敏感子块，配合专用加速器设计，可恢复基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数注意力logits可在低精度下计算而不损害数值稳定性，但现有注意力核心未能利用这一特性。

Method: 软硬件协同设计的混合精度FlashAttention：以8位累加key-query乘积并计算其指数，再自适应识别敏感子块并以16位重算；同时提出可高效执行该流水线的专用加速器规格。

Result: 基于Qwen3与Gemma 3的模拟实验表明，仅将少数子块重新路由到高精度即可恢复基线模型性能。

Conclusion: 选择性混合精度计算是一种有效策略，能以少量高精度重算换取与基线相当的模型表现，并为专用加速器设计提供依据。

Abstract: While most attention logits can be computed in low precision without degrading numerical stability, current attention kernels fail to exploit this phenomenon. We introduce a novel hardware-algorithm co-design in the form of mixed-precision FlashAttention. Our method accumulates key-query products and evaluates their exponentials in 8-bit formats, then adaptively identifies sensitive sub-blocks and recomputes them in 16-bit formats. We propose the specifications for a dedicated accelerator capable of executing this pipeline efficiently. Simulated experiments with Qwen3 and Gemma 3 show that rerouting a selective minority of sub-blocks to high precision is sufficient to recover the baseline model performance.

</details>


### [150] [Wavelet Flow Matching for Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39374)
*Lucas Poinsignon,Jorge da Silva Gonçalves,Samuel Ruipérez-Campillo,Julia E. Vogt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在小波域中通过流匹配进行多变量时间序列生成，结合通道令牌Transformer建模跨通道依赖，隐式实现由粗到细的生成过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成时间序列在数据增强、隐私保护数据共享和下游模型开发中应用日益广泛，但现有方法难以同时忠实再现多尺度时间结构和跨通道依赖关系。

Method: 在多级离散小波系数（而非直接在时域）上执行流匹配，将粗结构和逐步细化的细节表示在不同尺度上；利用不同尺度系数的天然方差差异，隐式诱导由粗到细的生成过程而无需显式多尺度调度；由于小波变换对每个通道独立作用，搭配通道令牌Transformer，用其注意力直接建模跨通道依赖。

Result: 在七个基准数据集和四种序列长度上，该方法在大多数"数据集-指标"组合中达到最优或并列最优，且在Context-FID和判别分数上提升最大且最稳定。

Conclusion: 在小波域进行流匹配并结合通道注意力建模，可有效兼顾多尺度时间结构与跨通道依赖，为多变量时间序列生成提供了一种有竞争力的方案。

Abstract: Synthetic time series are increasingly used for data augmentation, privacy-preserving data sharing, and downstream model development, yet faithfully reproducing both multi-scale temporal structure and cross-channel dependencies remains challenging. We study multivariate time-series generation through flow matching in the wavelet domain. By operating on multilevel discrete wavelet coefficients rather than directly in the time domain, the model represents coarse structure and progressively finer details at separate scales. Their naturally different variances further induce an implicit coarse-to-fine generative process without requiring an explicit multi-scale schedule. Since the transform acts independently on each channel, we pair it with a channel-token transformer whose attention directly models cross-channel dependencies. Across seven benchmark datasets and four sequence lengths, our method is best or tied on a majority of dataset-metric combinations, with the largest and most consistent improvements in Context-FID and discriminative score.

</details>


### [151] [From Search to Signal: Online Post-Training in Automatic Heuristic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39383)
*Yilun Yuan,Tianyu Zhou,Zhenzhou Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM自动启发式设计(AHD)中的在线后训练问题，将其形式化为上下文相关的信号构建，并比较从有效性、性能与生成上下文到更新信号的不同映射，以判断在线更新能否带来超越冻结生成器搜索的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AHD系统多保持生成器冻结，而EvoTune、CALM等会依据被评估的候选更新生成器。当这类结果驱动RLVR时形成"搜索耦合"回路：候选流既更新搜索状态又提供训练信号。但有效性与性能并不能唯一决定有用的模型更新，须考虑产生候选时的提示与演化搜索状态，因此需要研究如何把评估结果转化为学习信号。

Method: 将小型开源权重LLM在AHD中的在线后训练形式化为上下文相关的信号构建；设计从程序有效性、任务性能与生成上下文到更新信号的多种备选映射；在共享评估rollout与匹配更新预算下，跨AHD任务与模型族开展受控实验，与在线后训练基线进行比较，评估对有效性、有效提案性能、以及上下文比较下改进型有效提案产率的影响；并进行checkpoint、冻结搜索与实时系统评估，以及预设定成本核算下的资源匹配比较。

Result: （摘要未给出具体实验结果，仅说明实验设计）通过共享rollout和匹配预算的受控实验比较不同映射与基线；通过checkpoint、冻结搜索和实时系统评估检验提案级增益是否体现在更新后的checkpoint行为与后续搜索中；通过资源匹配比较检验在线更新相对冻结生成器增加搜索是否具有额外价值。

Conclusion: 该设计避免仅以端到端搜索增益作为更强启发式设计能力的证据，强调需区分在线更新带来的真实能力提升与单纯搜索状态累积的效果。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based automatic heuristic design (AHD) iteratively proposes and refines heuristics, pairing design rationales with executable code. Task-specific evaluators assess programs; execution outcomes and performance scores guide search. Many AHD systems keep the generator frozen; EvoTune and Co-Evolution of Algorithms and Language Model (CALM) instead update it from evaluated candidates. When such outcomes drive reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), they create a search-coupled loop: the evaluated candidate stream supplies both search-state updates and training signals for the model that generates future candidates. Yet validity and performance do not uniquely determine useful model updates; converting them into learning signals must account for the prompt and evolving search state that produced each candidate. We formulate online post-training of small open-weight LLMs in AHD as context-dependent signal construction and develop alternative mappings from program validity, task performance, and generation context to update signals. Using shared evaluated rollouts and matched update budgets, controlled experiments across AHD tasks and model families compare these mappings with online post-training baselines, testing their effects on validity, performance among valid proposals, and the yield of valid proposals that improve under contextual comparisons. Complementary checkpoint, frozen-search, and live-system evaluations assess whether proposal-level gains appear in updated checkpoint behavior and subsequent search, rather than arising solely from accumulated search state. A resource-matched comparison under pre-specified cost accounting tests whether online updating adds value beyond additional search with a frozen generator. Together, this design avoids treating end-to-end search gains alone as evidence of stronger heuristic-design capabilities.

</details>


### [152] [When, Not How Much: Evaluating Time-Series Foundation Models on Sparse Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39386)
*Daniel Schoess,Florian von Wangenheim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文评估预训练时间序列基础模型（TSFM）在稀疏事件排序任务上的表现，发现其发布的点预测相比无训练参考提升有限，而在冻结表征上训练轻量事件头能显著提升排序性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅将TSFM评估为数值预测器，但对稀疏序列而言，许多决策只依赖于未来哪些时段存在活动，而标准基准并未评估这种稀疏事件排序能力。

Method: 在五个稀疏数据集上，对预测窗口内含事件与零值的位置进行排序评估；比较12个TSFM发布的点预测、无训练参考、六种冻结骨干的线性探针、分位数平均、随机初始化对照及原始上下文学习器（如梯度提升树）。

Result: 发布的点预测在机会校正平均精度上最多提升0.031，机会校正AUC中位数在每个数据集上均低于无训练参考；事件监督下六种冻结骨干的线性探针在30个骨干-数据集对中29个优于其骨干点预测；平均预测分位数优于取中位数；探针优势依赖数据集，预训练特征在六种骨干中五种优于随机初始化，最佳探针在五个数据集中三个超过原始上下文的梯度提升树。

Conclusion: 对稀疏事件排序，发布的点预测相较简单参考提升甚微，而冻结TSFM上的轻量事件头排序更优。评估预训练预测器需同时报告其输出、表征监督探针及原始上下文与随机化对照，因为三者支持不同结论。

Abstract: Pretrained time-series foundation models (TSFMs) are evaluated as forecasters of future values, yet for sparse series many decisions depend only on which future periods contain activity. Standard benchmarks do not assess this. On five sparse datasets, we rank positions within forecast windows that contain both events and zeros. The released point forecasts of 12 TSFMs improve chance-corrected average precision over training-free references by at most 0.031, and in chance-corrected AUC the median TSFM falls below them on every dataset. With event supervision, linear probes of six frozen backbones improve on their backbone's point forecast in 29 of 30 backbone--dataset pairs. Averaging the predicted quantiles instead of taking their median improves the ranking of most TSFMs that forecast the median, and on two datasets the strongest such outputs rival the probes. The probes' advantage over raw-context learners depends on the dataset, and under the same probe, pretrained features outperform randomly initialized ones for five of six backbones. For sparse-event ranking, released point forecasts thus add little over simple references, whereas lightweight event heads on frozen TSFMs rank events better than these forecasts, and the best of them exceed gradient-boosted trees trained on the raw context on three of the five datasets. More broadly, assessing pretrained forecasters on tasks beyond value forecasting requires reporting their outputs, supervised probes of their representations, and raw-context and randomized controls side by side, since each supports a different conclusion.

</details>


### [153] [Beyond Simulation: Retain-and-Repair Neural Operators for Real-World Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39387)
*Woojin Cho,Junghwan Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 R²NO 框架，将仿真预训练的神经算子适配到真实数据，通过频域分格的自适应精修实现保留与修复的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子虽可从数值仿真预训练中获益，但适配到真实世界预测仍具挑战，需要在利用预训练结构与适配真实数据之间取得平衡。

Method: 先对预训练算子用真实数据微调并冻结，得到源预测；再用共享修复模块学习一系列精修。借助正交傅里叶投影，在傅里叶域的每个单元内拟合小型岭回归，并以留出真实数据拟合权重来组合各次精修。单元由径向范围、角向扇区和测量通道共同定义，使精修深度随频率幅度、方向和通道变化。将源预测作为候选即可在每个单元保留原结构，独立训练的修复模块也作为额外候选参与组合。

Result: 在 RealPDEBench 的所有系统以及六种骨干网络上，R²NO 均稳定优于完全微调与迭代精修方法。

Conclusion: 该框架将适配深度视为从真实数据中学习到的、因单元而异的选择，为仿真预训练神经算子的真实世界适配提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Neural operators increasingly benefit from pretraining on numerical simulations, yet adapting them for real-world prediction remains challenging. We introduce the Retain-and-Repair Neural Operator (R$^2$NO), a framework for adapting simulation-pretrained operators to real-world data while retaining useful pretrained structure. The pretrained operator is first finetuned on real data and then frozen to provide a source prediction, and a shared repair module learns a sequence of refinements from the same observations. Using orthogonal Fourier projections, a spectral ensemble fits a small ridge regression within each cell of the Fourier domain and combines the refinements by weights fitted on a held-out split of the real data. The cells are defined jointly by radial ranges, angular sectors, and measured channels, allowing refinement depth to vary with frequency magnitude, with orientation, and across channels. Including the source prediction as a candidate makes retention available in every cell, and independently trained repair modules enter the same combination as additional candidates. On all RealPDEBench systems and six backbones, R$^2$NO consistently outperforms full finetuning and iterative refinement. The framework treats adaptation depth as a cell-specific choice learned from real data.

</details>


### [154] [Decoupled and Distilled: Task-Adaptive LoRA-Teachers with Ensemble Knowledge Transfer for Few-Shot Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39390)
*Hongwei Zhao,Rui Liu,Yansong Liu,Zhiyuan Zou,Yong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TALON：为每个增量任务分配独立LoRA-Teacher进行任务特定学习，再通过集成知识蒸馏将多个冻结teacher融合进统一LoRA-Student，实现推理高效的FSCIL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FSCIL需在极少样本下学习新类并保留旧知识。现有基于预训练模型的高效微调方法存在两类问题：严格梯度约束在数据极度稀缺时不可靠；多专家方法带来高昂推理成本。

Method: TALON动态为每个增量任务分配独立LoRA-Teacher学习任务特定表示，再通过集成知识蒸馏将多个冻结teacher蒸馏进单一LoRA-Student，避免运行时模块选择或生成；并采用语义引导蒸馏，依据特征空间相似度加权各teacher贡献，以缓解灾难性遗忘与过拟合。

Result: 三次类序运行下，TALON在四个基准上取得相当或更优的平均准确率：CUB200为86.68±1.22%，CIFAR100为90.39±0.27%，ImageNet-R为78.38±0.94%，miniImageNet为96.34±0.33%；部署参数最多减少33倍，平均单任务推理时间降至26.7s，较ASP减少41.70%。

Conclusion: TALON是一种推理高效且有效的FSCIL框架，通过多LoRA-Teacher集成蒸馏为单一LoRA-Student，在保持性能的同时显著降低部署参数与推理成本。

Abstract: Few-Shot Class-Incremental Learning (FSCIL) addresses the challenge of learning new classes from very limited samples while retaining knowledge of previously learned ones. Although parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods with pre-trained models show promise for class-incremental learning, strict gradient-based constraints can be unreliable under severe data scarcity, while multi-expert approaches can impose substantial inference-time costs. We propose TALON (Task-Adaptive LoRA-Teachers with Ensemble Knowledge Transfer), an inference-efficient FSCIL framework. TALON dynamically allocates an independent LoRA-Teacher to each incremental task for task-specific representation learning, then distills multiple frozen teachers into a unified LoRA-Student through Ensemble Knowledge Transfer, eliminating runtime module selection or generation. A semantic-guided distillation strategy weights teacher contributions by feature-space similarity to mitigate catastrophic forgetting and overfitting. Across three class-order runs, TALON achieves comparable or better mean average accuracy across four FSCIL benchmarks, obtaining 86.68 +/- 1.22% on CUB200, 90.39 +/- 0.27% on CIFAR100, 78.38 +/- 0.94% on ImageNet-R, and 96.34 +/- 0.33% on miniImageNet. TALON uses up to 33x fewer deployment parameters and reduces average inference time per task to 26.7 s, a 41.70% reduction relative to ASP.

</details>


### [155] [No Task Vector Is an Island: A Comprehensive Study on the Composability of Task Vectors from On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39405)
*Jingang Zhou,Feiyu Han,Han Zhu,Yuyi Zhou,Ruiyang Zhang,Jian Xu,Sirui Gao,Qingpei Guo,Xu-Yao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究on-policy distillation (OPD)产生的task vector的可组合性，发现OPD task vector既能补充更强的RL教师更新，也能跨任务有效组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: task vector可通过模型合并组合已学能力，但OPD产生的task vector的可组合性尚未被充分探索。OPD用教师反馈在学生生成的轨迹上训练学生，其参数更新与教师模型（通常通过RL）产生的更新不同，因此需要探究二者能否互补及跨任务组合。

Method: 在五个领域和两种模型架构上，对比OPD与RL task vector的合并效果：任务内合并OPD与RL task vector；跨任务进行OPD task-vector组合并与RL组合对比；通过参数空间分析考察OPD与RL更新的非共线性、方向互补性及前馈通道更新能量的重叠度。

Result: 任务内，合并OPD与RL task vector可超越两者，即使OPD学生弱于其RL教师。跨任务，在八种backbone-merging-rule比较中的七种，OPD组合平均分高于RL组合。参数空间显示OPD与RL更新存在显著非共线性；在SMOLLM3-3B的CODE实验中，组合方向在测试的全局更新范数下优于任一组成方向；跨任务中OPD更新的top-10%前馈通道重叠度更低。

Conclusion: 较弱的单独性能并不意味着较弱的task-vector可组合性。OPD task vector能补充更强的RL教师更新并跨任务有效组合，表明可组合性是理解和评估后训练更新的一个独立属性。

Abstract: Task vectors provide a simple mechanism for composing learned capabilities through model merging. However, the composability of task vectors produced by on-policy distillation (OPD) remains largely unexplored. OPD trains a student using teacher feedback on student-generated trajectories, yielding parameter updates that differ from those produced by the teacher model, usually by reinforcement learning (RL). We therefore ask whether OPD task vectors can complement their RL teacher updates and compose effectively across tasks. Across five domains and two model architectures, we find evidence for both forms of composability. Within a task, merging OPD and RL task vectors can outperform both constituent models, even when the OPD student is weaker than its RL teacher. Across tasks, OPD task-vector compositions achieve higher average scores than corresponding RL compositions in seven of eight backbone-merging-rule comparisons. Parameter-space analyses reveal substantial non-collinearity between OPD and RL updates. Experiment in CODE domain on SMOLLM3-3B shows that the combined direction outperforms either constituent direction at the tested global update norm, supporting directional complementarity in this configuration. Across tasks, OPD updates also show lower overlap among the top-10% feed-forward channels ranked by update energy. Together, these results show that weaker standalone performance does not imply weaker task-vector composability. OPD task vectors can complement stronger RL teacher updates and combine effectively across tasks, highlighting composability as a distinct property for understanding and evaluating post-training updates.

</details>


### [156] [Awakening of the Buddha: Subspace Learning During Population-Loss Plateaus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39408)
*Akash Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明：神经网络在总体损失几乎不变（平台期）时，其表示（由AGOP衡量）仍可获得显著提升，即损失与表示学习之间存在可分离性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于：现有认知常以损失下降判断学习进展，但总体损失可能长期停滞而网络内部表示却在持续改善。作者希望从理论上严格刻画"损失平台期"与"表示改进"之间的分离现象。

Method: 方法上，作者对两层ReLU和leaky-ReLU网络在高斯输入下做全体参数的同步定步长总体梯度下降。针对结构化加性教师（链接为高斯衰减三次函数在H^1(γ)中的正混合），给出小IID高斯初始化下高概率保证的显式条件。使用平均梯度外积（AGOP）度量教师子空间与预测器前r维特征子空间的对齐度。另对等权/不等权三次教师、含Sobolev扰动的设定使用投影特征重拟合。SwiGLU网络部分在精确拟合截距、固定宽度和维度下，证明当高斯初始化趋于零时平台期出现前导AGOP对齐；并对秩一三次特例给出无限制重拟合增益，及限制脊神经元共享正交轴时的近似下界。实验上以ReLU学生网络对21个教师、每个教师50次初始化做总体矩实验。

Result: 结果为：在高损失平台期的某一检查点，秩-r教师子空间与前r维AGOP特征空间的最小对齐度相对初始化至少提高1/2，而在系数预算不变时最小重拟合MSE下降超过0.399；同一轨迹随后训练损失低于平台窗内的所有值。SwiGLU情形证明损失平台期内前导AGOP对齐随初始化消失而成立。近似下界表明将脊神经元限制在教师子空间内共享正交轴时，某些交互目标仍保留非零误差。总体矩实验与理论一致。

Conclusion: 结论是：总体损失的近乎恒定并不意味着表示停滞，网络可在损失平台期显著改善预测性表示；AGOP可作为刻画该表示改进的有效工具，且该分离现象在ReLU、leaky-ReLU及SwiGLU架构中均得到理论与实验支持。

Abstract: Population loss can remain nearly constant while a neural network learns a substantially more predictive representation. We establish this separation for two-layer ReLU and leaky-ReLU networks trained on Gaussian inputs by simultaneous fixed-step population gradient descent on all parameters. For structured additive teachers whose links are positive mixtures of Gaussian-damped cubics in $H^1(γ)$, we give explicit conditions under which small IID Gaussian initialization yields a high-probability guarantee: at a checkpoint during a high-loss plateau, minimum alignment between the rank-$r$ teacher subspace and the leading $r$-dimensional eigenspace of the predictor's average gradient outer product (AGOP) increases by at least $1/2$, and the minimum refit MSE under unchanged coefficient budgets decreases by more than $0.399$, both relative to initialization. The same trajectory subsequently attains a trained loss below every value in the plateau window. A complementary result treats unequal-weight cubic teachers and small additive Sobolev perturbations using projected-feature refits. For SwiGLU networks with an exactly fitted intercept, we prove leading-AGOP alignment during a loss plateau at fixed width and dimension as Gaussian initialization vanishes, for square-integrable teachers with nonzero Hermite content of degree one, two, or three. A rank-one cubic specialization also gives simultaneous unrestricted-refit gains at a prescribed width. An approximation lower bound further shows that certain interaction targets retain nonzero error when ridge neurons are restricted to shared orthogonal axes within the teacher subspace. Population-moment experiments with ReLU students across 21 teachers and 50 initializations per teacher complement the analysis.

</details>


### [157] [From Imitation to Reward Discovery: On-Policy Warmup for Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39436)
*Yitong Qiao,Tiantian He,Lei Liu,Yue Shen,Jian Wang,Jinjie Gu,Zhixuan Chu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现"在线策略加速现象"，提出在线策略预热（OPW）方法，即在RLVR前用教师对学生在自身交互轨迹上的监督进行预热，并从理论上解释其加速机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR可扩展地训练语言模型智能体，但稀疏的结果奖励导致训练早期缺乏策略改进信号，亟需有效的预热手段。

Method: 提出OPW：学生在自身交互轨迹（含不完美动作与恢复情形）上接受教师监督预热，再转入RLVR；并将在线策略反向KL蒸馏与轨迹级分布匹配建立联系进行理论分析。

Result: 在有效教师和小群体蒸馏损失下，该联系给出了初始验证器成功率下界及奖励发现复杂度上界；对组相对RLVR，刻画了成功率提升何时产生更具奖励信息量的组。

Conclusion: 在线策略蒸馏是智能体RLVR的有效预热方式，初始奖励发现是其加速现象的潜在机制。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with a verifiable reward (RLVR) offers a scalable approach to training language-model agents, yet sparse outcome rewards can leave early training with little signal for policy improvement. We identify an On-Policy Acceleration Phenomenon: in our main comparisons, RLVR initialized with on-policy distillation reaches high performance earlier in training and achieves both higher average performance during subsequent RLVR and higher final performance than the alternative baselines. Motivated by this observation, we study On-Policy Warmup (OPW), a teacher-guided stage in which the student trains with teacher supervision on its own interaction trajectories before transitioning to RLVR. Unlike imitation on fixed teacher-generated trajectories, OPW targets states induced by the student's own decisions, including imperfect actions and recovery situations. We provide a theoretical explanation by connecting on-policy reverse-KL distillation to trajectory-level distribution matching. Under a competent teacher and sufficiently small population distillation loss, this connection yields a lower bound on initial verifier success and a corresponding bound on reward-discovery complexity. For group-relative RLVR, we further characterize when increased success probability produces more reward-informative groups. Together, our findings support on-policy distillation as an effective warmup for agentic RLVR and identify initial reward discovery as a mechanism that can contribute to the observed acceleration.

</details>


### [158] [Network-based Spatial Context Retrieval for Open-weight LLMs: A Faithfulness Benchmark for Grounded Geographic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39437)
*Joan Perez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出基于步行路网的空间上下文检索流程，并构建"忠实性基准"来考察LLM是依据注入的空间简报推理，还是用自己的参数记忆覆盖它。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码了大量潜在地理知识，但空间推理能力差，仅给出坐标时不可靠；只有提供结构化空间上下文时才有可用表现。已有地理评测未回答一个关键问题：当正确上下文已给出时，模型是据其推理，还是用自身参数知识覆盖它。

Method: 提出开放流程NSCR：以行人路网定义某点周边实际步行可达范围；仅用开放数据和开放权重模型，从OpenStreetMap与GHS-POP人口栅格检索要素，用代码在网络集水区上计算指标，并注入为紧凑空间简报。据此构建忠实性基准：对模型每条陈述按来源（基于简报或来自训练知识）与正确性打标，并用简报可反驳的植入虚假前提逐例探测。评估三个家族（Qwen、Gemma、Llama，Gemma含两代）、四个规模等级、含思考与非思考模式的十六种配置，覆盖三个对比城市，每例十个种子重采样。

Result: 对植入虚假前提的抵抗力随模型家族与代际的差异，强于随规模的差异；简报阅读能力构成一个部分独立的维度。这些行为无法被传统世界正确性分数或单次评测捕捉。

Conclusion: 需区分"能读懂简报"与"愿遵从简报"，地理评测应引入忠实性与抗诱导维度；实现、空间简报、模型输出与陈述级标注已开源为可复现流程。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) encode substantial latent geographic knowledge, yet they reason poorly over space and are unreliable when queried from coordinates alone. Useful behaviour emerges only when structured spatial context is supplied in the prompt. This raises a question geographic evaluation has left unexamined: once the right context is supplied, does the model reason from it, or override it with its own parametric recall? We take up this question with an open pipeline for network-based spatial context retriev-al. In it, the surroundings of a selected point are defined by the pedestrian street network, the area actually reachable on foot. Using only open data and open-weight models, the pipeline retrieves features from OpenStreetMap and the GHS-POP population grid, computes indicators over the network catchment in code, and injects them as a compact spatial brief. On this basis we build a faithfulness benchmark. It labels every claim a model makes by its source (grounded in the brief, or drawn from training knowledge) and its correctness, and it probes each case with a planted false premise that the brief refutes. We evaluate sixteen open-weight model configurations across three families (Qwen, Gemma and Llama, with Gemma in two generations), four size classes and, where available, both thinking and non-thinking modes, on three con-trasting cities, resampling every case over ten seeds. The results show that resistance to the planted premise varies more strongly by model family and generation than by scale, while brief-reading competence forms a partly separate dimension. These behaviours are not captured by conventional world-correctness scores or single-shot evaluation. We release the implementation, spatial briefs, model outputs, and claim-level labels as a reproducible workflow at github.com/perezjoan/NSCR-LLM.

</details>


### [159] [Raw-Routed Mixture of Adapters: A Causal Intervention for Routing Collapse in Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39445)
*Hung Phan,Thuy T. Nguyen,Minh Ngoc Dinh,Nhat-Quang Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文发现实例归一化骨干上的混合专家路由会发生"归一化诱导路由崩溃"，并提出在原始（归一化前）输入上路由的RR-MoA方案，在冻结骨干下显著优于现有适配方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型（TSFM）通常通过在冻结骨干上附加单个可训练头来适配新数据，这种"一刀切"方式对异质数据机制欠拟合。将单头替换为混合专家（MoE）是常规升级方案，但在占主流的实例归一化骨干上会失败：路由熵塌缩为零，单一专家吸收所有输入（归一化诱导路由崩溃）。标准MoE补救机制无效，因为问题根源在路由器的输入而非优化过程——预编码器归一化剥离了路由器区分不同机制所需的统计信息。

Method: 作者通过互信息分解精确刻画该失效机制，导出一个在训练前即可计算的"信噪比"指标以预测数据集的脆弱性；设计八项因果控制实验（含视觉模态复现）隔离实例归一化为成因。提出的方案是极小因果干预：Raw-Routed Mixture of Adapters（RR-MoA），即在归一化前的原始输入上进行路由。

Result: 信噪比与数据集脆弱性高度相关（Spearman ρ = -0.88）。在严格冻结骨干下，RR-MoA 在 54/54 次对比中胜过最强固定适配器，并显著优于 LoRA、TRACE、AdaMix 与全量微调。效果可泛化到六个骨干及一个插补任务。冻结的 RR-MoA 还比全量微调高出 12-79%（"冻结悖论"）。

Conclusion: 归一化诱导路由崩溃源于路由器输入而非优化，通过在原始输入上路由即可修复。RR-MoA验证了该原则，且两种架构上不同的变体证实其可推广至特定路由器之外，为TSFM的高效适配提供了新范式。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) commonly adapt to new data by attaching a single trainable head to a frozen backbone, a one-size-fits-all setup that underfits heterogeneous regimes. Replacing the head with a mixture of experts is the standard upgrade, but on instance-normalized backbones (the dominant TSFM design class) it fails: routing entropy collapses to zero and one expert absorbs every input, a failure we call normalization-induced routing collapse. Standard MoE rescue mechanisms do not repair it, because the cause is in the router's input, not its optimization. Pre-encoder normalization strips the statistics a router would need to tell regimes apart. A mutual-information decomposition makes this precise and yields a signal-ratio that, computed before training, predicts dataset vulnerability (Spearman $ρ= -0.88$). Eight causal controls, including a vision-modality replication, isolate instance normalization as the cause. The prescription is a minimal causal intervention: Raw-Routed Mixture of Adapters (RR-MoA), which routes on the raw, pre-normalization input. Under a strictly frozen backbone, RR-MoA wins 54/54 comparisons against the strongest fixed adapter and significantly outperforms LoRA, TRACE, AdaMix, and full fine-tuning. The effect generalizes across six backbones and an imputation task. Frozen RR-MoA also beats full fine-tuning by 12-79% (the Frozen Paradox); two architecturally distinct variants confirm the principle generalizes beyond this specific router.

</details>


### [160] [Mutual Equilibrium: Multimodal Representation Learning through Reciprocal Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39456)
*Ho-min Park,Byungkon Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种相互反馈架构MEQ，将两种模态的输入精炼为一对耦合嵌入，每个嵌入都反映对方的信息，最终输出定义为该交互的不动点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态方法通常在早期或晚期进行简单融合（如拼接），难以让两模态之间进行充分的信息交互，因此作者希望建立一种能够持续交换信息、彼此精炼的机制。

Method: 提出MEQ（Mutual Feedback Architecture），由两个组件构成，各自输出反馈给另一方，形成连续的信息交换；模型最终输出为该交互系统的不动点。作者还提供了理论分析，解释该模型的含义并给出避免失败情形的设计选择。

Result: 在多模态分类任务上，模型在基于拼接的分类问题上取得优于或具竞争力的表现；在视觉定位任务上，与互补模态配对时，交互机制能够逐步精炼视觉定位结果。

Conclusion: 相互反馈机制在多种数据集和任务（分类、视觉定位）中展现出有效性，说明持续的信息交换与耦合嵌入设计能够提升多模态学习性能。

Abstract: This work proposes a mutual feedback architecture, MEQ, that refines the two inputs, of possibly different modalities, into a pair of coupled embeddings such that each embedding reflects the information of the other. The core idea is to incorporate continuous interchange of information between the two inputs. This idea leads to a mutual feedback architecture consisting of two components whose outputs are fed back into the other. The final output of this model is defined as the fixed point of this interaction. We provide theoretical analysis that offers interpretation of this model as well as design choices to prevent failure cases. We show the benefits of MEQ through classification and visual grounding tasks spanning various datasets. Quantitatively, our model outperforms or shows competitive performance on concatenation-based multimodal classification problems. Qualitatively, the proposed interactive mechanism allows the model to progressively refine the visual grounding when paired with complementary modality, thus demonstrating the power of mutual feedback under such settings.

</details>


### [161] [Understanding Head Geometry and Dynamics in Federated Regression through a Natural Solution Selection Rule: An Unconstrained Feature Model Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39464)
*Chuang Ma,Tomoyuki Obuchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦多元回归中局部最优头部不唯一导致的聚合歧义问题。作者提出"客户端返回最接近广播头部的最优头部"这一自然选择规则，并证明在头部上加消失的近端惩罚可实现该规则；共享Gram矩阵收敛到客户端最优Gram矩阵的唯一Bures-Wasserstein重心，且一般不同于集中式最优。作者将差距分解为三项，并提出一次性交换目标均值与协方差的修正方法，在理论和实验上验证了结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联邦平均中，局部目标可能存在多个最优头部，导致聚合结果依赖于客户端返回哪一个头部，产生歧义。作者希望理解并消除这种由头部选择不确定性带来的偏差，并揭示其与集中式训练的差距来源。

Method: 采用无约束特征模型（UFM），将训练样本特征视为自由变量。提出自然选择规则：每个客户端返回距离广播头部最近的最优头部，并用带消失近端惩罚的全局最小化实现该规则。在客户端最优Gram矩阵与初始共享Gram矩阵正定时，证明共享Gram矩阵满足闭式递推并收敛到唯一Bures-Wasserstein重心。进一步将极限与集中式最优的差距分解为三个半正定项，并提出基于一次性交换目标均值与协方差的修正方案。

Result: 理论表明共享Gram矩阵收敛到客户端最优Gram矩阵的Bures-Wasserstein重心，但一般不等于集中式最优Gram矩阵。差距可分解为客户端目标均值差异、协方差异质性和对齐头部平均三部分。一次性交换目标均值与协方差的修正，在精确局部优化和相同选择规则下，一轮即可恢复集中式最优Gram矩阵。在UFM数值验证以及在五个表格和五个图像回归数据集上使用深度网络（特征正则化与长局部训练）的实验中，普通训练接近预测重心，弱近端惩罚改善端点一致性且轨迹贴近预测Gram动态，修正使最终Gram矩阵接近集中式UFM预测。

Conclusion: 联邦多元回归中头部不唯一性会导致聚合偏离集中式最优，但这种偏差可通过Bures-Wasserstein重心刻画并分解。所提出的选择规则与近端惩罚机制可稳定训练，而一次性交换统计量的修正可在一轮内恢复集中式最优，理论和实验均支持该结论。

Abstract: In federated averaging, local objectives can admit multiple optimal heads, making the aggregate depend on which heads clients return. We study this ambiguity in federated multivariate regression with private backbones and a shared linear head, using an unconstrained feature model (UFM) that treats training-sample features as free variables. We introduce a natural selection rule: each client returns the optimal head closest to the broadcast head. We show that global minimization with a vanishing proximal penalty on the head realizes this rule. When the clients' optimal Gram matrices and the initial shared Gram matrix are positive definite, the shared Gram matrix follows a closed recursion and converges to the unique Bures-Wasserstein barycenter of the clients' optimal Gram matrices. Even with this alignment, the limit generally differs from the centralized optimal Gram matrix. We decompose this gap into three positive-semidefinite terms arising from differences in client target means, covariance heterogeneity, and averaging the aligned heads. A correction based on a one-time exchange of target means and covariances recovers the centralized optimal Gram matrix in one round under exact local optimization and the same selection rule. We verify these results numerically in the UFM and test its predictions on five tabular and five image regression datasets using deep networks with feature regularization and long local training. In these experiments, ordinary training approaches the predicted barycenter, while a weak proximal penalty improves endpoint agreement and yields trajectories that closely follow the predicted Gram dynamics. The correction moves the final Gram matrices close to the centralized UFM prediction.

</details>


### [162] [T-ARC: Topology-Aware Randomized Clustering via Distributionally Robust Stochastic Block Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39466)
*Serena Grazia De Benedictis,Andersen Ang,Nicoletta Del Buono,Flavia Esposito,Laura Selicato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 T-ARC 聚类方法，将拓扑信息嵌入 K-means 优化目标以纠正其几何偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K-means 存在几何偏差，难以处理非凸、弯曲或交错簇结构，本文希望借助数据的潜在拓扑与连通性信息来纠正这一偏差。

Method: 通过隐图建模数据隐藏结构，在 K-means 数据保真项上加入图割惩罚；隐图用随机块模型（SBM）建模，其标量参数在分布鲁棒优化（DRO）框架下优化并得到闭式近端更新；SBM 与 DRO 均由基于零维持续同调（H0）的相似度矩阵提供先验。整体采用块坐标下降求解，并用全局 Lyapunov 函数证明收敛（确定性块单调下降，随机图更新期望下降）。

Result: 在非凸合成数据集和 Fashion-MNIST 随机子集上，T-ARC 能恢复 K-means 失败的潜在拓扑结构，在弯曲和交错簇上取得最高准确率，在真实数据上与 K-means 相比具有竞争力且明显更稳定。

Conclusion: T-ARC 通过融合持续同调拓扑先验、SBM 与 DRO，有效纠正 K-means 的几何偏差，并在非凸结构聚类中优于 K-means。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce a new clustering method, namely T-ARC (Topology-Aware Randomized Clustering), that corrects the geometric bias of K-means by embedding topological information directly into the optimization objective. Building on the assumption that the data admits an underlying hidden structure modeled via a latent graph, the idea is to uncover this information through the interplay between the standard K-means data-fidelity term and a graph-cut penalty, which discourages cluster assignments inconsistent with the connectivity structure of the data.
  To render this coupling tractable, the latent graph is modeled as a random realization from a Stochastic Block Model (SBM), whose scalar parameter is optimized within a Distributionally Robust Optimization (DRO) framework, yielding a closed-form proximal update. Both SBM and DRO are informed by a persistence-based similarity matrix derived from zero-dimensional persistent homology ($H_0$), which translates the multiscale connectivity structure of the data into a pairwise topological prior. The overall optimization proceeds via Block Coordinate Descent; convergence is established through a global Lyapunov functional: the deterministic blocks satisfy monotonic descent, while the stochastic graph update satisfies descent in expectation, so that the expected energy converges.
  Experiments on synthetic datasets with non-convex geometries and on random subsets of Fashion-MNIST show that T-ARC recovers latent topological structures where K-means fails, achieving the highest accuracy on curved and interleaved clusters while remaining competitive, and markedly more stable than K-means, on real data.

</details>


### [163] [Also Small Models Can Reasonably Self-Evaluate Their Confidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39478)
*Idil Kapikiran,Thomas Decker,Thomas Runkler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了基于自评价的不确定性量化方法在不同规模语言模型和不同知识领域问答任务上的表现，发现自评价置信度的可靠性对模型规模和领域专精度具有独立性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自评价方法让模型判断自身预测的可靠性，是识别错误、支撑资源受限部署的关键手段。然而，模型规模与领域专精度如何影响自评价置信信号的质量尚不明确，这限制了该方法在实际场景中的可靠应用。

Method: 采用多种自评价方法，让模型对自身预测进行判断，在从通用到专业的知识领域问答任务上，对不同规模的语言模型进行系统性评估，考察模型规模与领域专精度对自评价置信信号质量的影响。

Result: 准确率如预期般随模型规模减小和领域更专业化而下降，但自评价置信度的可靠性在这两个维度上基本保持稳定。这意味着能力最强的模型并不一定是自评价预测可靠性最好的模型。较小模型虽准确率较低，仍能获得合理的自评价置信度，适用于资源受限的部署场景。

Conclusion: 自评价置信度的可靠性与模型规模和领域专精度相互独立，因此规模较小的模型在资源受限场景下具备可行性。选择模型时不应简单以整体能力作为自评价可靠性的替代指标。

Abstract: This study systematically evaluates self-evaluation-based uncertainty quantification across different language models of varying sizes on question-answering tasks spanning general to specialized knowledge domains. Using various self-evaluation methods where models judge their own predictions, we examine how model scale and domain specificity affect the quality of self-assessed confidence signals. Our results reveal that while accuracy predictably declines with smaller models and more specialized domains, the reliability of self-evaluated confidence remains largely stable across both dimensions. This independence means the most capable model is not necessarily the best at self-assessing prediction reliability. These findings suggest that smaller models can achieve reasonable self-assessed confidence despite lower accuracy, making them viable for resource-constrained deployments.

</details>


### [164] [Correcting CondOT: Exact Finite-Step Sampling in Gaussian Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39488)
*Ron Levy,Michael Elad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究高斯目标下流匹配采样误差对调度方案的依赖性，证明CondOT调度可消除中点法的首要误差，并揭示可使高斯采样精确实现的噪声调度存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配通过将噪声逐步变换为数据来生成样本，但有限采样步数会引入依赖调度方案的数值误差，因此需要分析调度选择对采样误差的影响。

Method: 针对高斯目标，采用精确流场与显式中点采样法，以目标分布与中点采样器最终分布间的平方Wasserstein距离度量采样误差；在均匀网格上固定信号调度（α_t=t），并构造标量噪声调度β_t进行分析。

Result: 标准条件最优传输（CondOT）调度能消除中点法的首要误差并改进一般收敛界（即使步长非均匀也成立）；在S步均匀网格上存在噪声调度β_t以1/S速率逼近CondOT噪声调度1−t，且对每个充分大的S都能实现精确高斯采样；受控高斯实验验证了收敛速率与精确校准。

Conclusion: CondOT调度在中点采样下具有更优误差特性，且存在能保证精确高斯采样的噪声调度，为流匹配调度设计提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Flow matching generates samples by gradually transforming noise into data. In practice, using a finite number of sampling steps introduces a numerical error that depends on the chosen schedule. We study this dependence for Gaussian targets and the explicit midpoint sampling method, using the exact flow field. We measure sampling error by the squared Wasserstein distance between the target distribution and the final distribution produced by the midpoint sampler. We show that the standard conditional optimal transport (CondOT) schedule cancels the leading midpoint error and improves the general convergence bound, even when the sampling steps are unequally spaced. On a uniform grid of $S$ sampling steps, we fix the signal schedule at $α_t=t$ and prove the existence of scalar noise schedules $β_t$ that approach the CondOT noise schedule $1-t$ at rate $1/S$ and yield exact Gaussian sampling for every sufficiently large $S$. Controlled Gaussian experiments illustrate the convergence rates and exact calibration.

</details>


### [165] [Towards Robust Time Series Learning via Capacity-Centric Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39489)
*Siru Zhong,Senzhang Wang,James T. Kwok,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出以样本自适应正则化为核心的CCM原则及SACM框架，利用频谱稀疏性为每个样本分配激活路径上的dropout概率，可无改造地嵌入现有骨干网络且不改变确定性推理流程，在301组数据集-骨干组合上取得一致的性能提升且零测试开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度时间序列学习中普遍存在样本级可靠性异质性，但标准训练流程对所有样本使用统一的正则化设置，导致对损坏样本正则不足、对干净样本过度约束。现有鲁棒方法多依赖数据空间过滤或潜在表示先验，存在局限，因此需要一种互补的样本自适应正则化原则。

Method: 提出Capacity-Centric Modulation（CCM）这一样本自适应正则化原则，并在其下设计SACM（Sample-Adaptive Capacity Modulation）框架：任务无关，利用频谱稀疏性为内部激活路径分配逐样本的dropout概率；可直接集成到现有骨干网络，无需架构重设计，并保持确定性推理流程。

Result: 在涵盖9个预测、32个分类和4个异常检测数据集的301组真实数据集-骨干组合上，相对未修改的骨干网络，SACM平均降低预测MSE 6.7%，分类准确率提升3.04%，point-adjusted F1提升17.05%，且无任何测试时开销。

Conclusion: 样本自适应正则化是缓解可靠性异质性的有效补充思路；SACM以零测试开销、易集成的方式在多种时间序列任务上稳定提升性能。

Abstract: Sample-level reliability heterogeneity is common in deep time series learning. Standard training pipelines apply a uniform regularization setting to all samples, which can under-regularize corrupted samples and over-restrict clean samples. Common robustness approaches filter observations in data space or impose priors on latent representations. We propose Capacity-Centric Modulation (CCM) as a complementary, sample-adaptive regularization principle. Under this principle, we introduce SACM (Sample-Adaptive Capacity Modulation), a task-agnostic framework that exploits spectral sparsity to assign sample-wise dropout probabilities along internal activation paths. SACM integrates into existing backbones without architectural redesign and preserves the deterministic inference pipeline. Across 301 real-world dataset-backbone pairs covering 9 forecasting, 32 classification, and 4 anomaly-detection datasets, SACM reduces forecasting MSE by 6.7% on average and improves classification accuracy and point-adjusted F1 by 3.04% and 17.05%, respectively, relative to unmodified backbones, with zero test-time overhead.

</details>


### [166] [When the Right Answer Is Missing: An Arithmetic-Dependent Rejection Bottleneck in Jev](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39496)
*Jike Zhong,Ming Li,Yuxiang Lai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 类型化决策模型（如 Jev）在候选集无正确答案时存在"算术依赖的拒答瓶颈"：有答案时准确率达 99%，而正确拒答仅 7%，但通过简单决策阈值可将拒答率提升至 79%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类型化决策模型（如 Jev）因直接从预定义候选中选择，比生成式 LLM 更高效，而 TypeSafe 等方法建议加入"other/none-of-the-above"选项以实现拒答。然而作者发现这一拒答机制在算术场景下并不真正可靠，因此本文旨在揭示并刻画这一失败模式。

Method: 作者在成对的算术问题上对比模型的"答案存在"与"答案缺失"两种情形，测试其跨越数值量级、运算深度、上下文表述和拒答标签的稳健性，并扩展至时间计算、容量取整等场景；同时测试原生布尔验证的表现，最后用单独开发集上选定的简单决策阈值来缓解该问题。

Result: 成对算术问题上，答案存在时准确率达 99%，而正确拒答率仅 7%；该差距在多种量级、深度、表述与标签下持续存在，并扩展到时间计算与容量取整。原生布尔验证在同样的答案缺失算术案例中达到 99% 精确匹配准确率。基于开发集选定的简单决策阈值将算术拒答准确率从 7% 提升至 79%，同时保持 97% 的答案存在准确率。

Conclusion: 类型化决策模型的分类式拒答可能失败，即使模型本身能正确验证候选答案的正确性；这一算术依赖的拒答瓶颈可通过简单决策阈值显著缓解，且无需重训练或额外推理。

Abstract: Typed decision models such as Jev offer an efficient alternative to generative LLMs in decision-making workflows by selecting directly from predefined options. When candidate sets contain no valid answer, TypeSafe recommends including an "other" or "none-of-the-above" option to enable rejection. In this report, however, we identify an arithmetic-dependent rejection bottleneck: Jev reliably selects correct numerical answers when available but frequently accepts incorrect alternatives when they are absent despite an explicit rejection option. On paired arithmetic problems, answer-present accuracy reaches 99%, while correct rejection falls to 7%. Moreover, this gap persists across numerical magnitudes, operation depths, contextual formulations, and rejection labels, and extends to scenarios such as time calculation and capacity rounding. Yet native Boolean verification achieves 99% exact-match accuracy on the same answer-absent arithmetic cases, showing that categorical rejection can fail even when the model successfully verifies candidate correctness. Finally, we show that a simple decision threshold selected on separate development problems raises arithmetic rejection accuracy from 7% to 79% while retaining 97% answer-present accuracy, substantially mitigating the failure without retraining or additional inference.

</details>


### [167] [The Geometry of Randomized Smoothing on Feasible Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39497)
*Syed Izhan Khilji,Alireza Furutanpey,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究随机平滑在“可行性/置信度过滤”场景下认证半径的错误来源，指出直接代入条件概率比值可能认证出包含决策边界的球；进而区分几何问题与认证问题，给出凸保留集保持高斯比较、一般集需控制保留分布、联合保留-标签概率总有效、以及基于散度与有限样本界的替代认证方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机平滑中，过滤式预测器只报告被保留提案上的标签概率，形成条件比值。其分子是固定高斯事件质量，分母是随中心变化的保留概率。若直接套用普通平滑公式，可能把决策边界也纳入认证球，导致虚假或无效认证。因此需要澄清条件化何时保持高斯比较，以及如何在过滤预测器上给出严格、有效的认证。

Method: 作者将问题拆为几何与认证两部分：几何上分析条件化是否保持高斯比较，凸保留集保持完整比较，一般集合则需对中心移动时保留分布的几何控制；认证上比较条件概率路径与联合保留-标签概率路径，并用均匀协方差界将散度认证迁移到保留分布。随后给出两类方法的有限样本界，并在学习图像分类器与非凸过滤器、以及发布的置信过滤器和自适应高斯组合场景中验证。

Result: 条件概率在缺乏几何控制时不给出正统一半径，且朴素代入可认证出含决策边界的球；联合保留-标签概率对同一过滤预测器始终给出有效证书；均匀协方差界可将散度证书迁移到保留分布，即使高斯事件比较失败也可能得到更大半径。实验中，条件 Rényi 界在训练选出的非凸过滤器上比联合质量界认证更多图像且无需额外模型评估；发布置信过滤器上出现由条件代入得到的半径内已验证标签变化；自适应高斯组合可覆盖带路径能量界的历史依赖中心偏移的因果有限时程执行。

Conclusion: 过滤式预测器的认证不能简单套用条件概率比值；需要明确几何条件或改用联合质量/散度迁移认证。联合质量证书保守但总有效，条件 Rényi 在适当条件下更强。该框架统一了条件化、联合概率与自适应组合，并为非凸过滤与因果执行提供可计算认证路径。

Abstract: Randomized smoothing certifies the probability of a fixed output event as the center of Gaussian noise moves. Feasibility or confidence filtering reports label probabilities only among retained proposals, producing a ratio. Its numerator is a fixed Gaussian event mass, while its denominator is the probability of retention and can change with the center. Substituting this ratio into the ordinary smoothing formula can therefore certify a ball that contains a decision boundary. We separate the problem into a geometric question and a certification question. Geometry determines when conditioning preserves Gaussian comparisons. Convex retained sets preserve the full comparison, while general sets require geometric control of the retained law as the center moves. Without such control, conditional probabilities imply no positive universal radius. Joint retention-and-label probabilities always yield a valid certificate for the same filtered predictor. A uniform covariance bound transfers divergence certificates to the retained law and can yield larger radii even when the Gaussian event comparison fails. Both methods admit finite-sample bounds. For a learned image classifier with a training-selected nonconvex filter, conditional Rényi bounds certify more images than joint-mass bounds without additional model evaluations. A released confidence filter exhibits verified label changes inside radii obtained by conditional substitution. An application of adaptive Gaussian composition covers causal finite-horizon executions with history-dependent center shifts under a pathwise energy bound.

</details>


### [168] [Can Domain Generalization be Guaranteed in Small-Sample Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39512)
*Hong Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为域泛化（DG）场景下的结构风险最小化（SRM）建立理论保证，基于稳定性概念推导了学习一致性与泛化误差界，并讨论了其在深度学习中的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小样本学习是机器学习的根本挑战，有限训练数据导致模型估计与泛化不稳定。SRM在经典i.i.d.假设下被视为原则性解决方案，但域泛化违背了i.i.d.假设，使SRM在DG中的理论作用长期未被探索。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 基于稳定性（stability）概念，在温和假设下为DG中的SRM建立理论保证；推导学习一致性与泛化误差界，证明当假设满足稳定性条件时界变得紧致；在特定假设空间假设下建立SRM的稳定性、学习与泛化界；并讨论这些界在深度学习中的适用性。

Result: 获得了DG下SRM的首个理论保证；推导出的泛化误差界在满足稳定性条件时趋于紧致；在特定假设空间假设下成功建立了SRM的稳定性、学习和泛化界，并分析了其向深度学习的推广。

Conclusion: 本文为分布偏移下的SRM奠定了理论基础，并为小样本场景下鲁棒DG算法的设计提供了理论启示。

Abstract: The small-sample learning problem remains a fundamental challenge in machine learning because limited training data lead to unstable model estimation and generalization. Structural Risk Minimization (SRM) has long been regarded as a principled solution under the classical i.i.d. assumption. However, domain generalization (DG) violates this assumption, leaving the theoretical role of SRM in DG largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we establish the first theoretical guarantees for SRM in DG under mild assumptions. Specifically, based on the concept of stability, we derive learning consistency and generalization error bounds and prove that these bounds become tight when the hypotheses satisfy the stability condition. Building upon this, under a specific hypothesis space assumption, we establish stability, learning, and generalization bounds for SRM. We further discuss the applicability of these bounds to deep learning. This work establishes theoretical foundations for SRM under distribution shifts and sheds light on the design of robust DG algorithms in small-sample scenarios.

</details>


### [169] [CIDER-FM: Foundation Models for Causal Inference from Diverse Experimental Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39523)
*Yuche Gao,Arik Reuter,Siyuan Guo,Anish Dhir,Bernhard Schölkopf,Adrian Weller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究因果基础模型（CFM），提出 CIDER-FM，用观测数据与替代干预数据结合预测目标条件干预分布（CID），并通过干预感知表示与三维注意力实现跨变量、样本和实验机制的信息交换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠观测数据往往无法唯一确定因果模型，而对目标变量本身进行干预的实验数据又常常不可得，因此需要一种方法融合有限的观测数据与替代干预数据，以更准确地预测目标条件干预分布。

Method: 首先形式化替代实验的概念性收益；据此提出 CIDER-FM，采用干预感知表示和分层三轴注意力，在变量、样本和实验机制之间交换信息；并在多种合成图与机制族以及与 Causal Chambers 的模拟和真实数据上，与大量基线方法进行比较。

Result: 在多种合成图结构与机制族，以及 Causal Chambers 的模拟与真实数据上，CIDER-FM 展现出较强的 CID 预测性能；结果表明引入实验上下文可以优于仅使用观测数据的预测。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将观测数据与替代干预数据结合，并通过干预感知的因果基础模型进行建模，能够有效提升目标条件干预分布的预测效果。

Abstract: Causal foundation models (CFMs) amortise causal inference over priors of synthetic structural causal models (SCMs), predicting the effect of an experiment on a specific variable. However, observational data alone may leave multiple causal models compatible with available evidence, while experimental data with interventions on exactly the variable of interest might be unavailable. This work studies CFMs as a method to combine finite observational and surrogate-interventional datasets in order to predict a target conditional interventional distribution (CID) more accurately than with observational data alone. We first formalise the conceptual benefits of surrogate experiments. Building on this analysis, we introduce \textsc{Foundation Models for Causal Inference from Diverse Experimental Regimes} (\emph{CIDER-FM}), a causal foundation model that uses an intervention-aware representation and hierarchical three-axis attention to exchange information across variables, samples, and experimental regimes. We evaluate CIDER-FM against a wide range of baselines across diverse synthetic graph and mechanism families, as well as on both simulated and real-world data from Causal Chambers. Our results demonstrate strong CID prediction performance and show that incorporating experimental context can improve predictions over observational data alone.

</details>


### [170] [Learning Reliable GUI Agents under Imperfect Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39547)
*Bo Han,Qianyi Wang,Shuai Liu,Xiong Zifan,Changqiao Wu,Yuanfa Li,Pengzhi Gao,Wei Liu,Jian Luan,Heng Qu,Yunpeng Song,Zhongmin Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种将知识获取与噪声鲁棒利用相结合的可检索增强执行框架，使GUI智能体能在不完美先验下正确行动，并在真机与在线模拟器基准上取得跨数据集可迁移的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言与视觉语言模型的GUI智能体在未见应用和复杂多步任务上表现不佳，因为完成真实GUI任务依赖应用专属且随时间易变的操作知识，而这类知识在预训练语料中稀缺。检索增强执行是自然解法，但存在两个耦合瓶颈：大规模知识难以获取，且自采集先验会因版本更新、促销、广告、A/B测试与个性化而与实时环境漂移。

Method: 论文主张GUI智能体不应追求完美知识，而应学会在不完美先验下正确行动，并据此提出一个耦合知识获取与噪声鲁棒利用的框架：一方面采用结构化探索策略遍历交互元素、构建UI状态转移图，并借助VLM在无人工标注下合成（任务，轨迹）对；另一方面提出噪声感知训练策略，基于真实GUI漂移模式分类学，向自探索轨迹注入五类真实错误，使智能体在行动前评估先验可靠性。

Result: 在物理设备与在线模拟器基准上的实验表明，该方法发现更多独特屏幕、覆盖更多基准任务，并能更有效地拒绝错误先验同时利用正确先验，其准确率增益可跨数据集迁移。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，与其追求完美先验知识，不如让GUI智能体学会在存在漂移与噪声的先验条件下稳健决策；结合结构化自探索与噪声感知训练，可显著提升知识覆盖与任务准确率，且增益具备跨数据集泛化性。

Abstract: GUI agents built on large language and vision-language models still struggle on unseen applications and complex multi-step tasks, as completing real GUI tasks depends on app-specific, temporally volatile operational knowledge that is scarce in pretraining corpora. Retrieval-augmented execution offers a natural remedy but faces two coupled bottlenecks: knowledge at scale is hard to acquire, and self-collected priors inevitably drift from the live environment due to version updates, promotions, ads, A/B tests, and personalization. We therefore argue that GUI agents should not pursue perfect knowledge but learn to act correctly under imperfect priors, and propose our framework that couples knowledge acquisition with noise-robust utilization: a structured exploration strategy traverses interactive elements, builds a UI state-transition graph, and synthesizes (task, trajectory) pairs via a VLM without human annotation; a noise-aware training strategy, grounded in a taxonomy of real GUI drift patterns, injects five types of realistic errors into self-explored trajectories to teach the agent to assess prior reliability before acting. Experiments on physical devices and online emulator benchmarks show that our method discovers more unique screens, covers more benchmark tasks, and more effectively rejects erroneous priors while leveraging correct ones, with accuracy gains that transfer across datasets.

</details>


### [171] [Hyperbolic Prototype Routing for Rehearsal-Free Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39550)
*HongWei Zhao,Rui Liu,Yong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HyPro框架，用双曲原型路由解决类增量学习中的灾难性遗忘与模块匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类增量学习（CIL）需在学习新类的同时保留旧知识。基于预训练模型的高效参数微调虽能减少参数更新，但现有方法仍面临两类问题：累积干扰导致的灾难性遗忘，以及推理阶段模块与样本匹配不佳。因此需要一个无需回放、能实现任务级可靠区分的方法。

Method: HyPro为每个增量任务分配专用的LoRA-Expert模块，实现隔离的表示学习；随后将路由特征投影到Poincaré球（双曲空间）上，通过测地最近原型匹配进行任务级判别。

Result: 在标准CIL和Few-Shot CIL基准上的大量实验表明，HyPro在平均准确率和最终阶段准确率上均持续优于强基线。

Conclusion: HyPro是一种无需回放的持续学习框架，通过任务专属LoRA-Expert与双曲原型路由相结合，有效缓解遗忘并提升任务判别能力，在多个基准上取得稳定提升。

Abstract: Class-Incremental Learning (CIL) aims to continually learn new classes while preserving prior knowledge. Parameter-efficient fine-tuning with pre-trained models enables CIL with minimal parameter updates, but existing approaches still suffer from catastrophic forgetting caused by cumulative interference and suboptimal module-sample matching at inference. We propose Hyperbolic Prototype Routing (HyPro), a rehearsal-free framework for continual learning. HyPro allocates a dedicated LoRA-Expert module to each incremental task for isolated representation learning, then projects routing features onto a Poincare ball and performs geodesic nearest-prototype matching for reliable task-level discrimination. Extensive experiments on standard CIL and Few-Shot CIL benchmarks show that HyPro consistently improves average and final-stage accuracy over strong baselines.

</details>


### [172] [Self-Repulsive Sampling for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39560)
*Michael Helcig,Martin Jaggi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Self-Repulsion (SR)采样器，通过让同伴承诺惩罚相同token的logit来增加掩码扩散语言模型输出的多样性，无需额外训练即可提升多数投票准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多次采样并投票可提升语言模型准确率，但重复答案限制了额外采样的收益；提高温度虽增加多样性却可能牺牲单样本准确率，因此需要一种在不损害准确率的前提下增强输出多样性的采样方法。

Method: SR用于掩码扩散语言模型：在每个受惩罚的去噪步骤中，每条路径依据同伴在同一位置已承诺该token的数量来降低其logit；各路径共享一次批量前向传播后按序承诺，使后续路径能观察到同一步中较早路径的选择。该耦合无需训练或额外前向/反向传播，温度为零时也能产生不同路径；当所有路径同时承诺同一位置时，其更新恰好最大化总logit减去凸重复代价。

Result: 在LLaDA-8B-Instruct上，采用10条路径和128个去噪步骤，确定性SR在GSM8K上达到80.38%的多数投票准确率，优于无惩罚贪心解码器的70.17%；在温度0.6且评估预算匹配时，计数惩罚在32块上比自一致性提高2.06个百分点，在纯扩散下提高14.50。GSM8K、MATH和TruthfulQA实验表明，投票增益主要来自正确答案覆盖率的提高，且增益因基准与解码机制而异。

Conclusion: SR是一种无需额外训练成本、能有效提升扩散语言模型采样多样性与投票准确率的采样方法，其增益主要源于正确答案覆盖率的提高。

Abstract: Sampling several responses and voting over their answers can improve a language model's accuracy, but repeated answers limit the benefit of additional samples. Raising temperature increases diversity at a potential cost to per-sample accuracy. We introduce Self-Repulsion (SR), a sampler for masked diffusion language models that uses peer commitments to diversify the pool. At each penalized denoising step, each path lowers a token's logit according to how many peers have committed that token at the same position. Paths share a batched forward pass and then commit in sequence, so later paths observe choices made earlier in the same step. This coupling requires no training or additional forward or backward pass and can produce distinct paths even at temperature zero. When all paths commit a position together from identical logits, the update exactly maximizes total logit minus a convex duplication cost. On LLaDA-8B-Instruct with ten paths and 128 denoising steps, deterministic SR reaches 80.38% plurality accuracy on GSM8K, compared with 70.17% for the unpenalized greedy decoder. At temperature 0.6 and matched model-evaluation budgets, the count penalty improves over self-consistency by 2.06 percentage points in blocks of 32 and 14.50 under pure diffusion. Experiments on GSM8K, MATH and TruthfulQA show that voting gains arise mainly from higher coverage of correct answers, with gains that vary by benchmark and decoding regime.

</details>


### [173] [Candidate Retention for Abductive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39561)
*Hao-Yuan He,Yu Liu,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对溯因学习（ABL）中同一符号目标存在多个有效解释、可能导致冲突标签的问题，提出通过保留候选子集来平衡监督的锐度与模型质量覆盖，并给出协调级监督误差上界，进而提出 Abductive Candidate Retention (ACR) 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在溯因学习框架中，神经网络感知与符号推理相结合，溯因生成的解释用于监督感知模型。然而，同一符号目标的多个有效解释会对相同输入赋予冲突标签。常见策略要么选择单一候选作为伪标签，可能强化错误分配；要么对所有候选项加权，可能将监督分散到相互竞争的标签上。这种风险促使研究者选择保留一个候选子集，以平衡监督锐度与模型质量覆盖。

Method: 论文首先利用保留不确定性、被丢弃的模型质量和模型不匹配对协调级监督误差进行界定。对于固定模型和训练对，仅前两项依赖于保留集合。基于此，提出 Abductive Candidate Retention (ACR) 方法，使用这两项指导贪婪添加：当某候选恢复的质量超过保留不确定性的增量时，接受该候选。

Result: 实验表明，在大多数评估的聚合 mod-addition 设置中，ACR 相比单候选基线和 A3BL 提升了概念准确率。目标消融实验支持不确定性与后验质量的联合使用。

Conclusion: 通过保留候选子集并利用监督误差上界指导候选选择，ACR 能在单候选策略与全加权策略之间取得更好平衡，从而提升溯因学习中的概念准确率，且不确定性项与后验质量项的结合是有效的。

Abstract: Abductive learning combines neural perception with symbolic reasoning, using explanations generated by abduction to supervise the perception model. Multiple valid explanations of the same symbolic target can assign conflicting labels to the same inputs. Common policies select a single candidate as a pseudo-label, which may reinforce mistaken assignments, or weight all candidates, which may spread supervision across competing labels. These risks motivate selecting a retained subset to balance supervision sharpness and model-mass coverage. To guide this choice, we bound the coordinate-level supervision error using retained uncertainty, discarded model mass, and model mismatch. For a fixed model and training pair, only the first two terms depend on the retained set. We propose Abductive Candidate Retention (ACR), which uses these terms to guide greedy additions, accepting a candidate when its recovered mass exceeds the increase in retained uncertainty. Experiments show that ACR improves concept accuracy over single-candidate baselines and A3BL in most evaluated aggregated mod-addition settings. Objective ablations support the joint use of uncertainty and posterior mass.

</details>


### [174] [Robust Transfer Learning for Paper ECG Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39581)
*Yinghao Xie,Zhenbang Dai,Haojun Wang,Jinyu Cai,Fabio Bonassi,Hongwu Chen,Johan Sundström,Jiawei Li,Antônio H. Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RobECG-CL，一种排序感知对比学习框架，用于纸质ECG图像的鲁棒表征学习，在严重退化和少样本场景下优于对比学习基线及波形基础模型ECG-FM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的纸质ECG图像在版式、物理伪影和标签可得性方面差异很大，使ECG识别任务面临挑战，亟需能应对这些变化的鲁棒表征学习方法。

Method: 从标准12导联ECG记录出发，构建具有异构版式的、逐步退化的纸质ECG视图，训练模型在同记录不变性与退化感知排序之间取得平衡，即排序感知对比学习（rank-aware contrastive learning）。

Result: 在CODE-II和EchoNext的合成压力测试中，RobECG-CL在严重退化及少样本迁移下鲁棒性提升，优于对比学习基线，并在1%标注设置下超过波形基础模型ECG-FM；在医院312个样本、37个标签数据上取得最佳macro AUROC。

Conclusion: RobECG-CL通过退化感知的排序对比学习有效提升纸质ECG表征的鲁棒性，在少样本和真实医院数据上均表现优异，为纸质ECG识别提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Paper ECG recognition is challenging because real-world ECG images vary in layout, physical artifacts, and label availability. We introduce RobECG-CL, a rank-aware contrastive learning framework for robust paper ECG representation learning. Starting from standard 12-lead ECG recordings, we construct progressively degraded paper ECG views with heterogeneous layouts and train the model to balance same-recording invariance with degradation-aware ordering. Across synthetic stress tests on CODE-II and EchoNext, RobECG-CL improves robustness under severe degradation and few-shot transfer, outperforming contrastive learning baselines and surpassing the waveform-based foundation model, ECG-FM, in the 1% labeled setting. On 312 samples of hospital data with 37 labels, RobECG-CL achieves the best macro AUROC.

</details>


### [175] [Convergence of Practical Muon with Finite Newton-Schulz Iterations and Nesterov Momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39595)
*Hanyng Peng,Hui Wang,Yue Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在耦合非凸目标上联合分析Practical Muon的分层有限步更新（含Nesterov修正），在梯度相关光滑性与条件无偏随机梯度假设下，得到期望平均Frobenius梯度范数的O(T^{-1/4})收敛界，且常数不含显式矩阵维度或秩因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Practical Muon对每个参数矩阵分别做少量固定次数的Newton–Schulz迭代并保持动量，现有分析多将其替换为精确极分解或全局正交化，无法刻画真实分层有限步算法的耦合行为，因此需要直接联合分析这些分层更新。

Method: 在梯度相关的(L0,L1,q)-光滑性与条件无偏、分层有界方差的随机梯度下，直接对带Nesterov递推的分层有限步更新进行联合分析；通过下降不等式，并将动量跟踪误差分解为初始化、噪声与漂移三部分来证明收敛。对原始五步五次标量映射解析验证所需界，也允许满足同样界的步依赖系数。

Result: 在固定块数与问题常数时，得到期望平均Frobenius梯度范数的O(T^{-1/4})上界，常数不含显式矩阵维度或秩因子；分析不要求随机梯度有界、噪声对称或非零输出奇异值的统一正下界。补充的核范数结果在更强谱条件下量化了秩依赖；消失速率采用耦合的学习率与动量调度（含标准单系数Nesterov规则）。

Conclusion: 该工作为实用的分层Muon更新提供了直接的非凸收敛保证，避免了理想化极分解假设，并在温和噪声条件下保持维度与秩无关的常数；其框架还可容纳步依赖系数与核范数变体。

Abstract: Practical Muon maintains momentum and performs a small, fixed number of Newton--Schulz iterations separately for each parameter matrix, often with a Nesterov correction. We analyze these layer-wise finite-step updates jointly on a coupled nonconvex objective, rather than replacing them by exact polar factors or one global orthogonalization. Under gradient-dependent $(\mathcal L_0,\mathcal L_1,q)$-smoothness and conditionally unbiased stochastic gradients with bounded layer-wise variance, we establish an $\mathcal O(T^{-1/4})$ bound on the expected average Frobenius gradient norm. The analysis retains the Nesterov recursion and requires neither bounded stochastic gradients, symmetric noise, nor a uniform positive lower bound on the nonzero output singular values. Its constants contain no explicit matrix-dimension or rank factors when the number of blocks and problem constants are fixed. The proof follows a descent inequality and a decomposition of the momentum tracking error into initialization, noise, and drift. For the original five-step quintic, we verify the required scalar-map bounds analytically; the result also allows step-dependent coefficients satisfying the same bounds. A complementary nuclear-norm result quantifies rank dependence under a stronger spectral condition. The vanishing rate uses coupled learning-rate and momentum schedules, including the standard single-coefficient Nesterov rule.

</details>


### [176] [Hybrid Methods for Robust Tabular Data Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39613)
*Jinwei Li,Michelle Bruch,Daniel Tenbrinck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两种混合插补方法 NuclearForest 和 SoftForest，用低秩初始化结合单步随机森林精化，在保持高精度的同时大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺失数据是统计分析与机器学习中的基础性难题，不同插补方法会显著影响下游推断。现有迭代式方法（如 MissForest）精度较高但计算代价大，需要一种兼顾精度与效率的方案。

Method: 将基于核范数的低秩初始化（分别采用奇异值阈值 SVT 与 SoftImpute）与非迭代的随机森林精化相结合。对 SVT 分量引入自适应步长规则，证明自适应步长界，并建立相应零初始化迭代的收敛性。低秩初值捕捉全局协方差结构，随机森林步骤恢复局部依赖的非线性残差信号，且支持混合类型变量。

Result: 在多个应用领域的多样数据集上，针对 MCAR、MAR、MNAR 三种机制及不同缺失率，与七种既有插补方法对比。NuclearForest 和 SoftForest 的插补保真度达到或超过 MissForest 等先进迭代方法，同时计算成本显著降低，相对 MissForest 分别提速约 5.81 倍和 9.52 倍。

Conclusion: 该方法有效利用真实表格数据的低秩结构并兼容混合类型变量，为生物信息学、经济学等领域的数据插补提供了高效稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Missing data are a fundamental challenge in statistical analysis and machine learning, as the choice of imputation method substantially impacts downstream inference. In this work, we propose two hybrid imputation methods called NuclearForest and SoftForest, which combine nuclear-norm-based low-rank initialization using Singular Value Thresholding (SVT) and SoftImpute, respectively, with a non-iterative Random Forest refinement. For the SVT-based component, we further introduce an adaptive step-size rule, prove adaptive step-size bounds, and establish convergence for the corresponding zero-initialized iteration. The low-rank initialization provides a structured warm start that captures the global covariance patterns in the data, while the subsequent Random Forest step recovers residual nonlinear signals encoding local dependencies.
