We conduct an extensive benchmark on diverse datasets from different application domains, comparing the proposed methods with seven established imputation methods under the Missing Completely at Random (MCAR), Missing at Random (MAR), and Missing Not at Random (MNAR) mechanisms across varying missingness rates. Our results demonstrate that NuclearForest and SoftForest match or exceed the imputation fidelity of state-of-the-art iterative methods such as MissForest, while significantly reducing computational cost. In particular, they achieve speedups of approximately 5.81 times and 9.52 times over MissForest by replacing iterative cycles with a single refinement step. Our approach effectively exploits the low-rank structure of real-world tabular data and accommodates mixed-type variables, providing an efficient and robust solution for data imputation in bioinformatics, economics, and beyond.

</details>


### [177] [Parameterization method of reservoir properties for ensemble-based data assimilation using intermediate latent space of StyleGAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39626)
*Marcio A. Sampaio,Paulo H. Ranazzi,Martin J. Blunt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比了 VAE-GAN、Latent Diffusion 和 StyleGAN2 等深度学习参数化模型在集合平滑历史拟合中的应用，并创新性地采用 StyleGAN2 的中间 w 空间进行数据同化，结果表明 w 空间优于传统 z 空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集合平滑方法依赖高斯假设，当先验地质为复杂相分布（非高斯）时性能严重下降，因此需要高效参数化技术；而现有深度学习模型众多，尚未明确哪种最适合与集合方法结合使用。

Method: 基于文献综述筛选出最具潜力的 VAE-GAN、Latent Diffusion 和 StyleGAN2 模型；创新地对第二代 StyleGAN（StyleGAN2）分别使用潜在 z 空间和中间 w 空间进行数据同化，并在两个二维案例（三分类相和连续型）中开展研究。

Result: 三种模型均高效，其中 StyleGAN2 在生成地质真实感样本和实现优良数据匹配方面表现突出；使用中间 w 空间进行数据同化的效果优于传统的 z 空间，因为 ESMDA 采用线性更新，而 w 空间比高度纠缠的 z 空间更线性、更解耦，能保证更新向量接近真实地质模式。

Conclusion: StyleGAN2 结合中间 w 空间是历史拟合中更优的数据同化方案，相关结果已通过主要地质统计学和历史拟合指标验证。

Abstract: Ensemble smoothers are the most successful and efficient techniques currently available for history matching. However, because these methods rely on Gaussian assumptions, their performance is severely degraded when the prior geology is described in terms of complex facies distributions (non-Gaussian). In this way, for these methods, we need to apply efficient parameterization techniques. Currently, the most efficient methods for performing parameterization are deep learning models. However, given the variety of existing deep learning models, studies have not identified which is most suitable for use with ensemble-based methods, although some important models had already been evaluated. Based on a recent literature review, the most promising models selected were VAE-GAN, Latent Diffusion, and StyleGAN models. As a novel aspect of this work, data assimilation with the second generation of StyleGAN (StyleGAN2) model was performed using the latent z-space and intermediate w-space, separately. They were applied in two 2D case studies: one categorical (three facies) and the other continuous. The results demonstrated that all three models are highly efficient, with the StyleGAN2 model standing out for generating samples with geological realism and achieving excellent data matching in the cases studied. Our findings show that performing data assimilation with StyleGAN2 using the intermediate space (w-space) yielded better results than the traditional application in the latent space (z-space). This is due to the fact that ESMDA uses linear updates and the w-space is much more linear and disentangled than the highly entangled z-space, thereby ensuring that the updated vectors remain close to realistic geological patterns. These results were validated using main geostatistical and history matching metrics.

</details>


### [178] [MIND: Marginal-Invariant Neural Dependency Diffusion for Mixed-Type Tabular Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39628)
*Pengfei Li,Mohammad Khalil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MIND是一种边际不变神经依赖扩散模型，通过分离边际建模与依赖学习，在混合类型表格数据生成中实现边际保真度、联合依赖保持与下游任务效用的稳定平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合类型表格数据同时包含连续、离散等多种变量类型，直接在原始异质特征空间中学习联合分布面临困难：边际分布与跨列依赖交织在一起，难以分别准确刻画。现有统一方法往往难以在边际保真度与依赖保持之间取得稳定平衡。

Method: MIND不在原始异质特征空间直接学习联合分布。首先通过逐列边际传输将不同变量类型映射到统一的潜在依赖空间；随后用条件扩散模型学习跨列关系，并采用Copula切线去噪将已知边际成分与可学习的依赖残差分离；采样阶段引入秩投影以缓解逆向扩散中的边际偏移。

Result: 在九个多样化表格基准上，MIND在边际保真度和依赖保持方面持续优于现有统一方法，并在边际保真度、联合依赖保持与下游预测效用之间取得较强且稳定的平衡。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将边际建模与依赖学习显式分离，是复杂混合类型表格生成的一条原理性强且高效的范式。

Abstract: This paper proposes MIND, a marginal-invariant neural dependency diffusion model for mixed-type tabular data. MIND does not directly learn the joint distribution in the original heterogeneous feature space. Instead, it first maps different variable types into a unified latent dependency space via column-wise marginal transport. A conditional diffusion model then learns cross-column relationships. Copula-tangent denoising separates known marginal components from learnable dependency residuals. Rank projection during the sampling phase further mitigates marginal shift in reverse diffusion. Experiments across nine diverse tabular benchmarks show that MIND consistently improves marginal fidelity and dependency preservation over existing unified approaches. By explicitly isolating marginal modelling from dependency learning, MIND achieves a strong and stable balance among marginal fidelity, joint dependency preservation, and downstream prediction utility. This work supports separating marginal and dependency modelling as a principled and highly effective paradigm for complex mixed-type tabular generation.

</details>


### [179] [Certification-Based Differentially Private Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39629)
*Mihnea Ghitu,Matthew Wicker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将形式化方法（抽象解释）从分类场景的隐私预测推广到连续无界回归，并提出 Abstract Gradient Sampling 算法为隐私学习提供基于可达性分析的更紧致隐私保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私机器学习通常通过向模型参数或模型输出加噪实现，而近期基于抽象解释的形式化方法虽能提供更紧致的隐私保证，但仅限于分类场景下的隐私预测。作者希望将形式化方法发展为更通用的紧致隐私分析工具。

Method: 1）将抽象梯度训练（AGT）框架推广至连续、无界回归的隐私预测；2）通过将参数化模型的学习归约为参数空间上的回归问题，提出 Abstract Gradient Sampling（AGS）算法，使基于可达性的分析能够为隐私学习提供保证；3）为 AGT 框架提供更紧致的隐私核算，并理论分析平滑敏感度上界能带来有利隐私-效用权衡的条件。

Result: 在回归基准上，所提回归边界比全局敏感度基线更紧致；在全局预测敏感度先验无界的场景下，首次给出有限隐私保证；在匹配条件下，提出的隐私学习算法可优于标准隐私学习器。

Conclusion: 抽象解释类形式化方法可作为通用工具，同时改进隐私预测与隐私学习的隐私分析，并在回归任务中实现更紧致的保证与更优的隐私-效用权衡。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) in machine learning is typically achieved by adding noise to model parameters (private learning) or to model outputs (private prediction). Recent work uses formal methods, namely abstract interpretation, to provide tighter privacy guarantees, but only for private prediction in classification settings. In this work, we investigate the use of formal methods as a general tool for tighter privacy analysis. First, we generalize the abstract gradient training (AGT) framework to private prediction in continuous, unbounded regression. Second, by reducing learning in parameterized models to a regression problem over the parameter space, we introduce Abstract Gradient Sampling (AGS), an algorithm that enables reachability-based analysis to provide guarantees for private learning. In both private prediction and private learning, we provide tightened privacy accounting for the AGT framework and a theoretical analysis demonstrating when our smooth sensitivity upper-bounds yield favourable privacy-utility trade-off. In practice, we validate that our regression bounds are tighter than global-sensitivity baselines on regression benchmarks, and, notably, yield the first finite privacy guarantees in settings where global prediction sensitivity is a priori unbounded. We also find that under matched conditions, our private learning algorithm can outperform standard private learners.

</details>


### [180] [PEG-Tab: Sampling-Time Record Repair and Release Control for Tabular Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39630)
*Pengfei Li,QinYi Liu,Mohammad Khalil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PEG-Tab 是一种针对冻结表格生成器的后训练修复与发布控制框架，通过生成器原生算子产生候选并校准打分，降低近复制率至 0.027，并将精确复制降至零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练表格生成器即便聚合效用很高，仍可能复现训练记录。当无法重新训练或成本过高时，采样与发布成为仅剩的干预点，因此需要一种后训练阶段的风险控制方法。

Method: 为每个生成行，使用生成器原生算子创建两个替代候选；通过共享的校准分数比较三个候选，偏向低风险记录，并执行最终发布检查。该方法在 GReaT、CTGAN、TVAE、TabDDPM 上实例化，且不更新其参数。

Result: 在五个数据集和四类生成器上，PEG-Tab 将平均 Near Copy 从 0.078 降至 0.027，并将聚合 Exact Copy 降为零。相较于 3 倍事后过滤，在 16 个迁移设置中有 12 个保持更高效用，并在 8 个设置中帕累托支配该过滤器。收益集中在复制与邻近相关风险上。

Conclusion: PEG-Tab 能作为冻结表格生成器的通用后训练修复与发布控制方案，在不重训的前提下有效降低复制与邻近风险，同时较好保留效用。

Abstract: Pretrained tabular generators can reproduce training records even when aggregate utility remains high. When retraining is unavailable or too costly, sampling and release are the remaining intervention points. We present PEG-Tab (Post-Training Energy Guidance for Tabular Synthesis), a post-training repair and release-control framework for frozen tabular generators. For each generated row, a generator-native operator creates two alternatives. A shared calibrated score compares the three candidates, favours lower-risk records, and applies a final release check. We instantiate this interface for GReaT, CTGAN, TVAE, and TabDDPM without updating their parameters. Across five datasets and four generator families, PEG-Tab reduces mean Near Copy from $0.078$ to $0.027$ and lowers aggregate Exact Copy to zero. Relative to a $3\times$ post hoc filter, it retains higher utility in 12 of 16 transfer settings and Pareto-dominates the filter in eight. Gains are concentrated in copy and proximity-related risks.

</details>


### [181] [Towards Better Exploration in Sequential Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39632)
*Joseph Rance,Fabio Pizzati,Juil Sock,Woody Bayliss,Marc Górriz Blanch,Philip Torr,Adel Bibi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 序列式测试时扩展常因过早陷入“吸引子”而停止改进，模型混合干预可帮助逃离吸引子，扩大解覆盖并提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展可提升语言模型推理，但并行与序列两类方法在长时间尺度上往往难以持续改进：并行方法在模型单次难以解决的问题上扩展性差，序列方法虽能基于先前答案探索新想法，但此前未被证明能超越并行扩展的解。

Method: 首先分析序列式扩展为何停止改进，提出“吸引子”概念，即在进入某组答案后阻碍探索不同答案；随后提出一种简单的模型混合（model-mixing）干预以帮助逃离吸引子。

Result: 在27种扩展方法、模型与基准组合中，53.8%的序列式扩展轨迹在四次迭代内进入吸引子；模型混合干预将吸引子命中率平均降低21.2个百分点，解覆盖超越算力匹配的并行基线，并使递归自聚合准确率提升至少2.2个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持将长时程测试时扩展的重心从并行方法转向能够改进先前答案的序列式方法。

Abstract: Test-time scaling improves language model reasoning by spending additional compute at inference. However, both classes of existing methods often fail to continue improving over long timescales. Parallel methods repeatedly sample independent answers from the model, scaling poorly on problems the model is unlikely to solve in a single attempt. In contrast, sequential methods build on previous answers to access new ideas, yet so far have not been shown to reach answers beyond those found by parallel scaling. First, we show that sequential scaling often stops improving because it becomes prematurely trapped in an attractor: a set of answers that prevents exploration of different answers once entered. Across 27 combinations of scaling methods, models, and benchmarks, we find that 53.8% of sequential scaling trajectories enter an attractor within four iterations. Second, we show that a simple model-mixing intervention helps escape attractors. This reduces the attractor hit rate by 21.2 percentage points on average, expands solution coverage beyond a compute-matched parallel baseline, and improves accuracy of recursive self-aggregation by at least 2.2 percentage points. Our results motivate refocusing long-horizon test-time scaling from parallel methods to sequential methods that improve previous answers.

</details>


### [182] [Free Everywhere, Exact on Trees: PPO's Dropped Correction Buys Sample Efficiency Under Aggressive Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39634)
*Nima H. Siboni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在history-injective dynamics下，被丢弃的状态访问比率可精确等于每步策略比率的乘积，从而用PPO已计算的log-prob恢复精确修正，并推广为一个含PPO与全修正两端的单参数偏置–方差调节族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TRPO、PPO、GRPO等策略改进方法在行为策略的状态访问分布下估计改进，而非在改进策略自身的分布下。这种替换虽使目标可从行为策略轨迹估计，但引入了随策略偏离而增大的偏差，因此需要信赖域或裁剪，也因此无法在远离on-policy的批次上复用数据。作者希望消除这一偏差，实现更激进的样本复用。

Method: 作者在history-injective dynamics（每个状态只由唯一历史到达）下证明：被丢弃的状态访问比率等于沿采样前缀的每步策略比率乘积，且对每条轨迹逐点成立而非仅期望成立。因此可从PPO已计算的log-prob精确恢复该比率。由于精确修正带来随horizon增长的重要性采样方差，作者将其推广为单参数家族，PPO（α=0）与全修正（α=1）为两端，形成一个偏置–方差旋钮。并对未裁剪surrogate做梯度级分析，识别修正作用的两个通道与三个有效条件，并用可枚举测试台验证。

Result: 可枚举测试台确认了三个条件的预测。在硬信用分配调度任务上，短修正warmup配合激进早期样本复用比PPO以及未修正的同复用学得更快；边际增益随任务难度增长（学习曲线AUC提升+0.02至+0.09），且相对PPO的早期优势与复用带来的前缀偏差相关。而全程施加修正、或在裁剪已含复用偏差处施加修正，则无效甚至有害。

Conclusion: 历史单射条件下的精确状态访问比率修正可消除策略改进估计中的偏差，为离策略样本复用提供理论依据；通过单参数族在偏置与方差之间权衡，短时修正warmup加早期复用能带来实际收益，但修正的使用时机与位置至关重要。

Abstract: Common policy improvement methods, including TRPO, PPO, and GRPO, estimate policy improvement under the behavioral policy's state-visitation distribution rather than the improved policy's own. The substitution makes the objective estimable from the behavioral policy's rollouts but adds a bias growing with policy divergence, hence the trust region or clip, and hence no reuse of a batch far off-policy. We show that under history-injective dynamics, where each state is reached by exactly one history, the dropped state-visitation ratio equals the product of per-step policy ratios along the sampled prefix, on every trajectory and not only in expectation. The ratio is therefore restored exactly, from log-probabilities PPO already computes. Autoregressive generation and canonical-order constructive optimization are both history-injective. The exact correction pays importance-sampling variance that grows with the horizon, so we generalize it to a one-parameter family with PPO ($α{=}0$) and the full correction ($α{=}1$) as endpoints: a single bias--variance knob. A gradient-level analysis of the unclipped surrogate identifies two channels the correction acts through and three conditions under which it carries signal; an enumerable testbed confirms the conditions' predictions. On hard credit-assignment scheduling tasks, a short corrected warmup with aggressive early sample reuse learns faster than PPO and than the same reuse uncorrected; the marginal gain grows with task difficulty ($+0.02$ to $+0.09$ learning-curve AUC), and the early win over PPO tracks the prefix bias that reuse incurs. A correction held throughout, or applied where clipping already contains the reuse bias, is null to harmful.

</details>


### [183] [RiboUnmix: Learning Shared Translational Dynamics from Biased and Noisy Ribo-seq Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39644)
*Gabriele Martino,Denis Skibinski,Ivo L. Hofacker,Sebastian Tschiatschek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RiboUnmix，一个概率性多数据集框架，通过"共享序列依赖信号×数据集特异乘性因子"的分解方式，从多个Ribo-seq实验中分离出可复现的核糖体占据模式与实验技术噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ribo-seq测量的核糖体占据谱既含真实生物学信号，也含实验特异性的畸变和随机变异。因此，仅能准确预测测量谱的模型可能只是在复现技术效应而非还原生物学本质。作者希望验证：联合建模不同实验条件下的数据集，能否揭示共享的、序列依赖的核糖体占据模式。

Method: 提出RiboUnmix概率多数据集框架：将每个期望测量谱表示为共享的序列依赖信号与数据集特异乘性因子的乘积；用负二项观测模型刻画重复样本间的变异。在受控合成基准上评估，该基准同时整合了程序化翻译动力学、核糖体交通、随机计数采样和序列依赖的实验畸变，且因底层动力学与畸变已知，可分别评估共享谱与数据集特异效应的还原情况。

Result: 在合成基准中，推断出的两个成分均与其目标强相关，证明RiboUnmix能解耦共享动力学模式与实验效应。在四个物种特异的真实数据基准上，RiboUnmix在预测测量谱方面优于序列到谱的基线模型。在114个HEK来源数据集子集上独立训练的模型，对留出转录本恢复出一致的共享谱；改变训练数据集数量和组成的实验表明所学表示保持稳定。

Conclusion: RiboUnmix将实验间变异转化为可复现的序列依赖核糖体占据模式的证据，支持从多样化Ribo-seq数据集中进行生物学假设生成。

Abstract: Ribosome profiling (Ribo-seq) measures ribosome distributions along mRNAs, but observed occupancy profiles also contain experiment-specific distortions and stochastic variability. Consequently, models that accurately predict measured profiles may reproduce technical effects rather than recover the underlying biology. We ask whether jointly modeling datasets collected under different experimental conditions can reveal shared, sequence-dependent patterns of ribosome occupancy. We introduce RiboUnmix, a probabilistic multi-dataset framework in which each expected measured profile is represented as a shared sequence-dependent signal modulated by a dataset-specific multiplicative factor. A negative-binomial observation model captures variability across replicates. We evaluate RiboUnmix on a controlled synthetic benchmark combining programmed translation kinetics, ribosome traffic, stochastic count sampling, and sequence-dependent experimental distortions. Because the underlying kinetics and distortions are known, recovery of the shared profile and dataset-specific effects can be assessed separately. Both inferred components correlate strongly with their targets, demonstrating that RiboUnmix can disentangle shared kinetic patterns from experimental effects. Across four organism-specific real-data benchmarks, RiboUnmix outperforms sequence-to-profile baselines in predicting measured profiles. Models trained independently on subsets of 114 HEK-derived datasets recover concordant shared profiles for held-out transcripts, and experiments varying the number and composition of training datasets show that the learned representation remains stable. RiboUnmix thus converts variation across experiments into evidence for reproducible sequence-dependent patterns of ribosome occupancy, supporting biological hypothesis generation from diverse Ribo-seq datasets.

</details>


### [184] [Beyond Uniform Compression: Budgeted Transmission Allocation for Extreme Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39646)
*Pengfei Li,Mohammad Khalil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LBAT将联邦学习上行通信视为资源分配问题，分层动态评估各层传输价值，并用动态规划分配秩与比特预算，在极端带宽下优于均匀压缩基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中客户端上传高维模型更新造成严重通信瓶颈；现有方法对所有层统一压缩，忽略了不同参数块的异质价值，导致在敏感度低的层上浪费有限带宽。

Method: 提出分层预算自适应传输（LBAT）。将极端上行预算下的联邦通信重构为资源分配问题：利用本地训练信号动态估计各层的传输价值，再通过精确字节动态规划分配器，在严格预算下确定最优的秩与比特配置。

Result: 在高度异质的联邦表格预测与数据生成任务上验证。实验表明，LBAT在多种极端预算情境下持续优于均匀秩、均匀量化和固定压缩等基线，并实现了更优的通信—效用权衡，同时保持必要的分布保真度。

Conclusion: 分层自适应地分配通信预算比统一压缩更有效，LBAT能在严格带宽约束下提升联邦学习效用并维持分布保真度。

Abstract: Federated learning faces severe communication bottlenecks when clients upload high-dimensional model updates. Existing methods often compress these updates uniformly across all layers. This uniform approach ignores the heterogeneous value of different parameter blocks and wastes limited bandwidth on insensitive layers. To address this issue, we propose Layer-wise Budgeted Adaptive Transmission (LBAT). LBAT reframes federated communication under extreme uplink budgets as a resource allocation problem. Our framework dynamically estimates the transmission value of different layers utilising local training signals. It then employs an exact byte dynamic programming allocator to determine optimal rank and bit configurations under strict budgets. We validate LBAT on highly heterogeneous federated tabular prediction and data generation tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LBAT consistently outperforms uniform rank, uniform quantisation, and fixed compression baselines across various extreme budget regimes. Furthermore, it achieves significantly better communication and utility tradeoffs while preserving essential distributional fidelity.

</details>


### [185] [From Modes to Memories: Characterizing the Scale-Space Dynamics of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39648)
*Cristina López Amado,Marco Fumero,Francesco Locatello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从确定性动力系统视角分析扩散模型，提出临界噪声尺度σ_c来量化样本的记忆/异常持久性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型通常被视为将噪声变换为数据的随机过程。作者希望从互补视角（固定噪声尺度下的确定性动力学）理解其行为，并解释记忆化（memorization）现象，即某些样本因重复、过拟合或作为异常值而获得过多概率质量。

Method: 将扩散模型视为一族按噪声尺度σ索引的确定性动力系统，在每个固定σ下把去噪器（denoiser）当作自映射并研究其动力学。精确去噪器的不动点对应平滑后数据密度的临界点，吸引子对应其众数；随σ增大，样本级众数逐渐合并为更粗的众数。据此提出用临界尺度σ_c（样本仍被固定尺度动力学保留的最大噪声尺度）量化这种持久性。条件模型中相同构造自然扩展到图像-文本对。

Result: 在受控实验和大规模模型上，σ_c能够追踪由数据重复、过拟合和异常值引起的记忆化，并能识别Stable Diffusion中完全记忆和部分记忆的样本。此外，σ_c还能提供关于图像空间分布及记忆化对文本描述依赖性的可解释度量。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个关于扩散模型记忆化的几何视角，通过临界噪声尺度σ_c统一刻画重复、过拟合与异常值导致的记忆现象，并在图像与条件（图像-文本）模型中得到验证与扩展，为理解与检测记忆化提供了可解释的工具。

Abstract: Diffusion models are typically viewed as stochastic processes that transform noise into data. We take a complementary perspective: a diffusion model defines a family of deterministic dynamical systems indexed by noise scale. At each fixed scale $σ$, we treat the denoiser as a self-map and study its dynamics. For an exact denoiser, fixed points correspond to critical points of the smoothed data density, while attractors correspond to its modes; as $σ$ increases, sample-level modes merge into progressively coarser ones. This suggests a geometric view of memorization: examples that receive excess probability mass due to duplication or overfitting, as well as outliers, should remain distinguishable under stronger smoothing than ordinary examples. We quantify this persistence by the critical scale $σ_c$, the largest noise scale at which an example is retained by the fixed-scale dynamics. In conditional models, the same construction extends naturally to image--caption pairs. Experiments in controlled settings and on large-scale models show that $σ_c$ tracks memorization arising from duplication, overfitting, and outliers, and identifies both memorized and partially memorized examples in Stable Diffusion. Moreover, $σ_c$ yields interpretable measures of the image spatial distribution and caption dependence of memorization.

</details>


### [186] [Graph Residual Conjugate Diffusion: SNR-Equalized Heat Flow for Graph Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39658)
*Jinwei Li,Daniel Tenbrinck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出图残差共轭扩散（GRCD），用模式相关时钟替代图热扩散的共享时钟，使各图傅里叶模式具有相同条件信噪比，并结合拟合的零均值图谱高斯参考与解析高斯传播，在极少采样步数下大幅提升图信号生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图扩散模型沿用各向同性加噪，对每个图频率模式加相同噪声方差；但真实图信号（如路网交通、传感器网络）频谱能量高度不均匀。将全部模式驱至近零终端信噪比需要很强加噪，在固定采样预算下需覆盖过大噪声范围，导致采样效率低、生成质量差。

Method: GRCD以模式相关时钟替代图热扩散的共享时钟，为每个图傅里叶模式分配相同条件信噪比；在训练集上拟合零均值图谱高斯参考，并在有限终端信噪比处停止（此时传播参考仍保留拟合的谱方差）。高斯分量在概率流ODE中具有精确的模式传播子，因此采样时解析推进高斯部分，仅对学习的残差分数进行数值积分。

Result: 在五个设定（METR-LA交通、Molene天气、三个随机块模型）上与七个对比方法（GAD、EDM图骨干、两种WSD适配、三种预处理控制）在匹配协议下对比：4次函数评估时，GRCD在全部五个设定上将平均最大均值差异（aMMD）较最优对比方法降低22至36倍；METR-LA上达0.054，以比最省时达标对比方法少87%的采样墙钟时间突破aMMD 0.1目标。有限终端信噪比下拟合终端参考使aMMD降低2.7至7.3倍，近零时该因子缩至1.00–1.01。

Conclusion: GRCD通过模式相关信噪比均衡、图谱高斯参考拟合与解析高斯传播，显著提升图信号扩散采样的效率与质量；终端参考拟合的收益集中于有限终端信噪比区间，验证了设计选择的有效性。

Abstract: Diffusion models generate data by reversing a forward corruption process that typically approaches a simple Gaussian prior. Recent work has extended this framework to signals supported on fixed graphs, e.g., road-network traffic and sensor-network measurements. Many graph signals have nonuniform spectral energy, whereas isotropic corruption adds the same conditional noise variance to every graph-frequency mode. Driving all modes to near-zero terminal signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) requires strong corruption, which increases the noise range that must be covered under a fixed sampling budget. We introduce Graph Residual Conjugate Diffusion (GRCD), which replaces the shared clock of graph heat diffusion with a mode-dependent clock that gives every graph-Fourier mode the same conditional SNR. GRCD fits a zero-mean graph-spectral Gaussian reference on the training split and stops at a finite terminal SNR at which the propagated reference still carries the fitted spectral variances. The Gaussian component has an exact modewise propagator in the probability-flow ODE, so sampling advances it analytically and integrates only the learned residual score numerically. We evaluate GRCD on five settings (METR-LA traffic, Molene weather, and three stochastic block models) against seven comparators under a matched protocol: Graph-Aware Diffusion (GAD), EDM (graph backbone), two adaptations of Whitened Score Diffusion (WSD), and three preconditioning controls. At four function evaluations (NFEs), GRCD lowers averaged maximum mean discrepancy (aMMD) by 22 to 36 times over the best comparator on all five settings, reaching 0.054 on METR-LA, where it clears an aMMD 0.1 target with 87% less sampling wall-clock time than the cheapest comparator that reaches it. Fitting the terminal reference reduces aMMD by 2.7 to 7.3 times at finite terminal SNR, while the factors shrink to 1.00 to 1.01 near zero.

</details>


### [187] [NodeGround: A Node Classification Benchmark in the Graph Foundation Model Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39673)
*Jinmo Lee,Dooho Lee,Minho Jeong,Jaemin Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 NodeGround 基准，统一评估图基础模型（GFMs）与数据集专属监督学习在节点分类任务上的预测质量与计算成本，覆盖 51 个数据集、6 个 GFM 与 15 种监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答"预训练图模型能否替代为每个数据集单独训练和调参的预测器"这一问题，需要同时评估预测质量和计算成本，而现有评估缺乏统一框架，难以系统比较。

Method: 构建 NodeGround 节点分类基准：51 个数据集，6 个 GFM 与 15 个监督方法，在两种标签可用性设定下评估；采用共享数据划分、仅用验证集做模型选择、受控超参数搜索、多种预测指标；并测量适配、训练、调参和推理等流程成本。

Result: 整体结果更有利于经过精细调参的图神经网络。标签较多时，GraphPFN 在 Elo 排名中位列第三，但其相对优势随数据集属性差异显著。效率方面，GVT 和 GraphPFN 在监督方法取默认配置与完全调参配置时出现在 Pareto 前沿；加入中间调参预算后，GVT 的优势消失，GraphPFN 仅在标签丰富设定下仍扩展估计前沿。

Conclusion: 复用预训练参数目前尚不能稳定带来更强的预测或更低的流程成本，因此还不能可靠替代针对每个数据集的训练与调参。作者公开了评估流程、运行级记录和开放排行榜。

Abstract: Can a pretrained graph model replace training and tuning a separate predictor for each dataset? Answering this requires evaluating prediction quality alongside computational cost. We present NodeGround, a node classification benchmark that puts graph foundation models (GFMs) and dataset-specific supervised learning under a common evaluation framework. The benchmark spans 51 datasets and evaluates six GFMs alongside 15 supervised methods under two label-availability regimes. Shared data partitions, validation-only model selection, controlled hyperparameter searches, and multiple predictive metrics make comparisons systematic, while workflow measurements account for adaptation, training, tuning, and inference. The results favor carefully tuned graph neural networks overall. GraphPFN reaches third place by Elo when more labels are available, yet its relative strengths vary substantially with dataset properties. Efficiency comparisons further qualify the benefits of pretrained reuse: GVT and GraphPFN appear on the Pareto frontiers when supervised methods are represented by their default and fully tuned configurations. Adding intermediate tuning budgets removes this advantage for GVT and leaves GraphPFN extending the estimated frontier in the label-rich setting alone. Thus, reusing pretrained parameters does not yet provide a broadly reliable route to either stronger predictions or cheaper workflows. We release the evaluation pipeline, run-level records, and an open leaderboard at https://github.com/nums-ai/nodeground.

</details>


### [188] [GFD-OPD: Guidance-Folded On-Policy Distillation of Diffusion Models Across Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39692)
*Zhenxing Zhang,Jiayan Teng,Wenxu Wu,Zhuoyi Yang,Jiazheng Xu,Wendi Zheng,Jie Tang,Dan Guo,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究扩散模型中的大规模教师到小规模学生的在线蒸馏（OPD），指出标准做法会失败，提出的 Fixed-State KL 指标揭示失败原因，并提出 GFD-OPD 方法解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型 OPD 主要关注将多个专家模型合并为单一模型，教师与学生共享相同骨干和规模。而大教师到小学生的扩散 OPD 尚未被充分研究，且标准蒸馏方法在此场景下失效，需要找出根因。

Method: 提出 Fixed-State KL，一种有效且公平地度量扩散模型 OPD 训练中学生与教师分布差距的方法；基于此分析发现小学生在分布上难以完美匹配大教师，且无分类器引导（CFG）会累积放大学生条件分支与无条件分支相对教师的分布差异。为此提出 GFD-OPD，一种在缩小师生差距的同时避免 CFG 组合误差放大的方法。

Result: 在大量实验中，GFD 在训练效率和最终性能上均优于之前基线，在所有基准上取得最先进结果。

Conclusion: 该工作首次阐明扩散模型大规模到小规模 OPD 困难的本质原因，并通过 GFD-OPD 有效解决，实现训练效率与性能的双重提升。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has demonstrated two important capabilities in language models: compressing large teachers into smaller students and merging expert models into a single model. Existing diffusion OPD, however, mostly focus on the latter, with teachers and students sharing the same backbone and scale. We investigate large-to-small diffusion opd from large teachers to a small student and find that the standard recipe fails. To find the underlying cause, we propose Fixed-State KL, an effective and fair way to measure the distribution gap between student and teacher during OPD training for diffusion models. We are the first to clarify why large-to-small OPD is challenging for diffusion models: a smaller student struggles to perfectly match the distribution of a larger teacher, while classifier-free guidance can accumulate and amplify the distributional discrepancies between the student's conditional and unconditional branches and those of the teacher. To solve this problem, we propose GFD-OPD, a simple yet effective method that reduces the student-teacher gap while avoiding the error amplification of the CFG composition. Across numerous experiments, GFD outperforms previous baselines in both training efficiency and final performance, achieving state-of-the-art results on all benchmarks.

</details>


### [189] [A library for differentiable signal processing and machine learning on the sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39737)
*Thorsten Kurth,Max Rietmann,Mauro Bisson,Andrea Paris,Alberto Carpentieri,Jean Kossaifi,Anima Anandkumar,Christian Hundt,Boris Bonev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: torch-harmonics 是一个用于球面数据的可微分信号处理与机器学习库，提供球谐变换、向量球谐、球面卷积及球面注意力等高效实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维球面 S2 在地球物理、行星科学、大气物理、量子化学、宇宙学与虚拟现实等领域应用广泛。随着机器学习深入这些领域，亟需能处理球面函数并尊重其拓扑与对称性质的稳健工具。

Method: 提出 torch-harmonics 库，包含球谐变换（SHT，傅里叶变换的球面类比）、向量球谐、离散-连续卷积与谱卷积，以及全局与邻域球面注意力机制的高效可微分实现。

Result: 该库除传统表示外，还提供构建先进球面 ML 架构（如球面 Transformer）的基础模块，支持可扩展、旋转感知的学习与推理。

Conclusion: torch-harmonics 为现代科学与工程应用中球面数据的机器学习提供了完整、高效且尊重球面拓扑与对称性的工具库。

Abstract: The two-dimensional sphere embedded in three-dimensional Euclidean space S2, plays a central role in a variety of scientific and engineering domains, including geophysics, planetary science, geodesy, atmospheric physics, quantum chemistry, cosmology, and virtual reality, among many others. As machine learning increasingly permeates these fields, the demand grows for robust tools that process and model functions on the sphere, while respecting the inherent topological and symmetry properties of the domain. We present torch-harmonics, a comprehensive library that offers efficient, differentiable implementations of advanced signal processing and machine learning (ML) methods for spherical data. These include the spherical harmonic transform (SHT), the spherical analogue of the Fourier transform, vector spherical harmonics, discrete-continuous and spectral convolutions, as well as both global and neighborhood spherical attention mechanisms. Beyond traditional representations, torch-harmonics provides the building blocks for state-of-the-art spherical ML architectures such as spherical transformers in order to enable scalable, rotationally-aware learning and inference in modern scientific and engineering applications.

</details>


### [190] [CNCGEN: A Dataset and Framework for Machining Process Planning and Toolpath Generation from B-rep Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39738)
*Xiaolei Zhou,Boyi Lin,Yuchao Feng,Jianwei Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出CNCGEN数据集与学习框架，从B-rep CAD生成三轴加工工艺规划与刀具路径，通过持久制造对象与演化毛坯状态建模，并引入学习型代理验证器提供材料去除反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从B-rep CAD生成加工工艺规划和刀具路径需将离散操作决策与连续刀具运动耦合起来。仅预测正确的操作序列并不能保证正确的材料去除，因为每条刀具路径都作用在前序切削留下的毛坯上。现有方法缺乏对规划决策与其几何效果之间对应关系的监督。

Method: 问题围绕"持久制造对象"形式化：对象身份决定操作目标，演化的毛坯状态条件化刀具路径生成。基于此提出CNCGEN数据集与学习框架。数据集含约5万条几何验证的合成加工流与800条留出的真实CNC记录，每条流对齐B-rep几何、对象引用操作、参数化刀具路径、中间毛坯状态和验证结果。模型逐步为选定对象生成操作与刀具路径，更新紧凑的加工状态以指导后续预测；训练时用学习型代理验证器提供材料去除反馈。

Result: 在合成数据和留出的真实CNC记录上，CNCGEN相比改造后的CNC生成基线，改善了工件几何，并减少了残余材料和过切。

Conclusion: 通过持久制造对象建模与加工状态更新，结合代理验证器的材料去除反馈，CNCGEN有效耦合了离散操作决策与连续刀具运动，提升了加工工艺规划与刀具路径生成的几何质量。

Abstract: Learning to generate machining process plans and toolpaths from B-rep CAD requires coupling discrete operation decisions with continuous tool motion as the workpiece evolves. Correctly predicting an operation sequence does not by itself ensure correct material removal, because each toolpath acts on the stock left by preceding cuts. We formulate this problem around persistent manufacturing objects: object identity determines the target of an operation, while the evolving stock state conditions the generation of its toolpath. Based on this formulation, we propose CNCGEN, a dataset and learning framework for three-axis machining. CNCGEN-Dataset contains approximately 50k geometrically verified synthetic machining flows and 800 held-out real CNC records. Each flow aligns B-rep geometry with object-referenced operations, parameterized toolpaths, intermediate stock states, and verification outcomes, enabling supervision of the correspondence between planning decisions and their geometric effects. CNCGEN generates operations and toolpaths for selected objects step by step, updating a compact machining state to guide subsequent predictions. During training, a learned surrogate verifier provides material-removal feedback that links local predictions to their geometric consequences. Experiments on synthetic and held-out real CNC records show that CNCGEN improves the resulting workpiece geometry and reduces residual material and overcut compared with adapted CNC generation baselines.

</details>


### [191] [Stable Transformers for Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39739)
*Luca Miglior,Alessio Gravina,Davide Bacciu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从动力系统视角分析图Transformer去噪器随深度增长变得耗散、导致梯度消失与表征崩溃的问题，并设计非耗散与可调阻尼机制验证耗散程度对图生成性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图生成模型日益依赖图Transformer捕捉节点与边的复杂依赖，但更深层结构虽理论上表达力更强、感受野更广，实际效果却随深度下降：重复自注意力会逐步收缩节点表征，阻碍信息流动与梯度传播。作者希望从动力系统角度揭示这一现象背后的机制。

Method: 作者从动力系统视角分析去噪器的谱动力学，指出标准GT去噪器随深度增加愈发耗散。为隔离该动力学效应，构建了保持置换等变、具有内在稳定非耗散输运的GT，并引入阻尼机制，让模型在非耗散与逐步收缩状态间连续插值，从而直接评估耗散对生成的影响。

Result: 在合成与分子图生成基准上，耗散与非耗散机制之间的性能差距随深度扩大：非耗散动力学能保持表征多样性与梯度流动，维持较强生成性能；而收缩越强，生成性能越受损。

Conclusion: 去噪器的动力学状态（是否耗散）是深度图生成模型的关键设计因素。

Abstract: Graph generative models increasingly rely on Graph Transformers (GT) to capture complex dependencies among nodes and edges. While deeper architectures should provide greater expressive capacity and a broader receptive field, their effectiveness can decline with depth: repeated self-attention progressively contracts node representations, impeding information flow and gradient propagation. We analyse this phenomenon from a dynamical systems perspective, focusing on how the denoiser's spectral dynamics affect graph generation. We show that standard GT denoisers become increasingly dissipative as depth grows, leading to vanishing gradients and representation collapse. To isolate the effect of these dynamics, we construct a permutation-equivariant GT with inherently stable, non-dissipative transport. We also introduce a damping mechanism that continuously interpolates between non-dissipative and increasingly contractive regimes, enabling a direct assessment of how dissipation influences generation. Experiments on synthetic and molecular graph generation benchmarks show that the gap between these regimes widens with depth: non-dissipative dynamics preserve representation diversity and gradient flow, sustaining strong generative performance, whereas greater contraction progressively impairs it. These findings identify the denoiser's dynamical regime as a key design factor for deep graph generative models.

</details>


### [192] [The Nixtlaverse: An Open-Source Ecosystem for Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39741)
*Olivier Sprangers,Max Mergenthaler Canseco,Marco Peixeiro,Saul Caballero Ramirez,Mariana Menchero García,Jing-Qiang Goh,Han Wang,Nikhil Gupta,Rogelio Melo,Senbong Gee,Cristian Challu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文以Nixtlaverse开源Python时间序列预测库生态为案例，提出共享长格式面板数据与键控输出契约、各模型族保留专用实现的设计方案，并通过M5数据的三个案例验证其成本、边界与效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型预测应用常混合统计、机器学习和神经模型。这些模型族解决同一问题，但在拟合状态、训练流程和并行方式上存在差异。现有软件要么用单一估计器接口掩盖差异，要么将各模型族分散到不同包中，迫使用户为每个包重写数据准备与评估代码。作者希望探索第三种设计，兼顾统一的数据契约与各模型族的专业化实现。

Method: 提出Nixtlaverse——一个开源Python时间序列预测库生态。其核心设计是：所有库共享相同的长格式面板数据和键控预测输出，而每个模型族保留各自专门的实现。通过公开M5竞赛数据上的三个用例进行展示：（1）在单次滚动原点评估中，用逐序列及层级加权指标评估统计、机器学习、神经模型以及来自另一生态系统的外部引擎；（2）在100至30,490条序列上分析运行时间和峰值内存，定位各族瓶颈；（3）对包括外部引擎在内的多引擎预测进行协调，覆盖M5层级全部42,840条序列，在密集实现内存耗尽时采用稀疏协调。

Result: 据发现统计拟合大致随序列数量线性扩展；特征构建主导机器学习的内存占用；在固定训练预算下，神经模型训练时间几乎与面板规模无关。稀疏协调成功实现了全层级42,840条序列的多引擎预测协调，而密集实现会耗尽内存。Nixtlaverse已获得大量公开分发、学术复用，并被其他预测框架采用，同时以宽松开源许可证发布，提供公开数据集、可复现示例和可验证基准工件。

Conclusion: 这些用例确立了共享数据与输出契约的成本、边界和效用。Nixtlaverse作为第三种设计方案的案例，证明了在统一数据/输出接口下保留模型族专用实现是可行且有用的，其在分发、复用和采纳方面的表现也印证了该设计的实际价值。

Abstract: Large forecasting applications often combine statistical, machine-learning, and neural models. These families solve the same problem but differ in fitted state, training procedures, and how they parallelize work. Forecasting software must therefore either hide these differences behind a single estimator interface, or keep the families in separate packages, forcing users to rewrite data preparation and evaluation for every package. We present the Nixtlaverse, an ecosystem of open-source Python libraries for time series forecasting, as a case study of a third design: all libraries share the same long-format panel data and keyed forecast outputs, while every model family keeps its own specialized implementation. We demonstrate this design through three use cases on the public M5 competition data. First, we evaluate statistical, machine-learning, and neural models, and an external engine from a separate ecosystem, in a single rolling-origin evaluation with per-series and hierarchy-weighted metrics. Second, we profile runtime and peak memory from 100 to 30,490 series and locate each family's bottleneck: statistical fitting scales approximately linearly in the number of series, feature construction dominates machine-learning memory, and neural training time is nearly independent of panel size under a fixed training budget. Third, we reconcile the forecasts of multiple engines, including the external one, over all 42,840 series of the M5 hierarchy, with sparse reconciliation where dense implementations exhausted memory. These use cases establish the costs, boundaries, and utility of shared data and output contracts. The Nixtlaverse has seen substantial public distribution, scholarly reuse, and adoption through other forecasting frameworks, and is released under permissive open-source licenses with public datasets, reproducible examples, and verifiable benchmark artifacts.

</details>


### [193] [Validity-Preserving Hierarchical RL for Joint Routing and Switch Placement in EDA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39749)
*Dorian Gailhard,Ugo Lecerf,Enzo Tartaglione,Donatello Conte,Jhony H. Giraldo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层强化学习框架，用于芯片设计中逻辑通信路由层面的布线拓扑与交换机放置联合优化，通过Gumbel蒙特卡洛树搜索探索可行解空间，效果优于非学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布线拓扑与物理布局的联合优化是芯片设计中的基础组合优化问题，需在结构、几何和逻辑约束下求解。现有方法多依赖嵌入强问题偏置的人工启发式规则来应对巨大的设计空间，缺乏泛化能力。

Method: 提出分层强化学习框架，从最小路由图出发，通过三个耦合操作逐步构建解：交换机扩展、交换机放置和路由细化。这些操作在构造上保持路由有效性，将搜索限制在每个通信的发起者-目标对都有唯一无环路由的可行配置中，并用Gumbel蒙特卡洛树搜索探索诱导解空间。

Result: 实验表明，神经引导的搜索在解质量上显著优于非学习的优化方法；此外，跨不同布局图（floorplan）的预训练可为未见实例的微调提供良好初始化。

Conclusion: 该分层强化学习框架能够有效处理布线拓扑与交换机放置的联合优化问题，神经引导搜索与跨布局预训练的结合提升了求解质量与泛化能力。

Abstract: Routing and switch placement are fundamental combinatorial optimization problems in chip design, requiring the joint optimization of routing topology and physical placement under strict structural, geometric and logical constraints. Existing approaches typically rely on carefully engineered heuristics that incorporate strong problem-specific biases to navigate the enormous space of possible designs. In this work, we introduce a hierarchical reinforcement learning framework for joint routing and switch placement at the level of logical communication routes. Starting from a minimal routing graph, our method progressively constructs increasingly expressive solutions through three coupled operations: switch expansion, switch placement, and route refinement. These operations preserve routing validity by construction, restricting exploration to feasible configurations where every communicating initiator-target pair has one assigned loop-free route. We explore the induced solution space using Gumbel Monte Carlo Tree Search, showing that neural-guided search substantially improves solution quality over non-learning optimization methods. Furthermore, pretraining across floorplans provides a strong initialization for fine-tuning on unseen instances.

</details>


### [194] [Revisiting On-policy Adversarial Black-Box Distillation: Calibrating Groupwise Reward Geometry for Effective Advantage Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39757)
*Xiao Cui,Mo Zhu,Yulei Qin,Yuze Wu,Wengang Zhou,Houqiang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GRGC两阶段框架，通过塑造学生侧奖励组几何结构，改进黑盒蒸馏中的优势构建，缓解GRPO与critic之间的目标不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒蒸馏中，GAD等on-policy对抗方法通过critic与学生形成对抗循环，但GRPO基于组内相对奖励计算优势，而critic主要区分教师与学生响应，二者目标不匹配，导致奖励组尺度崩塌或边界脆弱，优化信号不稳定。

Method: GRGC包含两阶段：1）critic侧条件化，采用高斯组内最优传输校准，将排序后的提示内奖励匹配到组中心高斯分位数，使奖励组具有非崩塌的分布和光滑的排序间隔；2）策略侧组功率调制，在转换为优势前重塑提示内奖励组，在保留critic诱导排序的同时提升边际可分性。

Result: 在多种教师模型、学生模型家族与规模、训练数据集上进行实验，GRGC在分布内和分布外评测上均有效，相比GAD仅引入可忽略的额外开销。

Conclusion: GRGC通过同时条件化critic训练与策略优化阶段的奖励组几何结构，有效改善了黑盒蒸馏中的优势构建质量，是一种实用且开销极低的改进方案。

Abstract: Black-box distillation is a practical route for transferring capabilities from API-accessible large language models that expose only text outputs into smaller student models. Recent on-policy adversarial methods such as GAD improve over SeqKD by forming an adversarial loop between a critic and a student, where the critic provides rewards for GRPO-based student policy optimization over the student's sampled responses. However, GRPO computes advantages from the within-group relative rewards of student samples for the same prompt, whereas the critic is trained primarily to distinguish teacher responses from student responses. This objective mismatch can produce reward groups with collapsed scale or fragile margins, leading to brittle grouped optimization signals. We propose Groupwise Reward Geometry Conditioning (GRGC), a two-stage framework that improves advantage construction by shaping student-side reward groups during both critic training and policy optimization. To improve critic-side conditioning, Gaussian groupwise Optimal Transport calibration regularizes the critic during training to produce reward groups with non-collapsed spread and smooth rank-wise gaps by matching sorted prompt-wise rewards to group-centered Gaussian quantiles. Building on this conditioned reward geometry, policy-side group power modulation reshapes the prompt-wise reward groups before they are converted into advantages, preserving the critic-induced ordering while increasing optimization-relevant margin separability. Extensive experiments across diverse teachers, student model families and scales, and training datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of GRGC on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution evaluations, while introducing negligible overhead over GAD. The code is available at https://github.com/2018cx/GRGC.

</details>


### [195] [How Does Local Landscape Geometry Evolve in Language Model Pre-Training?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39767)
*Zhanpeng Zhou,Yuhan Sun,Bingrui Li,Jinbo Wang,Huaijin Wu,Lei Wu,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 从损失景观几何视角分析语言模型预训练动态，揭示两个阶段，并提出学习率预热和动态批大小调度的调参策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练语言模型规模与成本巨大，使高效超参数调优至关重要，但目前缺乏有原则的指导。

Method: 从局部景观几何角度分析预训练动态，划分为两个阶段：Phase I 分析局部景观锐度从高到平缓的演变；Phase II 分析梯度噪声尺度对景观的支配作用，并提出深度-平坦度权衡理论。

Result: Phase I 中初始锐度高导致大学习率下不稳定和损失平台，景观早期由锐变平，解释了学习率预热的必要性并表明更大峰值学习率需要成比例更长的预热期；Phase II 中小批次高噪声拓宽损失盆地，大批次低噪声加深盆地。

Conclusion: 提出统一视角刻画损失景观演化，并转化为大规模预训练的可操作调参策略：更长预热期以及从小批次开始、后期增大的动态批大小调度。

Abstract: The scale and expense of pre-training language models make efficient hyperparameter tuning essential, yet a principled guidance is still missing. In this work, we analyze language model pre-training dynamics from a local landscape geometry perspective. Our study reveals two distinct phases. In Phase I, sharpness of the local landscape is initially high, leading to instability and loss plateaus under large learning rates (LRs). The landscape shifts from sharp to flatter regions early in training. This dynamic explains the necessity of LR warmup and further suggests that larger peak LRs require proportionally longer warmup periods. In Phase II, the local landscape is governed by the gradient noise scale. Our theory identifies a depth flatness trade-off: high noise from smaller batches widens the loss basin, whereas reduced noise from larger batches deepens it. This theory motivates a dynamic batch-size (BS) scheduler that begins with a small BS and increases it late in training. Together, we provide a unified view of loss landscape evolution, which translates into actionable tuning strategies for large-scale pre-training.

</details>


### [196] [Riemannian Flow Models with Reinforcement Learning for Molecular Crystal Structure Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39773)
*Thomas Egg,Harry Winston Sullivan,Maya M. Martirossyan,Philipp Höllmer,Cheng Zeng,Adrian Roitberg,Mingjie Liu,Richard Hennig,Sapna Sarupria,Ellad B. Tadmor,Stefano Martiniani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CG-OMatG，一种等变黎曼流生成模型，通过粗粒度层次化表示预测分子晶体结构，结合策略梯度强化学习生成低能候选结构，在 CSP blind test 上验证表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体结构决定材料性质，分子晶体结构预测（CSP）是关键难题。现有生成模型面临多晶型普遍、晶胞大、堆积几何复杂等挑战，难以有效处理分子 CSP 任务。

Method: 提出 CG-OMatG，基于等变黎曼流的生成模型，采用粗粒度层次化表示。将分子视为刚体，进行分子间与分子内消息传递以构建分子堆积的几何表示，学习重建分子质心位置、取向和晶格参数，条件为化学物种与构象几何。在 OMC25 和 CSD 数据集子集上训练，并用策略梯度强化学习微调以生成低能候选结构。

Result: 在 CSP blind test 基准上验证生成结构，使用 COMPACK 堆积相似性分析评估与实验测定晶体的吻合度，CG-OMatG 展现出较强的生成式分子晶体结构预测性能。

Conclusion: CG-OMatG 在生成式分子晶体结构预测方面表现优异，有望加速多晶型筛选和有机固态材料发现。

Abstract: Crystal structure governs material properties, making crystal structure prediction (CSP) a fundamental problem in materials science. Generative models are a promising approach for solving this problem, but the prevalence of polymorphism, coupled with large unit cells and complex packing geometry, makes the molecular CSP task challenging for existing models. To address this, we introduce Coarse-Grained Open Materials Generation (CG-OMatG), an equivariant Riemannian flow-based generative model. CG-OMatG predicts molecular crystal structures \textit{via} a coarse-grained, hierarchical representation. CG-OMatG treats molecules as rigid bodies---performing both inter- and intra-molecular message passing to construct a geometric representation for molecular packings---and learns to reconstruct molecule centroid positions, orientations, and lattice parameters, conditioned on chemical species and conformer geometry. We train the model on subsets of the Open Molecular Crystals (OMC25) and Cambridge Structural Database (CSD) datasets. Further, we fine-tune the model \textit{via} policy gradient reinforcement learning to steer the model towards generating low-energy candidate structures. We validate the generated structures on the CSP blind test benchmark, assessing agreement with experimentally determined crystals using COMPACK packing-similarity analysis. CG-OMatG exhibits strong performance for generative molecular crystal structure prediction, paving the way for accelerated polymorph screening and organic solid-state materials discovery.

</details>


### [197] [GraphMAS: A Systematic Benchmark of Multi-Agent Coordination for Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39777)
*Jiayi Yang,Yifang Chen,Yuanfu Sun,Xinyan Ge,Qiaoyu Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GraphMAS，一个面向图学习的LLM多智能体协作系统性基准，涵盖4种协作范式和7种方法，在7个图、3个领域、2类任务上评估，发现异质专家互补、实例自适应选择最具性价比，且协作策略可学习并可迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM图学习方法主要依赖单智能体推理，多智能体协作仅在通用推理场景研究，因此不清楚多专家智能体能否提升图学习、协作策略应如何设计与评估。图学习证据来自局部、长程、全局结构和语义等异质视角且重要性因样本而异，天然适合多智能体协作。

Method: 构建GraphMAS基准：建立共享的图推理专家池，沿"智能体间交互"与"运行时自适应性"两个维度组织协作，形成4种范式和7种代表性协作方法；在统一协议下于7个文本属性图、3个领域、2类图学习任务上进行评估。

Result: 异质图视角具有互补性；协调专家优于单个专家和单智能体图推理，收益主要来自跨专家分解推理而非更广的证据获取；更丰富的智能体间交互并不能稳定带来提升，而实例自适应的专家选择取得最佳准确率-效率权衡；协作可在固定专家池上学习并迁移到未见图。

Conclusion: GraphMAS提供了受控评估框架与经验性原则，帮助理解多智能体协作何时以及如何有益于图学习。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems coordinate specialized reasoning through aggregation, interaction, and adaptive control, yet their potential for graph learning remains unexplored. Graph learning is a natural setting for such systems because useful evidence may arise from heterogeneous local, long-range, global structural, and semantic perspectives whose relevance varies across instances. Existing LLM-based graph learning approaches primarily rely on single-agent reasoning, while multi-agent coordination has been studied mainly in general reasoning settings. Consequently, it remains unclear whether multiple specialized agents can improve graph learning and how coordination strategies should be designed and evaluated. To address this gap, we introduce GraphMAS, a systematic benchmark of multi-agent coordination for graph learning. GraphMAS builds a shared pool of graph reasoning specialists and organizes coordination along two dimensions, inter-agent interaction and runtime adaptivity, yielding four paradigms and seven representative coordination methods. Under a unified protocol, we evaluate these methods across seven text-attributed graphs, three domains, and two graph learning tasks. We find that heterogeneous graph perspectives are complementary, and that coordinating specialists improves over individual specialists and single-agent graph reasoning, with gains from decomposing reasoning across specialists rather than from broader evidence access alone. However, richer inter-agent interaction does not reliably help, whereas instance-adaptive specialist selection yields the strongest accuracy-efficiency trade-off. We further show that coordination can be learned over a fixed specialist pool and transfers to held-out graphs. GraphMAS therefore provides a controlled evaluation framework and empirical principles for understanding when and how multi-agent coordination benefits graph learning.

</details>


### [198] [CORD: Learning Reusable Degradation Representations Across Heterogeneous Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39784)
*Haibo Li,Zhiguo Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CORD通过类型特定的观测接口与共享退化骨干网络，结合观测内结构建模（ISM）和观测间动态建模（IDM）两个自监督目标，实现了跨异构物理系统的可复用退化表征学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究异构物理退化系统能否受益于联合预训练，并超越特定系统的预测，走向可复用的跨系统表征学习。

Method: CORD采用类型特定观测接口加共享退化骨干网络；使用两个互补尺度的自监督目标：观测内结构建模（ISM）捕获观测内部结构，观测间动态建模（IDM）捕获跨观测历史的潜在退化演化；在两种迁移边界（预训练包含的系统类型、预训练排除的系统类型）下评估。

Result: 在轴承、电池和切削刀具上，CORD（多域）在冻结适配下持续优于CORD（单域），在多数设置下全量微调进一步提升，并与代表性外部基线保持竞争力；源预训练初始化改善了向预训练排除的发动机类型的低标签适配；冻结表征分析显示多域预训练后跨单元生命周期一致性提升。

Conclusion: 跨异构物理系统的联合预训练可产生跨设备、数据集和系统类型均可复用的退化表征。

Abstract: Can heterogeneous physical degradation systems benefit from joint pretraining and move beyond system-specific prognostics toward reusable cross-system representation learning? CORD combines type-specific observation interfaces with a shared degradation backbone. Its two self-supervised objectives learn at complementary scales: Intra-Observation Structure Modeling (ISM) captures structure within observations, while Inter-Observation Dynamics Modeling (IDM) captures latent degradation evolution across observation histories. We evaluate CORD under two transfer boundaries: Pretraining-Included System Types, where downstream datasets and held-out units are unseen but their system types are represented during source pretraining, and Pretraining-Excluded System Types, where the entire turbofan-engine type is absent from pretraining. Across bearings, batteries, and cutting tools, CORD (Multi-domain) consistently improves over CORD (Single-domain) under Frozen adaptation, provides further gains under Full FT in most settings, and remains competitive with representative external baselines. Source-pretrained initialization also improves low-label adaptation to the pretraining-excluded engine type. Frozen-representation analysis further shows improved cross-unit lifecycle consistency after multi-domain pretraining. Joint pretraining across heterogeneous physical systems thus produces degradation representations reusable across devices, datasets, and system types.

</details>


### [199] [Pseudo-Label-Triggered Retraining from Forecast Errors for Online Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39789)
*Yeryeong Kwak,Yoo-Min Jung,Jonghun Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PILOT，一种基于预测误差动态的在线重训练触发框架，用伪标签训练轻量评分器决定何时重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实时间序列预测面临非平稳数据流，性能随时间下降；重训练虽可恢复性能但计算与运维成本高，因此关键问题不仅是"如何重训练"，更是"何时重训练"。现有策略依赖漂移告警或模型陈旧度等间接指标，本文改用实际预测误差作为直接反馈。

Method: PILOT（Pseudo-label-Informed Learned Online Trigger）：由于缺乏真实重训练标签，利用未来预测误差的上升构造伪标签，训练一个轻量评分器从观测到的误差状态预测该标签。部署时仅使用已完成的预测误差，可作为插件模块适配任意预测主干，无需修改架构。

Result: 在标准多变量预测设置下，于八个基准数据集、三种代表性主干（DLinear、iTransformer、TimesNet）上评估。在全部三种主干上，PILOT在重训练策略中取得最优平均排名，并保持较好的性能-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 以直接预测误差反馈替代间接指标来学习重训练时机是有效且通用的方案；PILOT作为即插即用模块，在多种主干与数据集上兼具性能与效率优势。

Abstract: Real-world time series forecasting systems operate under non-stationary data streams, where forecasting performance may degrade over time. Although retraining can recover the performance, it incurs non-trivial computational and operational costs. Under limited deployment resources, the key challenge is therefore not only how to retrain but also when to retrain. While existing retraining policies often rely on indirect indicators such as drift alarms or model staleness, we instead use realized forecast errors as direct deployment feedback. In this paper, we propose PILOT (Pseudo-label-Informed Learned Online Trigger), an online retraining framework that learns when to retrain from forecast-error dynamics. Since ground-truth retraining labels are unavailable, PILOT constructs a pseudo-label from future increases in forecast error and trains a lightweight scorer to predict it from observed error states. At deployment, PILOT uses only completed forecast errors and serves as a plug-in module for arbitrary forecasting backbones without architectural modification. We evaluate PILOT under standard multivariate forecasting settings across eight benchmarks with three representative backbones---DLinear, iTransformer, and TimesNet. Across all three backbones, PILOT achieves state-of-the-art average-rank performance among retraining policies while maintaining a favorable performance--efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [200] [TopTimeNet: Topologically-assisted time-series classification model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39792)
*Sharareh Sayyad,Sophia Bazzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TopTimeNet通过将固定几何拓扑特征提取与轻量可学习分类解耦，以极少参数实现与CNN相当、优于Transformer的周期/混沌动力学分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从时间序列中区分周期与混沌动力学是物理和工程中的基础难题；端到端学习架构需同时从数据中学习表示与决策边界，代价高昂。

Method: TopTimeNet分为两阶段：固定且不可学习阶段，从Takens延迟嵌入和持续同调中提取42维几何与拓扑描述符；轻量可学习阶段执行分类。

Result: 在49个非线性动力学系统基准上，1638参数的配置达到参数量多33倍模型的平均精度；平均精度与CNN相当，超过收敛Transformer的平均表现，而可训练参数少三到四个数量级。鲁棒性取决于噪声引入位置：对预计算特征的扰动可优雅退化，但对原始信号加噪并重算完整特征提取流程时急剧退化。

Conclusion: 将固定的几何与拓扑特征构建与轻量判别阶段解耦，可用显著更少的可训练参数达到可比的分类精度；对预计算特征的鲁棒性并不意味着完整原始信号到预测流程的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Distinguishing periodic from chaotic dynamics in a time series is a fundamental challenge in both physics and engineering. Yet, end-to-end learned architectures must discover both a representation and a decision boundary from data, at substantial cost. We introduce TopTimeNet, which decouples these tasks: a fixed, non-learned stage extracts a $42$-dimensional geometric and topological descriptor from Takens delay embeddings and persistent homology, and a lightweight learnable stage performs classification. On a benchmark of $49$ nonlinear dynamical systems, a $1{,}638$-parameter configuration matches the mean accuracy of one with $33\times$ more trainable parameters. Additionally, this approach delivers mean accuracy comparable to convolutional neural networks and surpasses the average performance of converged Transformer models, while requiring three to four orders of magnitude fewer trainable parameters. Robustness also depends sharply on where noise is introduced: TopTimeNet degrades gracefully under perturbations to its precomputed features, but degrades sharply when noise is introduced into the raw signal and the full feature-extraction pipeline is recomputed, showing that robustness to perturbations of the precomputed features does not imply robustness of the complete raw-signal-to-prediction pipeline. These results show that decoupling fixed geometric and topological feature construction from a lightweight discriminative stage can achieve comparable classification accuracy with substantially fewer trainable parameters.

</details>


### [201] [Probabilistic Adversarial Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39798)
*Andi Zhang,Xingyu Zhao,Siddartha Khastgir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从概率视角出发，提出KL下界目标并导出"概率对抗训练"方法，实验证明其能持续提升概率鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗样本源于距离分布p_dis与受害者分类器诱导分布p_vic的重叠。作者直觉认为：将这两个分布推得越开，重叠越小，对抗样本越难生成，鲁棒性越强。这一直觉自然引出基于KL的鲁棒性目标，但概率鲁棒性(PR)通常难以直接计算，需要可处理的替代目标。

Method: 作者证明KL(p_dis‖p_vic)−log Z_vic是概率鲁棒性(PR)的下界，其中Z_vic为p_vic的归一化常数。由于PR一般不可直接计算，最大化该KL下界可作为提升PR的可处理替代目标。进一步证明该目标可还原为一种缩放形式的对抗训练，从而给出对抗训练的概率解释，并据此提出"概率对抗训练"方法。

Result: 实验显示该方法能持续提升PR；消融研究表明，其所诱导的缩放因子甚至能增强非概率对抗训练方法的PR。

Conclusion: 该工作为对抗训练提供了概率解释，并给出了一个基于KL下界的、有原则的鲁棒性提升路径。

Abstract: Building on a probabilistic perspective in which adversarial examples arise from the overlap between a distance-based distribution $p_{\mathrm{dis}}$ and a victim-classifier-induced distribution $p_{\mathrm{vic}}$, we start from a simple intuition: adversarial examples become harder to generate when these two distributions are pushed apart, as their overlap becomes smaller, thereby increasing robustness. This intuition naturally motivates a KL-based robustness objective. We then prove that $\mathrm{KL}(p_{\mathrm{dis}}\|p_{\mathrm{vic}})-\log Z_{\mathrm{vic}}$ is a lower bound on probabilistic robustness (PR), where $Z_{\mathrm{vic}}$ denotes the normalizing constant of $p_{\mathrm{vic}}$. Since PR is generally intractable to compute directly, maximizing this KL-based lower bound provides a tractable surrogate objective for improving PR. We further show that this objective recovers a scaled form of adversarial training, offering a probabilistic interpretation of adversarial training and a principled route to robustness improvement. We call the resulting method probabilistic adversarial training. Experiments show that it consistently improves PR, and ablation studies demonstrate that the induced scaling factor can even enhance the PR of non-probabilistic adversarial training methods.

</details>


### [202] [Finite-Horizon Fisher Memory in Two-Sided Power-Bounded Recurrent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39800)
*Jeonghoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究有限时间范围线性高斯含噪循环记忆中的信息分配、写入准入与写后保持，用Fisher记忆刻画存储容量并给出误差界与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限时间范围的线性高斯含噪循环记忆中，信息如何被分配、写入时方向如何选择、写入后如何保持，缺乏统一的理论刻画与端到端误差保证。作者希望通过Fisher记忆矩阵的视角，说明非正规性对信息容量的作用，并给出可验证的预测。

Method: 在每个时间层分析方向性Fisher记忆M_n，证明其迹恒为N，导出双幂有界载体的1/n滞后界，并将M_n的极限与W^T的Cesàro渐近极限的逆联系起来。用时变耦合定义端到端存储算子，比较写入最优与存储最优方向。分析可逆保持与加性污染下Fisher矩阵的保留比例，并设计协方差感知解码器。实验上以训练输入掩码与线性读出器在160次运行中逼近任务最优，另进行320次运行的预设定验证。

Result: 迹恒等式tr M_n=N表明非正规性只重分配信息而不能提升球面平均，正规载体满足M_n=I。加性污染受α倍闭包协方差限制时至少保留1/(1+α)的Fisher矩阵。训练掩码与读出器在160次运行中位归一化Rayleigh效率高于0.998，二值精度与高斯预测平均绝对误差低于0.002。预设定320次运行中掩码在两种载体上均遵循指定目标，16/16次通过，并在未参与承诺的载体上复现。解码器若固定于训练时间层会降至随机水平，逆伴随传输可恢复数值精度。

Conclusion: 非正规循环记忆的信息容量受球面平均约束，存储保持与污染下的保留可由Fisher矩阵精确刻画；协方差感知解码与逆伴随传输能恢复精度。实验为预设定验证而非盲测，固定设计在未用过的载体上仍可复现目标特异性结果。

Abstract: We analyse allocation, admission and post-write retention in finite-horizon linear-Gaussian noisy recurrent memories. At every horizon, the directional Fisher memory $M_n$ satisfies $\operatorname{tr}M_n=N$: non-normality redistributes information but cannot raise its spherical average, while normal carriers satisfy $M_n=I$. For bi-power-bounded carriers, we derive uniform $1/n$ lag bounds, identify the limit of $M_n$ with the inverse of the classical Cesàro asymptotic limit of $W^\top$, and give finite-horizon error bounds. A time-varying coupling defines an end-to-end store operator. The writer-optimal direction need not be store-optimal. After writing ends, an invertible hold preserves the full stored Fisher matrix. Additive contamination bounded by $α$ times the closure covariance retains at least $1/(1+α)$ of that matrix; a covariance-aware decoder attains the corresponding accuracy. With recurrent carriers held fixed, training input masks and linear readouts approached the task-specific optimum in 160 runs, with median normalized Rayleigh efficiency above $0.998$. Binary accuracy matched the Gaussian prediction to mean absolute error below $0.002$ over more than four orders of magnitude in $J$. In a separate pre-specified study of 320 runs, trained masks followed the designated input-time objective in both carrier types, in 16 of 16 draws. These studies used development-seen carriers and are pre-specified validations, not blind holdouts. The same fixed design reproduced the objective-specific result in 16 of 16 draws on carriers unused before run commitment. Exact isolation preserved information, while a decoder fixed at its training horizon fell to chance; inverse-adjoint transport restored its sampled decisions to numerical precision.

</details>


### [203] [RATIO: Reasoning Analysis and Token-level Inference Optimization for Quantized Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39801)
*Chengzhu Bao,Xianglong Yan,Tianao Zhang,Jiaqi Chen,Shaoqiu Zhang,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RATIO框架，通过识别量化模型特有的过度思考token并施加定制化惩罚，在量化推理模型上实现更优的准确率-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练量化（PTQ）用于推理模型时不仅会降低推理性能，还会加剧过度思考，导致推理轨迹变长，抵消低精度推理的效率收益。现有方法多依赖复杂优化流程，或使用预定义的过度思考标记，适应性受限。

Method: RATIO包含两部分：1）量化感知推理行为分析（QRBA），通过分析全精度与量化模型之间的差异来识别过度思考token；2）Token特定惩罚确定（TSPD），借助全精度模型引导推导token特定的惩罚，无需额外训练。

Result: 实验表明RATIO比现有token级干预取得更好的准确率-效率权衡，相较量化基线最高提升9.8个点准确率，并将思维链长度最多缩短51.3%。

Conclusion: RATIO通过识别模型特有的过度思考token并施加定制惩罚，有效缓解量化推理模型的性能下降与过度思考问题，实现更优的准确率-效率权衡。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) has become a widely adopted technique for reducing the memory footprint and inference cost of large language models (LLMs). However, recent studies reveal that when applied to reasoning models, PTQ not only degrades reasoning performance but also exacerbates overthinking, leading to longer reasoning trajectories. These issues may offset the efficiency gains expected from lower-precision inference. Existing approaches mainly rely on complex optimization procedures. More recent lightweight inference strategies instead use predefined overthinking markers, limiting their adaptability across quantized models. To address these issues, we propose Reasoning Analysis and Token-level Inference Optimization (RATIO), a framework that identifies model-specific overthinking tokens and assigns each a tailored penalty. RATIO first introduces Quantization-aware Reasoning Behavior Analysis (QRBA) to identify overthinking tokens by analyzing discrepancies between full-precision and quantized models. It then adopts Token-Specific Penalty Determination (TSPD), which leverages full-precision guidance to derive token-specific penalties without additional training. Extensive experiments show that RATIO achieves a better accuracy-efficiency trade-off than existing token-level interventions. Specifically, RATIO achieves up to 9.8 points accuracy improvement and reduces chain-of-thought (CoT) length by up to 51.3% compared with quantized baselines. The code will be available at https://github.com/steven-bao1/RATIO.

</details>


### [204] [A Comprehensive Benchmark of Source-Free Universal Domain Adaptation on Time Series Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39810)
*Romain Mussard,Fannia Pacheco,Maxime Berar,Paul Honeine,Gilles Gasso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出首个针对时间序列的无源通用域适应（SF-UniDA）基准，并首次系统研究预训练基础模型在时间序列域适应中作为特征提取器的表现。作者指出所有现有 SF-UniDA 方法存在对未知样本拒绝阈值高度敏感的缺陷，并提出即插即用的自动阈值模块加以解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SF-UniDA 在图像领域受到越来越多关注，但时间序列领域尚无基准，而时间序列的域适应更具挑战性。同时，现有 SF-UniDA 方法的阈值选择问题尚未被充分研究。

Method: 构建首个时间序列 SF-UniDA 基准；评估预训练基础模型作为特征提取器的效果；提出可集成到任意 SF-UniDA 方法中的即插即用自动阈值模块。

Result: 在三个知名时间序列数据集上的实验验证了自动阈值模块的适用性；同时发现基础模型并未系统性地优于经典骨干网络。

Conclusion: 现有 SF-UniDA 方法对未知样本拒绝阈值高度敏感，所提即插即用自动阈值模块可有效应对该问题；时间序列领域仍缺乏专门定制的 SF-UniDA 方法。

Abstract: Source-Free Universal Domain Adaptation (SF-UniDA) extends Universal Domain Adaptation by removing access to source data at adaptation time while still handling label-set mismatches between domains. Despite growing interest in this setting for image data, no benchmark exists for time series, which are more challenging. We present the first SF-UniDA benchmark on time series. In addition, we provide the first study of pretrained foundation models as feature extractors for time series domain adaptation. In this context, we identify a critical and previously underexplored limitation of all existing SF-UniDA methods: the inference threshold for unknown-sample rejection is highly sensitive. We address this by proposing a plug-in auto-thresholding module that can be integrated into any SF-UniDA method. Experiments on three well-known time series datasets confirm the suitability of this module. They also highlight that foundation models do not systematically outperform classical backbones and that SF-UniDA tailored for time series is yet to be developed.

</details>


### [205] [Backward-State Policy Is Part of the Learning Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39813)
*Shuxiao Xie,Shuyang Xie,Dezhi Ran,Wei Yang,Tao Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 低精度训练中反向传播读取舍入值的策略（复用前向舍入值/原值/新随机舍入）是学习算法的一部分，无法用拷贝精度或最终损失来判定，需按每次使用逐一推导并检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低精度训练里，反向传播会多次读取同一张量（对应多个梯度），每次可读前向的舍入值、原始值或新的随机舍入值。这一"反向状态策略"常被视为内存与精度细节，仅用拷贝精度和最终损失来评判。作者认为它是学习算法的一部分，而这两种检验都无法判断其正确性。

Method: 以"前向实际运行时的梯度"（梯度原样通过舍入）为参照，推导每次使用该读取哪一个值，或哪种替代可在前向固定时给出平均相同的梯度；并在单个算子上、无需训练地检验这些逐次使用的要求。实验用 PyTorch 与 Transformer Engine 做三组测试，预先预测复用是否改变反向平均计算。

Result: 拷贝精度不能决定结果：三对 390M 模型、模拟 FP8 反向的实验中，注意力反向复用前向舍入输出时训练失败，改用同分布的新舍入则成功；即使最精确的拷贝（原值本身）按该参照也可能是错的（如归一化输出存低精度后馈入两个梯度，增益梯度需原值、下层权重梯度需舍入值）。最终损失同样无法排除"两处都读原值"的错误：该错误在如此训练的模型中持续存在，而预设损失比较仍落在事先设定的余量内。三组测试中，预先预测的判断全部成立。

Conclusion: 反向状态策略是学习算法的一部分，应逐次使用地予以指定和检查，而不应由拷贝精度和最终损失来决定。

Abstract: Low-precision training rounds tensors that the backward pass reads again, often for several gradients; each use can read the forward's rounded value, the original, or a new random rounding. This backward-state policy looks like a memory and precision detail, settled by copy accuracy and final loss. We argue that it is part of the learning algorithm, and that neither check shows whether it is right. Copy accuracy does not decide the outcome: in three pairs of 390M runs with an emulated FP8 backward, training fails when attention's backward reuses the forward's rounded output and succeeds with a new rounding from the same distribution. Even the most accurate copy, the original itself, can be wrong by our reference: the gradient of the forward pass as it actually ran, with gradients passed through rounding unchanged. For example, a normalization output stored in low precision feeds two gradients: the gain's gradient needs the original, but the next layer's weight gradient needs the rounded value that layer multiplied. Final loss, the other check, does not rule out the error of reading the original for both: it persists in models trained with such a store, while planned loss comparisons stay within a margin fixed in advance. We therefore derive from this reference which value each use must read, or which substitute gives the same gradient on average with the forward held fixed, and check these per-use requirements on single operators, without training. In three tests using PyTorch and Transformer Engine, the requirements predicted beforehand whether reuse changes what the backward computes on average relative to an independent copy, and every prediction held. Backward-state policy is thus part of the learning algorithm: it should be specified and checked use by use, not settled by copy accuracy and final loss.

</details>


### [206] [Beyond Accuracy: Prefix-Invariant Realizations of Low-Precision Fast Matrix Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39816)
*Shuxiao Xie,Shuyang Xie,Yuan Cao,Dezhi Ran,Wei Yang,Tao Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 快速矩阵乘法虽节省乘法，但混合token行的舍入会破坏前缀不变性，导致似然打分不稳定；作者提出可认证的二级Strassen实现，保证与行局部int8算子逐位一致，从而继承前缀不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多项选择似然打分依赖前缀不变性，即打分结果只能依赖其允许的前缀。然而快速矩阵乘法通过精确消去节省乘法，混合不同token行的舍入会使得后续token的贡献泄漏到较早的语言模型输出中。在Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct上，两种被修复至普通精度的快速FP8实现在仅替换允许前缀之后文本时，仍会改变5.83%和10.00%的OpenBookQA答案，而bf16模型和已部署的FP8矩阵乘法内核等行局部对照均无变化。这说明精度无法证明前缀不变性，且现有稳定性准则无法区分不同实现。

Method: 本文构造了在具有界整数编码上的二级Strassen可认证实现：先独立量化token行，随后精确混合与消去，最后重缩放，仅用49次块乘法而非64次。该认证保证在相同量化规格下与指定的行局部经典int8算子逐位相等，因此每个可认证实现都继承其前缀不变性。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct上，两个快速FP8实现即使修复到看似正常的精度，仍在240个OpenBookQA条目中分别有5.83%和10.00%的答案被改变；而行局部对照（bf16模型和已部署FP8内核）无任何改变。在两级Strassen的全部512个符号变体上，稳定性准则保持恒定，而teacher-forced困惑度在同一模型上跨度达772.4倍。认证实现使用49次块乘法，并保证与指定行局部int8算子逐位相等。

Conclusion: 精度不足以证明前缀不变性，现有稳定性准则也无法区分不同实现。通过认证机制，实现选择变为纯粹的成本决策：运行哪个可认证实现不再会改变任何被打分的似然，从而保障多项选择打分的可靠性。

Abstract: Fast matrix multiplication saves multiplications through exact cancellation, but rounding sums that mix token rows can leave contributions from later tokens in earlier language model outputs. This threatens prefix invariance, which multiple-choice likelihood scoring relies on: a scored likelihood must depend only on its allowed prefix. On Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct, two fast FP8 realizations repaired to ordinary-looking accuracy still change the answers chosen by likelihood on 5.83% and 10.00% of 240 OpenBookQA items when only the text after the allowed prefix is replaced with the bf16 model's own greedy continuation. Both row-local controls, the bf16 model and a deployed FP8 matrix multiplication kernel, change none. Accuracy thus does not certify prefix invariance, and the stability criteria we analyze cannot tell realizations apart: across all 512 sign variants of two-level Strassen they stay constant while teacher-forced perplexities span a 772.4$\times$ range on the same model. We therefore construct certified realizations of two-level Strassen on bounded integer codes that quantize token rows independently, then mix and cancel exactly before rescaling, using 49 block multiplications instead of 64. Our certificate guarantees bitwise equality to a prescribed row-local classical int8 operator at the same quantization specification, so every certified realization inherits its prefix invariance. Certification thus turns realization choice into a pure cost decision: which certified realization runs can no longer change a single scored likelihood.

</details>


### [207] [Should I stay or should I show? Learning to selectively disclose information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39818)
*Carlotta Giacchetta,Alessando Bogani,Cesare Barbera,Giovanni De Toni,Michele Caprio,Andrea Pugnana,Andrea Passerini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究"选择性披露"问题：在预算约束下学习何时向人类决策者披露支持信息，最优策略是价值信息(VoI)的阈值规则，实验表明其优于不披露和全披露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险场景中人类可在决策前获取支持信息，但获取信息有成本，且披露未必改善甚至可能损害人类决策。因此需要研究在预算约束下何时披露支持信息。

Method: 将选择性披露形式化为预算约束下的学习问题；证明最优策略是VoI的阈值规则；由于VoI在实践中未知，估计特定情境下的人类风险，并对插值策略相对不披露的退化程度及相对最优策略的遗憾进行界定；在基准数据集上实验，并开展两项用户研究及一项反事实基准。

Result: 基准数据集实验中，无论支持信息有益还是有害，选择性披露均优于不披露和全披露；两项用户研究表明由学习策略主导的披露可提升人机团队表现，但优势因任务而异；反事实基准（披露时用机器学习预测替代参与者预测）提示差异可能与自动提供信息时对建议的依从性较低有关。

Conclusion: 选择性披露能有效利用有限预算提升人机协作决策表现，但效果受任务与人类对建议依从性的影响。

Abstract: In many high-stakes settings, human decision-makers can acquire support information before making a decision. However, acquiring information is costly, and disclosure may fail to improve human decisions or may even impair them. We tackle this problem by studying selective disclosure, i.e., the problem of learning when to reveal support information to a human decision-maker under a budget constraint. We first show that the optimal policy is a threshold rule on the Value of Information (VoI), i.e., the expected reduction in human decision risk induced by disclosure. Since VoI is unknown in practice, we estimate the regime-specific human risks and bound the possible degradation of the resulting plug-in policy relative to lack of disclosure, as well as its regret relative to the optimal policy. Experiments on benchmark datasets show that selective disclosure outperforms both no disclosure and full disclosure, regardless of whether the support information is beneficial or harmful. Two user studies show that human-AI team performance can improve when disclosure is led by our learned policy and not human-selected, although this advantage varies across tasks. A counterfactual benchmark, which replaces participants' predictions with a machine-learning prediction when disclosure occurs, suggests that these differences might depend on lower adherence to advice when the information is automatically provided rather than self-requested.

</details>


### [208] [RainAtlas: A Multi-Continental Dataset for Precipitation Downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39833)
*Pierre-Louis Lemaire,Luca Schmidt,Wietze Suijker,Alex Hernandez-Garcia,David Rolnick*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文发布了 RainAtlas，一个大规模、多大陆、可直接用于机器学习的降水降尺度数据集，覆盖三大洲，将异构逐小时公里级观测统一到 2 公里网格，并对主流降尺度模型进行了基准评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端降水事件随气候变化不断增强，公里级降水预报对地方决策至关重要，但高分辨率降水观测稀缺，尤其在资源匮乏地区。机器学习降尺度方法虽有应用，却面临两大障碍：跨区域处理原始高分辨率数据需要大量工程与领域专业知识；跨区域泛化能力仍然较差。因此需要一个标准化、多区域的数据集来支撑研究与评测。

Method: 构建 RainAtlas 数据集：覆盖三大洲，将异构的逐小时公里级观测统一到共同的 2 公里网格。每个区域划分包含约 21 万对对齐的低分辨率（ERA5 再分析）与高分辨率（直接观测）降水数据。并使用多种指标对最先进的机器学习降尺度模型在 RainAtlas 上进行基准测试。

Result: 评测显示，域外泛化性能因训练区域不同而存在显著差异，说明跨区域、多来源的公里级评测十分必要。

Conclusion: RainAtlas 为降水降尺度研究提供了一个定位良好的基准数据集，推动跨区域、多源公里级评测的发展。

Abstract: Extreme rainfall events are increasing in intensity and frequency as climate change accelerates. While kilometer-scale precipitation forecasts are critical for supporting local decision-making, the limited availability of high-resolution precipitation observations hinders their accuracy, especially in under-resourced regions. Machine learning models are widely used to downscale precipitation data to km-scale, but their application to unseen geographies presents challenges. First, processing raw high-resolution precipitation datasets across regions requires significant engineering and domain expertise. Second, generalization across regions remains difficult. To help overcome these barriers, we release RainAtlas, a large-scale, ML-ready and multi-continental dataset for precipitation downscaling. Covering three continents, RainAtlas harmonizes heterogeneous hourly km-scale observations to a common 2-km grid. Each regional partition contains around 210,000 aligned low- and high-resolution precipitation pairs, respectively from ERA5 reanalysis and direct observations. We benchmark state-of-the-art ML-based downscaling models across RainAtlas using a wide range of metrics. Our evaluation reveals substantial variance in out-of-domain generalization depending on the training regions. This underscores the need for cross-regional, multi-source km-scale evaluation, establishing RainAtlas as a well-positioned benchmark for precipitation downscaling research.

</details>


### [209] [Fast Regularized Policy Mirror Descent with One-Step TD Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39837)
*Qipei Chen,Wenye Li,Yule Sun,Ke Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了策略镜像下降（PMD）与每步仅做一次时序差分（TD）更新的持续评论家耦合算法，在有限折扣MDP中证明了全局线性收敛，并在单条异策略轨迹下给出了无重置、无生成模型、无嵌套评估的样本复杂度保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PMD的快速收敛保证通常依赖精确或越来越准确的策略评估，这在实际中代价高昂。本文希望在评论家仅做一次TD更新的持续评估设定下，仍能获得PMD的快速收敛与可证样本复杂度。

Method: 对有限折扣MDP，分析坐标式Bellman精确更新下的TD-PMD；证明结合了基于预解式的辅助分布、衰减的Bellman违例修正，以及按坐标权重倒数加权的势函数。对随机TD-PMD，在一般强凸镜像映射、单条异策略马尔可夫轨迹下，采用常数步长与有限批量TD更新进行分析；利用正则项的轨迹级Lipschitz连续性（由顶点Bregman散度的一致界导出）以及针对带符号评论家误差传播的访问加权预解估计。

Result: 在精确坐标式Bellman更新下，TD-PMD对任意正常数演员步长和任意有限评论家初始化实现价值的全局线性收敛。在随机设定下，通过合适选取常数步长与有限批量TD，方法在约Õ(1/((1-γ)^5 σ̃_b ε))次转移后达到期望价值间隙ε，并得到对行为覆盖σ̃_b的逆线性依赖。

Conclusion: 该样本复杂度保证无需轨迹重置、生成模型访问或嵌套策略评估循环，数值结果与理论收敛分析一致。

Abstract: Policy mirror descent (PMD) enjoys fast convergence in regularized Markov decision processes (MDPs), but existing guarantees often rely on exact or increasingly accurate policy evaluation. We analyze PMD coupled with a persistent critic advanced by one temporal-difference (TD) update. For finite discounted MDPs, we establish global linear convergence in value for exact coordinate-wise Bellman updates, with any positive constant actor stepsize and arbitrary finite critic initialization. The proof combines a resolvent-based auxiliary distribution with a decaying Bellman-violation correction and a potential weighted by inverse coordinate weights. We then study stochastic TD-PMD with general strongly convex mirror maps under a single off-policy Markov trajectory. With suitably chosen constant stepsizes and a finite-batch TD update, the method achieves an expected value gap of $ε$ after $\widetilde{O}(1/((1-γ)^5 \widetildeσ_b ε))$ transitions. The stochastic analysis relies on the trajectory-wise Lipschitz continuity of the regularizer, derived from uniform bounds on vertex Bregman divergences, together with a visitation-weighted resolvent estimate for signed critic-error propagation that yields an inverse-linear dependence on behavior coverage $\widetildeσ_b$. In contrast to many prior guarantees for regularized policy optimization, our sample-complexity guarantee holds without trajectory resets, generative-model access, or nested policy-evaluation loops. Numerical results are consistent with the theoretical convergence analysis.

</details>


### [210] [Algorithmic Recourse Under Competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39877)
*Shahin Jabbari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文挑战了算法追索（algorithmic recourse）中"决策规则固定不变"的假设，提出在个体竞争有限资源的场景下，追索的大规模实施会抬高接受阈值，从而使原推荐失效；作者构建了"竞争下的追索"框架，联合优化推荐对象与推荐分数目标，并基于隐函数定理设计算法进行实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法追索通常为收到不利结果的个体提供最小成本的改进建议，但其核心假设是追索实施期间决策规则保持固定。然而当个体竞争有限资源时，即使评分模型不变，广泛实施追索也会改变接受阈值，导致原推荐不再有效。论文正是为应对这一现实问题而展开研究。

Method: 论文提出了"竞争下的追索"（recourse under competition）框架，联合优化推荐接收者（recommendation recipients）及其需要达到的推荐分数目标，以在最初被拒绝的个体之间平衡追索成本与转移后有效性（post-shift validity）。作者还基于隐函数定理（Implicit Function Theorem）开发了相应算法。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实数据集上的实验表明：个性化分数目标能实现更高的有效性，但成本也更高；而通用（common）分数目标在较低至中等的有效性取值范围内，通常能提供更优的成本—有效性权衡。

Conclusion: 论文揭示了竞争环境下追索有效性下降的问题，并通过联合优化推荐对象与分数目标提供了解决思路。研究结果表明个性化与通用分数目标在成本与有效性之间存在不同的权衡关系，为竞争场景下的追索设计提供了指导。

Abstract: Algorithmic recourse provides individuals who have received undesirable outcomes from machine learning models with suggestions for minimum-cost improvements to achieve the desired outcome. A central assumption when computing recourse is that the decision rule remains fixed throughout the recourse implementation phase. We challenge this assumption in settings where individuals compete for limited resources. In such settings, widespread recourse implementation can change the acceptance threshold even when the scoring model that is used to evaluate individuals remains the same. This change in acceptance threshold can, in turn, invalidate the original recourse recommendations (i.e., following the recourse may not lead to the desired outcome). To address this problem, we introduce a framework called recourse under competition that jointly optimizes for recommendation recipients and the recommended score target they need to satisfy to balance the recourse cost and post-shift validity among initially rejected individuals. We develop an algorithm based on the Implicit Function Theorem and empirically analyze its performance. Experiments on synthetic and real datasets show that personalized score targets can achieve higher validity, albeit at a higher cost. In contrast, common score targets generally offer favorable cost-validity trade-offs for lower to medium validity values.

</details>
