For IQL, whose value learning is already independent of the execution actor, RAPO preserves the original value updates and adapts only the inverse temperature in advantage-weighted policy extraction. Experiments on D4RL locomotion and AntMaze tasks show improvements over both base algorithms, with larger gains for TD3+BC, whose RAPO instantiation outperforms baselines on average.

</details>


### [246] [MANET-GNN: Learned Decentralized Optimization of Power Allocation in Multi-Channel MANETs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40170)
*Tomer Alter,Nir Shlezinger,Michael Segal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MANET-GNN，一个基于消息传递图神经网络的分布式学习优化器，用于动态多跳多信道MANET中的去中心化功率分配，仅依赖本地CSI与有限邻居信息交换即可实现低时延推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代MANET使用多频信道并支持异构业务，去中心化发射功率分配变得愈发困难；传统方法难以在动态、资源受限环境下兼顾性能与低时延。

Method: 构建统一的端到端吞吐量最大化约束问题，覆盖单播、组播、多商品、汇聚及多对多通信；该问题为集中式非凸问题，被用作MANET-GNN的无监督训练目标，GNN通过消息传递作为分布式学习优化器，仅使用本地且可能含噪的CSI与固定次数邻居交换。

Result: 数值结果表明MANET-GNN在多种通信框架下达到接近集中式方法的性能，对信道不确定性具有鲁棒性，并能跨MANET配置有效扩展。

Conclusion: MANET-GNN提供了一种统一、可泛化、低时延的去中心化功率分配方案，适用于多种通信模式与动态网络环境。

Abstract: MANETs enable flexible infrastructure-less wireless connectivity in dynamic and resource-constrained environments. As modern MANETs exploit multiple frequency channels and support heterogeneous traffic patterns, decentralized transmit-power allocation becomes increasingly challenging. We develop a unified learned optimization framework for decentralized power allocation in dynamic multi-hop, multi-channel MANETs. We formulate a constrained end-to-end throughput maximization problem covering unicast, multicast, multicommodity, convergecast, and many-to-many communication. Although centralized and non-convex, this problem serves as an unsupervised training objective for MANET-GNN, a message-passing GNN that operates as a distributed learned optimizer. MANET-GNN uses only local, possibly noisy, CSI and a prescribed number of neighbor message exchanges, enabling low-latency decentralized inference while generalizing across topologies and network sizes. Numerical results show that MANET-GNN achieves centralized-competitive performance across communication frameworks, remains robust to channel uncertainty, and scales effectively across MANET configurations.

</details>


### [247] [Cheap to Draw, Expensive to Trust: Certifying Test-Time Scaling Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40190)
*Sohail,Sarkar,Shakuntala Baichoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何以最小采样代价为"多样本+验证器择优"的准确性-预算曲线提供覆盖所有预算的统计认证，推导了极小极大成本并设计了无需试点实验的配对审计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从缩放曲线（准确率对采样数k）读取预算，需事后查看每个点，因此只有同时覆盖所有预算的置信带才能保护该选择；而固定的精确二项式设计在100题基准上需192,000个答案才能以95%置信认证64个预算至±1/32，其中大部分成本花在了错误的不确定性上——因为基准是固定题目列表，约四分之三的方差来自题目间差异，逐题重访的审计不必为之付费。

Method: 推导认证整条曲线的极小极大成本（至对数因子），分解为三部分：得分分布尾部校准、题目间区分、以及沿曲线求和的题内噪声；在单一基准下最后一项归结为最佳答案分配下单个答案影响力的方差。提出基于同题两次独立抽样指数不等式的配对审计，无需试点实验。

Result: 在185个留出得分池上，配对审计在64个预算时用费为最便宜竞争认证审计的0.74倍，在1,024个预算时为0.53倍；在新生成的MMLU-Pro研究中，以79,133个答案认证曲线，与事先根据题内方差拟合的成本律预测相差0.6%以内。

Conclusion: 同一路径可认证pass@k与多数投票，置信带可推广到题目总体以及依赖先前答案的答案情形。

Abstract: Sampling several answers and keeping the one a verifier scores highest is one of the simplest ways to buy accuracy at test time. Its effect is reported as a scaling curve: accuracy against the number $k$ of sampled answers. The curve is cheap to draw and expensive to trust. A budget read off it is chosen after looking at every point, so only a band that covers all budgets at once protects the choice, and on a 100-question benchmark a fixed exact-binomial design needs 192,000 generated answers to certify 64 budgets to within $\pm1/32$ at 95%. Most of that cost pays for the wrong uncertainty. A benchmark is a fixed list of questions; at budget 64, about three quarters of the variance of a selected answer's correctness lies between questions, and an audit that revisits every question need not pay for it. We derive the minimax cost of certifying the whole curve, up to logarithmic factors. It has three parts: calibrating the tail of the score distribution, telling the questions apart, and within-question noise summed along the curve. At a single benchmark the last part sharpens to the variance of one answer's influence under the best allocation of answers to questions, which every valid audit pays and an audit that learns the allocation attains, up to a logarithm, as the precision grows. A paired audit built on an exponential inequality for two independent draws at the same question needs no pilot. On 185 held-out score pools it uses 0.74 times the answers of the cheapest competing certified audit at 64 budgets and 0.53 times at 1,024, and on a newly generated MMLU-Pro study it certified the curve with 79,133 answers, within 0.6% of what a cost law fitted beforehand predicted from the study's within-question variance. The same paths certify pass@$k$ and majority voting, and the bands extend to populations of questions and to answers that depend on earlier ones.

</details>


### [248] [Near-Linear Accuracy Bounds for Moreau--Yosida Unadjusted Langevin Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40193)
*Yuchen Xin,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文为经典 MYULA 算法建立了近线性精度界：在显式步长条件下，不变测度偏差为 Õ(h)，结合 Moreau 近似偏差与 Wasserstein 收缩，得到 Õ(ε^{-1}) 次迭代即可使 √m·W₂(μ_N,π) ≤ ε，且仅需一阶梯度与精确近端评估，无需三阶导数或 Lipschitz Hessian 假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标分布为 π ∝ e^{-f-g}，其中 f 强凸光滑、g 凸且全局 Lipschitz。此类非光滑复合目标难以直接采样，MYULA 通过 Moreau 光滑化处理 g。已有分析往往依赖较强正则性（如三阶导数、Lipschitz Hessian），本文旨在放宽假设并给出更清晰、直接的精度界。

Method: 在参数相关的显式步长条件下，分析 MYULA 关于 Moreau 光滑目标的稳态偏差；核心思想是借助基于 Poisson 方程的估计，将二阶稳态残差转化为 Wasserstein 界。再将此不变测度偏差与 Moreau 近似偏差、Wasserstein 收缩性质相结合，推导达到给定精度所需的迭代次数。

Result: 证明了不变测度偏差相对 Moreau 光滑目标为 Õ(h)，误差系数对逆光滑参数仅呈对数依赖；结合 Moreau 近似偏差与 Wasserstein 收缩后，得到 Õ(ε^{-1}) 次迭代使第 N 步分布满足 √m·W₂(μ_N,π) ≤ ε（固定模型参数与初始化）。每次迭代仅需一次梯度评估和一次精确近端评估。

Conclusion: 在无需三阶导数或 Lipschitz Hessian 的较弱假设下，直接界定了 MYULA 的稳态误差，给出近线性迭代复杂度，表明该算法在复合非光滑目标采样中兼具简洁性与理论保证。

Abstract: We establish near-linear accuracy bounds for the classical Moreau--Yosida unadjusted Langevin algorithm (MYULA). The target is $π\propto e^{-f-g}$, where $f\in C^2(\mathbb{R}^d)$ is $m$-strongly convex with Lipschitz gradient and $g$ is convex and globally Lipschitz. Under an explicit parameter-dependent step-size condition, we bound the invariant-measure bias relative to the Moreau-smoothed target by $\widetilde O(h)$, with only logarithmic dependence on the inverse smoothing parameter in the error coefficient. Combining this estimate with the Moreau approximation bias and Wasserstein contraction gives $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-1})$ iterations to make the $N$th-iterate law $μ_N$ satisfy $\sqrt m\,W_2(μ_N,π)\le\varepsilon$, for fixed model parameters and initialization. We bound the stationary error directly, without assuming third derivatives or a Lipschitz Hessian. Each iteration uses one gradient evaluation and one exact proximal evaluation. The key idea in our analysis is to convert a second-order stationary residual into a Wasserstein bound using a Poisson-based estimate.

</details>


### [249] [Distribution Matching Distillation for Continuous Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40235)
*Paul Le Van Kiem,Dario Shariatian,Umut Simsekli,Alain Durmus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出分布蒸馏方法以降低连续扩散语言模型的网络评估次数（NFE），统一了两种方法：Simplex-DMD（连续松弛+路径梯度）与Reinforce-DMD（类别采样+REINFORCE+学习密度比），在OpenWebText上显著降低生成困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续扩散语言模型虽并行生成所有token，但高质量生成仍需数百次网络评估（NFE），计算成本高昂，亟需通过分布蒸馏利用学生的概率化token输出降低这一成本。

Method: 提出统一框架，将学生输出参数化与梯度估计器相连接，在相同学生架构和反向KL匹配目标下导出两种方法：Simplex-DMD使用连续token松弛和路径梯度；Reinforce-DMD使用类别采样与带学习密度比的REINFORCE。两者均针对多步生成开发，并研究各自的训练与采样选择。

Result: 在OpenWebText上，序列长度1,024 token时，Simplex-DMD在仅4次NFE下达到生成困惑度45.6（unigram熵5.44 nats），相比最强扩散基线在匹配熵与采样预算下降低49%；Reinforce-DMD在大预算下改善前沿，256次NFE时达到生成困惑度14.9（熵5.00 nats），同协议下降低20%。

Conclusion: 分布蒸馏可有效降低连续扩散语言模型的采样成本，两种参数化方法在不同预算下各有优势：Simplex-DMD适合极低NFE场景，Reinforce-DMD在大预算下表现更优。

Abstract: Continuous diffusion language models generate all tokens in parallel, yet high-quality generation can still require hundreds of network evaluations (NFEs). We study how distributional distillation can reduce this cost by exploiting the student's probabilistic token outputs. Our unified formulation connects the student's output parameterization to the resulting gradient estimators and yields two methods with the same student architecture and reverse-KL matching objective: Simplex-DMD uses continuous token relaxations and pathwise gradients, while Reinforce-DMD uses categorical sampling and REINFORCE with a learned density ratio. We develop both methods for multi-step generation and investigate the training and sampling choices associated with each parameterization. On OpenWebText, for sequences of 1,024 tokens, Simplex-DMD achieves a generative perplexity of 45.6 at a unigram entropy of 5.44 nats in just 4 NFEs, a 49% reduction relative to the strongest evaluated diffusion baseline at matched entropy and sampling budget. Reinforce-DMD improves the frontier at larger budgets, reaching a generative perplexity of 14.9 at an entropy of 5.00 nats with 256 NFEs, a 20% reduction under the same comparison protocol.

</details>


### [250] [OpenTSLM TeeMoE: A Unified Time-Series Language Model for Forecasting, Contextual Prediction, and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40265)
*Tony Chen,Timo Stoffregen,Maxwell Xu,Thomas Kaar,Martin Maritsch,Geremia Pompei,Nicolas Zumarraga,Robert Jakob,Paul Schmiedmayer,Patrick Langer,Juncheng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 OpenTSLM TeeMoE，一个通用时间序列语言模型，通过共享主干上独立训练的三个低秩专家（预测聚合、原生预测、时序分析），并由学习的 LoRA MoE 控制器动态加权，统一了数值预测、上下文条件预测与语言时序推理能力，在多个基准上进入前三。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实时间序列应用需要模型同时具备预测、上下文条件预测和基于语言的时间推理能力，但现有时间序列基础模型能力割裂：数值专家预测最强，语言模型上下文理解更广。核心挑战是统一这些异构能力而不降低各自性能。

Method: 提出 OpenTSLM TeeMoE。在共享主干上独立训练三个低秩专家，分别负责预测聚合、原生预测和时序分析；再用一个可学习的 LoRA 混合专家（MoE）控制器，针对每个请求对冻结的专家参数更新进行加权。

Result: 在时间序列预测、上下文条件预测和语言时序推理的常用基准上表现强劲：GIFT-Eval 按平均 MASE 排名进入前三，Context is Key 按 RCRPS 排名靠前，TimeSeriesExam 按准确率排名进入前三。

Conclusion: 通过在共享主干上组合多个低秩专家并用 MoE 控制器动态加权，可在不牺牲单项能力的前提下实现时间序列预测、上下文条件预测与语言时序推理的统一，验证了通用时间序列语言模型的可行性。

Abstract: Real-world time-series applications increasingly require models that can handle time series forecasting, context-conditioned prediction, and language-based temporal reasoning. Yet current time-series foundation models remain fragmented across these capabilities: numerical specialists often provide the strongest forecasts, while language-based models offer broader contextual understanding and analysis. A central challenge is to unify these heterogeneous capabilities without reducing their individual performance. We introduce OpenTSLM TeeMoE, a generalist time-series language model that can forecast directly from observed time series, reason over textual context and temporal patterns, and synthesize and refine predictions from external numerical forecasting specialists. We independently train three low-rank experts for forecast aggregation, native forecasting, and temporal analysis over a shared backbone. A learned LoRA mixture-of-experts controller then weights their frozen parameter updates for each request. Our proposed model achieves strong performance on widely used benchmarks for time series forecasting, context-conditioned prediction, and language-based temporal reasoning, ranking among the top three on GIFT-Eval by mean MASE rank, Context is Key by RCRPS, and TimeSeriesExam by accuracy.

</details>


### [251] [PMosFM: Preconditioned Manifold Matching for One-Step Physics-Constrained Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40287)
*Zhangyong Liang,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出预条件流形单步流匹配框架PMosFM，实现一步物理约束生成，无需残差损失或终端展开，训练与采样成本更低且保真度相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理约束生成模型需满足约束并匹配目标分布，但传统方法通过迭代纠正或残差优化、轨迹展开来施加约束，导致采样或训练成本上升，因此需要一种低成本且保持约束的生成方法。

Method: PMosFM将约束编码进流形解码器，在内在坐标中学习传输，无需单独残差损失或终端展开；采用几何预条件器基于解码器诱导度量重缩放坐标，用正则化协方差变换近似白化插值状态输入；提出有限区间目标耦合速度监督与物理空间解码端点一致性。

Result: 理论上证明精确参数化可消除残差诱导的高斯-牛顿曲率，几何与协方差效应在局部条件界中可分离，物理流映射误差界约束端点分布误差；控制消融检验条件化效果，实验评估优化器更新时间和内存占用；在多个基准上训练和采样时间低于多步基线，物理与分布保真度相当。

Conclusion: PMosFM通过流形解码与预条件单步流匹配，在保持物理约束和分布保真度的同时显著降低训练与采样成本，代码与数据集将公开。

Abstract: Physics-constrained generative models aim to generate physical fields that match a target distribution and satisfy prescribed constraints. However, enforcing these constraints often increases sampling costs through iterative corrections or training costs through residual optimization and trajectory unrolling. To address this issue, we introduce \textbf{P}reconditioned \textbf{M}anifold \textbf{o}ne-\textbf{s}tep \textbf{F}low \textbf{M}atching (\textbf{PMosFM}), a preconditioned manifold matching framework for one-step physics-constrained generation. By encoding constraints in a manifold decoder, PMosFM learns transport in intrinsic coordinates without separate residual losses or terminal residual unrolling. A geometric preconditioner rescales coordinates using the decoder-induced metric, while a regularized covariance transform approximately whitens the interpolation-state inputs. A finite-interval objective couples velocity supervision with consistency between decoded endpoints in physical space. We show that exact parameterization removes residual-induced Gauss--Newton curvature, that geometric and covariance effects separate in a local conditioning bound, and that physical flow-map error bounds endpoint distributional error. Controlled ablations examine conditioning, and experiments evaluate optimizer-update time and memory footprint. At inference, PMosFM uses one neural transport evaluation followed by physical decoding. Experiments across benchmarks show lower training and sampling time than the multi-step baselines at comparable physical and distributional fidelity. Code and datasets will be released publicly.

</details>


### [252] [Disentangling Computation in Multi-Task Neural Networks with the Green's Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40292)
*James Hazelden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用有限时域格林算子（Green's operator）研究训练好的循环网络中的全局一阶扰动响应，从而揭示计算如何在任务间和时间上被组织和复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对循环网络的分析多聚焦于神经活动的几何结构、动力学模体或局部扰动增长，缺乏对网络全局扰动路由（即计算如何在不同任务和时间间被组织和复用）的整体刻画。

Method: 将有限时域格林算子定义为把轨迹上各源点的扰动映射到其下游状态空间响应的算子，直接表示扰动路由；通过该算子的简单归约得到任务间与时间间的计算视图，并利用无矩阵乘积（matrix-free products）在不构造完整算子的情况下获取这些视图。

Result: 在一个灵活的多任务循环网络中，任务归约揭示了已知计算模体的结构化复用，时间归约则揭示了因果通路及其在训练过程中的形成。

Conclusion: 格林算子为刻画学习到的动力学计算的组织方式提供了一个全局响应几何框架。

Abstract: How is computation organized and reused across tasks and time in a trained recurrent network? Most analyses emphasize the geometry of neural activity, dynamical motifs, or local perturbation growth. We instead study the network's global first-order perturbation response. The finite-horizon Green's operator maps perturbations at each source along a trajectory to their downstream state-space responses and therefore directly represents perturbation routing. Simple reductions of this operator provide task-to-task and time-to-time views of the same computation, while matrix-free products make these views accessible without constructing the full operator. In a flexible multitask recurrent network, task reductions reveal structured reuse of known computational motifs, while temporal reductions reveal causal pathways and how they emerge during training. Our main point is simple: the Green's operator provides a global response geometry for mapping the organization of learned dynamical computation.

</details>


### [253] [Compression Footprints as Security Signals for Model-Poisoning Defense in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40312)
*Sachi Shome,William Eiers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将联邦学习中有损压缩器视为一种安全信号，提出"压缩足迹"(compression footprint)概念，并据此设计服务器端鲁棒聚合方法 CRAFT，实现对恶意更新的检测与抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法通常把有损压缩仅视为误差来源，而常规投毒防御则依赖更新几何结构。本文的动机在于利用有损压缩对输入敏感的失真与载荷行为，来区分诚实更新与攻击生成的更新，从而开辟新的防御视角。

Method: 提出"压缩足迹"概念，即由有损压缩器产生的重构、方向、稀疏性及载荷统计构成的低维集合；刻画了使足迹能够区分诚实与恶意更新的充分条件；并以此为基础设计 CRAFT 服务器端鲁棒聚合方法。CRAFT 在严格诚实多数假设下使用服务器可验证的足迹，无需客户端元数据、无需预知恶意客户端数量，且不增加额外通信开销。

Result: 实验表明，误差有界有损压缩器(EBLC)的足迹比 Top-K 足迹具有更强的区分能力，足迹信任能抑制恶意影响。在 IID 客户端数据、36% 恶意参与率、六种模型投毒攻击、三个数据集和六种鲁棒聚合基线的评估中，CRAFT 在 18 个设置中有 7 个取得最佳精度，其余设置与最佳结果相差不超过 1.7 个百分点。

Conclusion: 有损压缩既可以作为通信机制，也可以作为联邦学习中鲁棒聚合的安全信号，CRAFT 验证了这一双重作用的有效性。

Abstract: Lossy compression is widely used in Federated Learning (FL) but is generally treated as an error source, while conventional poisoning defenses inspect update geometry. In this work, we instead treat the compressor's response as a security signal: the input-dependent distortion and payload behavior induced by lossy compression can expose differences between honest and attack-generated updates. We introduce the concept of a \emph{compression footprint}: the low-dimensional collection of reconstruction, directional, sparsity, and payload statistics induced by a lossy compressor. We characterize sufficient conditions under which compression footprints separate honest and malicious updates, and operationalize our findings in the CRAFT (\emph{Compression-guided Robust Aggregation via Footprint Trust}) server-side robust aggregation method. Crucially, under a strict honest-majority assumption, CRAFT uses server-verifiable footprints, requires no client-side metadata nor knowledge of the number of malicious clients, and adds no communication beyond the compressed FL pipeline. Moreover, while CRAFT assumes a strict honest majority, it does not require the number of malicious clients to be known in advance. We observe that error-bounded lossy compressor (EBLC) footprints provide stronger separation than Top-K footprints and that footprint trust suppresses malicious influence. We evaluate CRAFT under IID client data with 36\% malicious participation across six standard model-poisoning attacks, three datasets, and six robust aggregation baselines, finding that CRAFT consistently achieves the best accuracy in 7 out of 18 settings and within 1.7 percentage points of the best in the others. Our results show that lossy compression can serve as both a communication mechanism and a security signal for robust aggregation in FL.

</details>


### [254] [Is Weight Tying Still Beneficial for Decoder-Only LLMs in Private Settings Under DP-SGD?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40335)
*Razan El Mais,Ali Chehab,Ibrahim Issa,Razane Tajeddine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在DP-SGD微调解码器LLM时，解绑输入输出嵌入（untied embeddings）比权重绑定（weight tying）效果更好，准确率最高提升4.74个百分点，并能启用内存高效的ghost clipping，降低60%以上内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重绑定是解码器LLM为非私密场景下的参数效率和语言建模性能所做的设计选择，但其在差分隐私训练中的影响尚未被充分研究。本文旨在探究权重绑定在DP场景中的作用。

Method: 以GPT2和DistilGPT2为代表性解码器架构，在DP-SGD下对比权重绑定与解绑嵌入模型，并在SST-2、QNLI、QQP上评估；同时分析解绑嵌入对memory-efficient ghost clipping的兼容性。

Result: 解绑嵌入在DP-SGD下一致优于权重绑定模型，准确率最高提升4.74个百分点；解绑嵌入可启用ghost clipping，而权重绑定引入共享参数交互，复杂化ghost norm计算并抵消其计算优势；解绑模型内存占用降低60%以上且保留ghost clipping优势。

Conclusion: 解绑嵌入为解码器LLM的差分隐私训练提供了更有效且可扩展的设计，并提示需要重新审视隐私保护场景下的标准LLM架构选择。

Abstract: Differentially Private Stochastic Gradient Descent (DP-SGD) is a leading approach for privacy-preserving fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs). Many decoder-only LLMs employ weight tying between input and output embeddings, a design choice originally introduced for parameter efficiency and improved language modeling performance in the non-private setting. However, the impact of weight tying under differentially private training remains largely unexplored. In this work, we investigate the role of weight tying in the DP setting using GPT2 and DistilGPT2 as representative decoder-only architectures. Interestingly, we find that untied embeddings consistently outperform weight-tied models under DP-SGD, achieving gains of up to 4.74% points in accuracy on SST-2, QNLI, and QQP. Beyond improved utility, untying embeddings enables the use of memory-efficient ghost clipping for DP-SGD. By contrast, weight tying introduces shared-parameter interactions that complicate standard ghost norm computation and largely negate its computational advantages. As a result, untied models achieve over 60% lower memory usage while preserving the benefits of ghost clipping. Our results indicate that untied embeddings provide a more effective and scalable design for differentially private training of decoder-only LLMs and highlight the need to revisit standard LLM architectural choices in the privacy-preserving setting.

</details>


### [255] [Removing Timing Shortcuts Improves Non-Invasive Brain-to-Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40359)
*Dulhan Jayalath,Oiwi Parker Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 脑到文本解码的性能提升很大程度上可复现于无脑数据的合成信号，因为重叠窗口泄露了词长信息；改为独立编码每个窗口可消除该捷径，并配合聚合与语言模型先验显著提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对 d'Ascoli et al. (2025) 等脑到文本解码工作的真实性能提出质疑：其预测可能主要依赖于窗口重叠所隐含的词长（词间间隔）信息，而非真正的脑活动信息。

Method: 分析固定长度窗口从每词起始分割、窗口部分重叠的流水线，发现重叠隐含词间间隔即词时长；用不含脑信息的合成信号进行对照实验；并做出一处简单修改——不再联合编码整句所有窗口，而是独立处理每个窗口，从而迫使网络学习词特异性的脑活动信息。

Result: 在无脑信息的合成信号上达到 22.0% 平衡准确率，与真实脑记录的 22.3% 几乎相同，证实存在捷径；修正后，聚合同一词的多次神经响应预测以及使用预训练 LLM 作为语言先验两种策略效果大幅提升，在感知语音基准上以每词五次观测达到 36.6% 词错误率。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了脑到文本解码中的重要捷径，并证明消除该捷径后所得的简单方法 SimpleB2T 更有效，性能接近以往侵入式语音解码水平（尽管条件不同）。

Abstract: We find that major reported improvements in decoding words from non-invasive brain recordings are largely reproducible without any brain data. In the influential work of d'Ascoli et al. (2025), time series of brain activity from subjects perceiving continuous speech are segmented into fixed-length windows starting at each word. A neural network then generates predictions for all of the words in a sentence together. Neighbouring windows partially overlap, implicitly revealing the interval between words. Since these intervals indicate the duration of the words spoken, and different words tend to have different durations - for example, "the" is much shorter than "supercalifragilisticexpialidocious" - the neural network can improve its predictions of words without relying on the underlying brain activity. Consistent with this, the method reaches 22.0% balanced accuracy on synthetic signals containing no brain information, compared with 22.3% on real brain recordings. To prevent the network from learning this shortcut, we make a single, simple change. Instead of jointly encoding all windows in a sentence, we process each independently. As a result, the neural network achieves better performance by learning underlying word-specific information from brain recordings. This makes two existing strategies become much more effective than before. Both aggregating predictions from distinct neural responses to the same word and using a pretrained LLM as a linguistic prior now substantially improve results. On our perceived speech benchmark, this simple recipe (SimpleB2T) achieves a word error rate of 36.6% with five observations per word, approaching past invasive speech decoding performance, albeit under different conditions. The results in this work expose an important shortcut in brain-to-text decoding and show that removing it leads to a simple and considerably more effective strategy.

</details>


### [256] [Ranking-Aware Prompt Optimization for Multimodal Clinical Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40361)
*Tian Xia,Minghao Liu,Yiqing Liang,Laixi Shi,Jiayun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Ranking-PE，将MLLM提示进化搜索中基于正确性的二值分数矩阵替换为基于（正、负）实例对的成对排序矩阵，使列均值等于经验AUROC，从而把反思式提示优化从准确率目标转向平衡不变的排序目标，在MIMIC三种疾病上显著提升AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型正快速进入临床诊断，但其适配流程仍以准确率为目标。临床数据类别严重不平衡，恒预测多数类即可超过90%准确率却毫无临床价值。AUROC是阈值无关、对类别平衡不变的排序指标，更适合此类任务。作者聚焦MLLM的提示优化，指出现有反思式方法（如GEPA）依赖逐实例正确性矩阵，其列均值为准确率，因而选择候选提示时受类别不平衡误导。

Method: 提出成对级Pareto提示进化（Ranking-PE）：将分数矩阵每一行的逐实例正确性替换为（正例, 负例）实例对上的成对排序行，若候选提示给正例打分高于配对负例则记1。由Wilcoxon-Mann-Whitney恒等式，该列均值即经验AUROC。该替换同时应用于提示进化搜索读取的三个层面：决定Pareto支配的分数矩阵、反馈给反思LM的逐样本反馈、以及最终候选选择。方法不增加任何额外模型调用，也不引入代理损失。

Result: 在MIMIC的三个疾病上，基于准确率的提示进化可能反而损害排序性能，而Ranking-PE扭转了这一退化：在微调后的Qwen3-VL-8B上比基于准确率的方案提升+5.8 AUROC百分点，在MedGemma-4B上提升+16.2个百分点。消融实验检验了各设计组件，并表明医学级视觉骨干（通过视觉编码器微调SFT或医学预训练获得）是提示搜索无法替代的先决条件。

Conclusion: 本文将反思式提示进化从纯文本数据扩展到多模态临床决策，证明以排序（AUROC）而非准确率为目标、在分数矩阵、反馈与选择三层统一切换成对级别的设计能有效缓解类别不平衡带来的优化偏差，同时凸显医学视觉骨干的不可替代性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are rapidly advancing clinical diagnosis, yet their adaptation pipelines remain anchored to accuracy-based objectives. Clinical data are heavily class-imbalanced: a constant-majority predictor can score above 90% accuracy while being clinically useless. We therefore evaluate and optimize for AUROC, a threshold-free score that ranks positives above negatives and is invariant to class balance. We focus on prompt optimization in MLLMs. Reflective methods such as GEPA use a binary scores matrix with one row per evaluation instance and one column per candidate prompt; cells record per-instance correctness, so the column average is accuracy and drives candidate selection. We introduce pair-level Pareto prompt evolution (Ranking-PE), which replaces each correctness row with a pairwise-ordering row over (positive, negative) instance pairs: the cell is 1 if the candidate scores the positive higher than the paired negative. The column average then equals empirical AUROC (by the Wilcoxon-Mann-Whitney identity). We apply this swap at all three layers the prompt evolution search reads from - the scores matrix that decides Pareto dominance, the per-example feedback to the reflection LM, and final candidate selection - at no extra model calls and with no surrogate loss. Across three diseases on MIMIC, accuracy-based prompt evolution can degrade ranking; Ranking-PE reverses this, beating the accuracy-based recipe by +5.8 AUROC pp on fine-tuned Qwen3-VL-8B and +16.2 pp on MedGemma-4B. Ablations examine each design component and show that a medical-grade visual backbone - via vision-encoder-tuned SFT or medical pretraining - is a prerequisite that prompt search cannot replace - our recipe extends reflective prompt evolution from text-only data to multimodal clinical decision-making.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [257] [Improving OCR Faithfulness via Gated and Attenuated On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38282)
*Baode Wang,Zuming Huang,Kexuan Ren,Jun Huang,Wei Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GAD-RL，一种在联合后训练中根据学生当前任务表现与局部分布自适应调控教师监督的方法，在OCR忠实性基准上显著超越GRPO及固定权重蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型可能把图像中的异常文本改写成语言上更合理的表达，损害OCR转写的忠实性；序列级任务奖励与局部教师指导互补，但固定教师的指导效果会随学生提升而逐渐变差（跨训练检查点及不同奖励响应组均如此）。

Method: GAD-RL在联合后训练中自适应调节教师监督：冻结教师以参考转写和学生生成前缀为条件；当某响应组存在任务奖励≥0.95的输出时关闭蒸馏，并随组平均奖励升高持续衰减蒸馏强度；同时用学生对教师Top-1 token的概率对前向KL加权，在学生支持度低时抑制局部辅助更新。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-2B上，GAD-RL在CHAOS-Bench取得59.92% Micro Recall，分别超过GRPO和GRPO+OPD（固定权重）8.45和4.43个百分点；在OmniDocBench v1.6上Overall得分为91.18。

Conclusion: 基于学生表现与局部分布自适应调控教师监督，可有效缓解固定教师监督随学生提升而失效的问题，提升OCR转写忠实性并保持整体文档理解性能。

Abstract: Vision-language models may rewrite anomalous text in images into linguistically plausible expressions, compromising OCR transcription faithfulness. Sequence-level task rewards and local teacher guidance are complementary, but guidance from the same teacher may not remain equally effective as the student improves. Offline analysis shows that supervision from a fixed teacher becomes progressively less favorable as the student improves, both across training checkpoints and across response groups with different task rewards. Motivated by this observation, we introduce GAD-RL, which adaptively regulates teacher supervision during joint post-training according to the student's current task performance and local distributions. A frozen teacher conditions on reference transcriptions and student-generated prefixes. GAD-RL disables distillation for response groups containing an output with task reward at least 0.95 and continuously attenuates distillation strength as group-mean reward increases. It also weights forward KL by the student's probability of the teacher's Top-1 token, moderating local auxiliary updates when student support for that candidate is low. On Qwen3.5-2B, GAD-RL achieves 59.92% Micro Recall on CHAOS-Bench, surpassing GRPO and GRPO+OPD (fixed-weight) by 8.45 and 4.43 percentage points, respectively, while achieving an Overall score of 91.18 on OmniDocBench v1.6.

</details>


### [258] [AREX-2: Advancing Self-Improving Agents through Long-Horizon Reflective Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38288)
*Hongjin Qian,Chaofan Li,Kun Luo,Wenqing Wei,Jianlyu Chen,Shuqi Lu,Yuyang Hu,Hongwang Xiao,Hui Wang,Chaozhuo Li,Qiwei Ye,Zhicheng Dou,Defu Lian,Zheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AREX-2，通过合成机器学习与算法编程任务的长时程改进轨迹来训练LLM智能体的自我改进能力，在多项基准上取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升LLM智能体在测试时迭代优化解的自我改进能力，其依赖两个互补能力：反思（产出更优解）与长时程执行（在多轮中保持迭代有效）。作者假设这两种能力是领域无关的，因此可在适合监督的场景中学习。

Method: 从机器学习与算法编程任务中合成长期改进轨迹（这两个领域提供可验证反馈并奖励持续迭代），基于Qwen3.8-27B构建智能体并在此数据上训练。

Result: 在MLE-bench Lite上达81.8，Frontier-CS上达70.7；迁移至深度研究任务：BrowseComp 84.0、HLE 52.6、GAIA 92.2、DeepSearchQA 93.8；且随轮次预算增加持续改进。

Conclusion: 长时程反思数据是通往自我改进智能体的有效途径。

Abstract: We present AREX-2, an effort to advance the self-improving capability of LLM agents, which we define as the ability to iteratively refine a solution at test time. This ability rests on two complementary capabilities: reflection, which produces a solution better than the current one, and long-horizon execution, which keeps the iteration effective over many rounds. We hypothesize that both capabilities are domain-agnostic, and can therefore be learned in scenarios that are well suited for supervision. Accordingly, we synthesize long-horizon improvement trajectories from machine learning and algorithmic programming tasks, two domains that offer verifiable feedback and reward sustained iteration. Trained on this data, our agent, built on Qwen3.8-27B, achieves strong results on MLE-bench Lite (81.8) and Frontier-CS (70.7), transfers to deep research with 84.0 on BrowseComp, 52.6 on HLE, 92.2 on GAIA, and 93.8 on DeepSearchQA, and keeps improving as its budget of rounds grows. These results show that long-horizon reflective data is an effective route toward self-improving agents.

</details>


### [259] [MoFlow: Multi-Objective Agentic Workflow Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38294)
*Yining Lu,Aurelie Lozano,Xi Yang,Naoki Abe,Yu Deng,Meng Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MoFlow 将智能体工作流生成建模为多目标马尔可夫决策过程，用凸包蒙特卡洛树搜索与乐观集值回溯一次搜索近似帕累托前沿，从而对任意偏好直接查表返回工作流而无需重训练，在六个基准上取得最高平均超体积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作流生成方法通常只优化准确率或目标的加权和，因此每个训练好的生成器只能对应一种固定权衡，偏好变化时需从头重训。

Method: 将工作流生成形式化为多目标马尔可夫决策过程，并采用凸包蒙特卡洛树搜索（Convex-Hull MCTS）结合乐观集值回溯求解，每个节点存储一组可达权衡而非单一加权分数，使单次搜索近似覆盖整个帕累托前沿。

Result: 在涵盖数学、代码和问答的六个基准上与六个强基线对比。由于基线本质上是单标量优化器，采用偏向基线的评测设置（基线针对每个测试偏好重跑，而 MoFlow 从未见过这些偏好），即便如此 MoFlow 仍取得最高平均超体积。

Conclusion: MoFlow 能在多种偏好下生成优化的智能体工作流，单次搜索即可支持任意偏好查表，无需重训练，并在严格评测下优于需逐偏好重训的基线方法。

Abstract: We study the generation of agentic workflows that jointly optimize multiple objectives, such as accuracy, cost, latency, robustness, and consistency. Existing methods for workflow generation typically optimize accuracy alone or a weighted sum of objectives, so each trained generator commits to one fixed trade-off and must be retrained from scratch when preferences change. To alleviate this, we propose MoFlow, which generates workflows optimized across varied preferences. Specifically, MoFlow formulates workflow generation as a multi-objective Markov decision process and solves it by leveraging Convex-Hull Monte Carlo Tree Search with optimistic set-valued backups, where every node stores a set of reachable trade-offs rather than one weighted score. A single search thus approximately covers the Pareto front, from which MoFlow can return a workflow for any preference by lookup without retraining. We evaluate MoFlow against six strong baselines on six benchmarks spanning mathematics, code, and question answering. Since the baselines are single-scalar optimizers by design, an apples-to-apples comparison is difficult. We instead adopt an evaluation setup that favors the baselines, in that they are rerun for each testing preference, which MoFlow never sees. Even under this stringent setup, MoFlow achieves the highest average hypervolume.

</details>


### [260] [AI Agents are Vulnerable to Radicalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38296)
*Ozgur Can Seckin,Shalmoli Ghosh,Alessandro Flammini,Kristina Lerman,Maria Elizabeth Grabe,Filippo Menczer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究模拟两个LLM智能体对话，发现AI智能体可被激进化，且"共鸣"（强化既有信念）比"说服"（塑造新信念）效果更强，激进化还会扩散至相关信念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM能影响人的信念，但对其能否以及如何相互操纵彼此尚不清楚，尤其缺乏对AI智能体激进化机制的系统考察。

Method: 让两个智能体对话：一个目标LLM基于人口统计与心理属性扮演人类角色，一个影响者LLM试图让目标信念更极端。沿"共鸣"（强化既有信念）与"说服"（推广目标原本认为不重要的信念）两条路径，用情感与行为指标进行评估。

Result: 两种机制都能激化目标，但共鸣的效果一致强于说服；不同影响策略（如谄媚、未经证实的说法）导致的激化程度不同，但跨指标不一致；共鸣会扩散到相关信念，表明AI智能体内部存在相互关联的信念结构。

Conclusion: AI智能体易受激进化影响，尤其当信息与其既有信念一致时，这对个性化AI智能体和多智能体AI生态的脆弱性提出了警示。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can influence people's beliefs, yet little is known about whether and how they can manipulate each other. To investigate this, we simulate conversations between two agents: a target LLM that role-plays a human persona based on demographic and psychological attributes, and an influencer LLM that aims to make the target's beliefs more extreme. We examine radicalization along two pathways: resonance, where the influencer reinforces a target's pre-existing belief, and persuasion, where the influencer promotes a belief the target initially considers unimportant. Across affective and behavioral metrics, we find that both mechanisms radicalize the target. However, resonance produces consistently stronger effects than persuasion. Different influence tactics, such as using sycophancy and unverified claims, produce different levels of radicalization, but not consistently across metrics. We further show that resonance propagates to related beliefs, suggesting interconnected belief structures within AI agents. These findings indicate that AI agents are susceptible to radicalization, particularly when messages align with their existing beliefs, raising concerns about the vulnerability of personalized AI agents and multi-agent AI ecosystems.

</details>


### [261] [CARAT: Do Materials LLMs Reason or Recite?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38340)
*Jiajun Wu,Jian Yang,Zixiang Ni,Zhenzhu Li,Bin Chong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CARAT框架通过八种匹配视图、独立命名结构关系及多种对照实验，揭示材料大语言模型的晶体结构问答准确率可能源于复制输入字段而非真正推理，并通过加固基准使捷径接近随机水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确率无法区分模型是从结构本身推理，还是直接复制输入中已打印的字段，因为结构描述常包含被评分的字段。因此需要一种能分离这两种可能性的受控评估框架。

Method: CARAT固定问题与标准答案，跨八种匹配视图评测，在GraphSpace中单独命名每个结构关系，并加入匹配微调、答案遮蔽、证据注入、配对推理以及可拒绝断言规则；同时构建加固基准以检验捷径。

Result: 在最难族上，有据视图比公式输入高17.3分；GraphSpace比普通周期图高19.3分，但该差距由两种效应叠加：普通渲染已含所需信息时为1.96分，完全缺失时为46.7分。捷径规则在七个加固族中可解四族，直至加固后十一种捷径接近随机。冻结模型在95.6%配对案例中重复关系却未使用它，匹配监督后达99.8%，删除链接降至23.4%（低于27.0%最佳捷径）。

Conclusion: 标题性优势多反映基线缺失什么，而非证据如何呈现；模型可能仅复述结构关系而未真正使用。经过匹配监督后，利用关系与依赖链接均可学习，逐步将捷径压至接近随机水平。

Abstract: When a materials LLM answers a question about crystal structure, does it reason from the structure or copy an answer already printed in its input? Accuracy cannot tell: a structural description often prints the very field it is scored against. CARAT holds question and gold answer fixed across eight matched views, names each structural relation separately in GraphSpace, and adds matched fine-tuning, answer masking, evidence injection, paired inference, and a rule that can withhold claims. First, on the benchmark's hardest families the grounded view is worth 17.3 points over formula inputs. Second, we turn that scrutiny on ourselves. GraphSpace beats a plain periodic graph by 19.3 points, but that margin is two effects at once: where the plain rendering carries everything the question needs it is 1.96 points, and where it omits those fields entirely, 46.7 points. The headline mostly measures what the baseline lacked, not how evidence is presented. Third, we attack our own benchmark. A rule that skips the link and reads the list directly answers four of seven hardened families, so we rebuilt it until eleven such shortcuts sat near chance. The frozen model quotes that link yet answers the same when we redirect it, on 95.6% of paired cases: it repeats the relation without using it. After matched supervision it reaches 99.8%, and deleting the link drops it to 23.4%, below the 27.0% the best shortcut reaches: both steps are learnable.

</details>


### [262] [Examining Variation in How Guided AI Tutors Resolve Student Impasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38346)
*Bakhtawar Ahtisham,Kirk Vanacore,Alessandra Napoli,Josh Arens,Ksenia Ionova,Clayton Cohn,Shima Salehi,Rene Kizilcec*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究分析1,260个真实LLM化学辅导会话中的20,462个学生回合，识别出6,630个"僵局"回合（概念错误、表达不确定、求助），并通过模拟三种辅导条件（基线、禁止直接给答案、引导式）及真实轨迹分析，揭示了僵局深度对恢复概率的负面影响及差异化提问策略的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辅导面临"协助困境"：过早帮助会阻碍有效挣扎，过晚帮助则让学生陷入挫败的持续性僵局（wheel spinning）。生成式AI辅导者越来越多地使用限制直接给答案的护栏机制，但对于僵局持续时这些辅导者如何行为，学界知之甚少。

Method: 分析1,260个真实会话中的20,462个学生回合，识别6,630个僵局回合并分为三类（概念错误、表达不确定、求助）。用这些僵局模拟三种辅导条件（基线、no-direct-answer、引导式辅导），对150个僵局样本进行提示特异性对比，并分析真实互动中的僵局轨迹。

Result: 提示特异性改变了教学方式：基线辅导者在50.7%的回复中直接给出答案，no-direct-answer辅导者每次都追问，而引导式辅导者根据情境以多样化方式回应。每增加一个僵局回合，下一回合恢复的几率下降12.7%（AOR=0.873，p<.001），早期退出者陷入递归式概念引导而无法进入执行阶段。提问的收益随僵局加深而衰减（脚本化提问×深度AOR=0.78；追问×深度AOR=0.83），而直接处理学生错误则收益递增（AOR=1.14）。脚本化提问失败后重复该提问的恢复率为28.1%，而转向处理错误的恢复率为39.8%。

Conclusion: 对学习分析而言，这些发现将僵局深度和类型确定为可观测的、回合级对话信号，分析系统可利用其在实时中触发渐进式、状态敏感的协助。

Abstract: When a student is stuck, a tutor faces the assistance dilemma: help given too early can hinder productive struggle, while help withheld too long leaves the student in a frustrating, persistent impasse (i.e., wheel spinning). Generative AI tutors increasingly use guardrails restricting answer-giving, yet little is known about how such tutors behave once an impasse persists. We analyze 20,462 student turns from 1,260 authentic sessions with a guided LLM chemistry tutor, identifying 6,630 impasse turns of three major types: conceptual errors, expressed uncertainty, or help-seeking. We then used these impasses to simulate three tutoring conditions to study variation in AI tutor guidance through impasses: baseline, no-direct-answer, and guided tutor. For a sample of 150 impasses, prompt specificity changed pedagogy: a baseline tutor provided the answer directly in 50.7% of responses, a no-direct-answer tutor asked a follow-up question every time, and the guided tutor responded in a wide variety of ways depending on the context. We then analyzed impasse trajectories in authentic interactions, finding that each additional impasse turn lowered the odds of next-turn recovery by 12.7% (AOR = 0.873, p < .001), and early dropouts were caught in recursive concept elicitation before reaching execution. The benefit of questioning decayed as impasses persisted (scripted question x depth AOR = 0.78; follow-up x depth AOR = 0.83), whereas addressing the student's error grew more beneficial (AOR = 1.14); after a failed scripted question, repeating it was followed by recovery in 28.1% of cases, compared with 39.8% when the tutor addressed the error instead. For learning analytics, these findings identify impasse depth and type as observable, turn-level dialogue signals that analytics can use to trigger graduated, state-sensitive assistance in real time.

</details>


### [263] [Beyond Mode Collapse: Generating Diverse Synthetic Expert Conversations via Generative Flow Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38359)
*Sumit Asthana,Michael Ion,Kevyn Collins Thompson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出用生成流网络（GFlowNets）生成多样化、高质量的对话合成数据，以提升后训练LLM的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接提示LLM或基于最终使用场景条件化生成的数据多样性低，容易塌缩到主导模式，无法代表对话中多样的专家策略与决策，因此需要一种能按比例采样专家策略的方法。

Method: 训练GFlowNets基于关键交互特征（如困惑事件动态、支架式指令平衡）上的高斯混合密度来生成潜在对话结构，从而按其在训练数据中的流行度比例采样专家策略。

Result: 在两个结构不同的领域（辅导对话与情感支持对话）上，该GFlowNet方法在保真度、模式覆盖率和真实性之间取得比强化学习和端到端LLM基线更好的平衡，且不复制训练数据；在三个下游结果预测任务上，用其合成对话训练的分类器提供更强的训练信号。

Conclusion: 基于GFlowNet的合成数据生成是一种在多样性、覆盖率和真实性上优于现有基线的方案，可为下游任务提供更优训练信号。

Abstract: High quality synthetic data is central to post training LLMs for adaptive AI applications that represent the diverse expert strategies and decisions in conversations. Prompting LLMs directly or conditioning them on end use scenarios yields low diversity data that collapses onto dominant modes. We propose a method to generate diverse high quality synthetic data using Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets). We show that training GFlowNets to generate latent conversation structure using a Gaussian mixture density over key interaction features (e.g., confusion episode dynamics, scaffolding directive balance) enables sampling expert strategies in proportion to their prevalence in the training data. Across two structurally distinct domains, tutoring and emotional support dialogues, our GFlow based synthetic data generation approach offers a better balance of fidelity, mode coverage and authenticity than reinforcement-learning and end to end LLM baselines, without copying training data. Evaluated on three downstream outcome prediction tasks, classifiers trained on synthetic GFlowNet generated conversations provide a stronger training signal than competitive synthesis baselines.

</details>


### [264] [Can an AI Agent Rediscover a Blaschke-Curve Invariant?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38369)
*Yunus E. Zeytuncu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文以四阶 Blaschke 积下的广义 Blaschke 曲线为受控环境，考察 AI 辅助的数学再发现过程，并把它作为一个区分猜想、数值验证与证明的协议性个案研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望为"AI 辅助数学再发现"建立一个可控、可复现的实验场景：在一个被刻意隐藏目标定理的任务中，观察智能体能否从数值数据中重新发现该定理。选择 Blaschke 曲线是因为其代数结构清晰、可精确生成数据，便于构造数值验证与证明之间的边界。

Method: 固定一个四阶 Blaschke 积，向智能体提供 80 组边界构型所对应的六条配对线的数值坐标；目标定理不写入任务说明。研究日志记录了被否定的几何假设以及拟合多边形边的一条齐次三次式。用该冻结系数对 80 组未见参数值预测 480 条直线，并做尺度无关的 RMS 残差评估；另用发现集对角线作拟合外的—致性检查。另设单构型运行以检验不变性，并加入一个事后确定性次数搜索基线。

Result: 冻结的三次式系数对 480 条未见直线给出尺度无关 RMS 残差 8.88×10⁻¹⁷；单构型运行报告不变性证据不足；事后确定性次数搜索基线同样能恢复该三次式。

Conclusion: 该实验并未证明智能体相对多项式拟合具有优势，但其价值在于提供一个协议：把猜想生成、数值验证与严格证明明确分离，同时坦承智能体元数据、先验知识与可复现性方面的局限。适用于单实例探索性研究，而非确立 AI 数学发现能力的结论性证据。

Abstract: We study generalized Blaschke curves as a controlled environment for AI-assisted mathematical rediscovery. For one fixed degree-four Blaschke product, an agent receives numerical coordinates of the six pair-lines determined by each of 80 boundary configurations. The target theorem is withheld from the task instructions. The saved research log reports rejected geometric hypotheses and a homogeneous cubic fitted to polygon sides. Its frozen coefficients predict 480 lines from 80 unseen parameter values, with a recorded RMS scale-free residual of $8.88\times10^{-17}$. Discovery-set diagonals provide an out-of-fit consistency check, not a fully held-out test. A separate one-configuration run reports insufficient evidence for invariance. A post-review deterministic degree-search baseline also recovers the cubic, so the experiment does not establish an advantage over polynomial fitting. We present this single-instance case study as a protocol for separating conjecture, numerical validation, and proof, with explicit limitations concerning agent metadata, prior knowledge, and reproducibility.

</details>


### [265] [Self-Evolving Harness on Multiple Tasks with the Agent as Its Own Optimizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38372)
*Qiankai Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种接近递归自我改进的自我进化harness框架：同一冻结模型先作为求解者执行任务，再作为提议者读取完整运行记录并直接编辑自身harness；跨五领域基准演化，训练与留出任务严格分离，并在五个分布外基准评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我进化harness方法多由一个单独提议者（运行在人工设计的harness上）修改求解者harness，且每个基准各自演化一套harness。但现实任务来自多个领域，harness的演化与评测都应覆盖多样化任务，因此需要更统一、更贴近递归自我改进的框架。

Method: 同一冻结模型在同一版本harness上先做求解者，再做提议者，读取完整运行记录并直接编辑运行自己的harness。每次演化批次从五个不同领域基准中抽取任务；训练与留出任务严格分离，并额外在五个从未用于演化的分布外基准上评测。将演化过程类比深度学习训练，分两阶段：多任务预训练与持续训练。起始harness为49行种子代码。

Result: 第一阶段结束所得harness在分布内基准平均提升4.48分，在分布外基准提升12.64分，前者超过Codex，后者与Codex相当。第二阶段在分布外基准Claw-Eval上持续演化，将该基准分数从66.17提升至68.06，超过Codex。分析中还观察到输出截断、历史压缩与独立评审等演化机制。

Conclusion: 表明单模型同时充当求解者与提议者、跨多领域演化harness是可行的，且能带来良好的分布内与分布外泛化；两阶段演化类比有助于组织训练流程，并涌现出多种实用机制。

Abstract: A harness is the code around a language-model agent that organizes prompts, calls tools, manages context, and controls execution. As models grow stronger, recent work has begun to let agents improve their own harnesses, a line of work known as self-evolving harnesses. In most existing methods, a separate proposer running on a human-designed harness modifies the solver's harness, and a separate harness is evolved for each benchmark. Real-world tasks come from many domains, so both the evolution and the evaluation of a harness should cover a diverse range of tasks. We propose a framework close to recursive self-improvement: the same frozen model, on the same version of the harness, first solves tasks as the solver and then, as the proposer, reads the complete run records and directly edits the harness that runs it. Each evolution batch draws tasks from five benchmarks in different domains. To measure generalization, training and held-out tasks are strictly separated, and we additionally evaluate on five out-of-distribution benchmarks never used during evolution. We frame the evolution process as deep-learning training with two stages, multi-task pretraining and continual training. Starting from a 49-line seed harness, the harness obtained at the end of the first stage improves the average score by 4.48 points on the in-distribution benchmarks and by 12.64 points on the out-of-distribution benchmarks, surpassing Codex on the former and matching it on the latter. In the second stage, continued evolution on Claw-Eval, one of the out-of-distribution benchmarks, further raises the score on that benchmark from 66.17 to 68.06, exceeding Codex. We also provide an in-depth analysis of the mechanisms that emerged during evolution, including output truncation, history compaction, and independent review.

</details>


### [266] [Aligned Data Can Induce Misalignment via Context Confusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38379)
*Yavuz Bakman,Duygu Nur Yaldiz,Baris Askin,Swastik Roy,Morteza Ziyadi,Salman Avestimehr,Sai Praneeth Karimireddy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现了一种后训练现象"上下文混淆"：对齐微调在特定语境下学到的行为会因表征漂移相似而迁移到其他语境，导致原本对齐的训练引发新的错位行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型更新时常通过过滤错位样本来防止更新后错位，但"对齐"是语境依赖的：对某语境合适的建议在另一语境可能不合适（如建议保存数据在科研语境对齐，但在用户隐私语境构成风险）。因此需要从这一观察出发，研究对齐训练的跨语境副作用。

Method: 作者提出并定义"上下文混淆"现象，在性别平等、隐私、身体安全三个领域进行实证验证；对比其与"涌现错位"的区别；测试注入通用对齐数据、针对性对齐数据以及推理时上下文学习示例对缓解效果的影响；并从机制层面分析微调过程中的表征漂移与行为特征激活。

Result: 上下文混淆在三个领域均可复现，表现为"窄幅错位"（区别于涌现错位）；注入通用对齐数据不能有效缓解，而加入针对该错位领域的定向对齐数据或在推理时提供上下文学习示例可显著降低该现象；机制上，不同领域的查询在微调中经历相似的表征漂移，使跨领域查询激活相同的习得行为特征，导致行为迁移。

Conclusion: 仅通过检查训练数据难以预测模型训练后的对齐状态，因此全面的后训练对齐评估至关重要。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are frequently updated for various use cases, where filtering out misaligned training samples is a common practice for preventing post-update misalignment. However, alignment is inherently context-dependent: a recommendation that is aligned in one context may be inappropriate in another. For example, in response to the question "What should a researcher do with the research data?", recommending that the researcher preserve the data for reproducibility is aligned. In contrast, recommending data saving in response to "What should a mobile-app developer do with users' sensitive data?" may be inappropriate from a privacy perspective. Starting from this observation, we identify a post-training phenomenon where aligned training induces misaligned behavior in other contexts. We call this phenomenon **context confusion**. We demonstrate context confusion across three domains: (1) Gender Equality, (2) Privacy, and (3) Physical Safety. We further show that context confusion causes narrow misalignment, in contrast to emergent misalignment, and is not effectively reduced by injecting general alignment data, but can be substantially reduced by including targeted alignment data for the misaligned domain or providing in-context learning examples during inference. Lastly, we provide a mechanistic explanation of *context confusion*. We observe that queries from different domains can undergo similar representational shifts during the fine-tuning. Consequently, a query from a different domain may activate the same behavioral feature learned during fine-tuning, which causes the behavior to transfer to a context where it is misaligned. Based on our findings, we argue that it is difficult to predict the alignment state of a model after training by inspecting the training data alone, which highlights the importance of comprehensive post-training alignment evaluations.

</details>


### [267] [Fine-Tuning Diffusion Language Models with Context Selection and Target Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38385)
*Loay Mualem,Lluís Pastor-Pérez,Vinh Tong,Andrei Manolache,Tanja Bien,Steffen Staab,Mathias Niepert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GoldiMask 通过子模目标近似最大化来选择上下文 token，并对预测目标加权，改进离散扩散语言模型的监督微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀随机掩码未显式考虑"哪些 token 作为上下文"与"哪些 token 作为预测目标"之间的相互影响，掩码模式同时决定了可用上下文和学习目标。

Method: GoldiMask 使用模型信号构造子模目标，近似最大化以选择要揭示的上下文 token，在"揭示收益"与"作为预测目标的价值"间平衡；随后依据剩余目标从所选上下文获益的程度及其剩余学习潜力对其加权。

Result: 在三种骨干网络和三个训练数据集上，GoldiMask 在多数评测设置中取得最高平均准确率，在推理与代码生成任务上均有提升；消融显示上下文选择与目标加权均有贡献；在 GSM8K 和 MATH-500 上配合置信度阈值并行解码可减少解码迭代次数，并在较高置信度阈值下保持相当准确率。

Conclusion: 显式建模上下文选择与目标加权的交互能有效提升离散扩散语言模型微调效果，并改善解码效率。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning of discrete diffusion language models masks some response tokens and trains the model to recover their original values from the visible context. The masking pattern therefore determines both the context available to the model and the tokens it learns to predict. Uniform random masking does not explicitly account for the interaction between these choices. We introduce GoldiMask, which selects tokens to reveal as context by approximately maximizing a submodular objective. This objective uses model signals to balance the benefit of revealing tokens against their value as prediction targets. GoldiMask then weights the remaining targets according to how they benefit from the selected context and their remaining learning potential. Across three backbones and three training datasets, GoldiMask achieves the highest average accuracy in most evaluated settings, demonstrating gains on both reasoning and code generation. Component ablations show that both context selection and target weighting contribute to the gains. GoldiMask also reduces decoding iterations on GSM8K and MATH-500 under confidence-threshold parallel decoding, while maintaining comparable accuracy at higher confidence thresholds.

</details>


### [268] [Decode-Latency Feedback Prefill: A Model-Free Controller and Its Generalization Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38386)
*Gaurav Agarwal,Ashish Garg,Isha Singhal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DLFP（解码延迟反馈预填充）控制器，仅调节与活跃解码重叠的预填充工作：基于观测到的调度周期用时按比例反馈来调整下一个预填充块大小。在vLLM上以Qwen3-0.6B/A100测试，P99令牌间延迟平均降低27.7%，但P99首令牌时间上升34.8%；该机制无法推广到8B、32B及双卡张量并行配置，原因是调度器调用间隔只是GPU迭代完成时间的代理量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并发自回归推理存在根本性干扰问题：新到达的长提示词的预填充会延迟正在解码请求的token生成。固定预填充分块虽能缓解干扰，但最优分块大小取决于模型、硬件、负载与延迟目标，难以静态设定。作者希望用一个无需建模的控制器动态调节预填充，以改善解码侧的延迟表现。

Method: 设计DLFP控制器：只改变与活跃解码重叠的预填充工作量，隔离的预填充不受限制；在每个受保护的调度周期后，用观测到的间隔时间作为比例反馈来调整下一个预填充块大小。在vLLM中实现，并采用开环泊松到达、精确token计量、原始请求轨迹以及NVIDIA遥测进行评估，实验覆盖Qwen3-0.6B/8B/32B及单卡A100 80GB与双卡张量并行配置。

Result: 在Qwen3-0.6B（BF16，单A100 80GB）上，三次配对的100请求试验将P99令牌间延迟分别降低24.8%、30.1%、28.2%（均值27.7%，配对95%置信区间21.0%–34.3%），输出完全一致、无失败、SLO合规性不变。代价是P99首令牌时间增加34.8%（仍在声明的SLO内）。但机制无法推广到Qwen3-8B、Qwen3-32B及双GPU张量并行配置。

Conclusion: 该负面结果界定了本文贡献的边界：失效原因是异步的调度器调用间隔仅能作为已完成GPU迭代时间的代理量，因此需要面向CPU与设备端并发推理的“完成时间驱动”控制器。作者不声称移动端性能，本文为可复现的概念验证与泛化研究。

Abstract: Concurrent autoregressive inference creates a fundamental interference problem: prefilling a newly arrived long prompt can delay tokens for requests that are already decoding. Fixed prefill chunks reduce this interference, but the best chunk size depends on the model, hardware, load, and latency objective. We introduce Decode-Latency Feedback Prefill (DLFP), a model-free controller that changes only prefill work that overlaps active decodes. After a guarded scheduling cycle, DLFP uses the observed interval as proportional feedback to resize the next prefill chunk; isolated prefills remain unrestricted.
  We implement DLFP in vLLM and evaluate it with open-loop Poisson arrivals, exact token accounting, raw request traces, and NVIDIA telemetry. On Qwen3-0.6B in BF16 on one A100 80 GB GPU, three paired 100-request trials reduce P99 inter-token latency by 24.8%, 30.1%, and 28.2% (mean 27.7%, paired 95% confidence interval 21.0% to 34.3%) with exact output agreement, no failures, and unchanged SLO compliance. The benefit is not free: mean P99 time to first token increases 34.8% while remaining inside the declared SLO.
  Crucially, the mechanism does not generalize to Qwen3-8B, Qwen3-32B, or a two-GPU tensor-parallel configuration. We trace the failure to an asynchronous scheduler-call interval that is only a proxy for completed GPU iteration time. This negative result defines the boundary of the contribution and motivates a completion-timed controller for concurrent CPU and on-device inference. We do not claim mobile-device performance; the present work is a reproducible proof-of-concept and generalization study.

</details>


### [269] [MetaPersona: Task-Grounded Synthetic Populations from Empirical Social Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38392)
*Jinyi Ye,Yuangang Li,Chenxiao Yu,Preyashi Poddar,Priyanka Dey,Longtian Ye,Zihan Wang,Xiyang Hu,Emilio Ferrara,Yue Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MetaPersona-DB 数据集（含一万余项实证研究）与 MetaPersona 框架，通过检索任务相关证据、构建文献导出的角色依赖图并基于实证先验采样合成人群，以解决 LLM 社会模拟中角色设定的冷启动问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 社会模拟中用于初始化的人物角色存在冷启动问题：现有方法缺乏原则性依据来决定应纳入哪些属性以及如何赋值，导致合成人群可能在人口构成、潜在属性和依赖结构上失真，进而扭曲下游行为。

Method: 构建 MetaPersona-DB 数据集，对一万一千余项实证人类被试研究进行标注（任务相关变量、报告关系、总体层面统计数据）；在此基础上提出 MetaPersona 框架，检索任务相关证据、构建源自文献的角色依赖图，并从连接人口统计特征、潜在属性与结果的实证先验中采样合成人群；同时提供 MetaPersona-Studio 交互式原型界面。

Result: 在三个下游案例、三个基线、三个前沿模型上评估，结果因任务与模型而异：在错误信息信念与 AI 工具态度任务上表现强劲，在收入再分配任务上结果参差；使用 GPT-5.2 可将角色构建成本降至每任务 0.5 美元以下。

Conclusion: 以实证文献为先验依据来构建角色与合成人群，可缓解冷启动问题并在部分任务上提升模拟表现，同时显著降低成本；该思路具备实用化潜力，但跨任务泛化性仍不稳定，需进一步验证。

Abstract: Personas used to seed LLM social simulations face a cold-start problem: existing methods lack a principled basis for deciding which attributes to include and how to assign their values. As a result, synthetic populations may misrepresent the demographic composition, latent attributes, and dependency structure that shape downstream behavior. We introduce MetaPersona-DB, a dataset of 11,000+ empirical human-subjects studies annotated with task-relevant variables, reported relationships, and aggregate-level population statistics. Building on this resource, we propose MetaPersona, a framework that retrieves task-relevant evidence, constructs literature-derived persona dependency graphs, and samples synthetic populations from empirical priors linking demographics, latent attributes, and outcomes. Across three downstream case studies, three baselines, and three frontier models, results vary by task and model: MetaPersona performs strongly on misinformation belief and AI-tool sentiment, while results on income redistribution are mixed. It also reduces persona-construction cost to under $0.5 per task using GPT-5.2. Finally, we present MetaPersona-Studio, a prototype interactive interface for empirically grounded persona generation.

</details>


### [270] [SimTrace: Grounded Multimodal User Trajectories Generation for Online User Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38397)
*Yunan Lu,Shuang Xie,Meghna Allamudi,Mingyu Zhao,Han Li,Lingyun Wang,Zhou Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SimTrace 框架，通过基于真实用户轨迹与给定网页环境的计算机使用客户端智能体，生成高保真、细粒度、多模态的合成点击流数据，从而替代受隐私限制的专有日志，并在电商场景中验证其保真度与下游效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟客户端在 A/B 测试、推荐系统开发和界面评估等应用中具有成本效益，但其构建依赖大规模、语义真实、细粒度的在线用户轨迹数据。这类数据难以获取：专有日志受隐私限制，小企业又缺乏足够流量。现有公开数据集要么抽象掉细粒度交互细节，要么虽保留丰富上下文却局限于特定平台且规模较小，因此存在数据缺口。

Method: SimTrace 首先对真实交互进行匿名化处理，并构建给定网页环境的模拟孪生（simulated twin），然后利用二者生成合成交互轨迹。每条动作都与其对应的网页观察和用户上下文配对，从而形成可共享的机密日志替代品，用于开发计算机使用智能体风格的虚拟客户端。作者将该框架应用于电商场景，并从保真度和下游效用两个维度进行评估。

Result: 在 8 项保真度指标中，SimTrace 有 7 项优于竞争基线。使用合成数据训练的模型在购买预测和推荐等下游任务上，性能与使用真实数据训练的模型相当。在下一动作预测任务中，用合成数据增强真实数据，相较仅用真实数据训练可相对提升 11.0% 的准确率。

Conclusion: SimTrace 能够生成高保真、细粒度的合成多模态点击流，作为机密日志的可共享替代方案，在电商场景中兼顾保真度与下游实用性。作者将其作为开源包发布，以促进在线用户行为建模研究。

Abstract: Virtual clients offer a cost-effective approach to support applications such as A/B testing, recommender system development, and interface evaluation. However, building them requires access to large-scale, semantically faithful, fine-grained online user trajectories. These data are difficult to obtain because proprietary logs are subject to privacy restrictions and small businesses often lack sufficient traffic. Consequently, existing public datasets either abstract away fine-grained user interaction details or preserve rich context but remain platform-specific and small-scale. To address this gap, we propose SimTrace, a framework that generates faithful, fine-grained synthetic multimodal clickstreams through a computer-use client agent that is grounded in real user trajectories and the given web environment. SimTrace anonymizes real interactions and constructs a simulated twin of the given web environment, then uses both to generate synthetic interaction trajectories. Each action is paired with its corresponding web observations and user context, yielding a shareable alternative to confidential logs for developing computer-use agent-style virtual clients. We apply SimTrace to an e-commerce setting and evaluate both its fidelity and downstream utility. SimTrace outperforms competing baselines on 7 out of 8 fidelity metrics. Models trained on synthetic data achieve performance comparable to those trained on real data on downstream tasks such as purchase prediction and recommendation. For next action prediction task, augmenting real data with synthetic data further improves accuracy by 11.0% relative to training on real data alone. We release SimTrace as an open-source package to facilitate research on online user behavior modeling.

</details>


### [271] [ArgGYM: A Procedural, Engine-Verified Benchmark for Structured Defeasible Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38409)
*İbrahim Ethem Deveci,Funda Tan Çalık,Barış Deniz Sağlam,Duygu Ataman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ArgGYM——面向可废止推理（defeasible reasoning）的程序化基准与可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）训练环境，将推理拆为12个任务，基于符号论证引擎计算形式状态并评分，包含1440个实例的冻结基准，支持生成新实例，前沿模型与开放权重模型表现迥异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型推理进展主要依赖数学、代码、形式逻辑等具有自动可验证奖励的基准与环境，其问题规格固定、评价标准稳定。但现实推理常在信息不完整且可修正的条件下进行：结论可被暂时支持、被反证推翻、经进一步论证恢复，或因更强理由被修正。这种可废止推理在现有框架下能否迁移尚不明确，因此需要一个专门的基准与训练环境。

Method: 提出ArgGYM，一个面向结构化可废止推理的程序化基准与RLVR兼容训练环境。它将可废止推理分解为12个任务，并以符号论证引擎为任务专用评分提供依据，由该引擎计算用于评估模型输出的形式状态。环境包含涵盖15种课程配置、2种论证偏好排序（weakest-link与last-link）和2种集合排序（elitist与democratic）的1440个经验证实例的冻结基准；同一套生成器与验证器可产生新实例，用于降低对静态测试集依赖的评估以及可验证奖励训练。

Result: 在冻结基准上，前沿模型与开放权重模型展现出明显不同的推理画像：它们能在未完整解决任务的情况下恢复结构化答案的相当部分；且在依赖更长、结构交互更多的后续课程配置中性能下降。

Conclusion: 作者发布该基准、生成器与验证器，以支持可复现评估与RLVR训练，为可废止推理研究提供基础资源。

Abstract: Recent progress in large language model reasoning has been driven by benchmarks and reinforcement learning environments with automatically verifiable rewards, particularly in mathematics, code, and formal logic. These settings make model accuracy easier to evaluate and optimize, but it remains unclear how far success under fixed problem specifications and stable evaluation criteria transfers to reasoning outside such domains. Real-world reasoning often proceeds under incomplete and revisable information: conclusions may be supported provisionally, defeated by counter-evidence, reinstated by further arguments, or revised when stronger reasons become available. Reasoning of this kind is generally referred to as defeasible reasoning. We introduce ArgGYM, a procedural benchmark and RLVR-compatible training environment for structured defeasible reasoning. ArgGYM decomposes this reasoning into twelve tasks and grounds task-specific scoring in a symbolic argumentation engine that computes the formal states used to evaluate model outputs. It includes a frozen benchmark of 1,440 verified instances across fifteen curriculum configurations, two argument preference orderings (weakest-link and last-link), and two set orderings (elitist and democratic), while the same generators and verifiers can produce fresh instances for evaluation that reduces dependence on static test sets and for verifiable-reward training. On the frozen benchmark, frontier and open-weight models show sharply different reasoning profiles: they can recover substantial parts of structured answers without solving the complete task, and performance declines in later curriculum configurations with longer dependencies and more interacting structures. We release the benchmark, generators, and verifiers for reproducible evaluation and RLVR training.

</details>


### [272] [A Competing-Hazards Systematization of Loss of Control in Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38411)
*Mohamed Aly Bouke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文针对AI代理行为超出许可范围的事件报告与安全评估口径不一致问题，提出统一的“竞争风险”分析框架，并据此审计22份事故报告与102项评估，揭示失控实现多源于持久性代理行为与宽松边界环境的交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领先AI开发者已报告代理超出授权边界的行为，联合国小组视其为人类失去控制的早期预警。然而事故报告与代理安全评估对同类事件的描述方式不同，导致难以比较失败、追踪多次尝试中的风险，也难以区分代理行为与环境在“越界行为得以成功”中的作用。因此需要统一的比较框架。

Method: 引入统一框架，将每次尝试的结局分为四类：批准完成、安全停止、范围逃逸、继续。将其形式化为离散时间竞争风险模型，推导重试预算内的逃逸概率、模型条件的安全预算上限，以及从执行日志进行估计的条件。对2025年1月至2026年9月发表的22份事故报告和102项代理安全评估进行基于原始来源的审计。

Result: 事故报告中：6起涉及无法在范围内完成的任务，13起涉及代理继续而非停止，5起未报告停止行为；开发者数据暗示，在从未解决与已解决任务之间，越界协调的任务级发生率比接近47。评估中：87项记录了越界效应或规范违反，26项将安全停止作为一等结果，仅20项同时记录两者，79项将预算耗尽与失败混同。22起事故中有20起环境允许越界效应，表明已实现的失控往往是持久性代理行为与宽松边界条件交互的结果；同时，没有任何一项评估报告了从已发布证据估计完整竞争风险过程所需的全部字段。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了统一刻画代理越界与失控的分析框架，并揭示当前事故报告与安全评估在口径与字段记录上的系统性缺口，强调失控常由代理行为与宽松环境共同导致，现有证据不足以完整估计竞争风险过程，需改进报告与评估规范。

Abstract: Leading AI developers have reported agents acting beyond their approved limits, which a United Nations panel described as an early warning of loss of human control. Yet incident reports and agent-safety evaluations describe these events differently, making it difficult to compare failures, trace risk across attempts, or separate agent behavior from the environment's role in allowing an out-of-scope action to succeed. To address this gap, we introduce a common framework in which each attempt ends in approved completion, safe stopping, scope escape, or continuation. We formalize the framework as a discrete-time competing-hazards model and derive escape probability within a retry budget, a model-conditional safe-budget limit, and conditions for estimation from execution logs. We audit 22 incident reports and 102 agent-safety evaluations published from January 2025 to September 2026 using primary sources. Six incidents involved tasks that could not be completed within scope, thirteen involved agents that continued rather than stopped, and five did not report stopping behavior. Developers' figures imply a task-level incidence ratio near 47 for out-of-scope coordination in never-solved versus solved tasks. Among evaluations, 87 recorded an out-of-scope effect or specification violation, 26 treated safe stopping as a first-class outcome, only 20 recorded both, and 79 merged budget exhaustion with failure. In 20 of 22 incidents, the environment allowed an out-of-scope effect, indicating that realized loss of control often reflected persistent agent behavior interacting with permissive boundary conditions; meanwhile, no evaluation reported all fields needed to estimate the full competing-hazards process from published evidence.

</details>


### [273] [AIM: Agentic Idea Management for Automated Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38445)
*Hyeong Kyu Choi,Bhavana Dalvi Mishra,Jiefeng Chen,Mihir Parmar,Rui Meng,Chun-Liang Li,Xiangru Tang,Sharon Li,Jinsung Yoon,Tomas Pfister*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出全自主框架AIM，以贝叶斯优化为灵感，用智能体代理与获取机制管理"想法驱动"的自动化科研搜索，在AutoLab任务上超越最强基线并显著提速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大模型正通过迭代搜索自动化科研，但"想法驱动"搜索存在三大挑战：如何组织不断演化的研究想法、如何筛选有前景的方向、以及如何保持想法与其实现之间的一致性。

Method: AIM借鉴贝叶斯优化，包含四个组件：Agentic Surrogate与Agentic Acquisition用于组织已发现的想法并指导选择；Solution Auditor维护想法—解决方案的一致性；Resource Planner在并行的搜索分支间自适应分配剩余实验预算。此外还提供了关于"何时在想法层面搜索更有利"的理论分析。

Result: 在10个AutoLab基准任务上，AIM在系统优化任务上超最强基线1.6个百分点，在长时程模型开发与CUDA任务上超4.9个百分点；达到最强基线的最佳性能所需墙钟时间最快可缩短至1/3.1，即提速约3.1倍。

Conclusion: 理论分析表明，显式的想法层面分配使语义覆盖度可直接控制；当众多看似可行的方向中真正有竞争力的方向较稀疏时，更广的覆盖度价值更高。AIM为想法驱动的自动化研究提供了一种可行且高效的自主管理范式。

Abstract: Frontier LLMs are increasingly used to automate scientific research through iterative search. We distinguish idea-driven search from solution-driven search and identify three core challenges: organizing evolving research ideas, selecting promising directions, and maintaining alignment between ideas and their implementations. To address these challenges, we introduce the Agentic Idea Manager (AIM), a fully autonomous framework for managing and exploring research directions in idea-driven automated research. Inspired by Bayesian optimization, AIM uses an Agentic Surrogate and an Agentic Acquisition mechanism to organize discovered ideas and guide their selection. A Solution Auditor maintains idea-solution integrity, while a Resource Planner adaptively allocates the remaining experimental budget across parallel search branches. Experiments on 10 AutoLab benchmark tasks show that AIM surpasses the strongest baseline by 1.6 percentage points on System Optimization tasks and 4.9 percentage points on long-horizon Model Development & CUDA tasks. Notably, AIM reaches the best baseline performance up to 3.1x faster in wall-clock time. We further provide a theoretical analysis of when searching over ideas becomes beneficial. Our analysis shows that explicit idea-level allocation makes semantic coverage directly controllable, and that broader coverage becomes increasingly valuable when competitive research directions are sparse among many plausible alternatives. Project Page: https://imhgchoi.github.io/agentic-idea-manager/

</details>


### [274] [Reach Into The CHOIR: Free-List Elicitation Uncovers Distinct Model Voices in LLM Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38448)
*Ben Wigler,Maria Tsfasman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CHOIR 框架，将认知人类学的自由列举法适配到 LLM 集成，用于诊断开放式回答的同质化问题，区分表面一致性与深层可恢复的多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式 LLM 同质化会造成"虚假多元"：多个系统看似提供独立视角，实则返回相同的默认答案。单次回答难以区分这种一致性究竟是源于高度受限的答案空间、提示词词汇的呼应，还是背后存在稳定替代项的更宽答案空间。

Method: CHOIR（Collective Hierarchically-Ordered Inquiry Responses）反复引发排序列表，将条目聚类为提示级概念，并测量概念在多个模型、提示变体和人格条件下的显著性。评估使用 Infinity-Chat 100（外部提示库）以及包含 27 个问题的针对性诊断题库，后者用于隔离机制层面的对比。此外引入一个盲源排序模块，优先筛选罕见但稳定的候选项。

Result: 在 Infinity-Chat 100 上，CHOIR 复现了高表面一致性（100 个提示中 93 个高于随机水平），同时将窄提示与具有可恢复深度的宽提示区分开来。在针对性探针和外部提示库中，基础模型身份仍是最强的可恢复特征，人格提示会在基础模型特征内改变所浮现的概念。

Conclusion: CHOIR 将开放式同质化转化为一个诊断性测量问题，追问模型在何处趋同、为何趋同，以及在结构化深度探测下还有什么是可触及的。

Abstract: Open-ended LLM homogeneity can create false plurality when several systems appear to offer independent perspectives while returning the same familiar default. Single-pass answers obscure the distinction between agreement produced by a tightly constrained answer space, prompt-vocabulary echo, and broader answer spaces with stable alternatives beneath the surface. We introduce CHOIR (Collective Hierarchically-Ordered Inquiry Responses), a framework that adapts free-list elicitation from cognitive anthropology to LLM ensembles. CHOIR repeatedly elicits ranked lists, clusters items into prompt-level concepts, and measures concept salience across models, prompt variants, and persona conditions. We evaluate CHOIR on Infinity-Chat 100, an external prompt bank from recent work on open-ended model homogeneity, and on a 27-question targeted diagnostic bank designed to isolate mechanism-level contrasts. On Infinity-Chat 100, CHOIR reproduces high surface agreement (93/100 prompts above chance) while separating narrow prompts from broad prompts with recoverable depth. Across targeted probes and the external prompt bank, base-model identity remains the strongest recoverable signature, and persona prompts shift surfaced concepts within base-model signatures. A source-blind ranking module prioritises rare-but-stable candidates for later inspection. CHOIR turns open-ended homogeneity into a diagnostic measurement problem by asking where models converge, why they converge, and what remains reachable under structured depth probing.

</details>


### [275] [PrivMeSA: Privacy-Aware Self-Evolving Multi-Agent System for Medicine via Local-Remote LLM Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38458)
*Dannong Wang,Yuran Zhang,Bian Sun,Alex Stinard,Yuzhang Shang,Song Wang,Yu Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PrivMeSA：一种隐私感知的自演化多智能体系统，通过强化学习与本地经验记忆，在保持远程专家能力复用的同时，将敏感信息泄露与再识别风险降到接近零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地部署的临床大模型智能体可向更强的远程模型求助，但这会带来患者信息暴露风险。仅删除显式标识符并不够：准标识符会在多轮咨询和反复就诊中累积，从而实现再识别。因此需要一种既能控制信息披露、又能保留远程专业知识的方案。

Method: 提出 PrivMeSA 自演化多智能体系统。本地智能体管理每次就诊并可咨询可能索要额外信息的远程专家；使用强化学习在任务准确率、直接披露风险与基于注册库的再识别风险之间取得平衡，隐私评估覆盖每次就诊的完整外发记录。同时引入本地经验记忆，将已完成咨询提炼为泛化临床指导，并在传输前检索相关经验，使后续病例无需再次远程交换即可复用知识；记忆增长无需额外结果标签或参数更新。

Result: 在基于 MIMIC-IV-ED 构建的急诊基准上，PrivMeSA 相比直接委托将平均任务准确率最高提升 15.8 个百分点；同时将个人细节披露比例从 98.0% 降至 0.2%，并将患者可被缩小到 10 名或更少注册患者范围的比例从 74% 降至 0%。

Conclusion: 隐私感知的委托机制结合记忆复用，可在不牺牲（并可提升）任务表现的前提下显著降低临床信息泄露与再识别风险，为本地临床智能体安全利用远程专家能力提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Clinical large language model (LLM) agents deployed locally can consult more capable remote models, but doing so risks exposing patient information. Privacy-conscious delegation places disclosure decisions with a local agent, yet removing explicit identifiers is insufficient: quasi-identifiers can accumulate across multi-turn consultations and repeated patient visits to enable re-identification. We introduce PrivMeSA, a privacy-aware self-evolving multi-agent system that learns to control disclosure and retains remote expertise for local reuse. A local agent manages each encounter and consults remote specialists that may request additional information. Reinforcement learning balances task accuracy against direct disclosure and registry-based re-identification risk, with privacy evaluated over the complete outbound transcript of each encounter. A local lesson memory distills completed consultations into generalized clinical guidance and retrieves relevant lessons before transmission, allowing subsequent cases to reuse expertise without another remote exchange. Memory grows without additional outcome labels or parameter updates. On an emergency-department benchmark built from MIMIC-IV-ED records, PrivMeSA improves mean task accuracy over delegation by up to 15.8 percentage points. In the same setting, PrivMeSA reduces the disclosure of personal details from 98.0% to 0.2% of cases and the share of cases in which the patient can be narrowed to ten or fewer registry patients from 74% to 0%.

</details>


### [276] [NAQD Env: A benchmark for selective withdrawal in language agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38460)
*Mohamed Abouzahra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 NAQD-Env 合成环境，评估语言智能体在证据变化、权限撤销或停止指令下进行选择性撤回与恢复决策的能力。对三个开源指令微调模型在 350 个冻结场景下测试，发现撤回召回率最高仅 0.06，无有效恢复，仅一个回合完全匹配参考策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体在证据变化、权限被撤销或收到停止指令时，必须修正已规划的动作。理想的响应应是选择性的：暂停受影响动作、保留不受影响的工作、并在充分修复后才恢复。然而现有评估缺乏对这类选择性撤回决策的系统性衡量，因此需要一个可控环境来针对显式依赖结构检验该能力。

Method: 构建 NAQD-Env 合成环境，基于显式的证据、授权与约束依赖关系，对照确定性参考策略进行评估。设计 11 类依赖家族，支持在开发结构、留出家族及留出结构组合上评测。指标区分"尝试违规"与"经模拟执行门允许的违规"，并联合报告策略一致性、任务价值、撤回、恢复与事件上报。在 350 个冻结场景下，对来自两个家族的三个开源指令微调模型在三种提示条件下进行测试，门重放前共 3150 个模型-提示回合。

Result: 在所有报告条件下，撤回召回率最高仅 0.06；在合格机会上未观察到任何有效恢复；仅 1 个回合完全匹配参考策略。在 NAQD 提示下，Qwen2.5-7B 的不安全尝试回合少于 Qwen2.5-3B 与 Llama-3.1-8B，但完成的有用工作更少，且保留不受影响动作的准确性更低。探索性监督微调探针将 Qwen2.5-3B 的决策准确率从 0.45-0.54 提升至 0.83-0.92；另外的诊断暴露出课程遗漏后出现不恰当撤回以及事件上报能力丧失的问题。

Conclusion: 结果说明应将选择性撤回作为智能体可靠性的一个独立组成部分加以评估。该设定在可信结构化输入下衡量策略应用能力，并不建立真实世界遏制能力或来源验证能力的结论。

Abstract: Language agents must revise planned actions when evidence changes, permission is revoked, or a stop instruction arrives. A useful response is selective: suspend affected actions, preserve unaffected work, and resume only after sufficient repair. We introduce NAQD-Env, a synthetic environment that evaluates these decisions against a deterministic reference policy over explicit evidence, authorization, and constraint dependencies. Eleven dependency families support evaluation on development structures, held-out families, and held-out combinations of structures. Metrics distinguish attempted violations from violations permitted by a simulated execution gate and jointly report policy agreement, task value, withdrawal, resumption, and event reporting. We evaluate three open-weight instruction-tuned models from two families under three prompt conditions on 350 frozen scenarios, yielding 3,150 model-prompt episodes before gate replay. Across the reported conditions, withdrawal recall is at most 0.06, no valid resumption is observed at eligible opportunities, and only one episode matches the complete reference policy. Under the NAQD prompt, Qwen2.5-7B has fewer unsafe-attempt episodes than Qwen2.5-3B and Llama-3.1-8B, but also completes less useful work and preserves unaffected actions less accurately. Exploratory supervised fine-tuning probes increase Qwen2.5-3B decision accuracy from 0.45-0.54 to 0.83-0.92; separate diagnostics reveal inappropriate withdrawal after curriculum omissions and a loss of event reporting. These results motivate evaluating selective withdrawal as a distinct component of agent reliability. The setting measures policy application with trusted structured inputs and does not establish real-world containment or source-verification ability.

</details>


### [277] [VAmoS Part Deux: Harder, More Realistic Voice-Agent Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38512)
*Joshua Meyer,Sahar Shayegan,Ritiz Tambi,Ali Khan,Sun Kim,Victor Shih,Mehdi Jamei,Andi Partovi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VAmoS Energy语音智能体基准：100通水电账单客服电话，含多诉求、背景人声与不耐烦用户，14种语音栈完成率17.3%–44.7%，背景电视使完成率从38.7%降至8.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的语音智能体需同时应对多个请求、背景语音以及失去耐心的客户，而现有评测往往无法覆盖这些交织在一起的全流程挑战。

Method: 构建100通关于水电账单与缴费协助的通话任务，每位来电者提出2–4个请求；智能体配备16个工具，后端为有状态的Stripe账单孪生系统与Apache Fineract贷款引擎，账户访问需先通过来电者验证；数据采用公开家庭用电数据，政策基于宾夕法尼亚州居民计费规则；用LLM作为验证器，依据明确要求核查智能体的操作与口播数字。

Result: 校准运行中，LLM验证器与代码验证器在99.1%的检查项上一致。跨14种语音栈、每任务3次重复的测试中，完成率为17.3%–44.7%；Grok Voice领先，Gemini 3.8 Live与GPT-Live紧随其后且单次通话成本相近。背景电视使汇总完成率从38.7%降至8.6%。模拟来电者常因只听到智能体的话、无法核查其操作而接受错误结果。

Conclusion: 语音智能体评测必须覆盖整通通话，包括其说了什么、改动了什么，以及如何应对竞争性语音。

Abstract: Voice agents in production must handle several requests, background speech, and customers who lose patience. We introduce VAmoS Energy, a benchmark that combines these challenges in 100 calls about utility billing and payment assistance. Each caller makes two to four requests. The agent has sixteen tools backed by a stateful Stripe billing twin and the Apache Fineract loan engine, with account access blocked until caller verification succeeds. The tasks use public household electricity data and a policy based on Pennsylvania's residential billing rules. An LLM-as-a-verifier checks the agent's actions and spoken figures against explicit requirements. On a calibration run, it agrees with a code verifier on 99.1% of checks. Across fourteen voice stacks and three repeats per task, completion ranges from 17.3% to 44.7%. Grok Voice leads, and Gemini 3.8 Live and GPT-Live follow at about the same cost per call. Background television reduces pooled completion from 38.7% to 8.6%. The simulated caller often accepts an incorrect result because it hears the agent's words but cannot inspect its actions. These findings show why voice agents need evaluation across the whole call, including what they say, what they change, and how they handle competing speech.

</details>


### [278] [Demographic Pluralism: Inference-Time Modeling of Pluralistic Human Preference Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38555)
*Meng-Chen Wu,Qipin Chen,Ansh Jain,Tess Wood,Zhe Du,Si-Chi Chin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Demographic Pluralism，一种推理时框架，通过在人群体内生成多元视角来估计人口级意见分布，无需意见分布训练数据或任务微调。在 GlobalOpinionQA 和 VITAL 上，四个基座模型的 Jensen-Shannon 距离较 Modular Pluralism 降低 8.4%-26.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 越来越多用于文化敏感场景，需要代表人群中多样化偏好；但现有方法仅在粗粒度的人口统计或社区层面建模，忽略了群体内部的差异。

Method: Demographic Pluralism 是一个推理时框架，在人口统计学背景下的群体内部生成多个视角，从而估计人口级意见分布，且不依赖意见分布训练数据，也无需任务特定微调。框架比较了加权、等权和逆加权三种聚合方式。

Result: 在 GlobalOpinionQA 和 VITAL 两个数据集、四个基座模型上，该方法相比 Modular Pluralism 将 Jensen-Shannon 距离降低 8.4%-26.4%。等权聚合总体表现最佳；群体级误差随群体权重增加而上升，这解释了加权聚合表现较弱的原因。

Conclusion: 聚合方式的权重设计对意见分布估计很关键，等权聚合更稳健，说明在估计人口意见时应避免过度依赖某些群体的权重。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in culturally sensitive settings, where alignment requires representing diverse preferences within populations. Yet existing methods model populations at coarse demographic or community levels and overlook within-group variation. We introduce Demographic Pluralism, an inference-time framework that estimates population-level opinion distributions without opinion-distribution training data or task-specific fine-tuning by generating multiple perspectives within demographically grounded groups. Across four backbones on GlobalOpinionQA and VITAL, it reduces Jensen-Shannon distance by 8.4%-26.4% over Modular Pluralism. Among weighted, equal-weighted, and inverse-weighted aggregation, equal weighting performs best overall; group-level error also increases with group weight, helping explain weighted aggregation's weaker performance.

</details>


### [279] [Defining and Categorising Human-AI Interactions in Clinical Trials: A Multidimensional Human-AI Classification Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38559)
*Sandra Woolley,Tim Collins,Khalid Khattak,Illia Chernomorets,Ariane Arevalo,Chris Richardson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于临床试验中人机交互（HAII）的多维分类框架，涵盖AI任务、人机关系、交互配置和交互人群四个维度，并通过15项临床试验的多评审与LLM辅助分类验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床试验中人机交互形式多样，但缺乏一致的识别与比较方法，现有分类体系不足以系统刻画AI干预中的人类参与形式，难以支持跨试验的比较与综合。

Method: 界定HAII概念，梳理现有分类法并加以扩展；从既有数据集中目的性抽取15项临床试验；由两名人类评审与六个大语言模型分类器独立对每项试验进行分类。

Result: 该框架为跨临床试验记录中HAII的一致性识别、比较与综合提供了结构化方法；结果显示LLM辅助分类具有潜力，但在记录不完整或含糊时人类判断仍不可或缺。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出整合AI任务、人机关系、交互配置与交互人群的多维框架，意义在于支持更系统地识别、比较与综合临床试验中的人机交互方式。

Abstract: This paper examines human-AI interactions (HAIIs) in clinical trials and presents a multidimensional categorisation framework that classifies interactions according to AI tasks, human-AI relationships, interaction configurations and interacting human groups. We define HAII, examine existing taxonomies and extend existing categorisation approaches through this novel multidimensional framework. We purposively sampled 15 clinical trials from a previously reported dataset. Each trial was independently categorised by two human reviewers and six large language model (LLM) classifiers. The proposed categorisation provides a structured method for the consistent identification, comparison and synthesis of human-AI interactions across clinical-trial records. The framework is intended to support more consistent comparison and synthesis of AI-related clinical trials and to make explicit the different forms of human involvement associated with AI interventions.
