Motivation: 语言智能体需解决日益复杂的任务，持续改进需求强烈。一种思路是保持底层语言模型不变，只进化管理工具调用、记忆与任务执行的外壳（harness）。已有自动化方法依赖元编码智能体的既有知识和观测到的失败来修改外壳，会限制探索空间，并使适应过程变得被动、滞后。

Method: 受人类专家从科研文献中学习新方案启发，ScholarEvolve 自动利用前沿研究来引导外壳进化。它将进化方向按功能模块组织，用主题建模为每个模块识别不同的改进策略；随后实现这些策略并评估其组合以提升任务表现。框架还支持随时间纳入新发表文献，让研究进展驱动主动的终身进化。

Result: 在 AppWorld 与 Tau2-Bench 上取得提升：AppWorld Challenge 上把 Qwen3.5-27B 的任务目标完成率从 49.6% 提升到 63.6%；Tau2-Bench Telecom 上把 GPT-5.4-mini 的 pass@1 从 72.7% 提升到 81.9%。

Conclusion: 以科研文献驱动的外壳进化能有效且主动地提升语言智能体的任务表现，并具备随新文献持续终身进化的潜力。

Abstract: Language agents are expected to solve increasingly complex tasks, creating a growing need for continual improvement. One promising approach is to evolve the agent harness, the software that governs tool use, memory management, and task execution, while keeping the underlying language model fixed. Recent methods automate this process by using a meta coding agent to modify the harness based on execution feedback. However, relying on that agent's existing knowledge and observed failures can restrict exploration and make adaptation reactive. Inspired by how human experts learn from the research literature for new solutions, we introduce ScholarEvolve, a framework that automatically draws on state-of-the-art research to guide harness evolution. ScholarEvolve organizes the harness evolution directions into functional modules and uses topic modeling to identify distinct improvement strategies for each module. It implements these strategies and evaluates their combinations to improve task performance. Moreover, the framework is designed to incorporate new publications over time, allowing research advances to drive proactive lifelong evolution. Experiments demonstrate improvements on AppWorld and Tau2-Bench. ScholarEvolve raises Qwen3.5-27B task goal completion from 49.6% to 63.6% on AppWorld Challenge, and raises GPT-5.4-mini pass@1 from 72.7% to 81.9% on Tau2-Bench Telecom.

</details>


### [384] [Belief-Aware Multi-Agent Path Finding under Map Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40269)
*Viraj Parimi,Shao-Hung Chan,Han Zhang,Jingkai Chen,Brian Williams*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出MAGIC框架，针对Belief-Aware MAPF问题，利用高斯马尔可夫随机场与高斯置信传播在线推断通行性，构建绕行感知代价，实验显示在96.3%的实例上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典MAPF假设所有静态障碍先验已知，但现实环境中会出现物体掉落、液体泼洒等意外扰动，且这些变化在空间上具有相关性，一次观测可为观测点之外的通行性估计提供信息。现有方法依赖直接观测的应急规划或重规划，无法利用这种空间依赖性推断邻近未观测位置的通行性，因而错失提前预判、避免后续高昂绕行的机会。

Method: MAGIC框架基于智能体的在线观测更新共享通行性置信。它采用高斯马尔可夫随机场建模空间相关性，并通过高斯置信传播进行近似推断，从而构造绕行感知的代价函数，供标准MAPF规划器使用。

Result: 在MAPF基准测试中，MAGIC在96.3%的实例上降低了执行后的总代价，优于现有方法；实验覆盖多种规划器家族以及最多800个智能体的团队规模，表明其适用于大规模MAPF问题。

Conclusion: MAGIC通过在线共享置信推断与空间相关性建模，有效提升Belief-Aware MAPF中执行阶段的总代价表现，并具备大规模扩展能力。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) aims to find collision-free paths for multiple agents in a shared environment. Classical MAPF assumes that all static obstacles are known in advance, but real-world environments can change unexpectedly due to fallen objects, spills, or other local disturbances. When such changes are spatially correlated, an observation can inform traversability estimates beyond the observed location. Prior approaches address uncertainty in traversability through contingent plans or replanning based on direct observations, but do not leverage this spatial dependence to infer the traversability of nearby unobserved locations. As a result, they cannot use one observation to anticipate nearby unobserved obstacles that may cause costly rerouting later. We focus on Belief-Aware MAPF, where map discrepancies are fixed during execution but initially unknown, and observations can be informative beyond the observed location. We propose Multi-Agent Gaussian belief Inference for Coordination (MAGIC), a framework that updates a shared belief about traversability online based on agents' observations. MAGIC uses a Gaussian Markov Random Field and Gaussian Belief Propagation to approximately infer traversability and construct detour-aware costs for standard MAPF planners. Our experiments on MAPF benchmarks show that MAGIC reduces the executed sum of costs compared to existing approaches on 96.3% of instances, across several planner families and teams of up to 800 agents, demonstrating its applicability to large-scale MAPF problems.

</details>


### [385] [PivotOPD: Learning to Recover from Pivotal Mistakes in Multi-Turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40285)
*Yinghui He,Yapei Chang,Khushi Bhardwaj,Daniele Molinari,Tugrul Konuk,Jan Kautz,Ali Hatamizadeh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PivotOPD：在多轮交互的在线策略蒸馏中，针对"关键错误"同时进行预防性蒸馏与恢复性蒸馏，显著提升语言智能体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）能为学生模型在其自身生成的轨迹上提供密集的教师监督，但在多轮交互中，一个错误动作会改变学生后续遇到的状态，导致错误逐轮累积。作者在三个Qwen3模型（8B–235B）上发现：超过一半的失败轨迹包含一个"关键错误"（使智能体偏离任务完成的动作），且通常出现在早期；但这些关键错误往往仍可挽回——在关键轮之后仅引导模型几轮即可恢复任务成功。

Method: 提出PivotOPD框架，联合训练学生"防止关键错误"与"从关键错误造成的状态中恢复"。在每个关键错误处，教师模型给出黄金动作，并在随后几轮中给出恢复动作。预防性蒸馏用黄金动作配合反向KL，使学生远离关键错误；恢复性蒸馏用恢复动作配合前向KL，迁移学生很少采样到的恢复行为。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和基于搜索的问答任务上对比13个基线，PivotOPD对Qwen3-1.7B和Qwen3-8B学生均取得最强平均性能；在ALFWorld上，1.7B学生比最强基线提升+5.5%。增益还可迁移到其他模型家族与软件工程领域：在SWE-Bench Verified上，将Nemotron-3.5学生的解决率提升+3.2%。

Conclusion: 通过定位并纠正多轮交互中早期出现的可恢复关键错误，PivotOPD有效缓解了错误累积问题，在多个任务与模型家族上取得一致的性能提升，验证了预防与恢复联合蒸馏策略的有效性和可迁移性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) is a promising approach for training language agents, providing dense teacher supervision on student-generated trajectories. However, in multi-turn interaction, an incorrect action changes the states the student encounters later, so errors compound across turns. In preliminary experiments across three Qwen3 models (8B to 235B), we find that more than half of the failed rollouts contain a pivotal mistake, an action that moves the agent farther from completing the task, and this mistake typically occurs early. These pivotal mistakes often remain recoverable: guiding the model for only a few turns after the pivotal turn can restore task success. We therefore propose PivotOPD, an on-policy distillation framework that jointly trains the student to prevent pivotal mistakes and to recover from the states they create. At each pivotal mistake, a teacher model provides a gold action and then names a recovery action at each of the next few turns. Preventive distillation uses the gold action with reverse KL to steer the student away from the pivotal mistake, while recovery distillation uses the recovery actions with forward KL to transfer recovery behaviors that the student rarely samples. Against 13 baselines on ALFWorld, WebShop, and Search-based QA, PivotOPD achieves the strongest average performance for both Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3-8B students, improving over the strongest baseline on ALFWorld by +5.5% with the 1.7B student. The gains also transfer to another model family on the software engineering domain, where PivotOPD raises the resolve rate of a Nemotron-3.5 student on SWE-Bench Verified by +3.2%. Project page: https://research.nvidia.com/labs/lpr/pivotopd/

</details>


### [386] [How Much of a Harness Does a Strong Agent Need for Autonomous ML Engineering?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40303)
*Kirill Brilliantov,Alejandro Hernández-Cano,Emmanuel Abbé*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在相同时间预算和相同前沿LLM骨干下，开源最先进的MLE智能体框架相比仅用read/write/bash原语的极简编码智能体单会话基线并无优势，性能主要由骨干模型驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主MLE智能体多依赖复杂机械层（多智能体编排器、专用检索子智能体等），其动机常是长周期进度停滞与LLM原语有限；但更原始、更强的编码智能体（LLM通过read/write/bash直接访问执行环境）在MLE领域受到的关注很少，作者希望检验这些复杂框架是否真有价值。

Method: 在等时间预算与相同前沿LLM骨干条件下，对比开源最先进MLE智能体框架与极简编码智能体单会话基线，并进行一系列大规模系统性消融研究。

Result: 开源最先进框架未表现出优于极简编码智能体单会话基线的优势，表明骨干模型是性能的主要驱动因素；消融研究显示这些机械层在编码智能体设定下变成冗余。

Conclusion: 在强模型周围手工构建复杂框架对当前MLE基准回报很低，投入产出不划算。

Abstract: Recent autonomous machine learning engineering (MLE) agents have made significant progress on public leaderboards. Often motivated by progress stagnation over long-horizon cycles and limited Large Language Model (LLM) primitives, modern MLE agents are deployed on top of increasingly elaborate machinery: multi-agent orchestrators, dedicated retrieval subagents, and more. While such harnesses expand, the use of more primitive but improved coding agents - where LLMs have direct access to the execution environment through read, write, and bash primitives - has received little attention in the field. In this paper we find that, under an equal time budget and the same frontier LLM backbone, open-source state-of-the-art harnesses provide no advantages over a single session of a minimal-harness coding agent baseline, pointing to the backbone as the primary driver for performance. Via a series of large-scale systematic ablation studies, we argue that the machinery layers become redundant in the coding agent setting. We conclude that the effort spent elaborating hand-crafted harnesses around strong models yields poor returns for current MLE benchmarks.

</details>


### [387] [Cogentic: Multi-Agent Orchestration for Automated Proof Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40324)
*Yang Cai,Vineet Gupta,Yanchen Jiang,Christopher Liaw,Aranyak Mehta,Grigoris Velegkas,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Cogentic 是一个多智能体框架，通过"证明—验证"迭代循环自动发现开放研究问题的证明，用 Gemini 在五个开放问题上取得并经验证的新结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单次生成虽能产生不错的数学想法，但对需要探索多个竞争性猜想、克服细微技术障碍、并在长周期内保留中间进展的开放问题往往不足。

Method: 采用迭代的证明—验证循环：编排器将一组独立证明者分配到不同证明方向，由多个专门组件对其输出进行对抗式验证，并将确认的中间结果提升到持久化"已验证账本"中，供后续轮次累积使用。

Result: 以 Gemini 为基础模型，Cogentic 在在线学习、拍卖理论和机制设计领域的五个开放问题上产生了新结果，每个结果均经领域专家独立验证，并在配套论文中完整展开。

Conclusion: 该框架旨在解决研究级数学与理论计算机科学问题，展示了多智能体长程迭代证明发现的有效性，相关结果发布在指定网站上并持续更新。

Abstract: We present Cogentic, a multi-agent harness for automated proof discovery on open research problems. While frontier language models can generate strong mathematical ideas in a single shot, single-shot generation is often insufficient for open problems that require exploring multiple competing conjectures, overcoming subtle technical obstructions, and retaining intermediate progress over a long horizon. Cogentic addresses these challenges through an iterative prove--verify loop in which an orchestrator allocates a population of independent provers across distinct proof directions, subjects their output to adversarial verification by several specialized components, and promotes confirmed intermediate results into a persistent verified ledger that later rounds build on. The harness is designed to be able to solve research-level math and theoretical computer science problems. Using Gemini as the base model, Cogentic produced novel results on five open problems across online learning, auction theory, and mechanism design. Each result was independently verified by domain experts and is developed in full in companion papers. We list these results, and new ones as they are verified, at https://sites.google.com/view/cogentic .

</details>


### [388] [WorldAuditBench: Interactive 3D World Auditing with Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40325)
*Ziyan Jiang,Jingbo Yang,Jiabao Ji,Yujian Liu,Qiucheng Wu,Tommi Jaakkola,Yang Zhang,Shiyu Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 WorldAuditBench 基准，用于评估多模态智能体在交互式 3D 世界中"动作+视觉推理"耦合的异常审计能力，发现现有前沿模型成功率（6.6%–42.3%）远低于人类（83.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式 3D 世界日益用于研究智能行为，亟需高效的异常检测流程（如漂浮物体、可穿墙、与场景不一致的物体）。虽然 VLM 和 VLA 有望自动化该任务，但 3D 世界审计需要"动作"（系统高效导航搜索异常）与"视觉推理"（理解环境并识别异常）的紧耦合，多模态智能体能否有效耦合这两种能力仍基本未被探索。

Method: 构建 WorldAuditBench 基准：包含 13 个环境（基于 Unreal Engine 5 与 Three.js）、5 类异常家族、共 213 个异常任务。在固定探索预算下评估 5 个前沿模型，采用两种审计范式：(1) VLA 先探索、再由 VLM 识别异常；(2) 端到端 VLM 智能体，由视觉推理直接指导动作选择。

Result: 在两种范式与所有评估模型中，成功率介于 6.6% 至 42.3% 之间，显著低于人类表现（83.4%）。

Conclusion: WorldAuditBench 为研究多模态智能体在交互式 3D 环境中如何耦合动作与视觉推理提供了测试平台，同时揭示了当前模型在探索过程中收集与解读证据能力的局限性。

Abstract: As interactive 3D worlds are increasingly used to study intelligent behavior, it becomes important to develop efficient pipelines for identifying anomalies in these simulated environments, such as floating objects, traversable walls, or objects inconsistent with the surrounding scene. Multimodal AI systems, including vision-language models (VLMs) and vision-language-action models (VLAs), have shown potential for automating this task. However, 3D world auditing is complex, requiring the close coupling of two distinct capabilities: action, to navigate the 3D world and search for anomalies systematically and efficiently; and visual reasoning, to understand the environment and identify anomalies from multimodal observations. It remains largely unexplored whether multimodal agents can effectively couple these two capabilities, using visual reasoning to identify potential anomalies while taking actions to validate them. In this paper, we introduce WorldAuditBench, a benchmark for 3D world auditing comprising 213 anomaly tasks across 13 environments built with Unreal Engine 5 and Three.js, spanning five anomaly families. We evaluate five frontier models under a fixed exploration budget using two auditing paradigms: VLA-based exploration followed by VLM-based anomaly identification, and an end-to-end VLM agent in which visual reasoning directly guides action selection. Across the evaluated models and two paradigms, success rates range from 6.6% to 42.3%, substantially below human performance (83.4%). Through the task of world auditing, WorldAuditBench provides a testbed for studying how multimodal agents couple action and visual reasoning in interactive 3D environments, while highlighting current limitations in their ability to gather and interpret evidence during exploration.

</details>


### [389] [Turbo Harness: Instance-Adaptive Harness Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40330)
*Tunyu Zhang,Hao Wang,Kai Xu,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Turbo Harness 提出一种能将全局优化后的 harness 自适应到每个任务实例的框架，通过复用原优化过程中的信息生成实例特定的补丁，在七个基准上持续超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 harness 优化通常只产出单一全局 harness 并统一应用于所有任务实例，但平均表现良好的 harness 未必对每个实例都最优，因此需要面向实例的自适应优化方法。

Method: Turbo Harness 复用已完成全局 harness 优化过程中产生的产物，将其总结为结构化 playbook；训练一个 harness editor，利用该先验优化经验为全局 harness 生成实例特定的补丁；推理时编辑器根据实例与 playbook 构建定制化 harness，执行模型在其中运行。

Result: 在涵盖交互式智能体任务、软件工程和长时程终端任务的七个基准上，Turbo Harness 一致优于现有 harness 优化基线。

Conclusion: 通过回收已有优化经验并生成实例级 harness 补丁，Turbo Harness 有效实现了从全局优化到实例自适应的扩展，是推动智能体递归自我改进的重要一步。

Abstract: Automating the search for effective harnesses is an important step toward enabling agents to recursively self-improve. Existing harness optimizations typically produce a single global harness that is applied uniformly across task instances. However, a harness that works well on average may not be optimal for every instance. We introduce Turbo Harness, a framework that can adapt a globally optimized harness to each instance by reusing information generated during the original optimization process. Specifically, Turbo Harness recycles artifacts produced during a completed global harness optimization run, and summarizes them into a structured playbook. We train a harness editor to leverage this prior optimization experience to generate instance-specific patches to the global harness. At inference time, the editor uses the instance and the playbook to construct a tailored harness in which the execution model operates. Through numerical experiments, we show that Turbo Harness consistently outperforms existing harness optimization baselines across seven benchmarks spanning interactive agent tasks, software engineering, and long-horizon terminal tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [390] [EmAvatar: Multimodal Empathetic Response Generation via Conflict Resolution and Expressive Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38182)
*Xiaolin Chen,Xuemeng Song,Jinlan Fu,Weili Guan,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出EmAvatar框架，用于基于虚拟人的多模态共情响应生成，通过冲突感知的情绪识别与迭代精炼的响应合成，解决现有方法的三项关键局限，并在四项任务上超越当前最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于虚拟人的多模态共情响应生成是人本系统的重要能力，需识别用户情绪并同步生成文本、音频与说话人脸视频。现有方法存在三大局限：(1) 忽视跨模态的情绪冲突；(2) 缺乏显式的多模态合成指导；(3) 忽略多模态响应生成中固有的误差传播问题。

Method: 提出EmAvatar框架。情绪识别方面，先通过暴露模态间预测冲突进行审慎的多模态情绪识别，再由冲突检查器与证据收集器之间开展多轮问答以收集证据、消解冲突，得到稳健且具证据意识的预测。响应生成方面，先合成一个将文本响应与表达性指令耦合的复合脚本，并采用迭代精炼机制对脚本进行评估与修订，直至符合预设标准，从而为后续音频与视频合成提供可靠指导。

Result: 在四项任务上的大量实验表明，EmAvatar的性能优于当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: EmAvatar通过冲突感知的情绪识别与迭代精炼的脚本合成，有效提升多模态共情响应的准确性与表现力，代码将公开释放。

Abstract: Avatar-based multimodal empathetic response generation has emerged as a pivotal capability in human-centric systems, aiming to recognize user emotions and synthesize responses with synchronized text, audio, and talking-face video. Despite recent progress, existing methods still suffer from three critical limitations: (1) overlooking conflicting emotions across modalities, (2) lacking explicit multimodal synthesis guidance, and (3) neglecting inherent error propagation of multimodal response generation. To address these limitations, we propose EmAvatar, a novel framework for precise emotion perception and expressive response generation. It first performs deliberative multimodal emotion recognition by exposing inter-modal prediction conflicts and then initiates a multi-round QA process between a Conflict Inspector and an Evidence Collector to gather evidence for conflict resolution, leading to a robust, evidence-aware prediction. Regarding response generation, EmAvatar first synthesizes a composite script that couples the textual response with an expressive instruction. Moreover, to ensure high-quality synthesis, an iterative refinement mechanism evaluates and revises the script until it aligns with predefined criteria, serving as reliable guidance for subsequent audio and video synthesis. Extensive experiments across four tasks demonstrate that EmAvatar outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Our code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [391] [Why and How People Check Generative AI Output for Mistakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38186)
*Patrick Gage Kelley,Derrick Feldmann,Reena Jana,Colleen Thompson-Kuhn,Allison Woodruff*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过在美国开展1503人代表性在线调查，研究发现公众对生成式AI错误（如幻觉）有较高认知，且许多人会核查AI输出，并就错误说明与产品内披露提出建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI输出可能包含幻觉、答非所问或不准确、潜在有害内容，但公众对这类错误的认知、态度与行为尚不明确，因此需要实证调查来填补这一空白。

Method: 在美国开展在线问卷调查，样本为1503名具有人口代表性的受访者。

Result: 公众对生成式AI错误具有较高认知度；许多受访者表示会核查AI输出，例如与其他在线资源进行比对。

Conclusion: 研究为生成式AI错误的解释说明及产品内披露设计提供了指导建议。

Abstract: Generative AI output can contain errors, such as hallucinations, non-responsive results, or otherwise inaccurate or potentially harmful content. To explore the public's emerging understanding, attitudes, and behavior regarding such mistakes, we ran an online survey in the United States with 1,503 respondents, with a representative sample of the population. We report high public awareness of generative AI mistakes. Further, many respondents report checking generative AI output, for example, by comparing results with other online resources. We conclude with guidance for explanations and in-product disclosures about generative AI mistakes.

</details>


### [392] [AI-Powered Symptom Assessment and User Experience: A Case Study of Simtomi and Simtomi-Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38187)
*Jinha Lee,Chan Hyung Lee,Hyunsung Lee,Seunghwan Kim,Ban Hyung Lee,Minjun Shin,Hojin Shin,Jungdo Park*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文通过韩国与美国两个国家的实证研究，考察多语言症状评估应用Simtomi及其面向医疗服务提供者的平台在真实使用中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字症状检查器虽被广泛用于健康问题的快速指引，但许多系统在准确收集信息、支持沟通以及与临床工作流程整合方面仍面临挑战，因此需要探究这类工具在真实使用情境中的运作方式。

Method: 以Simtomi系统为案例，该系统将多语言症状评估应用与面向服务提供者的平台相结合；在韩国和美国两国开展实证研究，韩国基于参与者的亲身使用体验，美国则收集潜在用户与医疗专业人员的反馈。

Result: 在韩国，系统改善了患者对症状的沟通方式，并借助契合诊断推理的结构化摘要帮助临床医生更高效地审阅病例；在美国，潜在用户与医疗专业人员肯定了多语言支持、结构化问诊的价值，以及该系统协助临床协调的潜力。

Conclusion: 研究提供了基于真实情境的证据，说明基于AI的症状评估工具如何在不同医疗环境中运作，并为数字健康领域的可用性、信任度和有用性提供了更广泛的洞见。

Abstract: Digital symptom checkers are widely used for quick guidance on health concerns, yet many systems still face challenges in collecting accurate information, supporting communication, or integrating with clinical workflows. To explore how these tools function in real use, we examine the case of the Simtomi system, which pairs a multilingual symptom assessment application with a provider-facing platform. Empirical studies were conducted in two countries. In South Korea, based on participants' firsthand experience, we found that the system improved how patients communicated their symptoms and helped clinicians review cases more efficiently through structured summaries aligned with diagnostic reasoning. In the United States, responses from prospective users and healthcare professionals highlighted the value of multilingual support, structured questioning, and the system's potential to assist clinical coordination. These findings offer a grounded account of how AI-based symptom assessment tools can operate across different healthcare contexts and provide broader insight into usability, trust, and usefulness in digital health.

</details>


### [393] [Game-Based versus Training-Based Virtual Reality for Cognitive Rehabilitation in Mild Cognitive Impairment and Dementia: A Systematization of Design Paradigms, Outcomes, and Evaluation Rigor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38514)
*Anowarul Faruk Shishir,M. Rasel Mahmud*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文系统综述2015-2026年间34项VR认知康复研究，构建分类体系并发现"游戏式"与"训练式"范式在技术上高度重叠，同时提出VR-RES七维质量评估框架，揭示证据基础的常见局限并提出研究建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 治疗性锻炼可延缓痴呆认知衰退，但传统方案枯燥难坚持。VR提供游戏式与训练式两种替代路径，然而既往综述结论不一致，且两种标签是否代表技术上真正区分的系统尚不清楚。

Method: 遵循PRISMA 2020指南，系统梳理2015至2026年发表的34项研究，包括16项对照疗效试验和18项设计与可行性研究，涵盖轻度认知障碍、痴呆与阿尔茨海默病。构建涵盖设计范式、沉浸度、反馈模态、难度适配与硬件的分类体系，并提出七维VR-RES研究质量评估框架。

Result: 分析显示游戏式与训练式系统存在大量重叠，两类标签无法清晰区分其技术特征。VR-RES框架的应用揭示证据基础中的反复性局限，尤其是随访有限、可重复性差，以及对年龄、性别、教育程度等人口学因素分析不足。

Conclusion: 基于上述发现，为未来VR认知康复研究提出建议，强调具体设计特征、严格评估以及改进报告规范。

Abstract: Therapeutic exercises can help slow cognitive decline in dementia, but conventional programs are often tedious and difficult to sustain. Virtual reality (VR) offers an alternative through game-based approaches designed to improve engagement and training-based approaches focused on structured functional recovery. Prior reviews report inconsistent findings about these paradigms, while it remains unclear whether the labels represent systems that are technologically distinct. Following PRISMA 2020 guidelines, we systematize 34 studies published between 2015 and 2026, including 16 controlled efficacy trials and 18 design and feasibility studies involving mild cognitive impairment, dementia and Alzheimer's disease. We develop a taxonomy covering design paradigm, immersion, feedback modality, difficulty adaptation and hardware. Our analysis shows substantial overlap between game-based and training-based systems, suggesting that these labels do not clearly distinguish their technological characteristics. We also introduce VR-RES, a seven-dimensional framework for evaluating study quality. Its application highlights recurring limitations in the evidence base, particularly limited follow-up, poor reproducibility and insufficient analysis of demographic factors such as age, sex and education. Based on these findings, we provide recommendations for future VR cognitive rehabilitation research, emphasizing specific design features, rigorous evaluation and improved reporting practices.

</details>


### [394] [Beyond Monoscopic Viewing: A Study on 3D Gaussian Splatting Quality in VR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38525)
*Shreyas Shivakumara,Gabriel Eilertsen,Karljohan Lundin Palmerius*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 立体观影下，3DGS重建质量评价需立体评估，标准图像指标难以反映VR中的感知伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立体视觉是VR深度感知的基础，3DGS虽适合沉浸式VR，但真实采集视角有限时会产生局部漂浮物和错位结构，这些伪影在立体观看下尤为明显，而标准图像质量指标却无法捕捉。

Method: 将3DGS重建以立体方式渲染并在头显中评估；对比仅用SfM的基线与SfM结合稠密VGGT网络的联合初始化（其他训练组件固定，以隔离初始化覆盖的影响）；开展用户研究，比较单目与立体HMD观看下的偏好，并检验图像质量指标能否预测偏好。

Result: 单目偏好较弱，仅为58.4%；立体偏好升至78.2%且在所有被试中一致；而PSNR、SSIM、LPIPS及立体感知指标iSQoe与StereoQA差异均不大，未能有效惩罚这些伪影。

Conclusion: 单目评估和标准图像质量指标会显著低估3DGS重建在VR中的感知伪影，立体评估对VR中的3DGS质量评价至关重要。

Abstract: Stereoscopy is fundamental to virtual reality (VR), providing depth perception through binocular viewing. Recent advances in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enable high-quality novel view synthesis, making it well suited to immersive VR. We render 3DGS reconstructions stereoscopically and evaluate them in a head-mounted display, replicating how they would actually be viewed in VR. Real-world capture provides only a limited number of views, and under this constraint 3DGS reconstruction often produces localized floaters and misplaced structures. Standard metrics miss these localized artifacts, which become salient under stereoscopic viewing, where geometry is placed at the wrong depth. We investigate whether standard image-quality evaluation reflects the perceptual quality of 3DGS reconstructions under reduced capture. We compare SfM-only baseline with a union initialization that combines SfM with a dense VGGT network. All other training components are held fixed, isolating the effect of initialization coverage. We conduct a user study comparing preferences under monoscopic and stereoscopic HMD viewing, and test whether image-quality metrics predict the observed preferences. Monoscopically, preference for the more consistent reconstruction is weak, reaching 58.4\% overall. Stereoscopically, the same preference rises to 78.2\% and is consistent across all participants, while image-quality metrics (PSNR, SSIM and LPIPS) and stereo-aware metrics (iSQoe and StereoQA) show only modest differences and fail to penalize them. Our results indicate that monoscopic evaluation and standard image-quality metrics substantially underestimate perceptual artifacts observed in 3DGS reconstructions for VR, making stereoscopic assessment essential for 3DGS quality evaluation in VR.

</details>


### [395] [Fairness Theatre: Evaluating Post-Hoc Fairness Interventions in Vendor-Controlled Early Warning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38552)
*Kelly McConvey,Angelina Zhai,Rebecca Li,Shion Guha*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 在模拟采购约束下评估六种事后公平干预，发现干预多将差异在群体间重新分配而非真正减少，弱势小群体仍受忽视，形成“公平表演”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共机构（如高校）采购的AI系统其设计不可审查或修改，专有预警系统（EWS）使院校只能通过调整模型输出来应对不公平。研究关注：在供应商、机构、顾问、学生权力不对称的情况下，公平工作如何被协调？

Method: 使用加拿大安大略省一所公立大学的学生记录，在模拟采购约束条件下，对研究型EWS（预警系统）评估六种事后公平干预方法；比较公平性、准确率与人口统计学差异，并引入“错误类型画像”追踪假阳性与假阴性的重新分配。

Result: 干预只是重新分配了差异，并未持续减少差异。两种实现因用群体规模来定义“劣势”，反而有利于本已占优势的群体；规模小、边缘化的群体仍得不到良好服务。

Conclusion: 采购约束与实现选择共同塑造了公平工作的可能性空间；作者将由此产生的状况称为“公平表演”（fairness theatre）——仪表盘指标趋同，而各群体的错误负担依旧存在甚至恶化。

Abstract: Public institutions increasingly procure AI systems whose design they cannot inspect or change. In higher education, proprietary Early Warning Systems (EWS) leave colleges with few options beyond adjusting model outputs to address inequity. This raises the question of how fairness work is coordinated among vendors, institutions, advisors, and students with unequal power to change these systems? Using student records from a public college in Ontario, Canada, we evaluate six post-hoc fairness interventions on a research EWS under simulated procurement constraints. We compare fairness, accuracy, and demographic disparities, introducing error-type profiling to trace how interventions redistribute false positives and false negatives. Interventions redistributed disparities without consistently reducing them. Two implementations favored already-advantaged groups because they used group size to define disadvantage; small, marginalized groups remained poorly served. These findings show how procurement constraints and implementation choices shape the possibilities for fairness work. We call the resulting condition fairness theatre; dashboard metrics converge while groups' error burdens persist or worsen.

</details>


### [396] [C-LISTEN: Cognitive Load Impacts of Sensory-Triggered Environmental Navigation in Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38589)
*Md. Monowar Hossain,M. Rasel Mahmud*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本预研通过方向性听觉线索设计，验证其在VR中降低认知负荷、改善用户体验的作用，并探讨姿势平衡与瞳孔直径同认知负荷的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VR系统在决策、训练与康复中作用显著，但多模态设置带来平衡与认知困难；过度的听觉、视觉与感官刺激会导致表现下降、认知负荷升高、用户不堪重负，并限制VR的可及性。因此需要研究如何通过线索设计缓解认知负荷。

Method: 开展预研，策略性设计方向性听觉线索，用于虚拟环境中的导航任务；同时测量并分析姿势平衡与瞳孔直径，考察二者与认知负荷的相关性；对听觉反馈降低认知负荷的效果进行统计分析。

Result: 统计结果确认听觉反馈可降低VR中的认知负荷，改善具有统计学意义（p = .0011）；并得到姿势平衡、瞳孔直径与认知负荷之间的相关关系。

Conclusion: 听觉反馈能有效降低VR认知负荷，方向性听觉线索设计有助提升用户体验；研究提供了面向可及性的现实设计指南，使VR体验对包括认知障碍人群在内的所有用户更安全、更有用。

Abstract: Nowadays, Virtual Reality (VR) systems are more helpful for making decisions, training, and rehabilitation. Multimodal settings in these systems impose a significant balance and cognitive difficulties. Additionally, decreased performance, higher cognitive load, user overwhelm, and limited accessibility of VR technology can be caused by excessive auditory, visual, and sensory stimulation. In our pilot study, we examine the strategic design of directional auditory cues to improve cognitive load and enhance user experience in virtual environments. This research also enhances fundamental knowledge that auditory feedback reduces cognitive load in VR, with statistical analysis confirming this improvement (p = .0011). This study investigates how postural balance and pupil diameter correlate with cognitive load while the users navigate. Moreover, this study provides realistic overview design guidelines for accessibility, which will make VR experiences safer and more useful for everyone, including people with cognitive impairments.

</details>


### [397] [Afterglow: A Place-Based Memorial Ecology for AI-Mediated Pet Bereavement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38729)
*Hanjing Shi,Dominic DiFranzo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文通过设计研究（RtD）开发了宠物纪念AI系统Afterglow，探讨生成式AI如何在不为哀悼者制造情感义务的前提下支持持续性联结，并提出"Legible Restraint"与"Designing for Goodbye"两项设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宠物丧失常缺乏社会认可，生成式AI虽能为持续性联结提供"有回应的声音"，但安慰性回复可能越权以权威口吻给予宽恕或索取注意力，从而使纪念变成负担。研究旨在探索纪念系统如何在维持联结的同时避免将怀念转化为义务。

Method: 采用Research through Design方法，开发了移动世界Afterglow，整合私人纪念、人类见证与象征性宠物消息。先进行形成性问卷（N=57）指导初始设计，随后开展六轮在线圆桌走查，涉及20位独立参与者、两轮原型迭代。通过解释性分析提炼设计张力。

Result: 分析揭示三对核心张力：可回返联结与情感义务、可识别的相似性与本体论清晰性、保护性干预与监视性权威。

Conclusion: 论文提出"Legible Restraint"——一项跨层要求，确保对关系性权威的限制在说话者、生成语境、触发逻辑、数据使用与参与方式变化时依然有效；其时间性后果"Designing for Goodbye"主张纪念应保持可用，但不将持续使用作为获得关怀的条件。

Abstract: Pet bereavement often receives little social recognition. Generative AI can give a continuing bond a responsive voice, but a comforting reply may also claim authority to forgive or request attention. We investigate how a memorial can support connection without turning remembrance into obligation. Through Research through Design, we developed Afterglow, a mobile world connecting private remembrance, human witnessing, and symbolic Pet messages. A formative survey (N=57) informed the initial design, followed by six online roundtable walkthroughs with 20 unique participants across two prototype iterations. Our interpretive analysis develops tensions between returnable connection and emotional obligation, recognizable likeness and ontological clarity, and protective intervention and surveillant authority. We contribute Legible Restraint, a cross-layer requirement that limits on relational authority survive changes in speaker, generation context, trigger logic, data use, and participation. Its temporal consequence, Designing for Goodbye, keeps remembrance available without making continued use a condition of care.

</details>


### [398] [Where the Evidence Lives: Auditing AI Companions' Self-Descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38753)
*Seiya Ikeda,Shin-nosuke Ishikawa*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出对陪伴型智能体的"自我描述"进行审计的方法：将智能体的自述与其用户判断及实现记录对照，标记每条主张为"被支持""被矛盾"或"未解决"，并在自研的陪伴智能体Lita上进行了为期一个月、9名同事参与的部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 陪伴型智能体常声称自己能记住、理解用户，关系也改变了它们，且用户体验评分似乎印证了这些说法。但作者认为，只有证据落在用户可及之处（智能体的行为或用户自身）时，这类说法才可被用户检验；当证据存在于机器内部机制时，流畅的自我描述和中等偏正面的评分并不能证明背后的机制真的运行过。

Method: 设计了一套审计流程，将智能体的自我描述与三方面证据对照：用户的判断、智能体的实现记录（implementation records）。对每一条自述主张给出"被支持/被矛盾/未解决"的判定。将该流程应用于作者自建并部署一个月的主动型陪伴智能体Lita，参与者为九名同事。

Result: 参与者认可风格类主张，但对关系类主张不予认可；对记忆能力的评分处于中点或以上。然而实现记录显示，三层记忆中有两层的"累积步骤"从未真正执行过。

Conclusion: 用户评分与自我描述无法确立内部机制是否真正运行。具备记忆能力的智能体应当报告其自我描述所无法确立的内容（即提供可核验的实现层证据），而非仅依赖流畅自述与体验评分。

Abstract: Companion agents describe themselves: they remember, they understand their users, the relationship has changed them. We argue that such accounts, and the experience ratings that seem to confirm them, are checkable by users only where the evidence is theirs: in the agent's behavior, or in themselves. Where the evidence lives in the machinery, fluent self-description and moderately positive ratings do not establish that the mechanisms behind them ran. We demonstrate an audit procedure that sets an agent's self-description against its users' judgements and its implementation records, reporting each claim as supported, contradicted, or unresolved, and apply it to Lita, a proactive companion we built and deployed for a month with nine colleagues. Participants endorsed stylistic claims, withheld endorsement from relational ones, and rated memory at or above midpoint, while two of three memory layers had never executed their accumulation step. Memory-bearing agents should report what their self-descriptions cannot establish.

</details>


### [399] [Characterizing Questioning Patterns and Student Engagement Through Contextual Analysis of Real-Time Classroom Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38907)
*Rohit Sharma,Pavani Ayinampudi,Aditya B. M. V.,Jinal Gupta,Prakash Hegade,Sakshi Sharma,Meenakshi V,SRS Iyengar*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过将604个课堂投票题与讲座转录文本对齐分析，证明投票的答案与教学功能需结合语境解读，并揭示学生参与度与正确率、学生感知与实际表现之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时课堂投票虽已普及，但其数据通常被狭隘解读为正确率分数或投票人数，无法说明投票在讲座中的作用及其如何塑造学生参与。尤其对于True/False等简短回答题型，同一形式可用于测试记忆、检查理解或引导学生注意误导性陈述，因此有必要结合语境重新理解投票的意义。

Method: 分析一个自然语料库，涵盖39天内47场直播课程，包含604个投票问题和来自2,807名学习者的340,668条回答，多数为True/False题。将每个投票与时间对齐的讲座转录文本及出勤数据对照阅读，并辅以对579名学生的独立问卷调查。

Result: 结合语境阅读至关重要：89%的投票答案可在讲座中找到，且一种反复出现的注意力检查手法只能通过语境识别。提问绝大多数属于低阶认知，可归为七种教学功能，投票的回答跟随其功能而非措辞。参与广泛但集中，班级多数88.5%的情况下回答正确，但存在一小部分高共识却错误的答案，仅凭一致性无法检测。独立调查显示学生对投票性质与参与情况的认知一致，但存在感知与现实的差距：学生无法判断自己的正确性，且他们认为最难的投票并非他们答得最差的。

Conclusion: 课堂投票的意义需结合讲座语境与教学功能来解读，而非仅看正确率或参与人数；学生自我判断与投票的共识度均不足以可靠反映真实学习状况，提示教学评估应更重视语境化分析。

Abstract: Real-time classroom polling is now routine, yet the data it produces is usually read narrowly, as a correctness score or a headcount. Such readings say little about what a poll is doing within a lecture or how it shapes engagement. This is particularly relevant for short-response formats such as True/False, where the same question format can be used to test recall, check comprehension, or direct students' attention to a deliberately misleading statement. This study asks whether a poll's answer and instructional function can be determined by reading it against its lecture transcript, what cognitive levels of Bloom's taxonomy and instructional-function clusters the corpus contains, and how student engagement relates to answering correctly. We analyse a naturalistic corpus of 47 live sessions over 39 days, comprising 604 poll questions and 340,668 responses from 2,807 learners, most items True/False, read against time-aligned lecture transcripts and attendance. Reading each poll in context proves essential: the answer to 89% of polls is locatable in the lecture, and a recurring attention-checking device is visible only through context. Questioning is overwhelmingly lower-order and falls into seven instructional functions, and a poll's response follows its function rather than its wording. Engagement is broad but concentrated, and the class majority answers correctly 88.5% of the time, though a small set of high-consensus yet incorrect answers cannot be detected by agreement alone. An independent survey of 579 students agrees on what the polls are and on their participation, but reveals a gap between perception and reality: students cannot judge their own correctness, and the polls they find hardest are not those they answer worst.

</details>


### [400] [Measuring Student Self-Assessment against Viva-Demonstrated Mastery in a Large First-Year Programming Course](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38951)
*Sakshi Sharma,Pavani Ayinampudi,Aditya B. M. V.,Jinal Gupta,Prakash Hegade,Rohit Sharma,Meenakshi V,S. R. S. Iyengar*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究基于203名学生、11,093次编程任务自报数据，通过口头答辩验证，发现98.1%的自报掌握度得到证实，但高估主要集中在少数学生和任务阶梯末端的难题上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掌握式教育日益让学生在学习仪表盘上自行记录任务完成情况，但自报的有用性取决于其与真实掌握程度的一致性。已有研究多比较整体自评与整体考试成绩，难以说明究竟是哪些任务或哪些学生导致了偏差，因此需要任务层面的分析。

Method: 研究以2023年某大学一年级编程课程为对象，涉及203名学生和12名考官，共六十个编程任务构成任务阶梯。每个被自报完成的任务都需通过口头答辩（viva）验证。由于任务只有被自报后才会进入答辩，设计是单侧的，只能捕捉高估而不能捕捉低估。

Result: 在11,093个被自报的任务中，10,885个（98.1%）得到了演示验证，表明自报与掌握的对应度很高。其余208个高估并非均匀分布：少数学生贡献了大部分错误，且高估主要出现在任务阶梯末端较难的任务上，而非分值更高的任务上。

Conclusion: 任务层面的分析表明，整体自评准确性高可以同时存在特定领域的自报与掌握分歧。这为将额外验证和形成性反馈有针对性地投向更可能出现分歧的学生和任务提供了实践依据。

Abstract: Mastery-based education increasingly places the reporting of learning progress in students' hands, who record task completion on learning dashboards. The usefulness of such self-reports depends on how closely reported mastery corresponds to demonstrated competence. Most evidence on student self-assessment compares an overall self-rating with an overall examination score and therefore provides limited evidence about which tasks or which students account for the mismatch. This study examines first-year students' self-assessment against viva-demonstrated mastery at the level of individual tasks across a ladder of sixty programming tasks. The study draws on a large first-year programming course taught in 2023, involving 203 students and 12 examiners, in which every reported task was verified through an oral viva. Because a task entered the Viva only after it was reported, the design is one-sided and captures over-estimation but not under-estimation. Of 11,093 reported tasks, 10,885 (98.1%) were demonstrated, indicating a high degree of correspondence between self-report and demonstrated mastery. The remaining 208 overestimations were not evenly distributed. A small number of students accounted for most of the errors, and they occurred mainly on difficult tasks near the end of the task ladder rather than on higher-point tasks. This task-level analysis shows that high overall self-assessment accuracy can coexist with specific areas where reported and demonstrated mastery diverge. It also provides a practical basis for directing additional verification and formative feedback toward students and tasks where such divergence is more likely.

</details>


### [401] [An Audit of Measurement Quality and Answer Bias in a Large Classroom-Poll Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38959)
*Rohit Sharma,Pavani Ayinampudi,Aditya B. M. V.,Jinal Gupta,Prakash Hegade,Sakshi Sharma,Meenakshi V,SRS Iyengar*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文把真实课堂投票题当作测量工具进行审计，发现题目整体信度仅约0.60、约四分之一题目区分度差，且学生存在显著的"选True"倾向，导致False为正确答案的题目难度高出约13个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 课堂投票（含自动生成的题目）被广泛使用，但题目本身作为测量工具的质量几乎从未被评估，缺乏对其信度、区分度和作答偏差的系统检验。

Method: 审计604道真实课堂投票题（47个课堂、2,807名学生、340,668次作答）；对其中539道可从讲义转录稿核实正确答案的题目，使用项目反应理论（IRT）把题目与学生置于同一量尺上，并分析True/False题目的作答结构。

Result: 发现一：投票题构成一个连贯但偏易、精度中等的量表（边际信度约0.60），约四分之一题目几乎无法区分强、弱学生。发现二：学生存在稳健的"选True"倾向（77%的学生偏向True），而题目又偏向以False为答案，因此答案方向可预测难度，False为答案的题目约难13个百分点，该效应在控制题目内容与选择性作答后依然存在。

Conclusion: 课堂投票题作为测量工具质量有限且存在答案方向偏差；但这些指标所依赖的信号投票系统本身已记录，因此可在题目生成阶段、到达学生之前自动运行同样的检查。

Abstract: Real-time classroom polls are widely used and increasingly generated with automated assistance, yet the questions themselves are rarely evaluated as measurements. We audit a large corpus of authentic classroom polls, 604 items across 47 sessions answered 340,668 times by 2,807 learners, as a measurement instrument. For the 539 items whose correct answer could be established and verified from the lecture transcript, we place every item and every student on a common scale using item response theory and analyse the answer structure of the True/False items. Two findings emerge. First, the polls form a coherent but easy scale of moderate precision (marginal reliability about 0.60), on which roughly a quarter of items barely separate stronger from weaker students. Second, students show a robust tendency to answer True, present at the individual level (77% of students lean True), which meets a milder tendency for items to be keyed False; as a result answer direction predicts difficulty, False-keyed items being about thirteen points harder, and the effect survives controls for item content and for selective answering. Both findings rest on signals a polling system already records, so the same checks can be run as items are generated, before they reach students.

</details>


### [402] [What Students Actually Ask: Demand Structure and Automation Potential in a Hybrid Support System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38967)
*Jinal Gupta,Pavani Ayinampudi,Aditya B. M. V.,Prakash Hegade,Rohit Sharma,Sakshi Sharma,Meenakshi V,S. R. S. Iyengar*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 一项历时九周的AI查询分流平台研究，处理4,093条查询，通过AI助手、同伴互助、常见问题库和人工升级四条路径解决，发现需求集中、状态类查询是AI助手的盲区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型在线项目在用户入门阶段会收到大量查询，其规模增长速度超过可用工作人员的增长速度，因此需要研究如何用AI集成平台来分流和解决这些查询。

Method: 研究了一个AI集成的查询解决平台，将查询分流至四条路径：AI助手、同伴回答、精选常见问题语料库、升级给管理员。在2026年5月2日至7月6日的九周内，系统处理了1,434名参与者提出的4,093条查询，并对查询文本进行分类分析。

Result: 几乎所有查询都得到了记录的解决方案，五分之一的查询在一小时内关闭。114条语料库条目被复用吸收了21.3%的量，参与者自行解决12.2%，132名参与者互相答疑，中位时间为9至15分钟。查询需求较为集中：单一流程步骤（证书提交及随后的录用通知）占语料库中介解决的56.8%；至少20.4%的查询涉及待提交进度而非信息请求，而AI助手仅处理了这类查询的1.3%。人工处理的查询中只有8.0%与语料库内容重复。

Conclusion: 知识库已被较好利用，大多数直接的人工管理关闭发生在同步批量活动中，这一模式影响了记录应如何解读；AI助手适用于静态信息请求，但无法应对个体状态查询，需针对性设计。

Abstract: Large online programmes receive heavy volumes of queries during onboarding, at a scale that grows faster than the number of staff available to answer them. This paper reports a study of an AI integrated query resolution platform that spreads incoming queries across four routes: an AI based assistant, answers from fellow participants, a curated corpus of frequently asked questions, and escalation to administrators. Over nine weeks, from 2 May to 6 July 2026, the system handled 4,093 queries raised by 1,434 participants. Nearly every query reached a recorded resolution, and one query in five closed within an hour. Reuse of 114 corpus entries absorbed 21.3% of the volume, participants resolved a further 12.2% on their own, and 132 participants answered questions for one another at a median of 9 to 15 minutes, showing that peer answering, where it occurred, was fast and broadly shared across the cohort. Classifying the query text shows that demand was narrow rather than varied. A single process step, the submission of a certificate and the offer letter that follows it, accounts for 56.8% of corpus mediated resolutions, and at least 20.4% of queries concern the progress of a pending submission rather than a request for information, a class the assistant served only 1.3% of the time, since a stored answer cannot report an individual's current status. Only 8.0% of the queries handled by a person duplicated content already in the corpus, which indicates that the knowledge base was already well used. Most direct administrative closures occur in synchronous bulk events, a pattern that shapes how the records should be read.

</details>


### [403] [Insights on Student Learning from Live Classroom Polls: More Than Right or Wrong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38969)
*Rohit Sharma,Pavani Ayinampudi,Aditya B. M. V.,Jinal Gupta,Prakash Hegade,Sakshi Sharma,Meenakshi V,SRS Iyengar*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过一次真实课堂的大规模部署（340,668份提交、603次投票、47节课、约2,400名学习者），在不使用答案键的前提下分析答题顺序与选项分歧两类信号，发现答题顺序是稳定的个人特征，分歧能标记分裂课堂的问题，两者基本独立，可共同刻画一节课的学习者与问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 课堂实时投票通常只被当作参与度和正确率的计数来解读，但同一份记录在固定答案键之前还包含更多信息：谁答得早、班级如何在选项间分布。这些无答案键信号是否稳定、能揭示什么，此前未在真实课程规模上得到检验。

Method: 在一次真实课堂部署中收集340,668份提交，覆盖603次投票、47节课、约2,400名学习者。对每次投票读取学习者的作答顺序和班级选项分布的分散程度；随后仅在已有确定答案的题目上引入作者指定的答案键，用以解释高分歧题目。采用了校正后的折半信度等方法检验稳定性。

Result: 学习者答题顺序是稳定的个人特征，校正折半信度为0.92，单节课内为0.87，且与作答是否正确无关。不看答案键时，约每五次投票中有一次未形成明确多数，分歧标记了分裂班级的问题；在358道有确定答案的题目中，有49道多数意见本身错误，无答案键的分歧指标能标出其中大部分，但仅靠分歧无法区分集体错误与单纯难题。两种读数大体独立，仅在班级几乎达成一致的问题上，早答者能预示最终多数。

Conclusion: 答题顺序与选项分歧这两类无答案键信号相互独立且各自稳定，能够在不依赖答案键的情况下共同描绘一节课的学习者与问题结构。

Abstract: A live classroom poll is usually read through its answer key, as a count of participation and correctness. Yet the same record holds more before any key is fixed, namely who answers early and how the class divides across the options. Whether these key-free signals are stable, and what they reveal, has not been examined at the scale of a real course. We study them in a live deployment of 340,668 submissions across 603 polls in 47 sessions from roughly 2,400 learners, reading each poll for a learner's response order and for how much the class divides. A single further step brings in the author-designated key, on items with a settled answer, to interpret the divided ones. A learner's response order is a stable individual signature. It reproduces at a corrected split-half reliability of 0.92, holds at 0.87 within single sessions, and is unrelated to whether the learner is correct. Read without a key, about one poll in five does not reach a clear majority, and division marks the questions that split the class. On the settled-answer items the majority is itself wrong on 49 of 358. The key-free measure flags most of these, though division alone does not separate a collectively wrong answer from a merely hard one. The two readings are largely independent, meeting at one point where early responders anticipate the eventual majority only on questions the class has nearly settled. Together they map the learners and questions of a session without a key.

</details>


### [404] [When Attention Guardrails Become Barriers to Learning: Towards the Tipping Point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39023)
*Meenakshi V.,Pavani Ayinampudi,Aditya B. M. V.,Jinal Gupta,Prakash Hegade,Rohit Sharma,Sakshi Sharma,S. R. S. Iyengar*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 一项混合方法研究考察网课"注意力监控"（摄像头检核+视频中断/回退）对学习者整个课程周期的影响：51份自由文本中18人主动提及监控，其中4人将之列为退课主因；无人质疑"被观察"本身，争议集中在误判日常动作与惩罚过重（已看视频被迫回到本段开头）。研究据此指出两个再设计靶点：标记后的响应强度与环境检核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线学习灵活但缺乏课堂中教师在场对注意力的引导结构。该平台通过摄像头在普通课程学习中监控学习者，出现分心即中断或重启视频，以恢复这种结构。然而，此类监控对学习者"整门课程"层面的影响（而非单次考试情境）几乎未被研究，本文旨在填补这一空白，探究监控如何塑造学习者的去留与体验。

Method: 采用收敛式混合方法，研究某暑期实习项目中一条受监控课程流程。（1）两份自由文本问卷：退课者退出问卷（15份可分析）、留存者反思问卷（36份），两份问卷均未提及"监控"，所有提及均为受访者自发提出。（2）三条平台数据通道：摄像头验证遥测（448名学生、14,529次标记）、视频内情绪小组件（273名学生、615次提交）、强制课程结束问卷（单项最多634名受访者）。

Result: 51名自由文本受访者中18人主动提及注意力监控：退课者15人中7人，留存者36人中11人。提及监控的退课者中，7人里有4人将监控列为退课的"首要原因"。18人中无一人质疑"被观察"这一原则本身。学习者真正质疑的是：对日常动作（喝水、转头）的误读，以及标记后果的严厉程度——已看完的视频会被回退到该片段开头。此外，环境检核可能在学习者尚未看到任何内容前就将其标记。

Conclusion: 研究识别出两个再设计靶点：标记后响应的严厉程度，以及环境检核（可在无内容观看前即触发标记）。作者主张，响应的"比例性"正是注意力护栏转变为学习障碍的临界点，也是本研究试图逼近的"临界点"。

Abstract: Online learning offers flexibility but lacks the structure of a classroom, where a teacher's presence guides attention. The platform we study restores that structure by monitoring the learner through the webcam during ordinary coursework, interrupting or restarting a video when the learner appears distracted. What such monitoring does to a learner across a whole course, rather than in a single examination, is largely unexamined. We report a convergent mixed-methods study of one monitored course pipeline in a summer internship program. We read two free-text surveys alongside three platform channels. The dropout exit survey yielded 15 analyzable responses; the persisting-learner reflection survey, 36. The channels are camera-verification telemetry (14,529 flags from 448 students), an in-video emotion widget (615 submissions from 273 students), and a mandatory end-of-course survey (up to 634 respondents per item). Neither survey named monitoring, so every mention analyzed here was raised by the respondent. Focus-monitoring was raised by 18 of the 51 free-text respondents: 7 of 15 dropouts and 11 of 36 persisting learners. Among the dropouts who raised it, focus-monitoring was the stated primary cause of departure in 4 of 7 cases. None of the 18 questioned being observed in principle. What learners contest is the misreading of ordinary actions, drinking water or moving the head, and the severity of what follows a flag: a video already watched returns to the start of its segment. These findings identify two targets for redesign: the severity of the response to a flag, and the environment check, which can flag a learner before any content has been seen. We argue that the proportionality of that response marks the point at which an attention guardrail becomes a barrier to learning, the tipping point this study approaches.

</details>


### [405] [Present After Presence: Subtraction, Givenness, and the Structure of Being-There](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39289)
*Koichi Toida*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过"减法"方法考察沉浸式视频中"在场"（Presence）的构成条件，区分稳定条件、经验韧性形式与先验极限条件三层，主张"为我性"（for-me-ness）是先验极限条件，并提出沉浸式媒介作为"解离装置"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有在场研究多以"加法"方式推进，将沉浸、具身、能动性、所有权、共在、互惠与时间同步性视为稳定"身临其境感"的条件。然而沉浸式视频表明，若干条件被削弱后在场感仍未消失，因此需追问条件被逐步减除时究竟揭示出什么。

Method: 现象学论证（非实验论证）方法。区分三个不应混淆的层次：起支撑作用的稳定条件；经验上具韧性的、在场被体验为"此时此地"的表述形式；以及关涉经验第一人称被给予性的先验极限条件。结合胡塞尔的被给予性、Leibhaftigkeit与图像意识，并借助Zahavi的前反思自我意识与Derrida对自我在场的批判，重读Bodyless Presence与Bodyless Presentness。

Result: 减法为"此地"与"此时"的韧性提供了证据，但经验韧性本身并不足以确立构成性或先验必然性。作者论证"为我性"是使任何空间或时间性表述得以被体验的极限条件；Bodyless Presence被重读为揭示"此地"的韧性，Bodyless Presentness被重读为揭示"此时"的韧性。

Conclusion: 本文提出关于在场的一种分层说明，桥接操作性在场研究与胡塞尔式被给予性；并提出将沉浸式媒介视为"解离装置"，用以松解通常在体验性"身临其境"中耦合在一起的条件。

Abstract: Presence research has often proceeded by addition, treating immersion, embodiment, agency, ownership, co-presence, reciprocity, and temporal simultaneity as conditions that stabilise the sense of being there. Yet immersive video suggests that several of these conditions can be weakened without eliminating Presence. Building on Bodyless Presence and Bodyless Presentness, this paper asks what is disclosed when such conditions are progressively subtracted. I distinguish three levels that should not be conflated: stabilising conditions that strengthen or support Presence; empirically resilient articulation-forms through which Presence is lived as here and now; and a transcendental limit-condition concerning the first-personal givenness of experience. Subtraction provides evidence for the resilience of here and now, but empirical resilience does not by itself establish constitutivity or transcendental necessity. I argue, on phenomenological rather than experimental grounds, that for-me-ness names the limit-condition within which any such spatial or temporal articulation can be experienced at all. The paper bridges operational Presence research with Husserlian givenness, Leibhaftigkeit, and image consciousness; rereads Bodyless Presence as exposing the resilience of here; rereads Bodyless Presentness as exposing the resilience of now; and develops a stratified account of Presence through Zahavi's pre-reflective self-awareness while taking Derrida's critique of self-presence seriously. It concludes by proposing immersive media as dissociation apparatuses for loosening conditions ordinarily coupled within experiential there-ness.

</details>


### [406] [When Data Becomes Judgment: Misaligned Interpretations and Accountability in Food Delivery Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39311)
*Yibo Meng,Shuoning Shi,Bingyi Liu,Hongkai Chen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过23名用户与17名外卖骑手的访谈，揭示外卖平台实时追踪数据使用户对骑手产生道德化归因，而骑手则进行"预期性数据管理"以生成界面可读轨迹，形成负担转移机制，并提出情境透明化的设计方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外卖平台的实时追踪数据被呈现为客观信息，但实际上掩盖了塑造配送工作的情境约束，导致用户感知与骑手体验之间存在系统性错位。作者希望揭示这一错位的社会技术机制。

Method: 对中国外卖平台的23名用户和17名骑手进行半结构化访谈，开展真实场景下的实时移动追踪系统社会技术分析。

Result: 识别出两个相互关联的动态：一是用户处于"数据即行为"的解释框架中，将时空异常转化为对骑手疏忽的道德化判断；二是骑手进行"预期性数据管理"这一隐性数字劳动，重塑自身物理行为以产生界面可读而非物理最优的轨迹。

Conclusion: 两者共同暴露了当前追踪架构中的"负担转移机制"——这是去情境化界面设计的系统性结果而非设计者有意图的安排，使零工劳动者承担了大部分解释负担。研究提出朝向情境透明化的设计方向，将解释负担从个体劳动者重新分配给掌握物流情境的平台。

Abstract: Food delivery platforms mediate service encounters through real-time tracking data. While presented as objective, such data often obscures the situational constraints shaping delivery work, producing systematic misalignments between user perception and courier experience. This study presents a socio-technical analysis of real-time mobile tracking systems in the wild. Through semi-structured interviews with 23 users and 17 couriers on Chinese food delivery platforms, we identify two interrelated dynamics. First, users operate within a data-as-behavior interpretive framework, translating spatial and temporal anomalies into moralized judgments of courier negligence. Second, couriers engage in anticipatory data management, a form of hidden digital labor in which they reshape their physical behavior to produce interface-legible trajectories rather than physically optimal ones. Together, these findings expose a burden-shifting mechanism---characterizing the systemic outcomes of decontextualized interface design rather than explicit designer intent---in current tracking architectures, demonstrating how current tracking architectures leave gig workers bearing much of the explanatory burden. We propose design directions toward contextual transparency, redistributing this explanatory burden from individual workers to the platforms that possess the logistical context to bear it.

</details>


### [407] [NarrativeSteward: Coordinating Delegation, Guidance, and Verification in Agent-Assisted Interactive Narrative Authoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39333)
*Wenjin Wang,Jiazhen Lei,Yuxin Sha,Nuwa Xi,Meng Zhao,Xingxi Yin,Qi Liu,Yuliang Shen,Zixun Sun*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出NarrativeSteward，一个支持作者组织与引导自主AI代理进行交互叙事创作的写作环境，通过链接大纲、世界构建与叙事图谱等文档，帮助作者理解结构、审查修改并指导创作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI代理能够独立完成叙事内容的生成与修改，但随着内容规模扩大，作者难以把握整体结构、局部细节及其关系，从而难以持续引导创作。现有通用代理缺乏对项目结构与变更的有效组织与呈现，导致作者难以理解和控制创作过程。

Method: NarrativeSteward将大纲、世界构建与叙事图谱组织为相互链接的文档，供代理执行与作者引导；提供代理对话与项目级结构审查功能，帮助作者理解演进中的作品并引导局部与跨层修改；通过变更记录与执行验证帮助作者评估生成结果。系统通过技术测试验证变更记录、恢复机制与执行诊断能力，并开展12人被试内用户研究进行对比评估。

Result: 技术测试验证了系统的变更记录、恢复机制与执行诊断功能；在12名参与者的被试内研究中，相比通用代理，NarrativeSteward更便于作者提出修改请求和检查变更，并提升了作者对变更内容与故事结构的理解。定性研究结果揭示了审查作品与反馈如何帮助作者形成需求并指导后续委托。

Conclusion: NarrativeSteward通过结构化文档组织、代理对话、结构审查、变更记录与执行验证，有效支持作者理解并引导自主AI代理的叙事创作过程，优于通用代理，并已开源。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents can turn authors' goals into interactive narratives by independently organizing and carrying out generation and revision. As agents generate and revise extensive content, authors struggle to grasp its overall structure, local details, and relationships, complicating continued guidance. We present NarrativeSteward, an authoring environment that organizes outlines, worldbuilding, and narrative graphs as linked artifacts for agent implementation and author guidance. Agent dialogue and project-wide structural review help authors understand the evolving work and guide local and cross-layer revisions, while change records and execution verification help authors assess the resulting work. Technical tests validated the system's change records, recovery mechanisms, and execution diagnostics. In a 12-participant within-subject study, NarrativeSteward supported easier formulation of revision requests and inspection of changes, and greater perceived understanding of changes and story structure, than general-purpose agents. Qualitative findings show how reviewing the work and feedback helps authors develop requirements and guide subsequent delegation. We open-source NarrativeSteward at https://github.com/Tencent/NarrativeSteward.

</details>


### [408] [Aligning the Incomplete: Joint Distribution Calibration for Multimodal EEG-Eye Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39413)
*Yang Wu,Jinpeng Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出GUARD，通过梯度引导的无监督非对称分布恢复，统一解决跨被试多模态情感识别中EEG域偏移与眼动模态缺失共现的联合分布崩塌问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨被试多模态情感识别依赖个体间联合分布一致性，但实际部署中常出现"非对称联合分布崩塌"：EEG存在严重跨被试分布偏移，眼动模态易受丢包与跟踪失败影响。现有方法将域适应与缺失模态填补视为彼此独立的任务，无法应对两种退化同时发生时的复合误差，要么通过填补信号传播域偏移，要么破坏联合决策边界。

Method: GUARD包含三部分：(1) 在源域以理论支撑的梯度加权目标建立可靠锚定流形，迫使鲁棒的EEG模态预先纠缠任务判别性眼动特征；(2) 用下游感知损失约束生成模块，从结构上恢复崩塌的联合分布，优先保留情感判别语义而非单纯信号保真；(3) 将目标域适应建模为病态逆问题，通过循环一致流在目标域流形上实现恢复后联合分布的无监督校准。

Result: 大量实验表明，GUARD显著优于现有最先进方法，即使在辅助模态完全失效的情况下仍能保持具有韧性的判别性能。代码与模型已公开以保证完全可复现。

Conclusion: GUARD将域适应与缺失模态填补统一为联合分布恢复问题，通过梯度引导锚定、判别语义生成与循环一致的目标域校准，有效缓解非对称联合分布崩塌，提升跨被试多模态情感识别的鲁棒性。

Abstract: The success of cross-subject multimodal emotion recognition hinges on maintaining the consistency of the joint data distribution across individuals. However, real-world deployment frequently triggers the \emph{asymmetric joint distribution collapse}: EEG signals suffer from severe cross-subject distribution shifts, while eye movements sensors are susceptible to packet loss and tracking failures. Existing methods treat domain adaptation and missing-modality imputation as disjoint tasks. Consequently, they fail to resolve the compounded errors when both degradations co-occur, either propagating domain shifts through imputed signals or destroying the joint decision boundary. To tackle this unified challenge, we propose GUARD (\textbf{G}radient-guided \textbf{U}nsupervised \textbf{A}symmetric \textbf{R}ecovery of \textbf{D}istributions). First, GUARD establishes a reliable anchor manifold in the source domain by employing a theoretically grounded gradient-weighted objective, which forces the robust EEG modality to preemptively entangle task-discriminative ocular features. Next, to structurally recover the collapsed joint distribution, we constrain a generative module with downstream perceptual losses, prioritizing emotion-discriminative semantics over mere signal fidelity. Finally, we formulate target-domain adaptation as an ill-posed inverse problem. By driving a cycle-consistent flow, we achieve unsupervised calibration of the recovered joint distribution directly on the target-domain manifold. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GUARD significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods, maintaining resilient discriminative performance even under complete auxiliary modality failure. Our code and models are made publicly available to ensure complete reproducibility.

</details>


### [409] [IDEAL: A Multimodal Domain Adaptation Framework for EEG-Eye Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39421)
*Yang Wu,Jinpeng Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出IDEAL框架，通过实例级课程扩展与特征级层次对抗对齐，解决多模态EEG情绪识别中的跨个体域适应问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG情绪识别是人机交互的重要接口，但个体间生理差异大，且EEG与眼动等多模态信号异质性强。现有域适应方法多针对单模态场景，难以处理多模态异构信号；同时主要依赖特征级对齐，忽略数据级差异，导致细粒度判别信息在激进适应中被破坏。

Method: 提出IDEAL统一框架：1）多模型协同筛选机制，以数量-质量均衡策略将高置信度目标样本传播，从数据级显式弥合分布差距，并提供理论分析证明该实例扩展策略严格收紧目标风险上界；2）层次对抗网络，结合角度对比约束，从低层统计到高层语义逐步对齐表示，同时保持类可分性。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，IDEAL显著优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: IDEAL通过实例级扩展与层次对抗对齐的协同，有效缓解多模态跨个体EEG情绪识别中的域偏移，且代码已开源以支持复现与后续研究。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) emotion recognition serves as a pivotal interface for human-computer interaction, yet the physiological variability across individuals complicates the already challenging task of fusing heterogeneous physiological signals (e.g., EEG and eye movements). However, most prevalent domain adaptation paradigms are tailored for unimodal scenarios, failing to address the heterogeneity of multimodal signals. Furthermore, they predominantly rely on feature-level alignment, overlooking the fundamental data-level discrepancy, which risks compromising fine-grained discriminative information during aggressive adaptation. To bridge these coupled gaps, we propose Instance-based Domain Expansion and Adversarial Learning (IDEAL), a unified framework that synergizes instance-level curriculum expansion with feature-level hierarchical adversarial alignment. IDEAL first introduces a multi-model collaborative screening mechanism, which propagates high-confidence target samples to explicitly bridge the distributional gap at the data level via a quantity-quality equilibrium strategy. We provide a theoretical analysis that this instance expansion strategy strictly tightens the upper bound of the target risk. Subsequently, a hierarchical adversarial network, augmented with the angular-contrastive constraints, progressively aligns representations from low-level statistics to high-level semantics while preserving class separability. Extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets demonstrate that IDEAL significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods. To facilitate reproducibility and future research, our source code is publicly available at https://github.com/WY-BCI-Club/IDEAL.

</details>


### [410] [Engagement-Led Segmentation of Gamified Participation Data in a Large-Scale Remote Internship: A Mixed-Methods Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39750)
*Sakshi Sharma,Pavani Ayinampudi,Aditya B. M. V.,Jinal Gupta,Prakash Hegade,Rohit Sharma,Meenakshi V,S. R. S. Iyengar*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究将大型持续招生的远程实习中游戏化参与数据按时间与机会维度解读，通过K-means聚类识别出四类参与模式，并证实早期参与轨迹具有较强预测力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏化积分常被用于表示学习者参与度，但累计总分难以反映参与随时间的变化；在持续招生的项目中，学习者的积分积累机会本就不均等，因此需要更合理的参与度解读方式。

Method: 使用匿名运营数据集，先对3,607名已开始学习的学习者按参与状态分类，剔除休眠者，保留1,871名活跃学习者；对其在归一化的纵向及机会感知参与特征上做K-means聚类；另以597份感知问卷作为补充证据，并按分群进行联合展示；最后用首次四周的分群结果对照后续十一周平台记录进行验证。

Result: 识别出四种参与模式：Thriving Core、Tapering、Fast Starters、Occasional Participants，其轨迹在水平与方向上均有差异。感知问卷显示各分群看法不同，但个体层面感知与行为相关性较弱，低参与者也常报告积极看法及外部障碍。验证结果显示，Thriving Core中94%在后续至少参加十次活动、22%完成实习，而低参与分群分别低于7%和低于1%。

Conclusion: 游戏化参与数据应被解读为纵向的、机会感知的行为指标，而非单纯的累计分数，这样能提供更具信息量的参与洞察与早期预警价值。

Abstract: Gamified points are widely used to represent learner participation, but cumulative point totals provide limited information about how participation changes over time. This limitation matters in continuously enrolling programmes, where learners have different opportunities to accumulate points. This study examines how gamified participation data can be interpreted as indicators of behavioural engagement in a large-scale, continuously enrolling remote internship. Using an anonymised operational dataset, 3,607 distinct started learners were first classified by participation status, separating dormant learners from those with observable participation. The 1,871 active learners were then grouped using K-means clustering on normalised longitudinal and opportunity-aware participation features. Four participation patterns emerged: Thriving Core, Tapering, Fast Starters, and Occasional Participants. Their trajectories differed in both level and direction. A perception survey of 597 respondents provided complementary learner-reported evidence, integrated with the behavioural strand through a joint display by segment. Perceptions differed across segments, while individual-level correlations between perceptions and behaviour were small, and learners with low recorded participation could report positive perceptions of the points system alongside external barriers. Segments assigned from the first four weeks were then checked against eleven weeks of later platform records: 94% of the Thriving Core attended at least ten further sessions and 22% completed the internship, against under 7% and under 1% in the low-engagement segments. The findings suggest that gamified participation data are more informative when interpreted as longitudinal, opportunity-aware behavioural indicators rather than cumulative scores alone.

</details>


### [411] [Making Sense of Animal-to-Human Drug Development Evidence: Stakeholder Practices, Challenges, and Requirements for AI Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39808)
*Rosni Vasu,Simona E. Doneva,Benjamin V. Ineichen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对13位利益相关者的半结构化访谈，研究揭示了动物到人类转化证据使用中的差异、挑战与AI期望，并提出面向角色敏感型AI工具的设计启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物模型广泛用于研究人类生物学和健康干预，但从动物研究到人类的转化仍具挑战；证据贯穿临床前与临床研究以支持实验和转化决策。AI工具正改变证据检索、综合与使用方式，但尚不清楚应如何支持参与动物到人类证据评估的多元利益相关者。

Method: 开展半结构化访谈，访谈13位利益相关者，考察其证据实践、挑战以及对AI支持的期望。

Result: 利益相关者以不同目标、专业知识和启发式方法处理同一不完整证据基础；参与者重视AI在定位、筛选和提取证据方面的作用，但对自动化解释和质量判断更谨慎；他们强调透明性、来源可追溯、不确定性沟通和人类监督。

Conclusion: 基于发现，提出面向角色敏感型AI工具的设计启示，以支持更系统、透明地推理动物到人类转化。

Abstract: Animal models are widely used to study human biology and health interventions, yet translating findings from animal studies to humans remains challenging. Evidence across preclinical and clinical research informs experimental and translational decisions. Artificial Intelligence (AI) tools are increasingly reshaping how this evidence is searched, synthesized, and used, but it remains unclear how they should support the diverse stakeholders involved in assessing animal-to-human evidence. We conducted semi-structured interviews with 13 stakeholders to examine their evidence practices, challenges, and expectations for AI support. We found that stakeholders approach the same incomplete evidence base with different goals, expertise, and heuristics. Participants valued AI particularly for locating, screening, and extracting evidence, but were more cautious about automated interpretation and quality judgments. They emphasized transparency, source traceability, uncertainty communication, and human oversight. Based on these findings, we derive design implications for role-sensitive AI tools that support more systematic and transparent reasoning about animal-to-human translation.

</details>


### [412] [Understanding Parents' Complex Views of AI for Children's Pretend Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39906)
*Sungho Oh,Mohammad Namvarpour,Maxi Heitmayer,Minahil Khalid,Afsaneh Razi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对10位美国父母的半结构化访谈与定性系统映射分析，研究了家长对AI支持儿童假装游戏的看法，发现AI的同一特性既是机遇也是担忧，家长在缺乏理解与信任时反对AI介入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI可能支持儿童假装游戏，也可能替代儿童主导游戏，而AI能否在儿童生活中扮演角色仍有争议，因为其对儿童的影响尚不确定。研究旨在了解家长如何权衡AI在儿童假装游戏中的角色。

Method: 对10位美国父母（每人至少有一个4-15岁孩子）进行半结构化访谈，先描述AI支持假装游戏的概念并提供两个边界案例故事板；采用编码本主题分析，结合归纳编码与围绕研究问题组织的亲和图，再用定性系统映射考察主题内部及主题间的关系。

Result: AI的相同特性（如扮演角色、响应性、适应性）被家长视为既有用又令人担忧。家长认为AI可使角色扮演对所有儿童可及，或帮助家长参与家庭游戏；但在不清楚其运作方式及长期影响时反对使用。担忧包括儿童想象力与创造力丧失、对AI的情感依恋、人际互动减少、AI或儿童的不当行为以及无法管理儿童使用AI。家长将AI视为游戏工具、社会行动者以及家庭动态中的潜在扰动因素。

Conclusion: 除技术保障与家长控制外，AI与儿童互动的适当性成为AI进入儿童假装游戏的要求。研究贡献了对家长相互依存判断的整合性理解，并强调需要针对儿童及其多元家庭开展纵向研究。

Abstract: AI could support children's pretend play, but it could also direct the play on behalf of children. Whether AI should have roles in children's lives is controversial because its influence on children remains uncertain. We conducted semi-structured interviews with 10 U.S. parents, each with at least one child aged 4-15. During the interview, we described the concept of AI-supported pretend play and provided participants with two boundary-case storyboards. We analyzed the interview data through codebook thematic analysis, using inductive coding and affinity diagramming organized around the research questions, and then used qualitative systems mapping to examine relationships within and across themes. We found that the same characteristics of AI, e.g., ability to assume characters, responsiveness, and adaptability, were seen by parents as potentially useful but also concerning. Parents imagined that AI could make role-based play accessible to all children or help parents participate in family play. However, they opposed the idea of AI for children's play without a clear understanding of how it works and its long-term influence on their children. Parents worried about children's loss of imagination and creativity, emotional attachment to AI, reduced human interaction, inappropriate behavior by AI and/or children, and their inability to manage children's AI use. Parents viewed AI not only as a play tool but also as a social actor and a possible perturbation in the existing family dynamics. The appropriateness of AI and child--AI interactions therefore emerged as a requirement for AI in children's pretend play, in addition to technical safeguards and parental control. We contribute an integrated account of parents' interdependent judgments and emphasize the need for longitudinal research with children and their diverse families.

</details>


### [413] [Diptych: Scoped, AI-Interpreted Comparison for Reference Listening in Music Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39963)
*Chongjun Zhong,Abhinaba Roy,Archishman Ghosh,Kejun Zhang,Dorien Herremans*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Diptyph 是一个 AI 辅助的音频对比系统，允许用户自定义对比范围（整轨或自选片段），并查看结构化音频特征与针对该范围的 AI 解读。12 名音乐人的被试内研究显示，用户借此发现了更多差异，其中九成获得专家部分认可，系统可用性与后续步骤清晰度良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参考聆听是音乐制作中的常见策略，但现有对比工具往往掩盖了一个关键的人类判断：确定"应该对比什么"。作者认为 AI 辅助创作对比应优先支持用户自定义范围、可检视的证据与可操作的指导，而非越出证据支撑范围给出权威性判断。

Method: 作者提出 AI 辅助系统 Diptych，支持用户在整轨或独立选择的片段间定义对比范围，并可查看结构化音频特征与针对特定范围的 AI 解读。评估采用 12 名音乐人的被试内研究，并辅以 4 名专家听众在盲源条件下的评分。

Result: 参与者使用该系统发现了额外的差异，其中十条中有九条至少获得专家的部分支持；参与者报告了良好的可用性，并对可能的后续步骤有更清晰的认识。

Conclusion: 面向创造性对比的 AI 支持应优先考虑用户定义的范围、可检视的证据以及可操作的指导，同时避免给出超出证据所能支撑的权威性判断。

Abstract: Reference listening is a common strategy in music production, but current comparison tools often obscure a key human judgment: deciding what should be compared. We present Diptych, an AI-assisted system that lets users define comparison scope across whole tracks or independently selected segments, while inspecting structured audio features and scope-specific AI interpretations. We evaluated Diptych in a within-participants study with 12 musicians, complemented by source-blinded ratings from four expert listeners. Participants used the system to surface additional differences, nine of ten of which received at least partial expert support, and reported good usability and greater clarity about possible next steps. These findings suggest that AI support for creative comparison should prioritize user-defined scope, inspectable evidence, and actionable guidance, while avoiding authoritative judgments that exceed what the evidence can support.

</details>


### [414] [Richard: Voice-First Mobile Interaction for Persistent Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39976)
*Xinyang Chen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了以语音为先、围绕"请求与委托任务"组织的移动交互系统 Richard，通过持久化请求记录分离管理会话、任务执行与结果交付，实现间歇性参与下的任务连续性与用户控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动终端既要提供应用与网络服务，又要支持用户对自身注意力的控制。现有语音交互往往要求用户持续在场，难以适应中断、离开后再回来处理的场景，因此需要探索可委托、可回访的语音优先交互范式。

Method: 设计并实现系统原型 Richard：将语音会话、任务执行与结果交付分别管理，通过持久化请求记录相互关联；提供对话视图与任务视图作为视觉反馈；后端协调即时响应、专用服务操作与智能体任务，并让请求修订、执行状态与通知始终与对应任务绑定。评估采用 Android 功能记录、受控生命周期验证以及一个真实编程请求的执行记录。

Result: 受控验证可复现修订、确认后执行以及结果保留等行为；已部署服务记录显示客户端断开后仍能提供后端进度与失败反馈，表明任务可在用户离场后继续推进并保留状态。

Conclusion: 这些观察为移动语音交互中的任务连续性、用户控制与服务集成设计提供了经验，并为支持间歇性用户参与的个人计算设备奠定了实现基础。

Abstract: Mobile terminals need to provide application and network services while supporting users' control over their attention. We explore voice-first interaction organized around requests and delegated tasks, allowing users to leave a conversation and later inspect, revise, and retrieve the work. We present Richard, a system prototype that manages voice sessions, task execution, and result delivery separately, linking them through persistent request records. Conversation and task views provide visual feedback, while the backend coordinates immediate responses, dedicated service operations, and agent tasks. Request revisions, execution states, and notifications remain associated with the relevant task. We examine this design through Android functional records, controlled lifecycle verification, and execution records of a real programming request. Controlled verification reproduces revision, execution after confirmation, and result retention; deployed-service records show backend progress and failure feedback after client disconnection. These observations inform the design of task continuity, user control, and service integration in mobile voice interaction, providing an implementation basis for personal computing devices that accommodate intermittent user participation.

</details>


### [415] [Conversational Capture: A Trajectory-Level Framework for Evaluating Generative Engine Optimization in Multi-turn Human-Agent Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40069)
*Junwei Yu,Jieyu Zhou,Mufeng Yang,Yepeng Ding,Hiroyuki Sato*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出"会话捕获"现象，认为GEO评估不应只看单轮答案，而应关注多轮对话轨迹层面的累积可见性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GEO评估通常把单轮答案作为分析单位，即固定查询下测量来源在一次回答中的可见性。但作者认为这不够充分：人机信息寻求构成闭环，智能体答案会改变用户信念并影响后续提问，进而决定检索内容，因此早期被引用的来源会更易再次被引用。

Method: 作者将互动形式化为两层闭环系统，并推导轨迹层面的指标：累积会话可见性、轨迹增益的直接/反馈分解、反馈项的机器侧与人类侧嵌套拆分、捕获系数、复利比率和错排诊断。利用强化过程（Pólya瓮）理论进行证明，并用模型推导的示例进行说明。

Result: 证明单轮评估下反馈项为零；随着会话延长且捕获发展，GEO的累积收益呈超线性增长。示例显示反馈项可超过直接项，复利比率在十轮内超过2，单轮与轨迹排名仅弱一致（Kendall's τ=0.4）。

Conclusion: 作者将人类侧渠道与信息觅食、信任校准和贝叶斯说服相联系，并讨论了对答案引擎的设计启示，强调应从会话轨迹而非单轮答案来评估GEO。

Abstract: Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) shapes content to increase its likelihood of being cited by answer engines built on retrieval-augmented large language models. GEO is typically evaluated as a single-turn property: for a fixed query, an evaluator measures a source's visibility in one answer. We argue that the single answer is an inadequate unit of analysis. Human-agent information seeking forms a closed loop: the agent's answer changes the user's beliefs and therefore the next question, which in turn determines what the agent retrieves. We introduce conversational capture, a phenomenon in which a source cited early becomes substantially more likely to be cited again. Capture operates through a machine-side channel, history-conditioned retrieval, and a human-side channel, follow-up questions directed toward the captured source. We formalize the interaction as a two-layer closed-loop system and derive trajectory-level constructs: cumulative conversational visibility; a direct/feedback decomposition of trajectory gain; a nested split of the feedback term into machine-side and human-side channels; a capture coefficient; a compounding ratio; and a misranking diagnostic. Using reinforcement-process (Pólya-urn) theory, we prove that the feedback term is zero under single-turn evaluation and that GEO's cumulative payoff grows superlinearly with conversation length while capture develops. A model-derived illustration shows that the feedback term can exceed the direct term, the compounding ratio exceeds two within ten turns, and single-turn and trajectory rankings agree only weakly (Kendall's $τ= 0.4$). We connect the human channel to information foraging, trust calibration, and Bayesian persuasion, and discuss design implications for answer engines.

</details>


### [416] [Who Asked for This? Inline Annotations as Authoring Transactions for Provenance in Agentic Authoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40126)
*Chang Xiao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出 Reactant 交互范式：作者在文档中放置带类型的行内注解，配合可验证事务协议记录请求、技能身份与增删改对应关系，内核通过见证校验建立词级纵向溯源，实现 AI 代理写作过程的可追溯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用 AI 代理写作时，一段文字往往由多次请求共同产生，但最终文档几乎无法说明哪次请求造成了哪处改动，导致创作过程缺乏可追溯性与可复用性。

Method: 提出 Reactant 范式：作者在原始文档中插入带类型的行内注解；设计可验证事务协议，记录每次请求、技能身份以及标识新增、删除、转换的对应关系；由内核将见证（witness）与已记录状态进行校验，从而建立词级纵向谱系（word-level longitudinal lineage）。

Result: 以本论文自身的修订历史作为示范，并报告三位同事为期四个月的自导使用情况，其用途包括对历史的对话式询问，以及从重复出现的请求中提炼可复用技能。

Conclusion: 事务记录可作为可扩展的底层基座（extensible substrate），支持代理式写作的溯源、询问与技能复用。

Abstract: Writing with AI agents turns a paragraph into the outcome of many requests, yet the finished document rarely explains which request produced which change. We introduce Reactant, an interaction paradigm in which authors place typed inline annotations in their original documents. A verified transaction protocol records each request, skill identity, and the correspondences that identify additions, deletions, and transformations. The kernel validates the witness against the recorded states to establish word-level longitudinal lineage. We demonstrate Reactant through this paper's revision history, and report four months of three colleagues' self-directed use. Their uses include conversational inquiry into history and deriving reusable skills from recurring requests, illustrating how the transaction record serves as an extensible substrate for agentic authoring.

</details>
